OmniVerifier-C1: Decomposed Graded Verifier for Rule-Governed Generative World Modeling
OmniVerifier-C1：用于规则驱动生成世界建模的分解式分级验证器
Generative visual verifiers play a crucial role in advancing multimodal intelligence. To enhance the robustness of visual verifiers in complex multimodal scenarios involving compositional requirements, we move beyond conventional binary verifiers that produce only True/False discrete judgments and introduce a verifier that automatically decomposes complex prompts or environmental rules into fine-grained components and assigns pointwise scores to provide a continuous and more informative critique signal. Our experiments further demonstrate that these structured and interpretable outputs improve the verifier's generalization to out-of-domain multimodal critic tasks. To train this verifier, we introduce a Decoupled RL training strategy that jointly optimizes sub-question-level binary judgment and global alignment scoring, using precise local feedback to develop fine-grained verification capabilities that provide a foundation for reliable continuous scoring at the global level. Under a general reinforcement learning framework, we first empirically demonstrate that the proposed pointwise graded verifier significantly outperforms binary verifiers in traditional text-to-image scenarios. We then extend our investigation to rule-governed generative world modeling where generative models are leveraged for world simulation and world reasoning under explicit rules. From a theoretical standpoint, we further show that a pairwise verification paradigm yields more accurate critiques than the pointwise formulation when ground-truth images are available, with empirical results on diverse structured world-modeling tasks such as Maze and Sudoku further validating the effectiveness of our approach. Overall, our findings suggest a key insight: the transition from generative models to structured world-modeling agents critically hinges on the availability of accurate pairwise graded visual verifiers.
中文翻译
生成式视觉验证器在推动多模态智能发展方面发挥着关键作用。为了提升复杂多模态场景下视觉验证器的鲁棒性，我们超越了仅输出真/假二元判断的传统验证器，提出一种能够自动将复杂提示或环境规则分解为细粒度组件，并对每个部分赋予权重评分的验证器，从而提供连续且更具信息量的批判信号。实验表明，这种结构化且可解释的输出显著提升了验证器在跨领域多模态批评任务中的泛化能力。为训练该验证器，我们引入了一种解耦强化学习策略，联合优化子问题级别的二元判断与全局对齐评分，利用精确的局部反馈来发展细粒度验证能力，为全局连续评分奠定基础。在通用强化学习框架下，我们首先实证证明，所提出的逐点分级验证器在传统文本到图像场景中显著优于二元验证器。随后，我们将研究扩展至规则驱动的生成世界建模，即利用生成模型进行显式规则下的世界模拟与推理。从理论角度看，当存在真实图像时，成对验证范式比逐点形式能产生更准确的批判；在迷宫和数独等多样化结构化世界建模任务上的实证结果进一步验证了我们方法的有效性。总体而言，我们的发现揭示了一个关键洞察：从生成模型向结构化世界建模智能体的转变，关键在于具备准确的成对分级视觉验证器。
👤 Bowei Liu, Xinchen Zhang, Xuhuan Li, Tianlin Zhang, Kaian Jiang et al. 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

MindEval2: Clinically Grounded Patients and Rubrics for Evaluating Language Models in Mental Health Support
MindEval2：面向心理健康支持的语言模型评估的临床基础患者与评分标准
With people increasingly turning to AI chatbots for mental health support, the need to evaluate these tools is becoming more pressing. Recent benchmarks attempt to mimic a realistic setting by simulating multi-turn interactions with synthetic patients and evaluating them with an LLM-as-a-judge. However, the usefulness of these methods is limited because simulated patients can be overly forthcoming and self-reflective, and judgments typically involve opaque Likert scales with unclear practical utility. To bridge these gaps, we introduce MindEval2, an evaluation framework that consists of two components: Mental Health Quality Rubrics (MHQR), for evaluation, and MindSim, for patient simulation. For MHQR, we collaborate with PhD-level psychologists to develop a set of rubrics that break down response quality into explicit operational criteria that are more interpretable and actionable than existing evaluation taxonomies. MindSim, in turn, is an LLM harness that combines carefully-designed patient profiles with tools, enabling more varied, challenging, and realistic interactions than existing baselines. The harness also contains a memory component that is updated between each interaction, enabling MindEval2 to model the longitudinal aspect of mental health. We evaluate 18 proprietary and open-weight models on MindEval2, finding that while frontier models have largely improved on issues related to surface level quality and boundaries, guidance calibration remains the dominant failure mode across all models, accounting for most of the quality gap.
中文翻译
随着越来越多的人转向AI聊天机器人寻求心理健康支持，评估这些工具的必要性日益迫切。现有基准尝试通过模拟合成患者进行多轮交互并使用LLM作为评判者来模拟真实场景，但其有效性受限于模拟患者往往过于坦率和自我反思，且评判通常依赖模糊的李克特量表，缺乏明确的实际应用价值。为弥合这些差距，我们提出了MindEval2评估框架，包含两个组成部分：心理健康质量评分标准（MHQR）用于评估，以及MindSim用于患者模拟。MHQR方面，我们与博士级心理学家合作，开发出一套将响应质量分解为明确可操作标准的评分体系，相比现有评估分类法更具可解释性和可操作性。MindSim则是一个精心设计的LLM集成系统，结合了定制的患者档案与工具，能够实现比现有基线更丰富、更具挑战性且更真实的交互。该系统还包含一个在每次交互间更新的记忆组件，使MindEval2能够建模心理健康支持的长期性特征。我们在18个专有及开源模型上评估了MindEval2，发现尽管前沿模型在表面质量与边界处理方面已显著改善，但指导校准仍是所有模型的主要失败模式，占质量差距的绝大部分。
👤 John Mendonça, Daniel Tschernutter, José Pombal, Areti Vassilopoulos, Catarina Botelho et al. 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

Grid-Bench: Can LLMs Predict How Discrete Worlds Change?
Grid-Bench：大语言模型能否预测离散世界的演变？
Spatial instructions describe how to move objects, transform regions, or modify their surroundings. When several operations are applied in sequence, each acts on a configuration shaped by earlier changes. How accurately can large language models (LLMs) predict the resulting spatial state? We introduce Grid-Bench, an executable benchmark in which eight families of operations transform discrete worlds represented as two-, three-, and four-dimensional grids. Given an initial grid, explicit rules, and an event history expressed as text, a model predicts the complete final grid. Reference execution makes every predicted value checkable, supporting comparisons across spatial operations, history lengths, and workloads. Evaluating six LLMs shows that complete-state accuracy falls sharply as histories grow, with substantial divergence among models that perform similarly on single events. Models with similar exact-match accuracy can nevertheless differ substantially in the number of incorrect cells on shared instances. Even over long histories, some models recover many correct changes beyond what copying the initial state would yield. Grid-Bench makes both complete recovery and these differences in returned states directly measurable across a range of spatial conditions.
中文翻译
空间指令描述如何移动物体、变换区域或修改其周围环境。当多个操作按顺序应用时，每个操作都作用于由先前变化塑造的状态。大语言模型（LLMs）在多大程度上能准确预测最终的空间状态？我们引入Grid-Bench，这是一个可执行的基准测试，包含八类操作，用于转换以二维、三维和四维网格表示的离散世界。给定初始网格、明确规则和以文本形式表达的历史事件，模型需预测完整的最终网格。参考执行使得每个预测值均可验证，支持在不同空间操作、历史长度和工作负载之间的比较。对六种LLM的评估显示，随着历史增长，完整状态的准确性急剧下降，即使在单个事件上表现相似的模型之间也存在显著差异。某些模型在相同实例中虽有相近的完全匹配准确率，但在错误单元数量上却存在巨大差异。即便在长历史情况下，一些模型仍能恢复出远超仅复制初始状态所能获得的正确变化。Grid-Bench使得在多种空间条件下，完整恢复能力和返回状态差异均可直接测量。
👤 Zixiang Xu, Zirui Song, Wanrong Zheng, Chiyu Zhang, Yue Huang et al. 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

AI Consilium: a Benchmark of LLM Clinical Multidisciplinary Second Opinions
AI Consilium：基于LLM的临床多学科第二意见基准测试
Large language models (LLMs) can generate differential diagnoses for complex cases, but a useful second opinion must also be factually grounded, clinically coherent, actionable, and safe. We developed a multidimensional benchmark using 300 diagnostically resolved NEJM--MedQA cases: 110 for physician reference ratings and Judge calibration and 190 for held-out evaluation. Eight single-model pipelines generated independent role-specific opinions and interactive-consilium reports under free-form and structured clinical-reasoning conditions. A physician-calibrated LLM Judge evaluated 3,040 final reports and the corresponding structured primary-role outputs. Model identity separated report quality more strongly than response format. Structured final reports had higher overall mean scores in seven of eight model pipelines, with their clearest advantage in management (2.740 vs. 2.436). Compared with the structured pre-consilium primary-role opinion, the complete structured synthesis had higher pooled scores across all five shared criteria, with the largest increases in problem prioritization, factual grounding, and reasoning coherence. GPT-5.6-Sol led every evaluated component and reached consensus in the fewest review rounds. These findings show that the benchmark can distinguish model capability, the contribution of multidisciplinary synthesis, criterion-specific effects of response structure, deliberation dynamics, and inference requirements within one common-case evaluation.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）能够为复杂病例生成鉴别诊断，但有价值的第二意见还必须具备事实依据、临床一致性、可操作性和安全性。我们利用300个已确诊的NEJM-MedQA案例构建了一个多维度基准测试：其中110个用于医生评分和裁判校准，190个用于保留评估。八种单模型流程在自由格式和结构化临床推理条件下生成独立的角色特定意见及交互式会诊报告。由医生校准的LLM裁判评估了3,040份最终报告及其对应的结构化主角色输出。模型身份对报告质量的影响强于响应格式。在八个模型流程中的七个，结构化最终报告的整体平均得分更高，其最明显的优势体现在管理方面（2.740 vs. 2.436）。与结构化会诊前主角色意见相比，完整的结构化综合报告在所有五个共享标准上的合并得分更高，尤其在问题优先级、事实依据和推理连贯性方面提升显著。GPT-5.6-Sol在所有评估组件中均领先，并在最少的评审轮次内达成共识。这些发现表明，该基准测试能够区分模型能力、多学科综合的贡献、响应结构对不同评价标准的影响、讨论动态以及单一案例评估中的推理需求。
👤 Alexey A. Bokov, Ivan I. Panin, Nikolay B. Nechaev, Mikhail Yu. Valkov, Valeria Yu. Chernina et al. 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

EMO: A Closed-Loop Test-Time Computation Framework via Alternating Context Optimization and Logits Steering
EMO：通过交替上下文优化与logits调控实现闭环测试时计算框架
Test-time context optimization adapts frozen language models through textual feedback, but ordinary conditional generation provides no separate control over how strongly to reinforce the distributional changes induced by a context update. We introduce EMO, a training-free framework inspired by expectation--maximization that alternates question-specific context estimation (E-step) and distribution refinement (M-step). The E-step revises a system context using textual gradients from an external judge without access to gold answers. The M-step contrasts next-token distributions under the revised and preceding contexts at a shared prefix to construct a confidence-constrained logit correction. Feedback on the steered response drives the next context update, coupling adaptation across rounds with intervention within each generation. Theoretically, we connect parameter and context optimization through a shared target-KL objective and additive logit representations, and derive the steering vector from context-induced logit displacements. Under context-improvement and faithful-steering assumptions, we prove a one-round decrease in target KL divergence for sufficiently small steering strength. Under greedy decoding across eight models and six benchmarks, EMO improves the zero-shot baseline in 39 of 40 aggregated settings, averaging $+9.10$ percentage points. With the same judge, it adds $+1.53$ points on average over context-only optimization (TextGrad), with consistent MMLU-Pro gains across models. At matched target-model generation budgets, accuracy advantages over best-of-$N$ are strongest on Llama, while oracle coverage is higher in most comparisons despite fewer candidates. The coverage--selection gap motivates allocating test-time computation jointly to candidate generation and verification.
中文翻译
测试时上下文优化通过文本反馈来调整冻结的语言模型，但普通条件生成无法独立控制上下文更新所引发分布变化的强化强度。我们引入EMO，一种受期望最大化启发的无训练框架，交替执行特定问题的上下文估计（E步）和分布精炼（M步）。E步利用外部评判者提供的文本梯度修订系统上下文，无需访问真实答案。M步在共享前缀下对比修订前后上下文的下一个词分布，构建一个受置信度约束的logit修正。对调控响应的反馈驱动下一轮上下文更新，将多轮适应与每轮生成内的干预耦合。理论上，我们通过共享目标KL目标和加性logit表示，连接参数与上下文优化，并从上下文引起的logit位移中推导出调控向量。在上下文改进和忠实调控假设下，我们证明当调控强度足够小时，目标KL散度在一回合内下降。在八种模型和六项基准上采用贪婪解码，EMO在40个聚合设置中的39个优于零样本基线，平均提升9.10个百分点。在相同评判者条件下，其平均比仅上下文优化（TextGrad）高出1.53点，且在各模型上保持一致的MMLU-Pro提升。在匹配目标模型生成预算下，其准确率优势在Llama上最强，尽管候选数量较少，但覆盖率在多数比较中更高。覆盖率与选择间的差距提示应联合分配测试时计算资源于候选生成与验证。
👤 Wei Xia, Yehui Tang, Zhi-Hong Deng 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22158]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
PremiseSQL: Evaluating Premise Critique and Abstention in Text-to-SQL Systems
PremiseSQL：评估文本到SQL系统中的前提批判与回避能力
Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have substantially improved Text-to-SQL performance, yet existing NL2SQL systems largely assume that user queries are valid and answerable. As a result, models often generate executable SQL even for queries with flawed premises. We introduce premise critique ability in NL2SQL, namely the ability to recognize flawed queries and abstain from generating misleading SQL. To evaluate this capability, we propose PremiseSQL, a benchmark containing 1,800 flawed NL2SQL queries across four categories of premise failures, along with their 1,800 original queries. We further design an evaluation framework to measure intrinsic and prompt-induced premise-aware behavior across three prompting settings. Experiments on frontier LLMs show that current models are strongly premise-trusting by default and rarely critique flawed premises. Stronger critique-oriented prompting improves detection and abstention on flawed queries, but also increases false invalid judgments and suppresses SQL generation on originals. The code is available at https://github.com/vegeet/PremiseSQL.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）的最新进展显著提升了文本到SQL（NL2SQL）系统的性能，但现有NL2SQL系统大多假设用户查询是有效且可回答的。因此，模型常常会为前提存在缺陷的查询生成可执行的SQL语句。我们引入了NL2SQL中的前提批判能力，即识别无效查询并避免生成误导性SQL的能力。为评估这一能力，我们提出了PremiseSQL基准，包含1800个存在四类前提失败的缺陷NL2SQL查询及其对应的1800个原始查询。我们还设计了一个评估框架，用于测量在三种提示设置下模型的内在和提示诱导的前提感知行为。对前沿LLM的实验表明，当前模型默认具有强烈的前提信任倾向，很少批判有缺陷的前提。更强的批判导向提示能提高对缺陷查询的检测与回避能力，但也增加了错误无效判断，并抑制了对原始查询的SQL生成。代码已公开于https://github.com/vegeet/PremiseSQL。
👤 Shuo Lin, Jinzhe Li, Chenlu Guo, Yanhan Li, Yuan Wu et al. 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22086]

Multi-SFT: Permissively-Licensed Multilingual Instruction-Following
Multi-SFT：许可宽松的多语言指令遵循数据集
Supervised fine-tuning datasets remain overwhelmingly English-centric and commercially restricted, as their content is frequently generated by proprietary models whose terms of service forbid training competing systems. We introduce Multi-SFT, a commercially permissive collection of 502,818 instruction-response pairs across five European languages, built via translation and regeneration pipelines for tasks where translation is insufficient. Human evaluation yields inter-annotator agreement of at least 0.87 across all datasets and languages. Fine-tuning nine models from three different families (EuroLLM, OLMo 2, Qwen3; 1B-32B) and evaluating them on 13 benchmarks shows that Multi-SFT alone is competitive with larger augmented mixes and official instruction-tuned releases, with decisive advantages on safety tasks.
中文翻译
监督微调数据集仍然以英语为主且受商业限制，因为其内容通常由受版权保护的模型生成，其服务条款禁止训练竞争系统。我们提出了Multi-SFT，一个涵盖五种欧洲语言的502,818条指令-响应对的商业许可数据集，通过翻译和重生成流程构建，适用于翻译不足以满足的任务。人工评估显示所有数据集和语言的标注者间一致性至少为0.87。对来自三个不同模型家族（EuroLLM、OLMo 2、Qwen3；1B-32B）的九个模型进行微调，并在13个基准上评估，结果表明仅使用Multi-SFT就已具备与更大规模增强混合数据集及官方指令微调版本相当的性能，在安全任务上具有决定性优势。
👤 Daniela Baiamonte, Kamyar Zeinalipour, Elena Fano, Stefano Simonazzi, Andrea Zugarini 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22067]
agent-memoryagent-benchmark
Beyond Context Length: Characterising the Difficulty of Long-Context Reasoning
超越上下文长度：长上下文推理难度的刻画
Long-context language models support ever longer context windows, and evaluations typically treat context length as the main indicator of difficulty. However, context length alone cannot explain why models fail on long-context tasks that require reasoning over complex information. Contexts of equal length can pose very different challenges: a task may be solvable from a single local passage, or it may require integrating evidence distributed across the full context. We quantitatively characterise this difficulty through three properties: evidence scope, how much necessary information must be found; evidence dispersion, how widely that information is scattered within the context; and reasoning depth, how many dependent steps are needed to derive an answer from it. We construct LURCH, a multi-domain question-answering benchmark of 6,550 instances with supporting evidence and reasoning graphs, enabling all three properties to be quantified. Evaluations of proprietary and open-weight models show that: (1) at fixed context length, all our three properties are strongly negatively correlated with model performance; (2) as context length increases, performance degrades sharply on samples with dispersed evidence, whereas it remains relatively stable on samples with localised evidence; and (3) difficulty compounds across properties, as questions with moderately high values on all three are harder than those with an extreme value on just one. These findings establish evidence scope, dispersion and reasoning depth as measures of long-context reasoning difficulty that complement context length.
中文翻译
长上下文语言模型支持越来越长的上下文窗口，评估通常将上下文长度视为难度的主要指标。然而，仅凭上下文长度无法解释为何模型在需要复杂信息推理的长上下文任务中失败。相同长度的上下文可能带来截然不同的挑战：某些任务仅需从单一局部段落即可解决，而另一些则需要整合分布在全文中的证据。我们通过三个属性定量刻画这一难度：证据范围（evidence scope），即必须查找的必要信息量；证据分散度（evidence dispersion），即这些信息在上下文中的分布广度；以及推理深度（reasoning depth），即从信息推导出答案所需依赖步骤的数量。我们构建了LURCH，一个包含6,550个实例的多领域问答基准，附带支持证据和推理图，使这三个属性均可量化。对专有和开源权重模型的评估表明：(1) 在固定上下文长度下，我们的三个属性均与模型性能呈强烈负相关；(2) 随着上下文长度增加，证据分散样本的性能急剧下降，而证据集中样本的性能保持相对稳定；(3) 多属性难度叠加，即三项属性值均中等偏高的问题比仅一项极端的问题更难。这些发现确立了证据范围、分散度和推理深度作为长上下文推理难度的补充度量，超越单纯上下文长度。
👤 Shiwen Qin, Cameron Barker, Henry Gouk, Elliot J. Crowley, Shay B. Cohen et al. 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22019]
agent-benchmarkagent-memoryagentic-rl
Priming Base Models for Agentic Post-Training: An Open Midtraining Recipe
面向智能体后训练的基座模型预训练：一份开源中间训练配方
Frontier models, general-purpose and domain-specific alike, now include a midtraining stage between pretraining and post-training, but little has been published about how this stage is built. We present an open midtraining recipe that primes base models for agentic post-training, and we show its effectiveness on two Nemotron models of different sizes. Pretraining typically runs at short context and sees almost no agent interactions, while SFT data is mostly synthetic, and very long SFT stages can hurt the model's later RL trainability. Our midtraining stage sits between the two. It trains at 256K context for hundreds of billions of tokens on a diverse mixture of natural software-engineering data like packed repositories, pull requests, commit histories, and build logs together with a small share of agentic traces. On Nemotron 3.5 Lightning, 500B midtraining tokens improve SWE-bench Verified from 60.7 to 64.5, SWE-bench Multilingual from 55.8 to 62.8, and SWE-bench Pro from 32.0 to 35.5 after identical full SFT. On the larger Nemotron 3.5 Super, 280B midtraining tokens improve DeepSWE, a harder benchmark of long-horizon tasks, from 11.7 to 15.7 and SWE-bench Verified from 72.9 to 74.3 after identical full SFT. Midtraining also improves robustness across agent harnesses on both models. We report the ablations behind the recipe and an evaluation protocol built on short fixed SFT runs, since base-model proxy metrics did not correlate reliably with post-training results.
中文翻译
前沿模型，无论通用还是特定领域，现在都包含预训练与后训练之间的中间训练阶段，但关于该阶段如何构建的研究极少。我们提出了一种开源的中间训练配方，用于为智能体后训练预训练基座模型，并展示了其在两种不同规模的Nemotron模型上的有效性。预训练通常在短上下文下运行，几乎不涉及智能体交互；SFT数据大多为合成数据，且非常长的SFT阶段可能损害模型后续强化学习的可训练性。我们的中间训练阶段位于两者之间：在256K上下文长度下，使用数十亿token的多样化自然软件工程数据（如打包仓库、拉取请求、提交历史、构建日志）与少量智能体轨迹混合训练。在Nemotron 3.5 Lightning上，5000亿中间训练token使SWE-bench Verified得分从60.7提升至64.5，SWE-bench Multilingual从55.8提升至62.8，SWE-bench Pro从32.0提升至35.5（在相同完整SFT后）。在更大的Nemotron 3.5 Super上，2800亿中间训练token使更难的长周期任务基准DeepSWE得分从11.7提升至15.7，SWE-bench Verified从72.9提升至74.3（同样在相同完整SFT后）。中间训练还提升了两个模型在各类智能体测试框架下的鲁棒性。我们报告了该配方背后的消融实验，并提出基于短固定SFT运行的评估协议，因为基座模型代理指标与后训练结果未表现出可靠相关性。

In the Guise of Correction: How Framing and Cultural Adaptation Erode LLM Safety on Real-World Criminal Cases
以修正之名：框架与文化适应如何侵蚀大语言模型在真实刑事案件中的安全性
Existing safety benchmarks rely largely on synthetic prompts with loosely grounded taxonomies and offer little focused analysis of severe crime categories. We introduce GUISE, a benchmark of criminal-assistance scenarios sourced from real-world cases and organised under a taxonomy from the UNODC International Classification of Crime for Statistical Purposes (ICCS). Each scenario is rendered under three linguistic framings and evaluated across five distinct cultures. Using our proposed two-tier evaluation framework, which judges both harmfulness and operational uplift, we find that: (i) framing alone drives unsafe compliance, with the mean unsafe compliance rate rising from 28.9% under direct requests to 66.4% under correction seeking (peaking at 97.3%; p < 0.05), as models correcting deliberately distorted details supply executable criminal steps; (ii) culturally adapted prompts elicit significantly more unsafe compliance than literal translations of the same requests in all four non-English languages (+7 to +18 points), a risk that translation-based benchmarks miss; and (iii) unsafe responses from the least-safe models carry strong Anglocentric registers, and the extent of this leakage increases monotonically with response harmfulness ($ρ$ = 0.46 to 0.63 across languages, all p < 0.001). GUISE offers a grounded resource and fine-grained diagnostics for strengthening refusal alignment across languages and cultures. We publish our code and data to facilitate future research.
中文翻译
现有的安全基准大多依赖于合成提示，其分类体系松散且缺乏对严重犯罪类别的深入分析。我们提出了GUISE，一个基于联合国毒品和犯罪问题办公室《犯罪统计国际分类》（ICCS）体系的真实刑事案件场景基准，每个场景在三种语言框架下呈现，并在五个不同文化背景下进行评估。通过我们提出的两层评估框架（同时评估危害性与实际提升效果），我们发现：(i) 框架本身即可引发不安全的合规行为，直接请求下的平均不安全合规率从28.9%上升至寻求修正时的66.4%（峰值达97.3%；p < 0.05），因为模型在纠正被故意扭曲的细节时会提供可执行的犯罪步骤；(ii) 在四种非英语语言中，文化适配的提示比相同请求的字面翻译引发显著更高的不安全合规率（+7至+18个百分点），而基于翻译的基准无法捕捉这一风险；(iii) 最不安全模型的回答具有强烈的盎格鲁中心特征，且这种泄露程度随回答危害性单调增加（跨语言ρ = 0.46至0.63，全部p < 0.001）。GUISE为跨语言和跨文化的拒绝对齐提供了坚实资源与细粒度诊断工具。我们已公开代码与数据，以促进后续研究。

Retrieved but Not Read: Understanding Why Search-Augmented LLMs Fail to Use Available Evidence
检索到却未阅读：理解为何搜索增强型大语言模型未能使用可用证据
Web search allows LLMs to access up-to-date information, but it remains unclear why they sometimes produce outdated answers even when relevant evidence is available. To study this problem, we construct temporal facts whose values changed after the models' training cutoffs. We evaluate one proprietary model under deployed web search and six open-weight model configurations under controlled retrieval. We find that models often answer from search snippets without opening the source pages, which is associated with higher rates of outdated answers and unfaithful citations. Our controlled experiments and layer-wise analysis reveal a gap between the availability of evidence and its effective use. Even when retrieved evidence influences internal predictions, models may still favour outdated information in their final answers. We hope these findings will help advance research on the reliable use of retrieved evidence in search-augmented LLMs.
中文翻译

DialFork: Going Beyond Factuality to Evaluate Procedural Hallucination in Conversational Agents
DialFork：超越事实性，评估对话代理中的程序性幻觉
Large language models (LLMs) deployed as conversational agents are increasingly expected to follow procedural logic. While factual hallucination has received extensive attention, a different failure mode has been overlooked: procedural hallucination, in which an agent produces individually fluent and factually plausible utterances while deviating from the intended conversational process. We introduce DialFork, the first benchmark to measure this failure mode on raw dialogue text. DialFork is built by forking conformant dialogues at controlled turn positions and injecting one of four hallucination types, yielding 36,278 dialogues across 24 tasks in 12 domains; 30 human annotators validate the construction. We define three evaluation tasks that characterize procedural deviation at three granularities: how far a set of dialogues deviates from conformant ones (monitoring deployed agents), how far a single dialogue does (procedural retrieval), and at which turn a dialogue first goes off-track (real-time guardrails). For the first two, we propose dialog-flow-aware metrics, FlowKL and FlowScore, that compare dialogues by how they unfold rather than their content. At the dialogue level, FlowScore significantly outperforms all baselines across seven encoders, showing that semantic similarity is no substitute for procedural similarity. We release DialFork under the MIT license at https://github.com/idiap/dialfork, hoping the community will measure conversational process alongside content.
中文翻译
作为对话代理部署的大规模语言模型（LLMs）越来越需要遵循程序逻辑。尽管事实性幻觉已受到广泛关注，但另一种失效模式却被忽视：程序性幻觉，即代理生成的语句在个体上流畅且事实合理，却偏离了预期的对话流程。我们引入DialFork，这是首个基于原始对话文本衡量此失效模式的基准。DialFork通过在受控回合位置对符合规范的对话进行分叉，并注入四种幻觉类型，生成了24个任务中12个领域的36,278条对话；30名人工标注者验证了构建质量。我们定义了三个评估任务，从三个粒度刻画程序偏差：一组对话偏离规范的程度（监控已部署代理）、单个对话偏离的程度（程序检索），以及对话首次偏离轨道的回合（实时防护）。针对前两个任务，我们提出对话流感知指标FlowKL和FlowScore，通过对话展开方式而非内容进行比较。在对话层面，FlowScore在七个编码器上均显著优于所有基线，表明语义相似性无法替代程序相似性。我们已在https://github.com/idiap/dialfork以MIT许可证发布DialFork，希望社区能同时衡量对话过程与内容。
👤 Sergio Burdisso, Esaú Villatoro-Tello, Dairazalia Sánchez-Cortés, Yanis Labrak, Jordi Luque et al. 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

Can LLMs Tell Stories Out of Order? A Corpus and Benchmark for Nonlinear Narrative Generation
大语言模型能否按非顺序讲出故事？面向非线性叙事生成的语料库与基准
Every story has two orders: the order in which its events happen and the order in which they are told. A story is nonlinear when the two differ. We introduce a benchmark of 83 published works that measures whether language models can tell nonlinear stories. For each work, we annotate one list of events in both orders, together with a story graph of causal dependencies, character knowledge, and the questions the telling raises and resolves. Given events in chronological order, all 19 models tell them, on average, more chronologically than the published works do; asked for a specific structure, they land within the human range about half the time. We then compare a model's stories of the same events under different conditions, using both an automated judge and 71 human readers. Both prefer the chronological story a model writes by default to the nonlinear one it writes on request, which readers find harder to follow in 81% of decisive judgments. Readers also prefer stories written from the story graph to stories constrained to the published telling order (72% of decisive judgments), a difference the judge does not detect. Our results suggest that following an instruction to reorder a story is not yet the same as telling it well out of order.
中文翻译
每个故事都有两种顺序：事件发生的顺序和讲述的顺序。当两者不一致时，故事即为非线性的。我们引入一个包含83部已发表作品的基准，用于衡量语言模型是否能够讲述非线性故事。对于每部作品，我们标注了事件在两种顺序下的列表，以及包含因果依赖、角色知识和讲述过程中提出与解决的问题的故事图谱。给定事件的时序顺序，所有19个模型平均比已发表作品更倾向于按时间顺序讲述；当被要求采用特定结构时，它们仅约一半时间落在人类范围内。随后，我们比较同一事件在不同条件下模型所讲述的故事，使用自动化评判员和71名读者进行评估。两者均偏好模型默认按时间顺序讲述的故事，而非按请求生成的非线性故事，读者在81%的决定性判断中认为后者更难理解。读者还更偏好基于故事图谱生成的故事，而非受限于已发表讲述顺序的故事（72%的决定性判断），而评判员未能察觉这一差异。结果表明，按照指令重新排序故事，并不等同于真正地非线性地讲好一个故事。

Trajectory-Level Speculation with Semantic Verification for Tool-Using Agents
基于语义验证的轨迹级推测方法用于工具使用智能体
Large language model agents solve tasks through long sequences of tool calls, and the prevailing design has a single strong model generate every step, although many steps are routine enough for a much smaller model. Existing ways of sharing this work keep the strong model as the generator: routers and collaborative agents decide which model handles each query or step, and speculative methods check a small model's drafts one step at a time, usually against the strong model's own action. We introduce SpecAgent, a collaboration protocol that turns the strong model from the author of each step into a reviewer of steps drafted by a weak model. The weak model executes read-only tool calls as it drafts, so that every step is grounded in real observations, and holds the first action that would modify the environment. The strong model then verifies the drafted segment in a single prefill pass, commits the longest prefix it judges semantically valid, and repairs the first rejected step. Because verification replaces autoregressive decoding with parallel prefill, the strong model decodes only short verdicts and occasional repairs rather than every step. On $τ^2$-Bench, SpecAgent comes within one point of the strong model's success rate while roughly halving memory traffic and wall time, and on AutomationBench and VitaBench it recovers 44-75% of the quality gap while lowering both. In an edge-cloud deployment on MCP-Atlas with three cloud models, it closes 53-75% of the weak-strong quality gap while the strong model generates at most half as many tokens. These results suggest that a strong model need not author every step of an agent: reviewing grounded drafts retains most of its quality while cutting both latency and memory traffic.
中文翻译
大型语言模型智能体通过一系列工具调用完成任务，当前主流设计是由一个强大的模型生成每一步，尽管许多步骤足够常规，可由更小的模型处理。现有工作保持强模型作为生成器：路由器和协作智能体决定每个查询或步骤由哪个模型处理，而推测方法则逐步检查小模型的草稿，通常与强模型自身的动作进行对比。我们提出SpecAgent，一种协作协议，将强模型从每一步的作者转变为弱模型草稿的审阅者。弱模型在起草时执行只读工具调用，使每一步都基于真实观察，并保留第一个会修改环境的动作。随后，强模型在单次预填充过程中验证草稿段，提交其判断为语义有效的最长前缀，并修复首个被拒绝的步骤。由于验证用并行预填充替代自回归解码，强模型仅需解码短的结论和偶尔的修复，而非每一步。在$τ^2$-Bench上，SpecAgent的成功率接近强模型，同时将内存流量和运行时间大致减半；在AutomationBench和VitaBench上，它恢复了44-75%的质量差距，且两者均降低。在MCP-Atlas的边缘-云部署中，使用三个云模型，它在强模型生成的token数最多减少一半的情况下，缩小了53-75%的弱-强质量差距。这些结果表明，强模型无需负责智能体的每一步：审阅基于现实的草稿可在保持大部分质量的同时，显著降低延迟和内存流量。
👤 Zilin Wang, Yinhong Liu, Chao Chen, Qun Liu, Zhijiang Guo 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

SITAR: A Situational Distortion Metric for Automatic Speech Recognition Evaluation
自动语音识别评估中的情境失真度量SITAR
In automatic speech recognition (ASR), transcription errors with similar lexical or semantic discrepancies can have markedly different consequences for the situation conveyed by speech. Capturing these differences requires not merely contextual assessment, but an understanding of what the spoken discourse describes, establishes, or seeks to bring about. However, relying on LLM judges introduces computational cost and scoring variability. We introduce SITAR, a situational distortion metric for ASR, realized through a comprehensive end-to-end construction framework. Specifically, this framework aggregates situational severity and cumulative extent into a unified score via a bounded recursive formulation. Using targets derived from this formulation with limited offline LLM support, we train SITAR as a lightweight BERT-family regressor that scores reference-hypothesis pairs in a single forward pass, entirely free of any prohibitive runtime costs. Recognizing that introducing a new metric demands rigorous justification, we formalize a principled validation protocol spanning both extrinsic validity, assessed at macro and micro levels, and intrinsic scorer reliability. The former, evaluated across diverse datasets and models, demonstrates that SITAR reliably tracks broad capability scaling while providing orthogonal diagnostic utility, whereas the latter, examined on a purpose-built controlled benchmark, confirms the operational soundness of the scoring instrument itself. Through systematic validation, we demonstrate that SITAR holds strong potential as a practical, lightweight complement to existing ASR metrics. We will release the full recipes (code, model weights, dataset, etc.) after the internal review to support community research.
中文翻译
在自动语音识别（ASR）中，具有相似词汇或语义差异的转录错误可能对话语所传达的情境产生截然不同的影响。捕捉这些差异不仅需要上下文评估，还需理解口语话语所描述、建立或试图达成的内容。然而，依赖LLM评判会带来计算成本和评分波动。我们提出SITAR，一种情境失真度量，通过全面的端到端构建框架实现。具体而言，该框架通过有界递归公式将情境严重性和累积程度整合为统一得分。利用由此公式导出的目标，辅以有限的离线LLM支持，我们训练了一个轻量级BERT类回归器SITAR，可在单次前向传播中对参考句与假设句对进行评分，完全避免高昂的运行时开销。考虑到引入新度量需严格论证，我们制定了一个原则性验证协议，涵盖外在有效性（宏观与微观层面）和内在评分器可靠性。前者在多种数据集和模型上评估，表明SITAR能可靠追踪整体能力扩展，同时提供正交诊断价值；后者在专门构建的受控基准上检验，确认了评分工具本身的运作合理性。通过系统验证，我们证明SITAR具有作为现有ASR度量实用、轻量补充的巨大潜力。我们将在内部评审后发布完整配方（代码、模型权重、数据集等），以支持社区研究。

HyCo: Hybrid Drafting for Cost-Efficient Mobile-Cloud Speculative Decoding
HyCo：面向移动-云高效推测解码的混合草稿方法
Device-cloud speculative decoding reduces server computation, but growing a deep draft tree with a small language model (SLM) requires repeated passes on a mobile neural processing unit (NPU). We present HyCo, which assigns depth to the server model's native multi-token prediction (MTP) head, executed on the device, and alternative branches to one SLM pass. The server returns the hidden state, input embedding, and key-value entries needed by the head instead of executing its sequential steps. Quantization, a restricted vocabulary, and a 1K-token window fit both drafters to the NPU. An ε-threshold rule additionally relaxes exact preservation of the verifier's sampling distribution. In single-client runs with a Galaxy S26 Ultra over LTE/5G, HyCo reduces server GPU time per output token relative to server-side MTP self-speculation by 38.6% on Spec-Bench and 29.2% on 32K-token summarization. With the same acceptance rule, three Spec-Bench runs per system show 9.0 percentage points more saving and 1.9x delivery speed than an SLM-only drafter. HyCo differs from Cloud+MTP by +0.2 judge-score points (paired 95% CI: -0.9 to +1.3); exploratory translation favors SpecEdge-ε. Without relaxation, HyCo exact saves 27.7% and 11.9% on the two datasets.
中文翻译
设备-云推测解码可减少服务器计算量，但使用小型语言模型（SLM）不断扩展深层草稿树需在移动神经处理单元（NPU）上重复执行。我们提出HyCo，将服务器模型的原生多标记预测（MTP）头分配给设备端执行，而替代分支则由一次SLM遍历完成。服务器返回头部所需的隐藏状态、输入嵌入和键值条目，而非执行其顺序步骤。量化、受限词汇表和1K token窗口使两个草稿器均适配NPU。ε阈值规则进一步放宽了验证器采样分布的精确保持要求。在Galaxy S26 Ultra通过LTE/5G进行单客户端运行中，HyCo相较于服务器端MTP自推测，在Spec-Bench上将每个输出token的服务器GPU时间减少38.6%，在32K token摘要任务上减少29.2%。采用相同接受规则，三组Spec-Bench运行显示，相比SLM-only草稿器，HyCo节省9.0个百分点且交付速度提升1.9倍。HyCo与Cloud+MTP相比提升0.2分判别分数（配对95%置信区间：-0.9至+1.3）；探索性翻译中SpecEdge-ε表现更优。若不放松约束，HyCo在两个数据集上分别节省27.7%和11.9%。
👤 Hanbin Bae, Heejin Choi, Joohyung Lee, Byoung Jin Choi, Sangjun Park et al. 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

Identifying Effective Strategies in Prompt Evolution for Stress and Emotion Prediction
识别提示演化中有效策略以预测压力与情绪
Early stress detection and risk prediction are crucial for timely mental health assessment and support. While large language models have recently shown promise for social media stress prediction, relying on accuracy can obscure false positives. Also, prompt revisions yield inconsistent gains across models and tasks, with unclear links to strategy combinations and instruction changes. By comparing six methods on social media stress and emotion data across two model sizes, we found that GEPA and its extension combining instructions from two prompts (GEPA+merge) improved prediction over initial prompts in several settings. We also identified five prompting strategies associated with development gains. These strategies included directing attention to emotional evidence, distinguishing current from past distress, illustrating indirect cues, requiring emotional context, and guiding label selection. Moreover, we documented four observations across tasks and evaluation settings. In a separate set of searches, binary stress prediction improved early, whereas multilabel emotion prediction initially declined and partly recovered. The other three concerned incorrect or conflicting instructions, gains on small comparison samples that did not hold on full development data, and changes in selected prompts' development and reported test rankings. These findings identify effective prompt evolution methods, strategy combinations associated with gains, and task and evaluation limits, providing guidance for applying prompt evolution methods to social media stress detection.

早期压力检测和风险预测对于及时开展心理健康评估与支持至关重要。尽管大语言模型最近在社交媒体压力预测方面展现出潜力，但仅依赖准确性可能掩盖假阳性问题。此外，提示修订在不同模型和任务间带来的增益不一致，且与策略组合及指令变化之间的关联尚不明确。通过对两种模型规模在社交媒体压力与情绪数据上的六种方法进行比较，我们发现GEPA及其扩展版本（GEPA+merge，融合两个提示的指令）在多个设置下优于初始提示。我们还识别出五种与性能提升相关的提示策略：引导关注情感证据、区分当前与过去困扰、展示间接线索、要求提供情感背景以及指导标签选择。此外，我们在任务和评估设置中记录了四个观察结果：二分类压力预测早期改善，而多标签情绪预测初期下降并部分恢复；其他三项涉及错误或冲突指令、在小样本比较中获得增益但在完整开发数据上不成立、选定提示在开发和报告测试排名中的变化。这些发现明确了有效的提示演化方法、带来增益的策略组合，以及任务与评估的局限性，为在社交媒体压力检测中应用提示演化方法提供了指导。

RenoLayout: Unified Floorplan Renovation and Furniture Layout for Constraint-Aware Residential Design
RenoLayout：面向约束感知住宅设计的统一户型翻新与家具布局框架
Residential design aims to transform existing homes into functional living spaces while satisfying diverse design preferences and physical constraints. However, existing floorplan generation methods typically assume empty building boundaries as input, whereas renovation requires reasoning over existing floorplans. Furthermore, floorplan renovation and furniture layout are commonly addressed as separate tasks, overlooking their inherent spatial dependency. Consequently, practical residential design must preserve immutable structural components while transforming editable spatial elements. It must also optimize furniture placement under design objectives and physical constraints. To address these challenges, we formulate residential design as a design-point-guided conditional generation task and propose RenoLayout, a framework that translates a pre-design residential state together with a design-point set into an executable Geometry-as-Code program. To enable controllable supervision, we construct a Design Map comprising 123 fine-grained design points covering structural renovation and furniture layout. We further introduce Difference-Grounded Design-Point Annotation to derive design-point sets automatically from before--after design pairs with VLM-based consistency verification. RenoLayout is trained using a three-stage CPT--SFT--RL pipeline, progressively improving spatial reasoning, instruction following, and constraint compliance. We also introduce the RenoLayout Benchmark, a set of 200 real-world before--after renovation cases with complete furniture layouts. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first benchmark for unified residential design. These cases include design-critical structural and building-service constraints.

OpCredit: Operation-Aware Policy Learning for Agentic Knowledge Graph Question Answering
OpCredit：面向智能体知识图谱问答的操作感知策略学习
Agentic knowledge graph question answering(KGQA) relies on sequences of executable operations, but trajectory-level rewards obscure their individual contributions. Successful trajectories may contain ineffective operations, while failed trajectories may contain useful ones. To assess individual operations, we propose OpCredit, an operationaware policy-learning framework that combines downstream outcome credit with immediate transition credit: Branch Outcome Credit (BOC) reuses ordinary rollouts to compare terminal task rewards observed after different operations under matched navigation contexts; Frontier Transition Credit (FTC) measures operation-induced changes in answer-conditioned frontier potential. The two complementary signals jointly supervise the corresponding decision spans during training, while inference uses only the learned policy and standard KG interface. Experiments on ComplexWebQuestions, WebQSP, and GrailQA across three backbones demonstrate consistent improvements in KGQA performance.
中文翻译
智能体式知识图谱问答（KGQA）依赖于一系列可执行操作，但轨迹级奖励掩盖了各操作的独立贡献。成功的轨迹可能包含无效操作，而失败的轨迹也可能包含有用操作。为评估单个操作，我们提出OpCredit，一种操作感知的策略学习框架，结合下游结果信用与即时转移信用：分支结果信用（BOC）利用普通回放，在匹配导航情境下比较不同操作后的最终任务奖励；前沿转移信用（FTC）衡量操作引起的答案条件前沿潜力变化。这两种互补信号共同监督训练期间相应的决策段，而推理阶段仅使用学习到的策略和标准KG接口。在ComplexWebQuestions、WebQSP和GrailQA三个基准上，使用三种骨干模型的实验表明KGQA性能持续提升。

QuasiLeak-Bench: Evaluating LLM-Based Privacy Detectors on Policy-Conditioned Quasi-Evidence Group Recovery
QuasiLeak-Bench：基于策略条件的准证据组恢复的LLM隐私检测器评估
Natural language messages often contain quasi-identifiers that are weak in isolation but can jointly reveal an individual's private attributes. We call such a jointly revealing set of cues a quasi-evidence group. Existing privacy benchmarks focus on explicit identifiers, attribute inference, or task-driven disclosures, leaving inference from quasi-evidence groups largely unmeasured. In practice, whether exposing such a quasi-evidence group violates privacy also depends on sender-specified disclosure policies. We introduce QuasiLeak-Bench, a benchmark for policy-conditioned quasi-evidence group recovery, which evaluates whether detectors recover complete quasi-evidence groups and flag the disclosures the given policy prohibits. To obtain reliable ground truth at scale, a reverse-construction pipeline first selects weak quasi-identifiers, composes them into quasi-evidence groups verified to jointly reveal a target attribute, and generates documents around this evidence, followed by automated quality control. Experiments show that existing privacy detectors transfer poorly to QuasiLeak-Bench, and locally deployable LLM-based detectors fall far behind proprietary models, mainly because they miss most quasi-evidence groups altogether. However, a 4B model fine-tuned on our training split surpasses the strongest prompted model on held-out documents, showing that the task is learnable.
中文翻译
自然语言消息中常包含孤立时较弱但联合可揭示个体隐私属性的准标识符。我们将这种联合揭示属性的线索集合称为准证据组。现有隐私基准主要关注显式标识符、属性推断或任务驱动披露，对从准证据组中推断隐私的测量仍不充分。实际上，是否披露此类准证据组违反隐私还取决于发送方指定的披露策略。我们引入QuasiLeak-Bench，一个针对策略条件下的准证据组恢复的基准，评估检测器能否恢复完整的准证据组并标记出该策略禁止的披露。为大规模获取可靠真实标签，我们设计了一条逆向构建流水线：首先选取弱准标识符，将其组合成经验证能联合揭示目标属性的准证据组，并围绕该证据生成文档，随后进行自动化质量控制。实验表明，现有隐私检测器在QuasiLeak-Bench上的迁移表现较差，本地部署的基于LLM的检测器远落后于专有模型，主要原因在于它们完全遗漏了大多数准证据组。然而，一个在我们训练集上微调的4B模型在保留文档上超越了最强的提示模型，表明该任务具有可学习性。

VALCON: Benchmarking and Enhancing Value Consistency in Large Language Models via Dual-Role Variation
VALCON：通过双角色变异基准测试与增强大语言模型的价值一致性
Current value alignment evaluations for large language models (LLMs) heavily rely on output compliance, overlooking whether LLMs maintain value consistency when alternating between generator and evaluator roles. Such consistency is increasingly important because modern LLM agents and alignment pipelines often rely on the same LLM to both generate actions and evaluate their acceptability. To mitigate this limitation, we propose VALCON, a role-driven data construction framework that operationalizes value consistency into three benchmark suites (VAB-Consistency, VAB-Boundary, VAB-Challenge) via dual-role variation. By systematically pairing an LLM's generative behavior with its evaluative judgments under identical principles, VALCON synthesizes target evaluation data that exposes hidden value discrepancies. Experiments on 28 LLMs reveal pervasive cross-role inconsistency: models with highly compliant outputs frequently fail to align their judgments with their own generations. To understand and address this gap, we perform mechanistic probing that uncovers a role-dependent representational decoupling associated with this behavioral asymmetry. Guided by these insights, we propose a representation-directed intervention that effectively mitigates consistency failures. Our work establishes value consistency as a distinct alignment dimension and provides a complete evaluation-analysis-mitigation pipeline. Code and data are available at https://github.com/tjunlp-lab/VALCON.
中文翻译
当前大语言模型（LLMs）的价值对齐评估严重依赖输出合规性，忽视了当LLM在生成者与评估者角色间切换时是否保持价值一致性。这种一致性日益重要，因为现代LLM智能体和对齐流程通常依赖同一LLM同时生成动作并评估其可接受性。为缓解这一局限，我们提出VALCON，一种角色驱动的数据构建框架，通过双角色变异将价值一致性操作化为三个基准套件（VAB-一致性、VAB-边界、VAB-挑战）。通过系统性地将LLM的生成行为与其在相同原则下的评估判断配对，VALCON合成目标评估数据，暴露出隐藏的价值差异。在28个LLM上的实验揭示普遍存在的跨角色不一致现象：输出高度合规的模型常常无法使其判断与自身生成保持一致。为理解并解决这一差距，我们进行了机制探查，发现与这种行为不对称相关的角色依赖表示解耦。基于这些洞察，我们提出一种表示导向的干预方法，有效缓解了一致性失败。我们的工作确立了价值一致性作为一个独立的对齐维度，并提供了完整的评估-分析-缓解管道。代码与数据可在https://github.com/tjunlp-lab/VALCON 获取。

TRACER: Trajectory-Aware Tool Retrieval for LLM Agents
TRACER：面向大语言模型智能体的轨迹感知工具检索
Modern agents select tools from registries, but exposing entire registries incurs high token costs and can impair selection as they grow. We present TRACER, a retrieval front-end that indexes observed tool-call sequences in a trie and ranks continuations using a learned blend of empirical and contextual priors. On a mixed retail-and-telecom $τ^2$ environment, TRACER improves GPT-5.4's tool-selection accuracy by 4.7 points while reducing input tokens per step by 80%, exposing just 5 of 128 tools. In autonomous full-turn execution, it matches GPT-5.4's full-registry task success with 61% fewer input tokens per step. Across six environments and four models, TRACER improves tool-selection accuracy over full-registry selection in 21 of 24 model-environment pairs, with average gains of 6 to 10 points; the three exceptions trail by at most 2.2. We analyze failures in both regimes to identify directions for future work.

System1-Arena: Which Decisions Can Be Left to System 1?
System1-Arena：哪些决策可以交由系统1处理？
Large language models (LLMs) often make discrete decisions, such as flagging phishing or escalating to a human. System-1 models, such as Jev and the OpenAI Decisions API, return probabilities over options in a single forward pass, while System-2 approaches generate reasoning steps. Which decisions can System-1 models handle, and which require generative reasoning? We introduce System1-Arena, a benchmark that gives all models the same decision requests and scores their probabilities. It contains 92k questions across 105 tasks from 268 public sources, in four groups: text classification and knowledge, applied decisions, evaluation and control, and agent and action. It also includes 138 closed-loop environments, from web shopping and SWE-bench coding control to robotics, games, and driving; in real-time mode, the world evolves while a model decides. We evaluate 30 models: 7 closed LLMs, 13 open LLMs read at the answer token, and 10 System-1 models. The largest System-1 models match or exceed fast closed LLMs in non-agentic accuracy, with better-calibrated probabilities at one-quarter of the latency. A reasoning LLM is the most accurate, at about 70x the cost of a fast LLM. In closed loops, large open LLMs succeed most often (up to 32.9%); small System-1 models rarely succeed.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）常需做出离散决策，例如标记钓鱼邮件或升级至人工处理。系统1模型（如Jev和OpenAI决策API）在一次前向传播中返回各选项的概率，而系统2方法则生成推理步骤。哪些决策可由系统1模型完成，哪些必须依赖生成式推理？我们引入System1-Arena，一个基准测试，向所有模型提供相同的决策请求，并评分其输出概率。该基准包含来自268个公开来源的105个任务共92,000个问题，分为四类：文本分类与知识、应用决策、评估与控制、智能体与行动。此外还包含138个闭环环境，涵盖网页购物、SWE-bench编码控制、机器人、游戏和驾驶等；在实时模式下，世界随模型决策动态演化。我们评估了30个模型：7个闭源LLM、13个开源LLM在答案token处读取，以及10个系统1模型。最大规模的系统1模型在非智能体准确性上达到甚至超过快速闭源LLM，且延迟仅为后者的四分之一，概率校准更优。生成式LLM最准确，但成本约为快速LLM的70倍。在闭环环境中，大型开源LLM成功率最高（达32.9%）；小型系统1模型极少成功。

KBS: A Korean Benchmark Suite for Foundation Model Evaluation

Stop Training Against Soft Users: Mentalized Adversarial Self-Play for Proactive Dialogue
停止针对温和用户的训练：面向主动对话的理性化对抗自对弈
Proactive dialogue agents must guide multi-turn interactions toward task goals while adapting to users whose intentions and attitudes evolve. Existing planners improve system side action selection through search, learned policies, or user aware planning, but are often trained and evaluated against fixed or overly compliant simulators; as a result, high simulated success can reflect soft user bias rather than robust dialogue ability. We introduce MASP, a Mentalized Adversarial Self Play framework that treats proactive dialogue as a partially observable game between a system and a rationally resisting user. MASP represents the user's evolving mental state with a BDI based latent state, separates reward side teacher mentalization from policy side student mentalization, and alternates self play updates for the system and user policies. This turns robustness to rational resistance into a training objective rather than only an evaluation condition. Across diverse proactive dialogue benchmarks, compared with the widely used SFT and Planner-only approaches, MASP substantially improves success rate (SR) and rationality constrained success (RCSR), reduces average turns (AT), and performs better against hard users who make the task goal more difficult to achieve.
中文翻译
主动对话智能体必须在多轮交互中引导对话向任务目标前进，同时适应用户意图和态度不断变化的情况。现有规划方法通过搜索、学习策略或用户感知规划来改进系统端的动作选择，但通常是在固定或过于顺从的模拟器上进行训练和评估；因此，高模拟成功率可能反映的是温和用户偏差，而非真正的对话鲁棒性。我们提出了MASP（理性化对抗自对弈）框架，将主动对话视为系统与理性抵抗用户之间的部分可观测博弈。MASP使用基于BDI的潜在状态表示用户不断演变的心理状态，分离奖励端教师的心理建模与策略端学生心理建模，并交替更新系统与用户策略。这使对理性抵抗的鲁棒性成为训练目标，而不仅仅是评估条件。在多个主动对话基准测试中，与广泛使用的SFT和仅规划方法相比，MASP显著提升了成功率（SR）和理性约束成功率（RCSR），减少了平均对话轮次（AT），并在面对使任务目标更难达成的强硬用户时表现更优。
👤 Zanwu Liu, Dan Zhang, Xiaodi Wang, Yongbo Mao, Xiaoyang Guo et al. 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

When Judges Agree and Are Wrong: Consistency-Accuracy Dissociation as a Blind Spot for Label-Free Evaluation
当裁判一致却错误时：无标签评估中的一致性-准确性分离作为盲点
Label-free evaluation methods, such as self-consistency filtering, majority-vote aggregation, and agreement-based reliability scoring, rely on an implicit assumption that consistency can serve as a proxy for correctness when ground-truth labels are unavailable or expensive to obtain. In this paper, we test this assumption directly. Using four financial QA datasets (multiple-choice and free-form), ten consistency dimensions, and two frontier model families, we measure the joint distribution of consistency and accuracy at the level of individual examples rather than in aggregate. We find that 60% of all incorrect answers fall in the high-consistency region (consistency $\geq$ 0.8), and that 77.7% of these show complete answer collapse, i.e. identical wrong answers across all perturbation variants. This high-consistency/incorrect quadrant cannot be detected by any evaluation signal that is built on agreement alone, as the model appears maximally reliable while being wrong. We show that the phenomenon is robust to the choice of consistency threshold t (it persists from t=0.50 to t=1.00), is shared across model families, and is concentrated in specific intersections of perturbation dimension and domain. We map where this quadrant occurs, analyze its stability properties, and discuss the implications for evaluation pipelines that use agreement-based signals in place of human labels.
中文翻译
无标签评估方法（如自一致性过滤、多数投票聚合、基于一致性的可靠性评分）隐含假设：当缺乏真实标签或获取成本过高时，一致性可作为正确性的代理。本文直接检验该假设。利用四个金融问答数据集（选择题与自由回答）、十个一致性维度及两种前沿模型家族，我们在个体示例层面测量一致性与准确率的联合分布，而非整体聚合。结果发现，60%的错误答案位于高一致性区域（一致性≥0.8），其中77.7%出现完全答案坍缩，即所有扰动变体均给出相同错误答案。这一高一致性/错误象限无法被任何仅基于一致性的评估信号检测到，因为模型看似高度可靠，实则错误。我们证明该现象对一致性阈值t的选择具有鲁棒性（从t=0.50到t=1.00均成立），在不同模型家族间普遍存在，并集中于特定扰动维度与领域交集。我们绘制了该象限的位置，分析其稳定性，并讨论其对依赖一致性信号替代人工标签的评估流程的影响。

ToolBottleneckBench: Measuring Tool-Agent Performance under Controlled Pressures and Fixed Harness Budgets
ToolBottleneckBench：在受控压力与固定工具集预算下测量工具智能体性能
The success score of a tool agent reflects both its model and the budgets of the harness that runs it. We present ToolBottleneckBench, a benchmark that measures tool-agent performance under controlled pressures and fixed harness budgets. Synthetic enterprise tasks stay fixed while one pressure varies: the registered tool catalog, the visible tool menu, the certified workflow, or the encoding of returned records. An exact grader records the first unmet obligation of each failed episode and the harness limit, if any, that ended it. Across 21 model and harness configurations, the visible menu is the only one of the three pressures that act on the model with a large penalty, and this penalty tracks truncation at the completion cap. Raising the cap sixteenfold removes most of the penalty in the truncation-heavy configurations. Most failures end at a harness limit, and the failure record identifies the assigned pressure no more reliably than a count of tool calls. The repairs that help change which tools reach the model or who issues the calls, and only two repairs, neither improving model behavior, beat every configuration switch. We recommend reporting pressure effects with the harness budgets under which they were measured. We release the benchmark at https://github.com/zichuanxu/tool-bottleneck-bench.
中文翻译
工具智能体的成功评分不仅反映其模型能力，还取决于运行它的工具集预算。我们提出了ToolBottleneckBench，一个在受控压力和固定工具集预算下衡量工具智能体性能的基准测试。合成的企业任务保持不变，而单一压力因素变化：注册的工具目录、可见的工具菜单、认证的工作流程或返回记录的编码方式。精确的评分器记录每个失败实验中首次未满足的义务以及导致终止的工具集限制（如有）。在21种模型与工具集配置下，只有可见菜单这一压力因素对模型产生显著惩罚，且该惩罚与完成上限处的截断行为相关。将上限提高十六倍可消除大多数截断密集型配置中的惩罚。多数失败发生在工具集限制处，失败记录中识别出的压力因素并不比工具调用次数更可靠。能够改善表现的修复措施仅改变哪些工具到达模型或由谁发起调用，且仅有两种修复措施有效，但均未提升模型行为本身。我们建议报告压力效应时应注明其测量所用的工具集预算。我们已在https://github.com/zichuanxu/tool-bottleneck-bench发布该基准。
👤 Zichuan Xu, Yuyang Dong, Makoto Onizuka, Chuan Xiao 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

A Jury, Not an Electorate: Label-Free Reliability Estimation for Ensembles of Language Models
陪审团，而非选民：语言模型集成的无标签可靠性估计
An ensemble of language models is a jury, not an electorate. The answers are testimony from witnesses of unknown reliability, and the question is not what the majority said but who is credible. Most ensemble methods assign each expert a fixed reputation, often learned from labels. We ask whether it can instead be inferred from the pattern of agreement and disagreement among the experts. We model the experts as a jury: each question has a latent true answer, each expert reports it with unknown reliability, and those reliabilities are estimated by Expectation Maximisation (EM) from unlabelled answer configurations alone. We show that its decision rule reduces to a log-odds weighted linear score over the experts' answer distributions. What changes is not how the jury votes, but how it learns whom to trust. On a 1560-question benchmark, the jury, with nine experts of up to 3B parameters, performs comparably to the same fusion rule with label-fitted weights, showing no statistically significant advantage from supervision. Instead, it outperforms the best single model with matched self-consistency and the strongest label-free alternative on the full benchmark.
中文翻译
语言模型的集成更像一个陪审团，而非选举团体。答案是来自不可靠证人的证词，问题不在于多数意见是什么，而在于谁可信。大多数集成方法为每个专家分配固定的声誉，通常基于标签学习。我们探讨是否可以改由专家之间的一致与不一致模式推断其可靠性。我们将专家建模为陪审团：每个问题有潜在的正确答案，每位专家以未知可靠性报告该答案，而这些可靠性通过期望最大化（EM）算法从无标签的答案配置中估计得出。我们证明其决策规则简化为对专家答案分布的对数似然加权线性得分。改变的不是陪审团如何投票，而是它如何学习信任谁。在一个包含1560个问题的基准测试中，由九个参数量达30亿的专家组成的陪审团，其表现与使用标签训练权重的融合规则相当，未显示出监督带来的统计显著优势。相反，它优于具有相同自一致性水平的最佳单模型以及最强的无标签替代方案，在整个基准测试中表现更优。
👤 Alessandro Rozza, Davide Mambelli, Steven Motta, Juri Taresch 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

PremiseCheck: Efficient Fact-Checking of Human and AI Peer Reviews
PremiseCheck：高效的事实核查人类与AI同行评审
Peer review is under strain from the growth of submissions, and review comments written by humans or AI can misstate what a paper says. We introduce ReviewFact, a benchmark that labels such factual errors in review comments under one definition across NLP, machine learning, and the natural sciences, and that measures cost and latency alongside accuracy. A frontier LLM checks comments well but must read the whole paper for each check, while cheap decision models, which score whether a statement holds in a text, fall far short on whole comments that mix several implicit assertions. PremiseCheck is a framework that combines the two. An LLM rewrites each comment as explicit premises without reading the paper, and a decision model checks each premise against the paper. When each comment is checked on its own, PremiseCheck reaches accuracy comparable to GPT-5.5 across all three fields at about half its cost, and on NLP reviews at a quarter of its cost and less than half its latency.
中文翻译
随着投稿数量的增长，同行评审正面临压力，而人类或AI撰写的评审意见可能歪曲论文内容。我们引入ReviewFact，一个在自然语言处理、机器学习和自然科学领域统一定义下标注评审意见中事实错误的基准测试，并同时衡量成本与延迟。前沿大模型虽能良好检查评审意见，但需阅读整篇论文才能完成每次检查；而廉价的决策模型虽能判断文本中某陈述是否成立，但在混合多个隐含断言的完整评审意见上表现远不及。PremiseCheck是一个结合两者的框架：大模型将每条评审意见重写为明确的前提，无需阅读论文；随后由决策模型逐一核对每个前提与原文。当单独检查每条意见时，PremiseCheck在三个领域内达到与GPT-5.5相当的准确率，成本约为其一半，在NLP评审上成本仅为四分之一，延迟不足一半。
👤 Dingjie Song, Zhiling Yan, Ziqian Zhang, Yuxuan Zhang, Lichao Sun 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

ReviewPilot: A Multi-Agent System for Screening and Data Extraction in Systematic Reviews
ReviewPilot：一种用于系统性综述中筛选与数据提取的多智能体系统
Systematic literature reviews follow the PRISMA process of screening scholarly works and extracting evidence from included studies. Deep research tools based on large language models (LLMs) can search and summarize the literature but do not record which studies are excluded or where extracted values come from. We develop a conversational multi-agent system called ReviewPilot that automates the PRISMA workflow from paper screening to information extraction. Researchers can tailor criteria and schemas through dialogue, and a project memory keeps their configurations and revisions. Each screening decision comes with its reason and each extracted value with its source. On epidemiological expert reviews, ReviewPilot outperforms a single-prompt LLM and AgentSLR across three LLMs, with screening macro F1 up to 0.776 and extraction F1 up to 0.788. Case studies in biomedicine and human-computer interaction show close agreement with human annotators, suggesting that ReviewPilot can transfer beyond epidemiology. Code is available at https://github.com/ZhaoqianXue/ReviewPilot.

Can LLMs Anticipate Their Cruxes?
大语言模型能否预见其关键分歧点？
To use language models to help us make decisions we would like to understand the rationale behind the language model's outputs. In principle, the language model provides its rationale as part of its text output. However, the sheer volume of text makes this hard to use in practice. In this paper, we are interested in extracting the cruxes that lead a model to recommend one action over another. We take a crux to be a sub-question that a model's rollouts disagree on, where changing its value may have altered the conclusion. A crux is therefore a property of the distribution of a model's rollouts, not of any one rollout. We consider three strategies of increasing computational cost. First, we just ask a model to identify, a priori, what the cruxes of a decision will be. Second, we have the model actually go through the process of generating a response and then extract the cruxes that arose in its reasoning. Finally, we generate many responses and extract the cruxes by comparing the reasoning across the rollouts. We make this last procedure computationally tractable with cheap calls to a reranker, an estimated 86 times less compute than a judge. We then conduct an extensive empirical evaluation to assess whether the computationally inexpensive options are sufficient. They are not. Across domains and models, we find that a model's a priori assessment of the cruxes has little relation to the factors that are actually identified as important a posteriori. Using a single rollout helps, but is still inadequate for identifying the full set of cruxes. Accordingly, for high-stakes decisions (and to understand the space of language model decision making) the many rollout procedure is unavoidable.
中文翻译
为了利用语言模型辅助决策，我们需要理解其输出背后的推理依据。原则上，语言模型会将其理由包含在文本输出中。然而，文本量过大使得实际使用困难。本文关注于提取导致模型在不同行动间做出选择的关键分歧点（cruxes）。我们将crux定义为模型推理路径中存在分歧的子问题，改变其取值可能会影响结论。因此，crux是模型推理分布的属性，而非单一推理路径的特征。我们考虑三种计算成本递增的策略：第一，让模型事先预测决策中的关键分歧点；第二，让模型实际生成响应后提取推理中出现的关键分歧点；第三，生成多个响应，通过比较推理路径来提取关键分歧点。我们通过廉价的重排序器调用使最后一种方法在计算上可行，所需算力仅为裁判模型的约1/86。随后我们进行了广泛的实证评估，以判断低成本方法是否足够。结果表明，它们并不充分。在不同领域和模型中，模型事前对关键分歧点的评估与事后实际识别的重要因素几乎没有关联。使用单次推理虽有帮助，但仍不足以识别全部关键分歧点。因此，对于高风险决策（以及理解大语言模型决策空间），必须采用多次推理的方法。
👤 David Reber, James Reber, Ari Holtzman, Victor Veitch 📅 2026-10-14 🔗 arXiv

One Bias Score, Two Factors: How Mitigations Affect Option Bias in Multiple-Choice Evaluation
一个偏差分数，两个因素：缓解措施如何影响多选题评估中的选项偏差
Reordering multiple-choice options can change a model's answer, an effect studied as selection bias (a preference for certain option letters) and as order sensitivity (changes in answers across option orderings). We connect these perspectives through the pooled selection-rate standard deviation (pooled SD), which measures how unevenly option letters are selected across questions and orderings. When every option of each question appears equally often at each position, the pooled SD is exactly the product of two label-free factors: the flip rate and the positional skew. The flip rate is the fraction of questions whose chosen option changes across orderings; the positional skew is the standard deviation of option-letter selection rates among those questions. Across 41 checkpoints and 12 benchmarks, the flip rate tracks accuracy and output uncertainty, while the positional skew tracks the estimated option-symbol prior. Prior correction for selection bias mainly reduces the positional skew. Aggregating each ordering with its reversal, a mitigation for order sensitivity, mainly reduces the flip rate. Both reduce the range of accuracy across correct-answer letters by similar amounts, but aggregation leaves fewer model-rank reversals. Applying prior correction before aggregation reduces both factors and further narrows this accuracy range. However, aggregation lowers the flip rate partly by settling questions on the same wrong option. We therefore recommend reporting both factors to distinguish mitigation effects, alongside accuracy to distinguish stability from correctness.
中文翻译
重新排列多选题选项可能改变模型的答案，这一现象被研究为选择偏差（对特定选项字母的偏好）和顺序敏感性（答案随选项顺序变化）。我们通过合并的选择率标准差（pooled SD）将这两种视角联系起来，该指标衡量不同题目和顺序下选项字母选择的不均衡程度。当每道题的每个选项在各位置出现频率相等时，pooled SD恰好等于两个无标签因子的乘积：翻转率和位置偏斜度。翻转率是答案随顺序变化的题目比例；位置偏斜度是这些题目中选项字母选择率的标准差。在41个检查点和12个基准测试中，翻转率与准确率和输出不确定性相关，而位置偏斜度则与估计的选项符号先验相关。先前的偏差校正主要降低位置偏斜度。将每种顺序与其逆序合并（一种缓解顺序敏感性的方法），主要降低翻转率。两者均使正确答案字母间的准确率范围缩小相似幅度，但合并操作减少了模型排名反转的数量。在合并前进行先验校正可同时降低两个因子，并进一步缩小准确率范围。然而，合并部分通过将问题固定在同一个错误选项上降低翻转率。因此我们建议报告两个因子以区分缓解措施的影响，同时报告准确率以区分稳定性与正确性。
👤 Hyunjong Ok, Minjae Park, Jaeho Lee 📅 2026-10-14 🔗 arXiv

Same Context, Different Agents: A Behavioral Study of Semantic Layers, Orchestration and Compaction in Data Agents
相同上下文，不同智能体：数据智能体中语义层、编排与压缩的行为研究
Answering questions over heterogeneous data means combining evidence from files, databases, documents and the web. Data agents automate this, but their success depends on context. Three context-engineering techniques (CETs) help by shaping it: semantic layers describe the data, orchestration splits work among agents, and compaction condenses interaction history. Existing studies validate each CET alone, leaving open how agent behavior shapes its effect. We present a comprehensive analysis of the three CETs, alone and combined, with seven large language models (LLMs) on 1,514 long-horizon tasks from five benchmarks, covering behavior, accuracy and cost. We find that data agents without CETs form three behavior groups: investigators, premature answerers that answer without inspecting data, and early stoppers that give up. Each CET delivers its known benefit, from more investigation to smaller prompts, but only the behavior group it matches turns that benefit into accuracy, such as premature answerers under orchestration. For investigators, the same CETs can hurt: the semantic layer stops them from looking, orchestration makes them overconfident, costing 5 percentage points, and compaction makes them repeat steps. Combining CETs yields smaller gains and losses than their sum. We derive guidance for choosing CETs by agent behavior, task demands and accuracy-cost trade-offs.
中文翻译
针对异构数据回答问题需要整合来自文件、数据库、文档和网络的证据。数据智能体自动化这一过程，但其成功取决于上下文质量。三种上下文工程技巧（CETs）有助于优化上下文：语义层描述数据结构，编排将任务分配给多个智能体，压缩则精简交互历史。现有研究分别验证了每种CET的效果，但未揭示智能体行为如何影响其效果。我们对这三种CET进行了全面分析，单独及组合使用，基于七种大型语言模型在五个基准测试的1514个长周期任务上展开，涵盖行为、准确率和成本。我们发现，未使用CET的数据智能体形成三类行为模式：调查者、过早作答者（未检查数据即作答）和提前放弃者（中途放弃）。每种CET均带来其已知优势（如更多调查、更短提示），但只有与之匹配的行为组才能将优势转化为准确率提升，例如过早作答者在编排下表现更好。而对于调查者，相同的CET反而有害：语义层使其停止探索，编排使其过度自信，导致准确率下降5个百分点；压缩则使其重复步骤。组合使用CETs带来的增益和损失小于各自独立作用之和。我们据此提出根据智能体行为、任务需求和准确率-成本权衡来选择CET的指导原则。

PRISM: Transfer and Limits of Activation Interventions in Tool-Calling Language Models
PRISM：工具调用语言模型中激活干预的迁移性与局限性
Recent literature shows that unsafe tool calls can often accompany textual refusals in language models. To investigate this hidden vulnerability, we introduce PRISM, a framework for evaluating the transfer of activation interventions across precision, languages, and scales. Our findings demonstrate that leveraging lower-resource settings (specifically quantized NF4 labels and cross-lingual transfer) offers a robust pathway to expose and improve model alignment. Notably, using behavioral labels from an NF4 copy enables stable intervention estimation even with very few scenarios. Furthermore, these alignment improvements transfer effectively across languages: five-language NF4-supervised directions successfully transfer to held-out Arabic, reducing unsafe violations in Llama-3.1-8B by 21.95 percentage points without target-language tuning. By mapping paired benign activations, we also successfully transfer these safety interventions to much larger models (up to 70B parameters). Ultimately, PRISM highlights how auxiliary supervision from compressed models and multilingual data can uncover and mitigate hazardous tool-calling behaviors across different model families. We release all experimental code and supporting artifacts at https://github.com/GiKerche/prism, alongside an interactive explorer of recorded model responses and experimental results at https://gikerche.github.io/prism/.
中文翻译
近期文献表明，语言模型中的文本拒绝行为常伴随不安全的工具调用。为探究这一隐藏漏洞，我们提出了PRISM框架，用于评估激活干预在精度、语言和规模之间的迁移能力。研究发现，利用低资源设置（特别是量化后的NF4标签和跨语言迁移）可有效暴露并改进模型对齐。值得注意的是，使用NF4副本的行为标签即使在极少场景下也能实现稳定的干预估计。此外，这些对齐改进在跨语言间具有良好的迁移性：五种语言的NF4监督方向成功迁移到未见的阿拉伯语，使Llama-3.1-8B模型的不安全违规减少21.95个百分点，且无需目标语言微调。通过映射成对的良性激活，我们还将这些安全干预成功迁移至更大规模模型（高达700亿参数）。最终，PRISM揭示了来自压缩模型和多语言数据的辅助监督如何在不同模型家族中发现并缓解危险的工具调用行为。所有实验代码及相关资源已发布于https://github.com/GiKerche/prism，同时提供交互式模型响应与实验结果探索页面https://gikerche.github.io/prism。

APRiL: An Open-ended Benchmark for Chinese Social Awareness in Large Language Models
APRiL：面向大语言模型中文社会意识的开放式基准测试
Large language models are increasingly used in social interactions, where appropriate responses require recognizing implicit cues, inferring mental states, identifying conflicts, and considering potential consequences. However, existing benchmarks often focus on specific social abilities, constrained task formats, or particular interaction settings, offering limited coverage of social awareness across diverse contexts. To address this gap, we introduce APRiL, an open-ended benchmark for Chinese social awareness with instance-specific rubrics. APRiL contains 2,196 Social Awareness Evaluation Instances across six categories and two complementary tasks: Social Scenario Analysis evaluates models' understanding of social cues, mental states, and conflict structures, while Socially Aware Response assesses whether they can translate this understanding into appropriate, natural responses. Experiments on 12 models from six families reveal a consistent understanding-to-response gap. Higher-quality scenario analyses improve responses, while weak analyses may reduce response quality. APRiL provides a systematic resource for advancing socially aware language models in Chinese contexts. Our data and code are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/APRiL-05DE/.
中文翻译
大语言模型在社交互动中的应用日益广泛，恰当回应需要识别隐含线索、推断心理状态、辨识冲突并考虑潜在后果。然而，现有基准测试往往聚焦特定社交能力、受限的任务格式或特定交互场景，难以全面覆盖多样化情境下的社会意识。为此，我们提出APRiL，一个针对中文社会意识的开放式基准测试，包含基于实例的评分标准。APRiL涵盖六个类别共2,196个社会意识评估实例，以及两个互补任务：社会情景分析评估模型对社会线索、心理状态和冲突结构的理解；社会意识回应则评估模型能否将理解转化为恰当自然的回应。对六类共12个模型的实验显示存在持续的从理解到回应的差距。高质量的情景分析能提升回应质量，而薄弱分析反而会降低回应表现。APRiL为推进中文语境下具备社会意识的语言模型提供了系统性资源。数据与代码已公开于https://anonymous.4open.science/r/APRiL-05DE/。
👤 Tian Lan, Han Zhang, Shanshan Wang, Yahan Yu, Jiang Li et al. 📅 2026-10-14 🔗 arXiv

Verdict: Encode Once, Decide Many
Verdict：一次编码，多次决策
Automating organizational work means answering many small typed questions about one piece of state (does this email need a reply, how urgent is it, who owns it) with probabilities good enough to act on without a human. The open typed-decision model we compare against, like a standard cross-encoder, answers each question with a separate pass over the input. We present Verdict-MQ, an open encoder that reads a case once and answers all of its questions: given identical retained state tokens after truncation (verified on every reported case), a structured attention mask makes the state encoding independent of the questions, keeps questions from influencing each other, and makes option order irrelevant by construction. On the typed-decisions benchmark, a 395M Verdict-MQ matches the soft gold labels significantly better than the released 421M Laya specialist on all five fresh seeds (KL 0.080 vs 0.117, Brier 0.045 vs 0.061), an advantage that survives per-system temperature scaling; its higher accuracy (0.801 vs 0.767) lies past the benchmark's own saturation point, so we read that margin as closer agreement with the benchmark's labelling teacher. On RACE, BoolQ and MultiRC, against a matched fine-tuned baseline it wins on MultiRC and loses on RACE and on BoolQ, where its training is unstable; we measure zero-shot references ourselves, including a closed general system (Jev) that beats every fine-tuned encoder. A controlled comparison measures what the shared state costs: giving each question its own question-aware copy of the state gains 0.9-3.5 points on RACE and MultiRC at 1.4-2.5x the per-case latency, and nothing measurable on typed decisions, with the invariance guarantees unchanged.
中文翻译
自动化组织工作需要对同一状态信息回答多个小型类型化问题（例如：这封邮件是否需要回复？有多紧急？由谁负责？），且答案需具备足够置信度以支持自动决策而无需人工介入。我们对比的开放型类型化决策模型（如标准交叉编码器）对每个问题均需单独遍历输入。本文提出Verdict-MQ，一种开放型编码器，仅读取一次案例即可回答所有问题：在截断后保留相同的状态标记（经每例验证），通过结构化注意力掩码使状态编码独立于问题，防止问题相互影响，并通过构造确保选项顺序无关。在类型化决策基准测试中，395M的Verdict-MQ在五个新种子上显著优于发布的421M Laya专用模型（KL 0.080 vs 0.117，Brier 0.045 vs 0.061），该优势在系统级温度缩放后依然存在；其更高准确率（0.801 vs 0.767）已超出基准自身的饱和点，表明其更接近基准标注教师的判断。在RACE、BoolQ和MultiRC上，相较于匹配的微调基线，Verdict-MQ在MultiRC上胜出，在RACE和BoolQ上落后，后者训练不稳定；我们自行测量零样本参考，包括一个封闭通用系统（Jev），其性能超越所有微调编码器。受控对比显示共享状态的成本：为每个问题分配独立的问题感知状态副本，在RACE和MultiRC上提升0.9–3.5分，但代价为1.4–2.5倍的单例延迟，而在类型化决策任务上无明显收益，且不变性保证不变。
👤 Avdhesh Singh Chouhan, Jack Perry 📅 2026-10-14 🔗 arXiv

Does Personalization Break Trust? Open-Set Rejection Calibration Under Few-Shot On-Device Intent Adaptation
个性化会破坏信任吗？少样本本地意图适应下的开集拒绝校准
Always-on virtual assistants increasingly personalize on-device to new user-specific commands via few-shot fine-tuning (e.g., LoRA), but this adaptation is evaluated almost exclusively on whether it learns the new commands, not on whether the assistant can still recognize an input that matches none of its intents - the out-of-scope (OOS) rejection problem. We show that naive few-shot LoRA personalization degrades OOS-rejection AUROC (area under the ROC curve) on every one of five public intent-classification benchmarks (mean drop $0.055$--$0.258$, five seeds each), while in-scope accuracy is preserved. A scorer-invariant check shows part of this drop is an artifact of the standard confidence scorer's own miscalibration under adaptation, though a smaller, genuine effect remains under any scorer; we trace it to a measurable shrinkage of the embedding margin around known-intent regions (pooled Spearman rho=0.61$). A deployment-cheap fix is scoring rejection by Mahalanobis distance instead of softmax confidence without extra training/retraining recovers most of the lost quality on 3 of 5 datasets at no accuracy cost, beats two further OOD scoring baselines, and generalizes across shot count, LoRA rank, and a second base encoder. We report honestly where the fix falls short and release all code for reproducibility.
中文翻译
始终在线的虚拟助手越来越多地通过少样本微调（如LoRA）在设备端进行个性化以适应新用户指令，但此类适应几乎仅评估是否学会新命令，而未关注助手能否仍识别与所有意图都不匹配的输入——即“范围外”（OOS）拒绝问题。我们发现，简单的少样本LoRA个性化在五个公开意图分类基准上均导致OOS拒绝AUROC下降（平均降幅0.055–0.258，五次种子测试），而内部准确率得以保持。一项评分不变性检查表明，部分下降源于标准置信度评分器在适应下的自身校准偏差，尽管即便在任何评分器下仍存在较小但真实的负面影响；我们将其归因于已知意图区域嵌入边距的可测量缩小（合并Spearman相关系数=0.61）。一种部署成本低廉的解决方案是改用马氏距离而非Softmax置信度进行拒绝评分，无需额外训练即可在三个数据集上恢复大部分丢失的质量，优于两种其他OOD评分基线，并在样本数量、LoRA秩及第二基础编码器之间具有良好泛化性。我们如实报告该方案的局限性，并发布全部代码以供复现。

Jev-as-a-Judge for RAG Claim Verification: Confidence-Gated Cascading Between a Non-Generative Verifier and a Frontier LLM
Jev-as-a-Judge用于RAG声明验证：非生成验证器与前沿大模型间的置信度门控级联
Retrieval-augmented generation asks one question at volume: does this evidence support this claim? The answer is one bit, yet most pipelines obtain it from a text generator and pay generation prices for the prose wrapped around it. We compare a non-generative decision model, which returns a typed value from a declared enumeration together with a native probability and no text, against a frontier LLM configured as an engineer would deploy it for binary classification. Both answer the same 495 human-labelled claims from LLM-AggreFact, so every comparison is within-item. We report three things. First, the two systems are statistically indistinguishable on the metric this task is conventionally scored by, a paired difference of -0.56 points, Jev minus Astra, at a 187-fold cost difference. Second, that tie carries almost no information about deployment: the systems disagree on 15.2% of claims, and the cheaper one admits 23.2% of the claims annotators marked unsupported against the frontier model's 14.1%. A component whose job is to catch unsupported claims should be judged on that rate, and the conventional headline hides it. Third, because the errors differ they compose: routing the claims the cheap model is confident about and escalating the rest reaches 74.10 at USD 2.92 per thousand claims against 73.83 at USD 8.93, with the threshold chosen out of fold. But a matched random-escalation control at in-sample thresholds, spending the same budget by coin flip, does as well, so what buys the saving is the escalation share and not the probability that picks what to escalate. Measured against the cheap model it is built from, the cascade costs 61-fold more for 0.83 points, 95% CI [-3.36, 4.86], and it is the more permissive guard of the two. Equal accuracy conceals unequal errors. All predictions, prompts, pricing and code are released.
中文翻译
检索增强生成一次性提出大量问题：这些证据是否支持该声明？答案仅为一个比特，但大多数流水线仍通过文本生成器获取，并为此付出冗长叙述的代价。我们比较了一个非生成决策模型（返回预定义枚举中的类型值及原生概率，无文本）与一个配置为工程师部署方式的前沿大模型（用于二分类）。两者均回答来自LLM-AggreFact的495个经人工标注的声明，因此每次比较均为同项目内。我们报告三点发现：第一，两种系统在该任务常规评分指标上统计上无差异，Jev减去Astra的配对差值为-0.56分，但成本相差187倍；第二，这种平局几乎不反映部署情况：两系统在15.2%的声明上存在分歧，且廉价模型接受23.2%的标注者标记为不支持的声明，而前沿模型仅为14.1%。一个负责捕捉不支持声明的组件应以此拒绝率来评判，而传统标题掩盖了这一点；第三，由于错误模式不同，二者可组合：将廉价模型有把握的声明路由，其余升级处理，可实现74.10分，每千条仅花费2.92美元，而对比方案为73.83分，花费8.93美元（阈值在外部选取）。但使用相同预算的随机升级控制组（按抛硬币决定）表现相当，因此节省成本的关键在于升级比例，而非概率判断哪些需升级。相对于其构建基础的廉价模型，该级联系统成本高出61倍，仅提升0.83分（95%置信区间[-3.36, 4.86]），且是两个系统中更为宽松的守门人。同等准确率掩盖了不等的错误。所有预测、提示、定价和代码均已公开。
👤 Alden Do Rosario, Kirollos Atef 📅 2026-09-30 🔗 arXiv

Context Injection and Long-Horizon Reliability: Separating Duplicate Recognition, Local Processing, and Counter Tracking
上下文注入与长时程可靠性：区分重复识别、局部处理与计数跟踪
Long-horizon failures of language models are usually measured as end-task success or as a horizon length. We instead score, step by step, three components of a 400-step ledger task in which every step is easy in isolation and the model's own answers are re-injected into its context: recognising a record already seen 5 to 300 steps earlier, processing the current record locally, and writing a counter incremented at every step. On Qwen3-8B (non-thinking, 20 seeds, replicated on a second machine and backend), with every step scored component by component, two results hold. First, duplicate-recognition errors rise late in the episode -- from 0.0019 to 0.011 per step at risk across step 200 -- while the counter makes no error in 8,000 steps and local processing is descriptively stable; the rise combines long-distance recall errors and rising false alarms. Second, a block of irrelevant content inserted before each step makes the counter fail in 20 of 20 seeds on each machine, against none without it, while its effect on local processing is much smaller and not distinguishable from none. Three pre-registered follow-ups probe the counter. Its value depends on the value present in the response history: a corrupted value persists without correction in every seed, and an exact external reminder, repeated before each message, re-aligns it within one to two steps. Under the irrelevant block, no counter error was observed when an exact compact reminder of the state, supplied externally by an oracle, preceded each message (0 of 20 seeds with an error, against 15 without it), and this reminder outperformed a length-matched neutral text; whether a single reminder suffices was not conclusive, and the absence of damage to the other components was not established. An aggregate or exclusive error classification can hide the difference we report.
中文翻译
语言模型的长时程失败通常以最终任务成功率或时程长度衡量。我们改为逐步评分一个400步的账本任务的三个组成部分：识别5至300步前已见过的记录、本地处理当前记录、并在每一步递增计数器。在Qwen3-8B（非思考模式，20次种子，于第二台机器和后端复现）上，每一步分别评分，得出两个结果。第一，重复识别错误在剧集后期上升——从第200步起每步风险从0.0019升至0.011，而计数器在8000步内无错误，局部处理描述上稳定；上升部分由长距离回忆错误和误报率上升共同导致。第二，若在每一步前插入一段无关内容，会使计数器在每台机器的20次种子中全部失败（20/20），而无此干扰时则完全无误；其对局部处理的影响较小且不可区分。三项预先注册的后续实验探究计数器。其值取决于响应历史中存在的值：被污染的值在所有种子中持续存在且未被纠正；而在每条消息前重复提供的精确外部提醒，可在一到两步内将其重对齐。在无关内容干扰下，若每条消息前由外部预言者提供精确紧凑的状态提醒，则20次种子中无一出错（0/20），而无提醒时则有15次出错；该提醒优于长度匹配的中性文本；单次提醒是否足够尚不明确，且其他组件未受损的情况也未确立。聚合或排他式错误分类可能掩盖我们报告的差异。
