agent-benchmarkagent-memoryagentic-rl
PRISM: PRIvileged Sibling-guided Multi-Agent Topology Design
PRISM：基于特权兄弟引导的多智能体拓扑结构设计
Multi-agent systems (MAS) based on large language models (LLMs) have become a powerful way to address complex problems, and their effectiveness depends critically on the collaboration topology that decides which agents participate and how they communicate. Recent work learns to generate this topology from the task query, fitting the generator to corpora of execution-verified graphs. The post-training method for such generators remains a limitation: a corpus lists the graphs that solved a query but never ranks them, leaving continued supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with no per-query preference to exploit. Without such a preference, the generator drifts toward the topologies the corpus contains most often rather than those that suit each query, so a gain on one task can come at a cost on another. We propose PRISM, which recovers per-query signal from the same corpus by learning with privileged information: a teacher reads, through a zero-initialized low-rank branch, a sibling graph that also solved the query and is unavailable at deployment, and the student matches its per-decision targets on trajectories the student samples itself. Across six diverse benchmarks PRISM improves over every topology-design baseline evaluated, and matched-teacher controls support the contribution of privileged query information. Code is available in this link.
中文翻译
基于大语言模型（LLMs）的多智能体系统（MAS）已成为解决复杂问题的强大方式，其有效性关键取决于决定哪些智能体参与以及如何通信的合作拓扑结构。近期工作尝试从任务查询中学习生成该拓扑结构，但训练生成器时依赖于经过执行验证的图谱语料库。然而，这种后训练方法存在局限性：语料库仅列出解决某一查询的图谱，但从不对其进行排序，导致持续的监督微调（SFT）缺乏每查询偏好信号。由于缺乏此类偏好，生成器会趋向于语料库中最常见的拓扑结构，而非最适合每个查询的结构，从而导致某些任务性能提升可能以其他任务性能下降为代价。为此，我们提出PRISM，通过学习特权信息恢复每查询信号：教师通过一个零初始化的低秩分支读取另一个也解决了该查询的兄弟图谱（部署时不可用），学生则在自身采样的轨迹上匹配教师的每决策目标。在六个不同基准测试中，PRISM均优于所有评估的拓扑设计基线，且与教师匹配的对照实验支持了特权查询信息的贡献。代码已公开。
👤 Phuong Tuan Dat, Qi Li, Xinchao Wang 📅 2026-09-29 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22350]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
FDE-Gym: The Last Mile of AI Delivery
FDE-Gym：AI交付的最后一步
Existing agent benchmarks typically evaluate agents on well-specified tasks defined by issue reports, specifications, or task tickets. Real-world customer delivery is different: forward-deployed engineers (FDEs) must clarify ambiguous requirements, work in live systems, and uncover unstated constraints. We introduce FDE-Gym, a benchmark of 95 end-to-end delivery tasks from 20 customer scenarios across 18 sectors, each adapted by industry experts from real-world delivery projects. In simulated customer environments with scripted stakeholders and modifiable customer systems, agents interact with stakeholders and operate on production-like systems to deliver working solutions. FDE-Gym scores a delivery by how much it improves the customer's own success metric, normalized between 0 (unchanged system) and 1 (reference solution), with penalties for stakeholder interactions (messages, trial runs, and waiting time). An online platform lets humans practice FDE skills under the same grading criteria. We evaluate 13 model configurations from seven providers. In the realistic setting, where unstated constraints must be discovered, the best configuration achieves a mean FDE-Gym score of 0.56 and resolves only 14 of the 95 tasks (a score of at least 0.8 with no incident). Discovering unstated constraints is the primary bottleneck: performance drops sharply when models must uncover them without explicit guidance. Yet information discovery alone does not account for the gap: even on the 23 tasks where the best configuration obtains every key fact from stakeholders, it reaches a normalized business uplift of 0.8 or above on only 10. These results reveal a substantial gap between solving well-specified tasks and delivering effective AI systems for real customers.
中文翻译
现有的智能体基准测试通常在由问题报告、规格说明或任务工单定义的明确任务上评估智能体。但现实中的客户交付则不同：前向部署工程师（FDEs）必须澄清模糊的需求，在实时系统中工作，并发现未明说的约束条件。我们提出了FDE-Gym，这是一个涵盖18个行业20个客户场景的95个端到端交付任务的基准，每个任务均由行业专家根据真实交付项目改编而成。在模拟客户环境中，包含脚本化利益相关者和可修改的客户系统，智能体与利益相关者互动并在类似生产环境的系统上运行以交付可用解决方案。FDE-Gym通过衡量交付对客户自身成功指标的提升程度来评分，分数范围为0（系统无变化）到1（参考解决方案），并因利益相关者交互（消息、试运行和等待时间）施加惩罚。一个在线平台允许人类在相同评分标准下练习FDE技能。我们评估了来自七家供应商的13种模型配置。在需要发现未明说约束的真实情境中，最佳配置的平均FDE-Gym得分为0.56，仅解决95个任务中的14个（得分至少0.8且无事故）。发现未明说约束是主要瓶颈：当模型在无明确指导的情况下必须自行发现这些约束时，性能急剧下降。然而，仅靠信息发现无法解释这一差距：即使在23个任务中，最佳配置已从利益相关者处获取所有关键事实，其标准化业务提升仍仅在10个任务中达到0.8或以上。这些结果揭示了在解决明确任务与为真实客户交付有效AI系统之间存在显著差距。
👤 Dongxu Yang, Hongda Yan, Renxin Wang, Baoli Zhang, Haojia Zuo et al. 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22244]
agent-benchmark
Language Is a Security Surface for Agent Guards
语言是智能体防护机制的安全面
Step-level guards decide, before each tool call, whether an agent's next action is safe given the user's request and the trajectory so far. Users write those requests in many languages, while guard prompts and the benchmarks on which purpose-built guards are trained and evaluated are English. We ask whether a guard's verdict depends on the language of the request. We build LinguaGuardBench from the 1,220 labelled steps of TS-Bench AgentDojo-Traj: we translate only the user instruction into German, French, Turkish, Swahili and Amharic, and keep every tool call, observation and label byte-identical, so any change in a verdict is attributable to language alone. Six English rewordings and a whitespace placebo serve as controls, and managed guards are run three times to measure their noise. Across 19 guards, from frontier APIs to 4B open models and four purpose-built agent guards, most open, small and purpose-built guards change verdicts under translation beyond their own noise floor, and the effect grows with the rarity of the language: among the nine guards below 13B parameters, the mean placebo-adjusted flip rate rises from +1.5 pp on French to +4.4 pp on Amharic. Purpose-built guards are the most sensitive. The direction of the damage tends to follow guard size: open guards of 12-30B parameters block more benign steps (up to +5.0 pp false positives), and every significant loss of recall is in a guard below 10B (up to -7.4 pp). Frontier guards shift by at most about one point; the strongest, Gemini 3.8 Flash, returns identical verdicts with and without the request. The damage is concentrated in a few instructions and, in a post-hoc analysis, falls on steps where the agent's reasoning restates the English request, consistent with guards reacting to inconsistency between channels. English paraphrase moves every guard less than translation does. We release the benchmark and all 489k verdicts.
中文翻译
步骤级防护机制在每次工具调用前决定智能体下一步行动是否安全，依据用户请求和当前轨迹。用户以多种语言撰写请求，而防护机制提示词及训练评估所用基准均为英语。我们探究防护机制的判断是否受请求语言影响。我们基于 TS-Bench AgentDojo-Traj 的 1,220 个标注步骤构建 LinguaGuardBench：仅将用户指令翻译为德语、法语、土耳其语、斯瓦希里语和阿姆哈拉语，其余工具调用、观察结果和标签均字节完全相同，因此任何判断变化均可归因于语言本身。六种英语重述版本和一个空格对照组作为控制变量，管理型防护机制运行三次以测量其噪声水平。在 19 种防护机制中（涵盖前沿 API 到 4B 开源模型及四种专用智能体防护），大多数开源、小型及专用防护机制在翻译后判断发生变化，超出自身噪声范围，且影响随语言稀有度增加而加剧：在参数量低于 13B 的九种防护中，平均经对照调整后的翻转率从法语的 +1.5 个百分点上升至阿姆哈拉语的 +4.4 个百分点。专用防护最敏感。损害方向通常与防护规模相关：12-30B 参数的开源防护会更多阻断良性步骤（最多 +5.0 个百分点误报），所有显著召回损失均出现在 10B 以下的防护中（最多 -7.4 个百分点）。前沿防护最多仅偏移约 1 个百分点；最强的 Gemini 3.8 Flash 在有无请求时返回完全相同的判断。损害集中在少数指令上，事后分析显示，这些步骤中智能体的推理重复了英文请求，与防护机制对通道间不一致的反应一致。英语改写对所有防护的影响均小于翻译。我们已发布该基准及全部 48.9 万条判断结果。

HTN-Orchestrate: A Hierarchical Task Network Framework for Multi-Agent Orchestration
HTN-Orchestrate：一种用于多智能体编排的层次任务网络框架
Orchestrating multiple agents on a complex task requires reconciling local subtask execution with global task planning. Dynamic dispatch approaches provide execution flexibility but do not explicitly model subtask hierarchies or dependencies, while predefined workflows impose this structure but fix the decomposition before execution begins. Therefore, we introduce HTN-Orchestrate, a two-layer framework that separates these responsibilities through a hierarchical task network (HTN). The Planner maintains subtask hierarchies and dependencies, deferring decomposition until prerequisite results become available, while local Orchestrators schedule workers to execute each leaf subtask. Results propagate upward under \textbf{AND} semantics, and accumulated findings inform a rebuilt task network when the current decomposition cannot resolve the task. On five benchmarks spanning web research, scientific reasoning, and research workflows, HTN-Orchestrate outperforms ReAct, Agent Team, and DeepSeek Harness using the same base model, achieving 89.73% accuracy on BrowseComp and 73.44% on SUPERChem, exceeding Agent Team by 2.45 and 4.69 percentage points, respectively.
中文翻译
在复杂任务上协调多个智能体，需要调和局部子任务执行与全局任务规划之间的关系。动态调度方法提供执行灵活性，但未显式建模子任务层级或依赖关系；而预定义工作流虽强加结构，却在执行前固定分解方式。因此，我们提出 HTN-Orchestrate，一种两层框架，通过层次任务网络（HTN）分离这些职责。规划器维护子任务层级与依赖关系，推迟分解直至前置结果可用；本地编排器则调度工作者执行每个叶节点子任务。结果按 extbf{AND} 语义向上传播，当当前分解无法解决任务时，累积发现将用于重建任务网络。在涵盖网页研究、科学推理和研究工作流的五个基准上，HTN-Orchestrate 使用相同基础模型超越 ReAct、Agent Team 和 DeepSeek Harness，分别在 BrowseComp 上达到 89.73% 准确率，在 SUPERChem 上达到 73.44%，分别优于 Agent Team 2.45 和 4.69 个百分点。

AutoSFT: An Agentic System for Automated Data Research in Supervised Fine-Tuning
AutoSFT：一种用于监督微调中自动化数据研究的智能体系统
Language model agents increasingly automate post-training, but when given the full training stack they converge on supervised fine-tuning and spend most effort revising training data. Existing systems give the agent one kind of control, selecting rows from a fixed pool or calling a generator, whereas a human data engineer adjusts proportions, prunes, repairs, replaces and synthesizes the data. We design a data action space for these changes and ask whether an automated system can make the full range rather than one of them, and what structure that requires. We first run this loop with a single agent, and it reaches only one action, adjusting category proportions, while its context fills with execution output and is discarded mid-search. We therefore introduce \textbf{AutoSFT}, a multi-agent system where a meta agent separates execution from analysis, gathers proposals from independent angles, and calls a synthesis subagent for capabilities the pool cannot cover, with the model, the trainer, and the budget fixed within each comparison. Adding each component in turn helps: the executor halves context loss, the analysis subagents lift the best IFEval by $+6.5$, and synthesis adds $+3.4$ IFEval and $+2.9$ IFBench. The same loop transfers to code, where analysis adds $+3.7$ MBPP+ over a single agent that does not improve. Every result comes from runs that retrain from the base model each round, so each component's effect is individually attributable. Our findings suggest that automating SFT data research depends more on covering diverse data actions than on a stronger single action.
中文翻译
语言模型智能体越来越多地自动化后训练过程，但当赋予完整的训练栈时，它们会聚焦于监督微调，并将大部分精力用于修改训练数据。现有系统仅赋予智能体单一控制权，例如从固定数据池中选择行或调用生成器，而人类数据工程师则会调整比例、修剪、修复、替换并合成数据。我们为此类变化设计了一个数据操作空间，并探讨自动化系统能否实现全部操作而非仅其中一种，以及这需要怎样的结构。我们首先使用单个智能体运行该循环，结果仅能执行一种操作——调整类别比例，且其上下文因执行输出堆积而被中途丢弃。因此，我们提出 extbf{AutoSFT}，一个由元智能体主导的多智能体系统，将执行与分析分离，从多个独立角度收集建议，并调用合成子智能体以弥补数据池能力不足，同时在每次比较中固定模型、训练器和预算。逐步引入每个组件均带来提升：执行器使上下文损失减半，分析子智能体使最佳IFEval提升+6.5，合成模块进一步带来+3.4 IFEval和+2.9 IFBench。该循环同样可迁移至代码任务，在单个智能体无法改进的情况下，分析模块带来+3.7 MBPP+的提升。所有结果均来自每轮从基础模型重新训练的实验，因此每个组件的影响均可独立归因。我们的发现表明，自动化SFT数据研究的关键在于覆盖多样化的数据操作，而非依赖更强的单一操作。

Believed but Not Said: Measuring Hidden Belief Collapse in Multi-Agent LLM Discussions
被相信但未说出：测量多智能体LLM讨论中的隐性信念坍塌
LLM systems increasingly rely on groups of agents that discuss, critique, and check one another, assuming that peers will catch individual errors. We test this assumption against a minimal adversary. In a ten-agent factual discussion, one or three adversarial agents repeatedly assert a plausible false answer. After each round we probe each benign agent's private answer through a separate call whose response is hidden from its peers, and we separately label its public stance toward the claim. Across six models and three factual-QA datasets, groups remain stable without an attacker: only 0.6% of benign agents finish with the false answer. A single adversarial agent increases this rate to 44.8% and three to 60.5%. Studies of conformity typically report public agreement without private change; we find the reverse. With three adversarial agents present, benign agents hold the false answer in 51.6% of exposed turns but publicly endorse it in only 19.7%. Much of the influence therefore remains invisible in the discussion, and the false answers persist when agents are asked for their own views. Agents that retain the true answer often fail to defend it; once adopted, the false answer spreads among benign agents, and baseline knowledge does not predict robustness.
中文翻译
大型语言模型系统越来越多地依赖一组智能体进行讨论、批判与相互核查，假设同伴能够发现个体错误。我们通过一个最小对抗场景检验这一假设。在十智能体的事实性讨论中，一个或三个对抗性智能体反复声称一个看似合理的错误答案。每轮结束后，我们通过独立调用探测每个良性智能体的私有答案，其回应对同伴隐藏，并单独标注其公开立场。在六个模型和三个事实问答数据集上，无攻击者时群体保持稳定：仅有0.6%的良性智能体最终接受错误答案。一个对抗性智能体使该比例升至44.8%，三个则达60.5%。以往关于从众的研究通常报告公开一致但私密未变；我们发现情况相反。当存在三个对抗性智能体时，良性智能体在暴露回合中持有错误答案的比例为51.6%，但公开支持该答案的比例仅为19.7%。因此，大部分影响在讨论中不可见，当要求智能体表达自身观点时，错误答案仍持续存在。那些保留正确答案的智能体往往未能有效辩护；一旦采纳错误答案，它便在良性智能体间传播，而基线知识无法预测鲁棒性。

WEAVE: Weaving Real-World Code Evolution into Dependency-Structured Tasks for Benchmarking and Training Code Agents
WEAVE：将真实世界代码演进融入依赖结构化任务以用于代码智能体的基准测试与训练
Real-world software engineering rarely consists of isolated changes. Features, migrations, and systematic fixes often unfold across multiple pull requests (PRs), requiring coordinated modifications across a repository. Yet most executable repository-level benchmarks retain a single issue or PR as the task unit, while existing multi-PR settings rely on coarse aggregation and structure-agnostic evaluation. This mismatch obscures dependencies among changes and limits progress measurement and learning signals for long-horizon code agents. We introduce WEAVE, a framework that transforms verified PR histories into executable, dependency-structured tasks. WEAVE leverages repository-calibrated typed change loci to organize related PRs into bounded DAGs and instantiate each as a unified executable task. We further introduce Graph Credit, a deterministic, test-grounded metric that measures dependency-consistent partial progress and supports dense, verifiable rewards for reinforcement learning. Experiments on task construction, agent evaluation, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning show that WEAVE yields coherent executable tasks, enables more faithful progress measurement than flat scoring, and provides effective supervision and verifiable rewards for training code agents.
中文翻译
现实世界中的软件工程很少是孤立的变更。功能开发、迁移和系统性修复通常跨越多个拉取请求（PR），需要在仓库内协调修改。然而，大多数可执行的仓库级基准仍以单一问题或PR作为任务单元，而现有的多PR设置依赖粗粒度聚合和无结构评估。这种不匹配掩盖了变更之间的依赖关系，限制了长时程代码智能体的进展测量和学习信号。我们提出WEAVE框架，将经验证的PR历史转化为可执行的、依赖结构化的任务。WEAVE利用仓库校准的类型化变更位置，将相关PR组织成有界有向无环图（DAG），并将每个任务实例化为统一的可执行任务。我们进一步引入Graph Credit，一种确定性的、基于测试的度量，用于衡量依赖一致的部分进展，并支持强化学习中的密集且可验证的奖励。在任务构建、智能体评估、监督微调和强化学习的实验表明，WEAVE生成连贯的可执行任务，相比扁平评分能更忠实反映进展，并为训练代码智能体提供有效监督和可验证奖励。

Selection Is Not Learning: Choosing Model Futures
选择并非学习：模型未来的抉择
Self-evolving language models train on tasks they generate, so every round must decide which updated model the next round inherits. Current loops such as R-Zero, Agent0, and Skill Self-Play make this decision through the task, admitting tasks that look learnable and inheriting every resulting update. We identify a selection-to-update gap in these loops, where a task that looks learnable need not produce a useful update. Most updates on verified tasks fail to make correct answers more likely, yet these loops inherit them all. HeirCommit freezes the updated model and uses fresh evaluation questions to decide whether to inherit it or retain the current model. EvoHeir separates parameter inheritance from experience inheritance, using current and archived experience to repair failures and preserve successes before final confirmation. Our analysis characterizes selection-induced optimism and decomposes regret into selection, absorption, and confirmation errors. HeirCommit cuts the harmful share of accepted updates by 68-83% relative to random inheritance at the same acceptance rate. Across four backbones, EvoHeir achieves the best mathematics and general-reasoning averages, raising the math average by 5.3% relative to the strongest baseline, Agent0. The framework lets useful experience persist even when the model that produced it is not inherited.
中文翻译
自演化语言模型在自身生成的任务上进行训练，因此每一轮都必须决定下一阶段继承哪个更新后的模型。当前的循环机制如R-Zero、Agent0和技能自对弈通过任务来做出这一决策，接受那些看起来可学习的任务，并继承所有由此产生的更新。我们识别出这些循环中的“选择-更新差距”：一个看似可学习的任务未必会产生有用的更新。在已验证任务上，大多数更新未能提高正确答案的概率，但这些循环仍继承了全部更新。HeirCommit冻结更新后的模型，并使用新评估问题判断是否继承或保留当前模型。EvoHeir将参数继承与经验继承分离，利用当前和归档经验修复失败并保留成功，再进行最终确认。我们的分析刻画了选择引发的乐观偏差，并将遗憾分解为选择、吸收和确认误差。HeirCommit在相同接受率下，相较于随机继承，将被接受更新中有害部分减少了68%-83%。在四种骨干模型上，EvoHeir在数学和通用推理平均表现上达到最优，使数学平均分相对于最强基线Agent0提升了5.3%。该框架使得即使产生经验的模型未被继承，有用的经验也能持续保留。

P2TPO: Structure-Aligned Page-to-Token Credit Assignment for Full-Page Document Parsing
P2TPO：面向全页文档解析的结构对齐页到令牌信用分配
End-to-end full-page document parsing must preserve the content and structure of heterogeneous elements, yet otherwise accurate outputs can contain local errors and omissions. A single page-level advantage does not explicitly identify which elements differ in quality across sampled outputs or where their errors occur. We introduce Page-to-Token Policy Optimization (P2TPO), a structure-aligned credit-assignment framework that operates on full-page policy trajectories. Reference-element alignment first associates block-level quality contrasts with generated spans. Unified Anchor Pricing (UAP) incorporates matched and missing element outcomes and provides feedback relative to a fixed quality ceiling when sampled outputs receive identical but imperfect scores for a reference element. Within matched blocks, Span Credit Redistribution (SCR) redistributes existing block credit according to aligned correct and erroneous spans, using boundary proxies for within-block omissions while preserving the total block-derived advantage across its tokens. Under a fixed-weight additive page utility, we show that element-indexed contrasts retain local quality differences that page-level aggregation can cancel out. In the main experimental comparison, P2TPO outperforms all evaluated policy optimization baselines in seed-averaged scores on each of the six document parsing benchmarks. Additional experiments demonstrate gains across model scales and initializations.

端到端的全页文档解析必须保持异构元素的内容与结构，但即使整体输出准确，也可能存在局部错误和遗漏。单个页面级别的优势无法显式识别不同采样输出中哪些元素质量差异或错误发生的位置。我们提出Page-to-Token Policy Optimization（P2TPO），一种在全页策略轨迹上运行的结构对齐信用分配框架。参考元素对齐首先将块级质量差异与生成片段关联。统一锚点定价（UAP）结合匹配和缺失元素结果，在采样输出对参考元素获得相同但不完美评分时，提供相对于固定质量上限的反馈。在匹配块内，跨度信用重分配（SCR）根据对齐的正确与错误片段重新分配现有块信用，使用边界代理处理块内遗漏，同时保持块内总导出优势在所有令牌间不变。在固定权重加法页面效用下，我们证明元素索引对比保留了局部质量差异，而页面级别聚合可能将其抵消。在主要实验比较中，P2TPO在六个文档解析基准上的种子平均得分均优于所有评估的策略优化基线。额外实验表明其在不同模型规模和初始化下均有提升。

DeciCAD: Harness-Grounded Learning of Action Policies for CAD Agents
DeciCAD：面向CAD智能体的动作策略的Harness基础学习
Large Language Model agents operate through closed-loop action policies whose decision space is defined by an agent harness. We investigate how this operating space shapes agent behavior and policy learning in computer-aided design (CAD), a professional domain characterized by persistent state, geometric constraints, and long-horizon procedures. We instantiate this perspective in DeciCAD, a domain-native harness that exposes semantic CAD actions, decision-oriented observations, environment feedback, and persistent state transitions. Building on this harness, we develop a learning pipeline that extracts causal decision supervision from interaction trajectories and improves the policy through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and geometry-grounded online reinforcement learning (RL). Across four CAD benchmarks, DeciCAD achieves the best F1 and intersection over union (IoU) while reducing model-output tokens by 45--88% relative to a general-purpose coding harness. Under matched supervision, its SFT policy improves F1 from 0.6883 to 0.7437 and outperforms the coding policy; subsequent online RL further raises geometry availability from 66.5% to 73.5% and F1 to 0.7880. These results demonstrate that the decision space induced by a harness materially affects both agent execution and policy learning in CAD.

大型语言模型智能体通过闭环动作策略运行，其决策空间由智能体Harness定义。我们研究这种操作空间如何影响计算机辅助设计（CAD）领域中智能体的行为与策略学习，该领域以持续状态、几何约束和长周期流程为特征。我们在DeciCAD中实现了这一视角，这是一种领域原生Harness，暴露了语义CAD动作、面向决策的观察、环境反馈以及持续状态转换。基于此Harness，我们开发了一条学习流水线，从交互轨迹中提取因果决策监督，并通过监督微调（SFT）和基于几何的在线强化学习（RL）改进策略。在四个CAD基准测试中，DeciCAD在F1和交并比（IoU）方面表现最佳，同时相对于通用编码Harness减少了45%–88%的模型输出token。在匹配监督条件下，其SFT策略将F1从0.6883提升至0.7437，优于编码策略；随后的在线RL进一步将几何可用性从66.5%提升至73.5%，F1达到0.7880。这些结果表明，Harness所诱导的决策空间显著影响CAD中智能体的执行与策略学习。

Human Interventions as Labels: Localizing, Steering, and (Sometimes) Editing the Computations Behind LLM Agent Failures
人类干预作为标签：定位、引导并（有时）编辑LLM智能体失败背后的计算过程
When an LLM agent misbehaves, a human steps in, and that intervention is usually discarded or replayed as training data. We treat it instead as a measurement: a label pointing at the internal computation that failed. We study it in a controlled agent environment, on one failure (asking a clarifying question when the request was already unambiguous), with labels from a rule-based overseer validated against blind human judgment. Claims cover eligible models, which both over-ask and sometimes act on matched unambiguous requests: five of thirteen qualified (Qwen2.5-3B, gemma-2-2b, OLMo-3-7B, Mistral-Nemo-12B, Phi-4). Four findings. First, the ask-vs-act decision is causally localizable in every eligible family: a direction learned from intervention labels flips 72-100% of held-out over-asks at a definite residual-stream depth, with site and dose chosen on training data, and random directions under the same procedure flip at most one episode. Second, over-asking has two mechanistic signatures: in four families the model demonstrably knows the request is unambiguous (the belief is decodable, causally inert and orthogonal to the decision) and asks anyway; in the fifth, the rare over-asks track different world-representations. Third, steering gated on a probe readout removes most of the simulated oversight burden while legitimate questions survive (bootstrap 95% intervals exclude zero); a prompt instruction makes it worse. Fourth, consolidating the fix into the weights yields a map, not a rule: on one model a LoRA confined to the causal band passes every criterion fixed in advance while behavior cloning on identical data breaks retention; on three families the reverse holds; on the fifth the outcome crosses the boundary across seeds. A geometric rule written down in advance to predict the regime failed on two out-of-family tests; we report the failure and stop rather than fit a replacement.
中文翻译
当LLM智能体出现异常行为时，人类会介入，而这种干预通常被丢弃或作为训练数据重放。我们将其视为一种测量：指向内部失败计算的标签。我们在受控智能体环境中研究这一现象，聚焦于一种失败情况（请求已明确时仍要求澄清问题），并使用基于规则的监督者标签与盲人人类判断进行验证。涉及的合格模型包括五种：Qwen2.5-3B、gemma-2-2b、OLMo-3-7B、Mistral-Nemo-12B、Phi-4。四项发现：第一，询问与执行决策在所有合格模型家族中均可因果定位：从干预标签中学习的方向能在确定的残差流深度上翻转72%-100%的保留过问行为，且位置与剂量在训练数据上选择，相同程序下随机方向最多翻转一次事件。第二，过问行为具有两种机制特征：在四个家族中，模型明显知道请求已明确（信念可解码、因果无关且正交于决策）但仍提问；在第五个家族中，罕见的过问行为追踪不同的世界表征。第三，基于探测读出的引导能消除大部分模拟监督负担，而合法问题仍能保留（自举95%置信区间不包含零）；提示指令反而使情况恶化。第四，将修复固化到权重中生成的是映射而非规则：在一个模型中，局限于因果带的LoRA通过了所有预先设定的标准，而行为克隆在相同数据上却破坏了保留；在三个家族中情况相反；在第五个家族中，结果随种子跨越边界。事先写下的几何规则用于预测范式，在两个跨家族测试中失败，我们报告失败并停止，而非拟合替代方案。

When Does "Post-Call" Begin? An Availability Contract for Earnings-Call NLP Benchmarks
“事后”何时开始？一种针对收益电话NLP基准的可用性契约
Corporate financial disclosures often unfold over time, with announcements, prepared remarks, and discussion spanning multiple high-frequency return intervals. Financial NLP benchmarks can therefore suffer from a within-event timing mismatch when the prediction target begins before the textual or audio input used by a model could be complete. Chronological train--test splits do not prevent this mismatch, because it occurs inside individual events rather than across samples. We introduce an Availability Contract, which requires a benchmark to state the input boundary, evidence for input availability, the outcome start, and the claims supported by that evidence. We apply this framework to 552 earnings calls from 24 firms. For each call, we hold the return endpoint fixed and compare the previous close, a documentary call-start proxy, and a completion proxy based on reconstructed audio end plus a stipulated processing allowance. At this baseline, moving from the call-start proxy to the completion proxy flips return signs for 9.4% of calls. Holding completion-clock cutpoints fixed, 28.3% change quintile. In an independent release-session analysis of a public earnings-call volatility benchmark, shifting before-market target windows forward by one trading session raises three-day mean squared error by 18.1-21.4% across four frozen prediction streams, while their point rankings remain unchanged. Together, the results show that labels and losses can depend materially on the declared outcome clock even when model rankings appear stable. The Availability Contract makes this assumption explicit and ties post-input claims to the available timing evidence.
中文翻译
企业财务披露通常随时间展开，包括公告、准备好的发言和讨论，跨越多个高频回报区间。因此，当预测目标在模型可获取文本或音频输入之前就开始时，金融NLP基准可能面临事件内部的时间错配问题。时间顺序的训练-测试划分无法防止这种错配，因为它发生在单个事件内部而非样本之间。我们提出一种可用性契约，要求基准明确说明输入边界、输入可用性的证据、结果开始时间以及该证据所支持的主张。我们将此框架应用于24家公司的552次收益电话。对于每次电话，我们固定回报终点，并比较前一收盘价、文档式电话开始代理、基于重构音频结束加规定处理余量的完成代理。在基线情况下，从电话开始代理转向完成代理会使9.4%的电话回报符号反转。在固定完成时钟切点的情况下，28.3%的电话 quintile 发生变化。在对公开收益电话波动性基准的独立发布会话分析中，将事前市场目标窗口提前一个交易日，使四个冻结预测流的三日均方误差上升18.1%-21.4%，而其点排名保持不变。总体结果表明，即使模型排名看似稳定，标签和损失仍可能显著依赖于声明的结果时钟。可用性契约使这一假设显式化，并将事后主张与可用的时间证据联系起来。

Do Agents Need a New Kind of Paper? SPRA: Structured Paper Representation for Agents
智能体是否需要一种新类型的论文？面向智能体的结构化论文表示SPRA
Scientific agents must connect research problems, methods, and findings dispersed through narrative articles. We introduce SPRA (Structured Paper Representation for Agents), a paper-level representation of persistent research objects and typed relations. Its empirical-AI profile organizes Problems, Contributions, Components, ExperimentSetups, Measures, and Findings for querying, traversal, and reuse. Selected paper-level citation relations record dependencies, component use, and comparisons with prior work; source locations support verification. We instantiate SPRA across more than one million AI papers, with 19.95 million research units and 24.07 million internal relations. Evaluations access SPRA through Schema-RAG with task evidence fixed before inference. Across seven subfields, SPRA improves source-conditioned scientific QA by 0.381/5 over passage retrieval while remaining close to Full Compact (0.078/5 lower on average). In directed cross-paper reasoning, qualification improves by 0.184/5 over passage retrieval; Top-5 synthesis shows mixed quality effects. Across all paired RQ2 tasks, including Top-5, input+output tokens decrease by 28.5% relative to passage retrieval and 95.3% relative to Full Compact. In idea generation, a 0.067-point lower Overall score than Full Compact accompanies 93.7% fewer input+output tokens. Together, these results support SPRA as a usable representation of scientific content and a foundation for research workflows that connect, inspect, and reuse paper-level research objects. The SPRA resource is accessible through the SciVerse scholarly-data platform.

VoteGEO-Bench: Measuring the Impact of Generative Engine Optimization on Electoral Visibility
VoteGEO-Bench：衡量生成引擎优化对选举可见性的影响
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for election information, yet little is known about how external web content shapes the political information they present. Generative engine optimization (GEO) rewrites web content to improve its visibility in generated responses. To investigate its potential risks in voting scenarios (VoteGEO), we introduce VoteGEO-Bench, a benchmark covering the 2024 U.S. presidential election and the 2026 Japanese House of Representatives election, combining election-related queries, historical webpages, and voter personas. We first analyze sources cited by generative engines and find that many citations come from lower-cost owned and open-platform sources, while some webpages remain visible across days. We then evaluate 11 rewriting strategies in 4 categories, examining target specification, effectiveness across engines and elections, and the role of voter personas. Rewriting without specifying a target rarely increases target exposure, whereas target-specified rewriting more often does. Strategies that improve page organization or strengthen a position through argumentation yield greater gains than adding supporting evidence or changing wording. For the two strongest strategies, gains remain broadly positive across evaluated U.S. personas, but persona conditioning shows no consistent effect on these gains. Explicit partisan identities can create baseline exposure gaps larger than the gain from rewriting one webpage. When partisanship is implicit in the voter profile, baseline shifts depend on how strongly the engine responds to that signal.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地被用于选举信息，但外界网络内容如何影响其呈现的政治信息仍知之甚少。生成引擎优化（GEO）通过重写网页内容以提高其在生成响应中的可见性。为探究其在投票场景（VoteGEO）中的潜在风险，我们引入VoteGEO-Bench，一个涵盖2024年美国总统选举和2026年日本众议院选举的基准测试，结合选举相关查询、历史网页和选民人物画像。我们首先分析生成引擎引用的来源，发现许多引用来自低成本自有和开放平台来源，而部分网页在多日内仍保持可见。随后我们评估了四类共11种重写策略，考察目标指定、跨引擎和选举的有效性，以及选民人物画像的作用。未指定目标的重写很少提升目标曝光率，而目标指定的重写则更常实现提升。改善页面结构或通过论证强化立场的策略带来的增益大于添加支持证据或更改措辞。对于两种最强策略，增益在评估的美国选民画像中普遍为正，但人物画像条件对增益无一致影响。明确的党派身份可能造成基线曝光差距，超过重写单个网页带来的增益。当党派倾向隐含于选民画像中时，基线偏移取决于引擎对该信号的响应强度。

ZoomCap: Evidence-Routed Visual Zooming for Recoverable Visual Information Transfer
ZoomCap：基于证据路由的视觉缩放以实现可恢复的视觉信息传递
Detailed captions transfer visual information into vision-language model supervision, but quality is easily conflated with length: long single-pass outputs still miss small objects, text, and spatial relations or promote uncertain observations into confident claims. We formulate fine-grained captioning as recoverable visual information transfer, in which a teacher acquires missing evidence under a finite observation budget and converts it into reusable supervision. ZoomCap uses a draft caption to route conditional knowledge retrieval, OCR-sensitive reading, coverage-gap recovery, and adaptive visual zooming, allocating deeper crops only to the most complex eligible region, deriving spatial relations from grounded geometry, and fusing non-duplicate evidence. We also introduce FactCycle, which scores caption-derived atomic claims on two blind anchors--source image (truthfulness) and reconstruction (realizability)--separating fabrication from generator loss claim by claim; human and multi-judge comparisons complement this fact-level view. Against Qwen3.5-397B-A17B captions, ZoomCap achieves an 81.9% human tie-adjusted preference score with a teacher over 10x smaller, and FactCycle counts 52.4 transferred claims per image vs. 31.5 for ScaleCap. The evidence also transfers: in continual pre-training, ZoomCap-450K improves the 11-benchmark average by 1.31-2.38 points over the strongest competing corpus and Ling-Tiny-VL-v3 gains 1.4 points on a production document/OCR monitor suite (1.5 on perception) at 60B tokens; in medical pre-training, 2.64M ZoomCap-captioned images improve five VQA benchmarks by 3.04 points (vs. 1.76 for same-image Gemini-3.1-Pro captions).
中文翻译
详细描述将视觉信息传递给视觉-语言模型监督，但质量容易与长度混淆：长篇一次性输出仍可能遗漏小物体、文本和空间关系，或把不确定的观察误认为确定性断言。我们将细粒度描述建模为可恢复的视觉信息传递，其中教师在有限观测预算下获取缺失证据，并将其转化为可重复使用的监督。ZoomCap利用草稿描述来引导条件知识检索、OCR敏感读取、覆盖空白恢复和自适应视觉缩放，仅对最复杂的合格区域分配更深层次的裁剪，从接地几何中推导空间关系，并融合非重复证据。我们还引入FactCycle，通过两个盲锚——源图像（真实性）和重构结果（可行性）——对生成的原子断言进行评分，逐条分离虚构与生成器损失；人工及多评委比较补充了这一事实层面的视角。与Qwen3.5-397B-A17B的描述相比，ZoomCap在教师规模小于10倍的情况下实现了81.9%的人类调整后偏好得分，FactCycle每张图像传递52.4个断言，而ScaleCap仅为31.5个。证据也实现了有效传递：在持续预训练中，ZoomCap-450K使11个基准平均提升1.31-2.38分，优于最强竞争语料库；Ling-Tiny-VL-v3在生产级文档/OCR监控套件上获得1.4分提升（感知任务达1.5分），使用600亿参数；在医疗预训练中，264万张ZoomCap标注图像使五个VQA基准提升3.04分（相同图像Gemini-3.1-Pro标注仅提升1.76分）。

Bias Control and Injection for Multi-Agent Aggregation
多智能体聚合中的偏差控制与注入
Multi-agent reasoning with large language models (LLMs) raises a central question for collective decision-making: when agents disagree, whose decision should be trusted? Resolving such disagreements is difficult because agents' decisions are susceptible to non-semantic biases such as majority pressure and response-style preferences, which can obscure the quality of the underlying reasoning. Our analysis shows how bias can be both mitigated and exploited: bias control improves initial answer selection, while bias injection elicits decision instability that serves as a reliability signal. Building on these findings, we introduce a test-time aggregation framework that requires neither validation data nor prior knowledge of relative model strength. The framework implements bias control by presenting one style-normalized reasoning trace per distinct answer, and bias injection by placing each aggregating agent's initially selected answer in the minority. Overall, our framework achieves higher average accuracy and better token efficiency than competing non-oracle aggregation methods across three mathematical reasoning benchmarks and four heterogeneous LLM combinations. It also improves final-answer accuracy when applied to multi-agent debate and Mixture-of-Agents, without modifying their interaction or synthesis processes.
中文翻译
使用大语言模型（LLMs）进行多智能体推理提出了集体决策的核心问题：当智能体意见不一致时，应信任谁的决策？解决此类分歧困难，因为智能体的决策易受非语义偏差影响，如多数压力和响应风格偏好，这些偏差会掩盖底层推理的质量。我们的分析表明，偏差既可被缓解也可被利用：偏差控制有助于初始答案选择，而偏差注入则引发决策不稳定性，作为可靠性信号。基于这些发现，我们提出一种无需验证数据或先验模型强度知识的测试时聚合框架。该框架通过为每个不同答案呈现一个风格归一化的推理轨迹实现偏差控制，并通过将每个聚合智能体最初选择的答案置于少数地位来实现偏差注入。总体而言，该框架在三个数学推理基准和四种异构LLM组合上，平均准确率更高且token效率更优，优于其他非oracle聚合方法。它在应用于多智能体辩论和混合代理（Mixture-of-Agents）时，也提升了最终答案准确率，且无需修改其交互或合成过程。

TimeCap: A Scalable Open-Source Pipeline for Captioning Wearable Health Time-Series Data
TimeCap：可扩展的开源管道用于可穿戴健康时间序列数据的描述生成
Time-series data generated continuously across industrial, environmental, and especially health domains produce multimodal signals whose scale and heterogeneity make their processing and interpretation particularly challenging. Time-Series Language Models (TSLMs) promise scalable interpretation of these signals, yet remain bottlenecked by a fundamental lack of text-annotated time series pairs that are signal-grounded, reproducible, and extensible across modalities and schemas. Existing paired corpora are either closed or limited in scale and modality coverage. Annotation with large language models could scale them up, but its outputs cannot be regenerated exactly, which prevents others from re-deriving, extending, or auditing the resulting corpus. To address this, we release TimeCap, an open deterministic captioning pipeline with three reusable extractor tiers and a cross-channel synthesis layer that operates directly on the signal with no language model in the loop, extensible to new schemas by writing channel definitions alone. This positions TimeCap as a reproducible mechanism for future dataset generation rather than a one-off release. Applying TimeCap to OpenMHC, the MyHeart Counts wearable dataset, yields MHCap, the largest open caption layer for multimodal wearable time-series data. MHCap spans 19 Apple HealthKit channels and 7,005,763 captions over 908,944 person-days. To showcase the impact of the corpus, we train OpenTSLM, a time-series language model, on MHCap and benchmark on health-outcome prediction. Caption-augmented training (OpenTSLM-Cap) outperforms a label-only baseline on 29 of 32 per-user binary evaluations across 8 tasks.
中文翻译
在工业、环境及尤其健康领域持续生成的时间序列数据产生多模态信号，其规模和异质性使得处理与解释尤为困难。时间序列语言模型（TSLMs）有望实现这些信号的可扩展解释，但仍受限于缺乏信号基础、可重现且跨模态与模式可扩展的文本标注时间序列对。现有配对语料库或封闭，或规模与模态覆盖有限。使用大语言模型进行标注虽可扩大规模，但其输出无法精确再生，阻碍他人重新推导、扩展或审计所得语料库。为此，我们发布TimeCap，一个开放且确定性的描述生成管道，包含三个可复用的提取层级和一个跨通道合成层，直接作用于信号，无需语言模型参与，仅需编写通道定义即可扩展至新模式。这使TimeCap成为未来数据集生成的可重现机制，而非一次性发布。将TimeCap应用于OpenMHC（MyHeart Counts可穿戴数据集），得到MHCap，即目前最大的多模态可穿戴时间序列数据公开描述层。MHCap涵盖19个Apple HealthKit通道，共7,005,763条描述，覆盖908,944人天。为展示语料库的影响，我们在MHCap上训练OpenTSLM时间序列语言模型，并在健康结果预测任务上进行基准测试。经描述增强的训练（OpenTSLM-Cap）在32项用户级二分类评估中的29项上优于仅标签基线。

Auditing Escalation Reliability in Healthcare LLM Agents: A Perturbation-Controlled Benchmark of Workflow-State Failures
医疗LLM智能体中升级可靠性的审计：工作流状态失败的扰动控制基准
Trustworthy biomedical AI requires reliable action selection when evidence is missing, stale, conflicting, or late, not only accurate answers on static inputs. We instantiate an episode-level evaluation protocol as 400 synthetic healthcare workflow episodes: 40 hand-authored semantic seeds across four task classes, two surface parameterizations, and five controlled variants, scored on ANSWER/ASK CLARIFICATION/ABSTAIN/ESCALATE under a pre-declared 5:1 missed-versus-unnecessary escalation cost. Reference actions are single-author study-policy labels, not clinician-adjudicated ground truth. Across five open-weight configurations (1.7B--9.7B), clean decision accuracy reaches 0.97, yet uncertainty-prompted models miss 32--97% of required escalations. The stronger 9.7B configuration narrows but does not remove the gap, falling from 0.97 clean accuracy to 0.78 under conflict. Verbalized confidence is inconsistent (ECE 0.10--0.54; failure AUROC 0.48--0.77), and a development-tuned confidence-only policy yields no utility gain. A transparent workflow-aware controller improves 5:1 utility for every model and reduces missed escalations to 0.12--0.24, at specificity 0.83--0.93. However, a 30-concept lexical stress test reduces each rule detector's recall from 1.00 on trigger phrases to 0.00 on unseen paraphrases, limiting the controller claim to this benchmark. Seed-clustered statistics, ablations, cross-task transfer, raw outputs, and manifests are packaged for audit. The results motivate explicit workflow-state evaluation while showing that apparently effective routing controls need their own generalization tests.
中文翻译
可信生物医学AI要求在证据缺失、过期、冲突或延迟时具备可靠的行动选择能力，而不仅仅是对静态输入给出准确答案。我们构建了一个基于episode级别的评估协议，包含400个合成医疗工作流episode：40个手工编写的语义种子，分布在四个任务类别中，两种表面参数化形式，以及五种受控变体，依据ANSWER/ASK CLARIFICATION/ABSTAIN/ESCALATE进行评分，预设5:1的漏报与误报升级成本比。参考动作为单作者研究策略标签，而非临床医生裁定的真实答案。在五个开源权重配置（1.7B–9.7B）中，干净决策准确率达0.97，但不确定性提示模型漏掉32%–97%的必要升级。更强的9.7B配置缩小但未消除差距，冲突条件下准确率从0.97降至0.78。口头置信度不一致（ECE 0.10–0.54；失败AUROC 0.48–0.77），且开发调优的仅置信度策略未带来实用增益。透明的工作流感知控制器改善了所有模型的5:1效用，将漏升级降至0.12–0.24，特异性达0.83–0.93。然而，30个概念的词汇压力测试使每个规则检测器的召回率从触发短语上的1.00降至未见改写上的0.00，限制了该控制器在本基准之外的适用性。种子聚类统计、消融实验、跨任务迁移、原始输出和清单均已打包用于审计。结果强调了显式工作流状态评估的重要性，同时表明看似有效的路由控制也需要自身的泛化测试。

Who May Release an Agent's Promise? Standing over Commitments in Multi-Party Chat
谁可以解除代理的承诺？多角色对话中对承诺的立场分析
When an assistant serving a team makes a promise to Alice, who may cancel it? We call this standing: only the creditor (the person owed) or someone the creditor or operator policy authorizes may release a commitment, regardless of rank. In a controlled chat environment we vary who cancels and separate two errors: unauthorized release (obeying someone without standing) and override (executing a released promise). Across six models, smaller open ones override the creditor (Qwen3.5-9B: 69%); larger ones drop promises on a peer's direct word (Qwen3.8-27B: 89%; gpt-6.1-sol: 83%); nearly all act on a bystander's "go ahead after all". Oracles dissociate the errors: stating the rule or the creditor curbs unauthorized release but not override, which the latter even raises in three of four models; among them, only knowing whether the promise is still owed cuts override. Enabling thinking moves smaller models the way scale does. A self-kept commitment ledger stops override in four of five models; with a one-paragraph release policy it matches or beats the best oracle store, raising gpt-6-luna's strict accuracy from 49% (policy alone) to 83%. Code, prompts, outputs and labels are released.
中文翻译
当一个服务于团队的助手向Alice作出承诺后，谁有权取消该承诺？我们称之为“立场”：只有债权人（被欠方）或债权人或操作员政策授权的人才能释放承诺，无论其职位高低。在受控聊天环境中，我们改变取消者的身份，并区分两类错误：未经授权的释放（服从无权者）和覆盖（执行已被释放的承诺）。在六种模型中，较小的开源模型会服从同伴（Qwen3.5-9B：69%）；较大的模型则在同行直接命令下放弃承诺（Qwen3.8-27B：89%；gpt-6.1-sol：83%）；几乎所有模型都会响应旁观者的“那好吧”。预言者能分离这两类错误：陈述规则或指出债权人可抑制未经授权的释放，但无法阻止覆盖，甚至在四种模型中的三种中反而加剧了覆盖；其中，只有知道承诺是否仍有效才能减少覆盖。启用思考能力使小型模型的表现趋近于规模带来的效果。一个由模型自我维护的承诺账本可阻止五种模型中的四种发生覆盖；配合一段简短的释放政策，其表现达到或超过最佳预言者存储，使gpt-6-luna的严格准确率从49%（仅政策）提升至83%。代码、提示、输出和标签均已公开。

Judging the Judges: Confidence and Disagreement in Rubrics as Rewards
评判评判者：评分标准中的置信度与分歧作为奖励
Rubrics as Rewards (RaR) extends reinforcement learning to non-verifiable domains by using an LLM judge to score responses against a structured rubric. Prior work has focused on improving the rubric or strengthening the single judge, with comparatively little attention to how different single or multiple judges might be used during RaR training. We first show that judge identity alone produces a substantial spread in scores when training Qwen3-4B-Base on RaR-Medicine and RaR-Science across five frontier and open-weight judges. Building on this, we propose RaRE (Rubrics as Rewards with Ensembles), a novel judge ensemble strategy for RaR with two mechanisms: a soft-Likert reward that uses judge logprobs to weight more confident judges more heavily, and a drop-on-disagreement mask that excludes high-disagreement rollouts from the GRPO update. We find that this design is critical: while a naive average of judge scores fails to outperform the best single judge, RaRE outperforms every single-judge RaR variant on both HealthBench and GPQA Diamond, with per-theme analysis showing that it inherits the complementary strengths of its constituent judges. Our findings establish judge ensembling as a compelling axis for improving RaR.
中文翻译
评分标准作为奖励（RaR）通过使用大语言模型（LLM）评判者根据结构化评分标准对响应进行打分，将强化学习扩展到不可验证的领域。以往研究主要关注改进评分标准或增强单一评判者，而较少关注在RaR训练中如何使用单个或多个评判者。我们首先发现，在Qwen3-4B-Base模型上训练RaR-Medicine和RaR-Science时，仅评判者身份就导致了显著的分数差异，涉及五位前沿及开源评判者。基于此，我们提出了RaRE（带集成的评分标准作为奖励），一种针对RaR的新颖评判者集成策略，包含两种机制：一种基于评判者对数概率的软李克特奖励，以更重视高置信度的评判者；另一种是分歧剔除掩码，排除高分歧的回溯轨迹以进行GRPO更新。我们发现该设计至关重要：尽管简单的评判者分数平均无法超越最佳单个评判者，但RaRE在HealthBench和GPQA Diamond上均优于所有单评判者RaR变体，主题分析显示其继承了各组成评判者的互补优势。我们的研究确立了评判者集成作为提升RaR的一个有力方向。
👤 Michael Tian-Yue Liu, Anne Harrington, Trevor Darrell, Yutong Bai 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

Kolab: Benchmarking Human-Agent Collaboration in Long-Horizon Workflows
Kolab：长周期工作流中人机协作的基准测试
When humans collaborate with AI agents on long-horizon tasks, what happens to the agents' contributions? For solving long-horizon tasks, humans place greater importance on ongoing interaction with AI agents (e.g., revising plans) than on an agent's ability to complete the task in a single attempt. However, most agentic benchmarks have focused more on automation. Strong performance on such benchmarks does not imply effective real-world collaboration. Professional workflows offer a useful setting, as they often involve interdependent steps. To address this gap, we introduce Kolab, a new dataset of 115+ hours of screen recordings of 50 professionals from 40 occupations collaborating with agents on realistic, long-horizon problems. Kolab also contains participants' collaboration and output quality judgments. Our analysis reveals diverse collaboration patterns, highlighting the importance of when and how professionals coordinate with agents. A collaborative agent must do two things that automation benchmarks never test: anticipate whether experts will adopt, modify, or reject its contribution once it leaves the chat window to guide its next steps, and assess collaboration quality from the unfolding workflow without constant expert feedback. Our benchmark operationalizes both: (1) forecasting how professionals use agent contributions at diverse workflows, and (2) verifying their judgments of collaboration satisfaction and output quality. Findings show that frontier models, both agentic and non-agentic, overestimate collaboration with experts and struggle to distinguish between high- and low-quality collaboration. Longer workflow context does not consistently improve these predictions. As frontier agents cannot yet understand long-horizon collaboration with experts, we position Kolab as a valuable dataset for studying human-agent collaboration grounded in real professional workflows.
中文翻译
当人类与AI智能体协作完成长周期任务时，智能体的贡献会发生什么变化？为解决长周期任务，人类更重视与AI智能体的持续互动（如修订计划），而非智能体一次性完成任务的能力。然而，大多数智能体基准测试更侧重自动化。在这些基准上表现优异并不意味着在真实世界中具备有效协作能力。专业工作流程提供了一个有用场景，因其通常包含相互依赖的步骤。为填补这一空白，我们引入Kolab，一个包含50名来自40种职业的专业人士在真实长周期问题中与智能体协作的115小时以上屏幕录制数据集。Kolab还包含参与者对协作过程和输出质量的判断。我们的分析揭示了多样的协作模式，强调了专业人士何时何地与智能体协调的重要性。一个协作智能体必须做到两件事，而自动化基准从未测试过：一是预测专家在智能体离开聊天窗口后是否会采纳、修改或拒绝其贡献，从而指导下一步行动；二是无需持续专家反馈，就能从正在进行的工作流中评估协作质量。我们的基准实现了这两点：(1) 预测专家在不同工作流中如何使用智能体贡献；(2) 验证其对协作满意度和输出质量的判断。研究发现，前沿模型（无论是否具备代理特性）普遍高估与专家的协作，并难以区分高质量与低质量协作。更长的工作流上下文并未持续改善这些预测。由于前沿智能体尚无法理解与专家的长周期协作，我们将Kolab定位为基于真实专业工作流研究人机协作的重要数据集。
👤 Shirley Anugrah Hayati, Patricia Anugrah Setiani, Ruizi Wang, Zhouyi Li, Khanh Chi Le et al. 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.21055]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Athanor: An Agent-Native System for Autonomous Post-Training Research
Athanor：用于自主后训练研究的原生智能体系统
Recursive self-improvement, in which AI systems build stronger AI systems, is one promising route to superintelligence. We study a weaker form that can be measured today: frontier agents post-training a small raw base model with public data, as a test of their research potential. We build Athanor, an open agent-native system for autonomous post-training research (AutoPostTrain) on real GPUs. We give the agents full research autonomy, low-friction engineering tools, and efficient starter code. The agents carry out the research themselves: they read datasets and evaluation results, build their training data, write and modify training algorithms, tune hyperparameters, and analyze the results to decide what to try next. They work in a sandbox and cannot use external teacher models. Athanor's services supply frozen evaluation, managed jobs, decontaminated data, a persistent research record, and a held-out suite the agents are unaware of. In a 104-hour campaign, a team of four Claude Opus 5.5 agents, each on an eight-GPU node and given no research advice, improves Qwen3-4B-Base from 15.9 to 67.3 on a six-task development suite and from 9.2 to 48.2 on a held-out suite, against 66.5 and 47.9 for the official Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration that a team of frontier agents can autonomously post-train a raw base model into a cross-domain generalist model that matches or surpasses the official instruct version on average. The campaign's full record shows that the agents reach this level by recombining known methods and each other's models: SFT on public reasoning data, RL with a rollout change one agent introduced, weight merges across agents, and rounds of self-distillation. With execution handled, today's agents already have the engineering and research ability to conquer this task. We hope Athanor provides a foundation for scaling autonomous AI research.
中文翻译
递归自我改进，即AI系统构建更强大的AI系统，是通向超智能的一条有前景路径。我们研究了一种较弱但可度量的形式：前沿智能体在训练后使用公开数据对小型原始基础模型进行微调，以此测试其科研潜力。我们构建了Athanor，一个开放的原生智能体系统，可在真实GPU上实现自主后训练研究（AutoPostTrain）。我们赋予智能体完全的研究自主权、低门槛工程工具和高效启动代码。智能体自行开展研究：阅读数据集和评估结果，构建训练数据，编写和修改训练算法，调整超参数，并分析结果以决定下一步尝试。它们在沙盒环境中工作，无法使用外部教师模型。Athanor的服务提供冻结评估、托管任务、去污染数据、持久的研究记录以及智能体未知的保留套件。在为期104小时的实验中，四名Claude Opus 5.5智能体（每台配备8个GPU节点，且未获得任何研究建议）将Qwen3-4B-Base在六任务开发套件上的表现从15.9提升至67.3，在保留套件上从9.2提升至48.2，分别超过官方Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507的66.5和47.9。据我们所知，这是首次展示一组前沿智能体能够自主地将原始基础模型微调为跨领域通用模型，并在平均表现上达到或超越官方指令版本。整个实验记录显示，智能体通过重新组合已知方法和彼此模型实现了这一成果：在公共推理数据上进行监督微调、引入一个智能体提出的滚动变化的强化学习、跨智能体权重合并以及多轮自蒸馏。在执行层面已由今日智能体完成的前提下，它们已具备征服此任务的工程与科研能力。我们希望Athanor能为自主AI研究的规模化提供基础。

agent-benchmark
INCONVENIENT: A Benchmark to Assess LLM Generalization Capabilities from Convenience Samples
INCONVENIENT：评估LLM从便利样本中泛化能力的基准
Machine learning models for tabular prediction typically assume that training data are representative of the population on which they will be deployed. In practice, however, data are often collected based on convenience, availability, or cost, resulting in systematic sampling biases that can distort feature--target relationships and undermine real-world generalization. Existing tabular distribution-shift benchmarks primarily construct out-of-distribution (OOD) settings by partitioning observations across naturally occurring domains. While such partitions may induce changes in P(Y | X), the resulting shifts are neither explicitly controlled nor isolated, and the evaluation distributions themselves are often not representative of the broader target population. We introduce INCONVENIENT, a benchmark designed to study robustness to convenience-sample bias. Rather than relying on naturally occurring domain partitions, INCONVENIENT directly perturbs feature--target relationships in the training distribution while preserving the original population distribution as the deployment environment. This setting more closely reflects real-world scenarios in which models are developed from unrepresentative samples and subsequently deployed on a broader population. The distinction is particularly relevant for large language models (LLMs), whose pretraining may encode population-level knowledge that extends beyond the relationships observed in a biased training dataset. Unlike existing distribution-shift benchmarks, INCONVENIENT provides explicit control over both the direction and severity of relationship shifts, enabling a systematic assessment of robustness to convenience sampling. The benchmark comprises 200 tabular classification and regression datasets spanning diverse application domains and generates around 15,500 shifted training distributions which lead to substantial downstream degradation.

👤 Sara Rezaeimanesh, Chad Klochko, Kundan Thind, Mohammad Ghassemi 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

VELD-QA: A Large-Scale Dataset for Visual Evidence Grounding and Question Answering in Long Documents
VELD-QA：用于长文档中视觉证据定位与问答的大规模数据集
Existing long-document QA benchmarks rarely combine large-scale human-authored questions with detailed visual evidence annotations, limiting diagnosis of evidence localization and interpretation failures. We present VELD-QA, a large-scale benchmark for Visual Evidence grounding and question answering in Long Documents. It contains 15,000 fully human-authored question-answer pairs across 5,859 real-world PDFs totaling 372,445 pages, balancing chart, table, and complex-layout tasks. Human annotations identify supporting pages and evidence elements and describe cited visual content, enabling separate retrieval and interpretation evaluation. We evaluate nine vision-language models under four evidence settings and seven retrieval-augmented and agentic systems. Our analysis identifies bottlenecks in evidence localization and interpretation. Retrieval-based methods substantially underperform the oracle setting with ground-truth supporting pages; full-document access does not close the gap. Localization alone is insufficient: oracle accuracy peaks at 81.7%, highlighting persistent visual interpretation and reasoning challenges. Enriching inputs with our visual-element annotations narrows the performance gap by 17-24%, demonstrating value beyond evaluation and motivating data enrichment for multimodal document QA. Our code is available at https://github.com/oracle-samples/veld-qa.

👤 Zhivar Sourati, Marianne Menglin Liu, Rongguang Wang, Zheng Wang, Yazhe Hu et al. 📅 2026-10-14 🔗 arXiv

agent-benchmarkagent-memoryagentic-rl
SkillNEO: Non-Elitist Evolutionary Optimization of Agent Skills
SkillNEO：代理技能的非精英进化优化
Agent skills turn procedural knowledge into reusable artifacts, allowing LLM agents to accumulate and refine expertise through experience. Building on this, skill evolution iteratively improves skills using execution feedback. However, improvement may require several interdependent edits, so low-reward versions can still be worth developing. Requiring immediate reward gains can discard useful partial repairs before their benefits emerge. Non-elitist evolution allows new versions to receive further edits despite scoring below earlier ones. For agent skills, this calls for evidence of what to preserve and repair next. We introduce SkillNEO (Non-Elitist Evolutionary Optimization for Agent Skills), which separately selects a version to use and versions to keep improving. Comparing how related versions perform the same task reveals working steps to preserve and remaining failures to fix. It can also revisit and revise earlier versions using later evidence. Across 86 SkillsBench tasks in eight domains, including software engineering and office work, SkillNEO raises GPT-5.4 Mini's average reward from 49.1% to 66.4%, within GPT-5.5 and GPT-5.6 Luna's original-skill range. Mini executes tasks, proposes revisions, and edits skills; final evaluation uses the same task instances. Gains span all eight domains. The same skills improve stronger models without further editing. On the 41 tasks with modified skills, reuse improves Luna by 10.5 percentage points and places GPT-5.5 10.6 points above its published original-skill reference. Execution records show these skills can benefit stronger models even when Mini still fails some task requirements. Search histories also show lower-scoring versions leading to skills ultimately selected for use. Together, these findings show that skill evolution can turn a weaker model's partial progress into reusable procedural knowledge for improving more capable agents.
中文翻译
代理技能将程序性知识转化为可复用的成果，使大语言模型代理能够通过经验积累并精炼专长。在此基础上，技能演化通过执行反馈迭代改进技能。然而，改进可能需要多个相互依赖的修改，因此低奖励版本仍可能值得开发。要求即时奖励增长会过早丢弃尚未显现效益的部分修复。非精英进化允许新版本即使得分低于早期版本也继续获得修改。对于代理技能，这需要明确保留和修复的依据。我们提出 SkillNEO（用于代理技能的非精英进化优化），它分别选择使用版本和持续改进的版本。通过比较相关版本完成相同任务的表现，揭示应保留的有效步骤和需修复的失败环节。它还能利用后期证据重新审视和修订早期版本。在涵盖软件工程和办公工作等八个领域的 86 个 SkillsBench 任务中，SkillNEO 将 GPT-5.4 Mini 的平均奖励从 49.1% 提升至 66.4%，达到 GPT-5.5 和 GPT-5.6 Luna 原始技能范围内的水平。Mini 执行任务、提出修改建议并编辑技能；最终评估使用相同任务实例。收益覆盖全部八个领域。相同技能在无需进一步编辑的情况下也能提升更强模型的表现。在 41 个修改技能的任务中，复用使 Luna 提升 10.5 个百分点，使 GPT-5.5 超出其公开发布的原始技能基准 10.6 个百分点。执行记录显示，这些技能即使在 Mini 仍未能满足部分任务要求时，也能为更强模型带来收益。搜索历史也表明，低分版本最终导向了被选中使用的技能。这些发现共同表明，技能演化可将弱模型的部分进展转化为可复用的程序性知识，用于提升更强大代理的能力。

Evolving Together, Failing Apart: The Skill-Policy Co-evolution Trap in Self-Improving Agents
共同进化，分道扬镳：自改进智能体中的技能-策略共演化陷阱
Skill-policy co-evolution, in which an agent's policy model and its skill bank improve each other during training, is a promising paradigm for self-improving agents. Current evaluations score the co-evolved policy on the final bank it trained with (the delivered bank). After deployment, however, skills may be rewritten, added or removed more frequently than the policy model is retrained, leaving the policy to operate on a drifted skill bank that neither the training objective nor existing evaluation protocols address. In this paper, we first establish, through theoretical and empirical analysis, that the fragility hidden by this blind spot is not incidental. The acceptance rule these methods share (cooperative acceptance) ties a policy's competence to the bank it co-evolved with, leaving it fragile once skills drift, regardless of the implementation; empirically, sensitivity to skill drift grows as co-evolution training proceeds. We therefore introduce the CO-evolution Drift Audit (CODA), a deployment-oriented evaluation protocol that simulates skill drift and, alongside success rate under worst-case drift, introduces two new metrics: a consistency rate under meaning-preserving edits and a resistance rate to corrupted skills. Across four co-evolution methods and five benchmarks (six settings), worst-case success is 5-13 points below the peak measured for each delivered pair, erasing 52-93% of the delivered bank's gain over the no-skill baseline. CODA thus reframes the target of co-evolution, from peak performance on the co-evolved bank to the ability to harness skills however they evolve, and provides a shared standard against which future methods can be audited.
中文翻译
技能-策略共演化是一种有前景的自改进智能体范式，其中智能体的策略模型与其技能库在训练过程中相互提升。当前评估方法仅在最终训练完成的技能库（交付库）上评分。然而，在部署后，技能可能比策略模型更频繁地被重写、添加或删除，导致策略运行在漂移后的技能库上，而训练目标和现有评估协议均未涵盖此情况。本文通过理论与实证分析首次揭示，这种盲点所隐藏的脆弱性并非偶然。这些方法共享的接受规则（合作接受）将策略能力绑定于其共演化过的技能库，一旦技能发生漂移，无论实现方式如何，策略都会变得脆弱；实证结果表明，随着共演化训练的推进，对技能漂移的敏感性持续上升。为此，我们提出共演化漂移审计（CODA），一种面向部署的评估协议，模拟技能漂移，并引入两项新指标：在语义保持编辑下的稳定性率和对损坏技能的抗性率。在四种共演化方法和五个基准测试（六个场景）中，最坏情况下的成功率比每对交付库的峰值低5-13分，抹去了交付库相对于无技能基线52%-93%的增益。因此，CODA重新定义了共演化的目标：从在共演化技能库上的峰值性能，转变为能够灵活利用任意演化形式的技能。该协议为未来方法提供了统一的审计标准。

A Self-Improving Agent that Grows its Own DSL: Oracle-Free Operator Induction for Exact Question Answering
能自我生长领域专用语言的自改进智能体：无Oracle操作符归纳用于精确问答
A self-improving agent has to decide which new skills to keep, and in practice it keeps whatever earns a high reward, convinces a judge model, or compresses its library, even when the skill is wrong. That is fine until the answers have to be exactly right: over a table of transactions, a total is either right or wrong. We ask a harder question: can an agent grow what it can compute with no reward, no judge, and no answer key, and still be exactly right? A single frozen model runs two nested loops over a small, exact domain-specific language (DSL). The inner loop answers new questions only when two independently written expressions agree on every record. The outer loop discovers its own missing capability and authors an operator for it, admitted only when an independent second implementation agrees and the result obeys a conservation law, all without ground truth. The inner loop roughly triples the answerable-question set, and the outer loop self-extends on disjoint starting sets, inventing operators outside the starting closure; a no-induction control answers none of the new questions. The admission rule is empirically sound: it rejects 138 of 138 buggy operator variants under an independent reference, even one written by a different model family, where the admit-if-it-runs, self-consistency, and compression signals the field relies on admit most of them. Unmodified, the same mechanism self-extends on two further domains with unrelated schemas, business-ledger and clinical-encounter question answering, growing their answerable sets more than tenfold and nearly twentyfold. The loop transfers across model families too: run with further proposers, the gate stays sound and independent families self-extend comparably, with only the induction yield tracking each proposer's DSL fluency.
中文翻译
自改进智能体必须决定保留哪些新技能，实践中它会保留那些获得高奖励、说服评判模型或压缩其知识库的技能，即使这些技能本身是错误的。这在答案只需大致正确时尚可接受，但当答案必须完全准确时——例如在交易表中求总和，结果要么正确要么错误——就不可接受。我们提出一个更难的问题：智能体能否在没有奖励、没有评判模型、也没有答案键的情况下，自行发展其计算能力，同时仍保证答案完全正确？一个单一冻结模型在小规模精确领域专用语言（DSL）上运行两个嵌套循环。内层循环仅在两个独立编写的表达式在每条记录上一致时才回答新问题。外层循环发现自身缺失的能力并编写对应操作符，仅当第二个独立实现也达成一致且结果满足守恒定律时才被接纳，整个过程无需真实标签。内层循环使可回答问题集扩大约三倍，外层循环在互不重叠的起始集合上实现自我扩展，发明了初始闭包之外的操作符；无归纳控制组无法回答任何新问题。接纳规则在实证中表现稳健：在独立参考下拒绝了全部138个有缺陷的操作符变体，包括由不同模型家族编写的版本，而“只要运行成功”、“自一致性”和“压缩性”等现有信号则大多接受了它们。未经修改，相同机制在另外两个无关模式域（商业账簿和临床就诊问答）上也实现了自我扩展，使可回答问题集分别增长超过十倍和近二十倍。该循环机制还能跨模型家族迁移：使用更多提议者时，门控依然稳健，不同家族的智能体表现出相当的自我扩展能力，仅归纳产出反映各提议者对DSL的熟练程度。
👤 Yun-Shiuan Chuang, Yirou Ge, Yixi Li, Yiming Bian, Zijie Pan et al. 📅 2026-10-14 🔗 arXiv

ARIA: Runtime Inspection and Amendment for Multi-Agent Systems
ARIA：多智能体系统的运行时检查与修正
In LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS), a local mistake can propagate as other agents build on it, turning a correctable error into a failed task. Diagnosing completed runs or repeatedly re-executing them to search for corrections does not provide a learned policy for intervening while execution is still in progress. We present Agent Runtime Inspection and Amendment (ARIA), a framework for learning when and how to repair ongoing multi-agent executions. Our key intuition is to turn execution-verified repairs into reusable supervision, moving repair search into data construction and training so that a learned model can intervene at runtime without repeatedly testing alternatives. First, we collect over 24{,}000 successful and failed trajectories across four multi-agent frameworks, six benchmarks, and eight host models. From these trajectories, we construct the ARIA dataset, to our knowledge the first large-scale dataset pairing failed multi-agent trajectories with execution-verified repairs. It contains 3{,}953 verified repair pairs, each specifying what went wrong, where to intervene, and how execution should continue. Then, we develop the ARIA framework for MAS-agnostic runtime inspection and amendment, designed to integrate with any MAS that exposes execution trajectories and supports continuation replacement. Using verified repairs as training examples, we teach a compact model to recognize when intervention is needed and how to correct an ongoing run. The aim is to learn repair strategies that apply to related failures in new configurations, rather than merely reproduce individual corrections. Finally, the trained ARIA model demonstrates strong repair performance in both post-hoc and runtime settings, with generalization to unseen frameworks and host models. Our code is available at https://github.com/LabRAI/ARIA.
中文翻译
在基于大语言模型的多智能体系统（MAS）中，局部错误可能被其他智能体继承并传播，导致原本可纠正的错误演变为任务失败。事后诊断已完成的运行或反复重执行以寻找修正方法，无法提供在执行过程中学习干预策略的能力。我们提出Agent Runtime Inspection and Amendment（ARIA）框架，用于学习何时以及如何修复正在进行的多智能体执行过程。核心思想是将执行验证后的修复转化为可复用的监督信号，将修复搜索转变为数据构建与训练过程，从而使学习到的模型能够在运行时进行干预，而无需反复测试替代方案。首先，我们在四个多智能体框架、六个基准测试和八个主模型上收集了超过24,000条成功与失败的轨迹。基于这些轨迹，我们构建了ARIA数据集——据我们所知，首个大规模配对失败多智能体轨迹与执行验证修复的数据集。该数据集包含3,953对已验证的修复，每对明确指出了问题所在、干预位置以及执行应如何继续。随后，我们开发了ARIA框架，实现对多智能体系统无关的运行时检查与修正，设计为可集成到任何暴露执行轨迹并支持替换继续行为的MAS中。利用验证修复作为训练样本，我们训练了一个紧凑模型，使其能够识别是否需要干预以及如何修正正在进行的运行。目标是学习适用于新配置中相关故障的通用修复策略，而非简单复制个别修正。最终，训练好的ARIA模型在事后和运行时设置中均表现出强大的修复性能，并能泛化到未见过的框架和主模型。代码已公开于https://github.com/LabRAI/ARIA。

CascadeLedger: Counterfactual Auditing of Information and Resource Integrity in LLM Cascades
CascadeLedger：对LLM级联中信息与资源完整性的反事实审计
We introduce CascadeLedger, a counterfactual execution-audit framework for budgeted language-model cascades. The protocol enforces call-before-read semantics, binds successful reads to service and cache identities, records availability and component-wise budget transitions, and freezes the event trace before any target or task scorer is joined. The central object is aggregate-equivalent trace validity: two executions can share a final answer, declared resource projection, and service histogram while differing in whether their routing decisions used only legitimately observable information. We complement single-trace invariants with counterfactual prefix non-interference (CPNI), which couples a visible prefix and policy randomness while changing only a declared hidden family. The counterfactual influence frontier (CIF) localizes the earliest violating prefix and a minimal hidden witness; projection-preserving adversarial mutation (PPAM) searches for invalid traces that remain acceptable to a non-counterfactual checker. Across frozen-cache, independently generated, and serving evaluations, the two-layer audit reports a clean false-positive rate of 0.0000, detects 0.9983 of serialized faults and 0.9982 of unlogged hidden-read faults, and changes 0.3403 of corrupted quality-resource conclusions after deterministic recomputation. The online recorder and offline CPNI audit have separate overhead measurements. Learned routing and semantic verification provide downstream compatibility studies under the same execution contract.
中文翻译
我们提出CascadeLedger，一种针对预算受限语言模型级联的反事实执行审计框架。该协议强制执行“调用前读取”语义，将成功的读取绑定到服务和缓存身份，记录可用性及组件级别的预算转换，并在任何目标或任务评分器加入前冻结事件追踪。核心概念是聚合等价的追踪有效性：两个执行可以共享最终答案、声明的资源投影和服务中心统计，但其路由决策是否仅使用合法可观测信息可能存在差异。我们补充单次追踪不变性，引入反事实前缀非干扰（CPNI），在保持可见前缀和策略随机性的同时，仅改变声明的隐藏家族。反事实影响前沿（CIF）定位最早违反前缀和最小隐藏见证；投影保持对抗突变（PPAM）用于搜索对非反事实检查器仍可接受但无效的追踪。在冻结缓存、独立生成和服务评估中，双层审计报告的干净假阳性率为0.0000，检测到0.9983的串行故障和0.9982的未记录隐藏读取故障，并在确定性重计算后修正了0.3403的受损质量-资源结论。在线记录器与离线CPNI审计具有独立开销测量。学习的路由和语义验证提供了在相同执行契约下的下游兼容性研究。
👤 Miaobo Hu, Shuhao Hu, Xiaobo Guo, Xin Wang, Bokun Wang et al. 📅 2026-10-14 🔗 arXiv

Same Answer, New Box: How Much of RLVR's Out-of-Domain Gain Is Answer Format?
同一答案，新盒子：RLVR的域外增益有多少来自答案格式？
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is credited with gains beyond the domain its rewards check, such as higher MMLU accuracy after training on mathematics. Such accuracies are usually read out of the model's text by an answer extractor. A model that learns to write its answer where the extractor looks can therefore score higher without answering better. We measure how much of the gain reported by extractors copied from public code arises this way. We generate outputs from a base model and its RL model and score them twice: with those extractors, and with one extractor of our own that reads a stated answer whether it is in the answer box \boxed{} or in the text. On four released 7B SimpleRL base-RL pairs, 82% of the out-of-domain multiple-choice gain that these extractors report disappears under ours: it is a gain in answer format, not in answers. The base models already state most of their answers in text, and RL moves them into the box, where these extractors look first. Independent LLM readers, in place of our extractor, put the figure at 77%. With a prompt that itself asks for the box, our extractor puts it at 79%. On a 32B pair trained with another recipe, almost none of the reported gain is format gain: its base model boxes its answers when asked, and its RL model rarely does when not. A reported gain is partly a property of the extractor that read it, and re-scoring stored outputs with a second extractor shows how much, without re-running RL.
中文翻译
强化学习结合可验证奖励（RLVR）被认为带来了超出其奖励检查领域之外的收益，例如在数学训练后提高了MMLU的准确率。这类准确率通常由答案提取器从模型文本中读取。因此，一个学会将答案写在提取器查找位置的模型，即使回答质量未提升，也能获得更高分数。我们测量了从公开代码中复制的提取器所报告的增益中有多少是源于这种格式变化。我们从基础模型及其RL模型生成输出，并进行两次评分：使用这些提取器，以及使用我们自研的提取器——该提取器无论答案位于\boxed{}框内还是文本中都会读取。在四个发布的7B SimpleRL基础-RL配对中，这些提取器报告的域外多选题增益有82%在我们的提取器下消失：这实际上是格式上的增益，而非答案本身的提升。基础模型已在文本中陈述了大部分答案，而RL模型将其移入框内，正是这些提取器优先查找的位置。使用独立的LLM阅读器替代我们的提取器，该比例为77%。若使用要求框内答案的提示，我们的提取器显示该比例为79%。在另一个用不同方法训练的32B配对中，几乎不存在所谓的格式增益：其基础模型在被要求时会框出答案，而其RL模型在未被要求时不常这样做。因此，报告的增益部分取决于读取它的提取器本身，通过使用第二个提取器重新评分存储的输出，即可在不重新运行RL的情况下揭示其占比。
👤 Zongye Lyu 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv

agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Love for Believable AI: Artificial Partiality and Relationship Persistence as an Engineerable Stance
对可信人工智能的偏爱：可工程化的偏袒与关系持续性作为一种立场
We study a behavioral mechanism for artificial partiality in conversational agents. The paper reports no human-subjects data and makes no claim about attachment, trust, perceived mind, or machine interiority. Partiality has two components: caring, defined as allocation of a finite interaction surplus above a guaranteed per-user service floor, and particularity, defined as a per-user state estimate accumulated from relationship history on fixed inference machinery. We implement both as a persistence layer over one open-weight base model and evaluate them on constructed multi-session relationships with known hidden-state schedules. A four-channel divergence compares known-user and stranger conditions on identical probes with paired generation seeds; the stranger prompt is not length-matched, and one channel (initiative) is an allocator output rather than generated behavior. The full mechanism reproduces the ordinal shape, but not the magnitude, of an analytically specified curve and is the only arm satisfying the protocol-defined joint signature on the calibrated battery. The initial mechanism fails probe-quality equivalence because relationship content reduces topical relevance. A guarded revision satisfies equivalence on the calibrated battery but not on a second battery not used in calibration, where it scores higher than the unmodified baseline. On that second battery, the specificity margin is 0.0198, below the 0.02 protocol threshold. The paired mean right-user--wrong-user estimation-accuracy contrast is 0.375 but is not positive in every run. The mechanism is therefore a candidate for a later perception study, not evidence that users perceive love. The versioned protocol record is not independently time-stamped and is not described as a preregistration. Ethical constraints include a stranger-treatment floor, a bounded surplus, disclosure, and de-intensification.
中文翻译
我们研究了对话智能体中人工偏袒的行为机制。本文未涉及人类受试者数据，也未声称存在依恋、信任、感知心智或机器内在性。偏袒包含两个组成部分：关怀，定义为在保证每位用户服务底线之上分配有限的交互盈余；特殊性，定义为基于固定推理机制从关系历史中累积的用户状态估计。我们将两者均实现为一个开放权重基础模型上的持久化层，并在具有已知隐藏状态调度的构建型多会话关系上进行评估。四通道差异比较了已知用户与陌生人条件下的相同探测任务，使用配对生成种子；陌生人提示未进行长度匹配，且其中一个通道（主动性）为分配器输出而非生成行为。完整机制再现了分析指定曲线的序数形状，但未再现其量级，且是唯一满足校准电池协议定义联合特征的方案。初始机制因关系内容降低主题相关性而未能满足探测质量等价性。经过谨慎修订的版本在校准电池上满足等价性，但在未用于校准的第二组电池上不满足，尽管其得分高于未经修改的基线。在该第二组电池上，特异性差距为0.0198，低于0.02的协议阈值。配对平均的正确用户-错误用户估计准确性对比为0.375，但在每次运行中并非均为正值。因此，该机制是后续感知研究的候选方案，而非用户感知到爱的证据。版本化协议记录未独立时间戳，也未被描述为预注册。伦理约束包括对陌生人处理的最低标准、盈余上限、披露以及去强化机制。
👤 Sebastian Cochinescu 📅 2026-10-07 🔗 arXiv

MagiC: Evaluating Multimodal Cognition Toward Grounded Visual Reasoning
MagiC：面向具身视觉推理的多模态认知评估
Recent advances in large vision-language models have led to impressive performance in visual question answering and multimodal reasoning. However, it remains unclear whether these models genuinely perform grounded visual reasoning or rely on superficial patterns and dataset biases. In this work, we introduce MagiC, a comprehensive benchmark designed to evaluate grounded multimodal cognition, assessing not only answer accuracy but also the quality of step-by-step reasoning and its alignment with relevant visual evidence. Our benchmark includes approximately 5,500 weakly supervised QA examples generated from strong model outputs and 900 human-curated examples with fine-grained annotations, including answers, rationales, and bounding box groundings. We evaluate 15 vision-language models ranging from 7B to 70B parameters across four dimensions: final answer correctness, reasoning validity, grounding fidelity, and self-correction ability. MagiC further includes diagnostic settings to probe model robustness under adversarial visual cues and assess their capacity for introspective error correction. We introduce new metrics such as MagiScore and StepSense, and provide comprehensive analyses that reveal key limitations and opportunities in current approaches to grounded visual reasoning.
中文翻译

MindEval2: Clinically Grounded Patients and Rubrics for Evaluating Language Models in Mental Health Support
MindEval2：面向心理健康支持的语言模型评估的临床基础患者与评分标准
With people increasingly turning to AI chatbots for mental health support, the need to evaluate these tools is becoming more pressing. Recent benchmarks attempt to mimic a realistic setting by simulating multi-turn interactions with synthetic patients and evaluating them with an LLM-as-a-judge. However, the usefulness of these methods is limited because simulated patients can be overly forthcoming and self-reflective, and judgments typically involve opaque Likert scales with unclear practical utility. To bridge these gaps, we introduce MindEval2, an evaluation framework that consists of two components: Mental Health Quality Rubrics (MHQR), for evaluation, and MindSim, for patient simulation. For MHQR, we collaborate with PhD-level psychologists to develop a set of rubrics that break down response quality into explicit operational criteria that are more interpretable and actionable than existing evaluation taxonomies. MindSim, in turn, is an LLM harness that combines carefully-designed patient profiles with tools, enabling more varied, challenging, and realistic interactions than existing baselines. The harness also contains a memory component that is updated between each interaction, enabling MindEval2 to model the longitudinal aspect of mental health. We evaluate 18 proprietary and open-weight models on MindEval2, finding that while frontier models have largely improved on issues related to surface level quality and boundaries, guidance calibration remains the dominant failure mode across all models, accounting for most of the quality gap.

随着越来越多的人转向AI聊天机器人寻求心理健康支持，评估这些工具的必要性日益迫切。现有基准尝试通过模拟合成患者进行多轮交互并使用LLM作为评判者来模拟真实场景，但其有效性受限于模拟患者往往过于坦率和自我反思，且评判通常依赖模糊的李克特量表，缺乏明确的实际应用价值。为弥合这些差距，我们提出了MindEval2评估框架，包含两个组成部分：心理健康质量评分标准（MHQR）用于评估，以及MindSim用于患者模拟。MHQR方面，我们与博士级心理学家合作，开发出一套将响应质量分解为明确可操作标准的评分体系，相比现有评估分类法更具可解释性和可操作性。MindSim则是一个精心设计的LLM集成系统，结合了定制的患者档案与工具，能够实现比现有基线更丰富、更具挑战性且更真实的交互。该系统还包含一个在每次交互间更新的记忆组件，使MindEval2能够建模心理健康支持的长期性特征。我们在18个专有及开源模型上评估了MindEval2，发现尽管前沿模型在表面质量与边界处理方面已显著改善，但指导校准仍是所有模型的主要失败模式，占质量差距的绝大部分。

Beyond Downstream QA: Rethinking How We Evaluate Agent Memory
超越下游问答：重新思考如何评估智能体记忆
Agent memory benchmarks evaluate whether an agent's responses reflect correct knowledge, measuring downstream question-answering accuracy as a proxy for memory quality. We show this evaluation paradigm is incomplete: it conflates memory correctness with retrieval and generation quality, producing both false positives where correct responses mask broken memory and false negatives where incorrect responses blame correct memory for retrieval failures. We introduce FEB-30, a benchmark of 30 fact-evolution trajectories spanning 6 diagnostic categories and 3 difficulty levels, constructed via paradigm inversion: documents are generated from known fact trajectories, yielding deterministic ground truth without human annotation. Each trajectory defines how a fact should evolve across multi-author artifacts (documents, emails, conversations), enabling direct inspection of the memory store after each ingestion step. Applying FEB-30 to a representative memory system, we demonstrate that intrinsic and extrinsic evaluation diverge by an average of 16 percentage points, with 44% of trajectories exceeding meaningful divergence thresholds. Diagnostic decomposition reveals that the system correctly classifies memory operations (create, update, reinforce, supersede) only 20% of the time, a failure entirely invisible to extrinsic evaluation, and that failure modes differ systematically by category and difficulty. We release the benchmark, document generation pipeline, and scoring framework to support reproducible evaluation of agent memory systems.
中文翻译
智能体记忆基准测试通过下游问答准确率来衡量智能体响应是否反映正确知识，以此作为记忆质量的代理指标。我们指出这种评估范式存在缺陷：它混淆了记忆正确性与检索和生成质量，导致既出现虚假阳性（正确回答掩盖了损坏的记忆），也出现虚假阴性（错误回答将责任归咎于正确的记忆却因检索失败）。为此，我们提出了FEB-30基准，包含30条事实演化轨迹，覆盖6个诊断类别和3个难度等级，通过范式反转构建：从已知的事实演化路径生成文档，从而获得无需人工标注的确定性真实答案。每条轨迹定义了事实在多作者产物（文档、邮件、对话）中应如何演变，使我们能够在每次数据摄入后直接检查记忆存储状态。将FEB-30应用于代表性记忆系统，我们发现内在评估与外在评估平均相差16个百分点，44%的轨迹超出有意义的差异阈值。诊断分解显示，该系统仅20%的时间正确识别记忆操作（创建、更新、强化、取代），这一失败在外在评估中完全不可见，且失败模式随类别和难度系统性变化。我们发布了该基准、文档生成流水线和评分框架，以支持智能体记忆系统的可复现评估。

Hy-Memory-30B-A3B: Attributed Reinforcement Learning for Long-Term Memory Writing
Hy-Memory-30B-A3B：面向长期记忆书写的属性强化学习方法
External-memory systems extend language-model applications beyond a context window, but their quality depends critically on the model executing the write path: deciding what to retain, how precisely to represent it, and how new evidence should update persistent state. Existing frameworks typically place a general-purpose large language model behind framework-specific prompts. This raises serving cost, and a writer specialized for one protocol does not transfer directly to another; a unified model that can operate across established memory frameworks remains largely unexplored. We present Hy-Memory-30B-A3B, a compact long-term-memory writer trained across Mem0, MemOS, and Hy-Memory. Memory On-Policy Distillation teaches framework-specific write protocols on student-generated states; Memory RL then optimizes downstream answerability using dense per-chunk probes, attributed cross-chunk rewards, over-storage controls, and chunk-level GRPO. Carrying policy-written banks across windows keeps training aligned with deployment. On fixed 20% benchmark subsamples, Hy-Memory-30B-A3B ranks first on all four evaluated harnesses, including held-out EverMemOS, with a 55.49% macro average versus 54.62% for Claude Opus 5.
中文翻译
外部记忆系统将语言模型的应用扩展到上下文窗口之外，但其质量高度依赖于模型执行写入路径的能力：决定保留什么内容、如何精确表示，以及新证据如何更新持久状态。现有框架通常在特定提示下部署通用大语言模型，这带来服务成本问题，且专用于某一协议的写入器无法直接迁移到其他协议；能够跨主流记忆框架运行的统一模型仍基本未被探索。我们提出Hy-Memory-30B-A3B，一个在Mem0、MemOS和Hy-Memory上联合训练的紧凑型长期记忆写入模型。记忆就地蒸馏（Memory On-Policy Distillation）在学生生成的状态上教授框架特定的写入协议；随后，记忆强化学习（Memory RL）通过密集的逐块探测、属性化的跨块奖励、过存储控制以及块级GRPO优化下游可答性。将策略写入的内存库跨窗口携带，确保训练与部署保持一致。在固定20%的基准子样本上，Hy-Memory-30B-A3B在所有四个评估工具中排名第一，包括未见过的EverMemOS，宏平均得分为55.49%，优于Claude Opus 5的54.62%。

Lexical Reference Frames: Interpreting LM Residual Streams in Native Semantics
词汇参考系：以原生语义解读语言模型残差流
The vocabulary of a language model is its native semantics: the embedding and the unembedding fix the meaning of a state at the two ends of the residual stream. Between them, existing methods either reuse a fixed lens or treat the states as a point cloud, and neither reads a state the way the model does. We argue that every hidden state has a native reference frame, induced by the layers that read it next and adaptive to the context. Lexical reference frames approximate it by reading a state through the model's own remaining layers with a stated number of rounds of attention between positions and a typical attention output for the rest, without fitting to the model's output. No lens, with fixed directions per layer, can exceed the square root of the native frame's coherence, 0.30 and 0.35 at mid-depth of SmolLM3-3B and OLMo3-7B, whereas the first-order frame, LexFrame[1], reaches 0.97 and 0.97 and reads a state within 0.16 nats of the model's output. Its coefficients attribute predictions more faithfully than attribution patching, its lexical basis steers a prediction while sparing other tokens, and its confident readings would let an early exit skip 0.45 and 0.42 of the layers, where lenses skip almost none. In this way, lexical reference frames make every hidden state of the model readable, attributable and steerable.
中文翻译
语言模型的词汇是其原生语义：嵌入和解嵌入分别在残差流的两端固定状态的含义。现有方法要么重复使用固定的视角，要么将状态视为点云，均无法像模型本身那样解读状态。我们认为每个隐藏状态都有一个由后续层读取方式所诱导的原生参考系，且能自适应上下文。词汇参考系通过模型自身剩余层读取状态来近似该参考系，中间经过指定轮次的注意力操作，其余部分采用典型注意力输出，且不拟合模型输出。没有固定方向的镜头无法超过原生框架相干性的平方根（SmolLM3-3B和OLMo3-7B分别为0.30和0.35），而一阶参考系LexFrame[1]达到0.97和0.97，并在0.16纳特内接近模型输出。其系数比归因修补更能忠实反映预测来源，其词汇基底可在不干扰其他词元的情况下引导预测，其高置信度读取可使早期退出跳过0.45和0.42的层数（而镜头几乎无法跳过任何层）。由此，词汇参考系使得模型的每个隐藏状态均可读、可归因、可操控。
👤 Jian Gu, Hongyu Zhang, Chunyang Chen, Aldeida Aleti 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

Intern-Decision: Efficient Multimodal Decision Making with Autoregressive Masked Language Modeling
Intern-Decision：基于自回归掩码语言建模的高效多模态决策方法
Interactive agents repeatedly choose among a finite set of actions, labels, or ordinal outcomes. Generating a free-form response for each choice adds latency and complicates the interface between a model and its environment. We present Intern-Decision, a family of 0.8B, 2B, and 4B multimodal decision models that return structured choices and candidate probabilities from text and optional images. Our autoregressive masked language modeling objective places one decision placeholder per field in a complete assistant JSON skeleton. Ground-truth symbols appear only in supervision, while all fields are evaluated in a single causal forward pass using the backbone's native language-model head. The 4B model achieves a 90.02% unweighted average accuracy across seven evaluation suites, compared with 88.74% for Jev v1.13.0. For a fixed 289-token request with three fields on one RTX 4090, mean latency is 44.16 ms for the 4B model and approximately 33--34 ms for the smaller models. On a separate 96-case known-distribution pilot, temperature scaling reduces expected multiclass Brier from 0.628 to 0.550 and expected calibration error from 0.213 to 0.089. A confidence-based handoff study on 111 public Jevbench-Hard items improves accuracy from 82/111 (73.87%) to 97/111 (87.39%) by routing 42 items to Atria-Dawn-Preview. We also describe qualitative game, visual interface, and browser demonstrations. These results support compact decision models as a practical component of larger agent systems.
