目的：Trofinetide于2023年获批作为首个Rett综合征治疗药物，但由于病历审查工作量大，缺乏可扩展的真实世界监测手段。我们开发了一种双大语言模型（LLM）共识管道，从临床笔记中提取18项治疗反应表型，包括4种不良反应、13项Rett综合征临床特征及药物拒绝行为。材料与方法：我们分析了德克萨斯儿童医院108名确诊持续接受Trofinetide治疗患者的26,763份笔记。该管道通过关键词过滤笔记，由两个LLM进行分类并要求达成共识，再聚合为患者-时间点矩阵。架构与提示在腹泻发展数据集上选定后冻结，并应用于其余17项表型，仅替换表型定义，作为样本外测试。报告的提示对比得分达到183个专家标注单元。结果：要求腹泻处于活跃且严重状态的提示表现最佳（准确率84.2 ± 0.0%；F1 = 0.674），比正则表达式匹配高出47个百分点（p < 0.0001）。专家评审员对每项表型的六次管道判断进行了评估，其中三正三负，总体一致性达90.2%（92/102）。13项Rett综合征临床特征是疾病本身的体现，而非Trofinetide的不良反应。粗大运动与精细运动障碍、语言/交流能力、刻板行为在每次随访中均有92%-100%的患者被记录为存在。讨论：基于上下文的LLM提取捕捉了多领域治疗反应。结果为每次随访的“存在”状态记录，而非方向性变化判断。结论：将基于上下文的LLM推理与共识架构结合，为真实世界证据提供了一种可扩展的替代方案，仅通过配置即可适应新表型和新药物，无需重新训练。

Effectiveness of Hidden-State Probes for Localizing Reasoning Failures
隐藏状态探针在定位推理失败中的有效性
Verifying the final answer of a long reasoning trace may show that the trace is wrong but it does not distinguish three cases. The problem may lie beyond the model's capability, another attempt would succeed, or one step in an otherwise recoverable trace made the trace fail. Standard hidden-state probes are trained on final-answer correctness, a label that conflates these cases. Monte Carlo rollouts distinguish the cases and locate the failing step at additional inference cost. We test the hidden states of 4B and 8B reasoning models for the failure type and the failing step and compare each probe with the strongest baseline that uses no hidden states. On incorrect 8B traces, the confidence probe, a calibrated correctness probe at a midpoint cutoff, misses 81% of cases in which a single step makes the trace unrecoverable and 47% of cases in which another attempt would succeed. A probe with a different training design over the same states reverses the ordering of these two kinds of failure, so either ordering can be read from those states. At both scales, no single tested probe locates the failing step more accurately than a rule based only on trace length, and at 4B a probe trained to predict that step fails to outperform this rule in any of 10 runs on identical traces. The confidence probe therefore indicates that an answer is wrong, but its failure-type ordering depends on its training design.
中文翻译
验证长推理链的最终答案可能发现其错误，但无法区分三种情形：问题超出模型能力、另一次尝试可能成功，或在本可恢复的链路中某一步骤导致失败。标准隐藏状态探针基于最终答案正确性进行训练，该标签混淆了上述三种情况。蒙特卡洛回溯虽可区分这些情况并定位失败步骤，但需额外推理成本。我们测试了4B和8B规模推理模型的隐藏状态，以识别失败类型与失败步骤，并将每种探针与不使用隐藏状态的最强基线进行比较。在8B模型的错误推理链上，置信度探针（在中间截断点校准的正确性探针）在81%的单步致败案例中漏检，且在47%的其他尝试可能成功的案例中漏检。另一种训练设计相同的隐藏状态探针改变了这两种失败类型的排序，说明该排序可从隐藏状态中读取。在两种规模下，没有任何单一探针在定位失败步骤方面优于仅基于推理链长度的规则；在4B规模下，一个旨在预测该步骤的探针在相同推理链上的10次运行中均未能超越该规则。因此，置信度探针仅表明答案错误，其失败类型排序取决于训练设计。

agent-benchmarkagent-memoryagentic-rl
Selection Is Not Learning: Choosing Model Futures
选择并非学习：模型未来的抉择
Self-evolving language models train on tasks they generate, so every round must decide which updated model the next round inherits. Current loops such as R-Zero, Agent0, and Skill Self-Play make this decision through the task, admitting tasks that look learnable and inheriting every resulting update. We identify a selection-to-update gap in these loops, where a task that looks learnable need not produce a useful update. Most updates on verified tasks fail to make correct answers more likely, yet these loops inherit them all. HeirCommit freezes the updated model and uses fresh evaluation questions to decide whether to inherit it or retain the current model. EvoHeir separates parameter inheritance from experience inheritance, using current and archived experience to repair failures and preserve successes before final confirmation. Our analysis characterizes selection-induced optimism and decomposes regret into selection, absorption, and confirmation errors. HeirCommit cuts the harmful share of accepted updates by 68-83% relative to random inheritance at the same acceptance rate. Across four backbones, EvoHeir achieves the best mathematics and general-reasoning averages, raising the math average by 5.3% relative to the strongest baseline, Agent0. The framework lets useful experience persist even when the model that produced it is not inherited.
中文翻译
自演化语言模型在自身生成的任务上进行训练，因此每一轮都必须决定下一阶段继承哪个更新后的模型。当前的循环机制如R-Zero、Agent0和技能自对弈通过任务来做出这一决策，接受那些看起来可学习的任务，并继承所有由此产生的更新。我们识别出这些循环中的“选择-更新差距”：一个看似可学习的任务未必会产生有用的更新。在已验证任务上，大多数更新未能提高正确答案的概率，但这些循环仍继承了全部更新。HeirCommit冻结更新后的模型，并使用新评估问题判断是否继承或保留当前模型。EvoHeir将参数继承与经验继承分离，利用当前和归档经验修复失败并保留成功，再进行最终确认。我们的分析刻画了选择引发的乐观偏差，并将遗憾分解为选择、吸收和确认误差。HeirCommit在相同接受率下，相较于随机继承，将被接受更新中有害部分减少了68%-83%。在四种骨干模型上，EvoHeir在数学和通用推理平均表现上达到最优，使数学平均分相对于最强基线Agent0提升了5.3%。该框架使得即使产生经验的模型未被继承，有用的经验也能持续保留。
👤 Haoyi Yang, Kshitij Mishra, Nils Lukas, Salem Lahlou 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

P2TPO: Structure-Aligned Page-to-Token Credit Assignment for Full-Page Document Parsing
P2TPO：面向全页文档解析的结构对齐页到令牌信用分配
End-to-end full-page document parsing must preserve the content and structure of heterogeneous elements, yet otherwise accurate outputs can contain local errors and omissions. A single page-level advantage does not explicitly identify which elements differ in quality across sampled outputs or where their errors occur. We introduce Page-to-Token Policy Optimization (P2TPO), a structure-aligned credit-assignment framework that operates on full-page policy trajectories. Reference-element alignment first associates block-level quality contrasts with generated spans. Unified Anchor Pricing (UAP) incorporates matched and missing element outcomes and provides feedback relative to a fixed quality ceiling when sampled outputs receive identical but imperfect scores for a reference element. Within matched blocks, Span Credit Redistribution (SCR) redistributes existing block credit according to aligned correct and erroneous spans, using boundary proxies for within-block omissions while preserving the total block-derived advantage across its tokens. Under a fixed-weight additive page utility, we show that element-indexed contrasts retain local quality differences that page-level aggregation can cancel out. In the main experimental comparison, P2TPO outperforms all evaluated policy optimization baselines in seed-averaged scores on each of the six document parsing benchmarks. Additional experiments demonstrate gains across model scales and initializations.
中文翻译
端到端的全页文档解析必须保持异构元素的内容与结构，但即使整体输出准确，也可能存在局部错误和遗漏。单个页面级别的优势无法显式识别不同采样输出中哪些元素质量差异或错误发生的位置。我们提出Page-to-Token Policy Optimization（P2TPO），一种在全页策略轨迹上运行的结构对齐信用分配框架。参考元素对齐首先将块级质量差异与生成片段关联。统一锚点定价（UAP）结合匹配和缺失元素结果，在采样输出对参考元素获得相同但不完美评分时，提供相对于固定质量上限的反馈。在匹配块内，跨度信用重分配（SCR）根据对齐的正确与错误片段重新分配现有块信用，使用边界代理处理块内遗漏，同时保持块内总导出优势在所有令牌间不变。在固定权重加法页面效用下，我们证明元素索引对比保留了局部质量差异，而页面级别聚合可能将其抵消。在主要实验比较中，P2TPO在六个文档解析基准上的种子平均得分均优于所有评估的策略优化基线。额外实验表明其在不同模型规模和初始化下均有提升。

N-Gram Memories at Two Bits: Quantizing Lookup Tables in Large Language Models
N元组记忆仅需两比特：大型语言模型中查找表的量化
Conditional memory expands language-model capacity through selective lookup, but the full embedding table must remain available for every possible input. We introduce Conditional Program (CP), a compressed representation that reconstructs only the rows an input requests. CP represents each row by a short code and a local scale, with a reconstruction program shared across the table. We instantiate this representation with a Gaussian trellis, norm restoration and independently decodable row records. CP's two-bit code lowers output KL by 41--63\% relative to 2-bit round-to-nearest. Analysis across three trained n-gram memory families shows that Gaussian-like coordinate statistics coexist with learned relationships among rows; at matched storage budgets, the shared trellis outperforms the fitted codebooks and low-rank residual variants we test. For DeepSeek, it reduces table storage from 203GB to 51GB, leaving room for larger workloads when the table is kept on GPU.

FIRM: Fisher-Informed Retention Merging for Federated LLM Unlearning
FIRM：用于联邦大模型遗忘的Fisher感知保留合并
Federated LLM unlearning addresses targeted knowledge removal when forget and retain data remain distributed across private clients and are inaccessible to the server. The resulting locally trained adapters encode conflicting objectives, making their aggregation fundamentally different from conventional federated optimization. A key failure mode is magnitude explosion, whereby unnormalized cross-client aggregation increases the merged update magnitude and degrades retained utility under stronger unlearning. We propose FIRM (Fisher-Informed Retention Merging), a single-round framework for stable and retention-aware server-side aggregation. FIRM uses client-local Fisher statistics as a unified sensitivity signal to construct a Fisher-normalized base merge and impose explicit control over retain-sensitive parameters. Its second-order retention projection admits a unique minimum-distance solution, while the corresponding dual scales with the number of retain clients rather than the model dimension. Experiments across WMDP, TOFU, and MUSE-HP using Zephyr-7B and Llama-3-8B show utility gains at comparable forgetting on WMDP and MUSE-HP, with metric-dependent trade-offs on TOFU. Controlled comparisons support benefits of the combined base merge and additional retention correction at selected operating points.

联邦大模型遗忘处理在遗忘数据和保留数据分散于私有客户端且服务器无法访问的情况下，实现目标知识移除的问题。由此产生的本地训练适配器包含冲突目标，使其聚合与传统的联邦优化根本不同。一个关键失败模式是幅度爆炸：未归一化的跨客户端聚合会增加合并更新的幅度，在更强遗忘条件下导致保留性能下降。我们提出FIRM（Fisher感知保留合并），一种单轮稳定且保留感知的服务器端聚合框架。FIRM利用客户端本地的Fisher统计信息作为统一敏感性信号，构建Fisher归一化基础合并，并对保留敏感参数施加显式控制。其二阶保留投影具有唯一最小距离解，而对应的对偶随保留客户端数量增长，而非模型维度。在WMDP、TOFU和MUSE-HP三个数据集上，使用Zephyr-7B和Llama-3-8B的实验显示，在WMDP和MUSE-HP上遗忘程度相当时，性能有所提升，而在TOFU上存在指标依赖性权衡。受控对比支持在特定操作点上结合基础合并与额外保留修正的优势。

Human Interventions as Labels: Localizing, Steering, and (Sometimes) Editing the Computations Behind LLM Agent Failures
人类干预作为标签：定位、引导并（有时）编辑LLM智能体失败背后的计算过程
When an LLM agent misbehaves, a human steps in, and that intervention is usually discarded or replayed as training data. We treat it instead as a measurement: a label pointing at the internal computation that failed. We study it in a controlled agent environment, on one failure (asking a clarifying question when the request was already unambiguous), with labels from a rule-based overseer validated against blind human judgment. Claims cover eligible models, which both over-ask and sometimes act on matched unambiguous requests: five of thirteen qualified (Qwen2.5-3B, gemma-2-2b, OLMo-3-7B, Mistral-Nemo-12B, Phi-4). Four findings. First, the ask-vs-act decision is causally localizable in every eligible family: a direction learned from intervention labels flips 72-100% of held-out over-asks at a definite residual-stream depth, with site and dose chosen on training data, and random directions under the same procedure flip at most one episode. Second, over-asking has two mechanistic signatures: in four families the model demonstrably knows the request is unambiguous (the belief is decodable, causally inert and orthogonal to the decision) and asks anyway; in the fifth, the rare over-asks track different world-representations. Third, steering gated on a probe readout removes most of the simulated oversight burden while legitimate questions survive (bootstrap 95% intervals exclude zero); a prompt instruction makes it worse. Fourth, consolidating the fix into the weights yields a map, not a rule: on one model a LoRA confined to the causal band passes every criterion fixed in advance while behavior cloning on identical data breaks retention; on three families the reverse holds; on the fifth the outcome crosses the boundary across seeds. A geometric rule written down in advance to predict the regime failed on two out-of-family tests; we report the failure and stop rather than fit a replacement.
中文翻译
当LLM智能体出现异常行为时，人类会介入，而这种干预通常被丢弃或作为训练数据重放。我们将其视为一种测量：指向内部失败计算的标签。我们在受控智能体环境中研究这一现象，聚焦于一种失败情况（请求已明确时仍要求澄清问题），并使用基于规则的监督者标签与盲人人类判断进行验证。涉及的合格模型包括五种：Qwen2.5-3B、gemma-2-2b、OLMo-3-7B、Mistral-Nemo-12B、Phi-4。四项发现：第一，询问与执行决策在所有合格模型家族中均可因果定位：从干预标签中学习的方向能在确定的残差流深度上翻转72%-100%的保留过问行为，且位置与剂量在训练数据上选择，相同程序下随机方向最多翻转一次事件。第二，过问行为具有两种机制特征：在四个家族中，模型明显知道请求已明确（信念可解码、因果无关且正交于决策）但仍提问；在第五个家族中，罕见的过问行为追踪不同的世界表征。第三，基于探测读出的引导能消除大部分模拟监督负担，而合法问题仍能保留（自举95%置信区间不包含零）；提示指令反而使情况恶化。第四，将修复固化到权重中生成的是映射而非规则：在一个模型中，局限于因果带的LoRA通过了所有预先设定的标准，而行为克隆在相同数据上却破坏了保留；在三个家族中情况相反；在第五个家族中，结果随种子跨越边界。事先写下的几何规则用于预测范式，在两个跨家族测试中失败，我们报告失败并停止，而非拟合替代方案。

The Self-Improvement Problem
自我改进问题
With the rise of large language models, agentic systems are becoming increasingly autonomous, as they write code, call tools, revise prompts, and propose modifications to their own components. This autonomy raises a fundamental question for safe self-improvement: can an automated gate like a reward model or an automated reviewer reliably decide whether a proposed update should be accepted? The difficulty is structural. The gate must be a terminating, computable procedure, while the property we ultimately care about, namely whether an update is a genuine semantic improvement, depends on potentially unbounded program behavior. We formalize this mismatch by modeling agents as programs and defining improvement in terms of a semantic score that need not be computable. We study two evaluation abstractions. The first is a certifier that approves or rejects an update. The second is a search procedure that, given access to a proposal generator, attempts to output some improving candidate if one exists. We show that, in the fully general setting, no terminating procedure can simultaneously guarantee reliability (never accepting a non-improvement) and non-conservatism (never missing an available improvement): self-referential constructions can force any such gate or searcher to fail. Randomized gates with bounded error face the same limit. We then identify a standard decidable regime used in practice (bounded evaluation and a finite proposal budget) under which exhaustive simulation yields a terminating search procedure with the desired guarantees. We also show the limit of this recovery: a chain of accepted updates can raise the bounded score in every round while the true score falls. Our results draw a boundary between unbounded, universal settings where correctness guarantees collapse and bounded settings where they can be recovered by design.
中文翻译
随着大语言模型的发展，智能体系统正变得越来越自主，能够编写代码、调用工具、修改提示词并对其自身组件提出改进建议。这种自主性引发了关于安全自我改进的根本问题：像奖励模型或自动化评审员这样的自动门能否可靠地判断一个提议的更新是否应被接受？困难在于结构层面。该门必须是一个终止且可计算的过程，而我们真正关心的属性——即更新是否为真正的语义改进——取决于可能无界的程序行为。我们通过将智能体建模为程序，并以无需可计算的语义得分来定义改进，形式化了这一不匹配。我们研究了两种评估抽象：第一种是批准或拒绝更新的验证器；第二种是给定提案生成器后，尝试输出某个改进候选者的搜索过程。我们证明，在完全一般的情形下，任何终止过程都无法同时保证可靠性（从不接受非改进）和非保守性（从不遗漏可用改进）：自指构造可迫使任何此类门或搜索器失败。具有有界误差的随机门也面临相同限制。随后，我们识别出实践中常用的标准可判定情形（有限评估和有限提案预算），在此情形下，穷尽模拟可产生具有所需保证的终止搜索过程。我们还展示了这种恢复的局限性：一系列被接受的更新可在每轮中提升有界得分，而真实得分却在下降。我们的结果划清了无界、通用设置中正确性保证崩溃与有界设置中可通过设计恢复保证之间的界限。

Spurious Attention Heads in Vision Transformers: Mechanistic Identification of Annotation-Driven Bias in Medical Chest X-Ray Analysis
视觉Transformer中的虚假注意力头：医学胸部X光分析中由标注驱动偏差的机制识别
Vision Transformers deployed in medical imaging exploit spurious correlations, yet the internal mechanisms remain unidentified and impossible to fix. We apply mechanistic interpretability to locate one such mechanism in ViT-Base 16: a single attention head (L8H10) that functions as a dedicated annotation-reader on chest X-rays, attending to corner text rather than lung pathology in 62% of 200 COVID-19 images. Causal validation via inpainting confirms annotation content rather than luminance or edge density drives the behaviour, increasing lung attention 1.52 times upon removal in affected images ($p{<}0.0001$). The finding replicates across NIH ChestX-ray14 (79%), DeiT-Base (88%), and ViT-Large 16 (80%), establishing spurious annotation-attending heads as a consistent property of ViT-family architectures across two datasets and three architectures. Full fine-tuning does not suppress the behaviour and may entrench it: 98.4% of spurious images remain spurious and the spurious rate rises marginally, as the shortcut is reinforced rather than removed. Critically, single-head ablation produces $p{=}0.340$ and multi-head ablation of all three identified carriers produces $p{=}0.532$, meaning a causally verified annotation-reading circuit is completely invisible to output-level evaluation. Mechanistic auditing finds what behavioural testing cannot.

DocPilot: Interactive Agent Orchestration and Training for Long Visual Document Understanding
DocPilot：面向长视觉文档理解的交互式智能体编排与训练
Multimodal long-document question answering requires models to seek sparse evidence across pages and modalities, understand heterogeneous document content, and reason over the collected evidence. Even advanced vision-language models (VLMs) can overlook sparse evidence and struggle with cross-modal reasoning in a single pass. Existing document agents further introduce multi-step interaction, but some still rely on predefined workflows or coarse-grained actions, limiting the flexibility and granularity of document reasoning. We introduce DocPilot, an agentic document framework that supports expressive orchestration and makes complex document solving learnable by small language models (SLMs). DocPilot orchestrates evidence seeking through page-level navigation and fine-grained evidence localization, and integrates local visual inspection, code-based numerical reasoning, and cross-modal reasoning for multimodal understanding. To transfer these capabilities to small models, we propose curriculum-based agentic training that progresses from single-ability learning to adaptive orchestration through supervision from data-grounded reasoning traces and executable interaction trajectories. Across MMLongBench-Doc, DocBench, FinRAGBench-V, and MADQA, DocPilot achieves strong gains with frontier VLM backbones, while DocPilot-4B delivers competitive performance against substantially larger models, demonstrating strong effectiveness and generalization across diverse long-document reasoning settings. The code is available at https://github.com/HKUSTDial/DocPilot.

PocketTSLM: Deploying an On-Device Soft-Prompt Time-Series Language Model for Privacy-Preserving Smartphone Reasoning
PocketTSLM：用于隐私保护智能手机推理的设备端软提示时间序列语言模型部署
Consumer phones and wearables increasingly collect physiological time-series data, yet most mobile systems treat these streams as summaries or text rather than as temporal evidence. This representation is poorly matched to personal health systems that need to retrieve, route, or summarize sensor evidence while preserving temporal structure. Cloud-hosted time-series language models avoid smartphone limits, but they repeatedly move sensitive physiology off the device and add latency and per-query compute cost. Mobile model alternatives that aggregate time-series data into statistical features or serialize raw samples as text either compress temporal structure or exhaust the context and memory budgets of small on-device models. We present PocketTSLM, a mobile-native deployment of OpenTSLM SoftPrompt, the soft-prompt variant of the OpenTSLM time-series language model. PocketTSLM runs fully on a consumer smartphone by injecting encoded time-series windows as embeddings instead of tokenizing time series as text. A numerical parity protocol verifies identical greedy-decoded token sequences between the Swift port and a Python reference using Apple's MLX machine-learning framework. Answers also match the PyTorch reference, although rationale wording can differ. An on-device context-economics benchmark compares the soft-prompt path with a text-serialized baseline on the same backbone. In our benchmark format, soft-prompting uses approximately 12 times fewer time-series tokens and reduces prompt-prefill cost. PocketTSLM processes 30-second Sleep-EDF electroencephalography clips for sleep staging and a 1,000-sample (10 s) 12-lead electrocardiogram clip from ECG-QA. In comparison, text-serialized baselines exceed the phone's context or memory budget at longer time-series windows. All inference runs entirely on the device, with no health data transmitted during inference.

消费级手机和可穿戴设备日益收集生理时间序列数据，但大多数移动系统将这些流视为摘要或文本，而非时间证据。这种表示方式与需要检索、路由或总结传感器证据并保持时间结构的个人健康系统不匹配。云端托管的时间序列语言模型虽能规避手机限制，但反复将敏感生理数据移出设备，增加延迟和每查询计算成本。移动端替代方案将时间序列数据聚合为统计特征，或将原始样本序列化为文本，要么压缩时间结构，要么耗尽小型设备模型的上下文和内存预算。我们提出PocketTSLM，是OpenTSLM SoftPrompt（OpenTSLM时间序列语言模型的软提示变体）的原生移动端部署。PocketTSLM通过将编码的时间序列窗口作为嵌入注入，而非将时间序列分词为文本，在消费级智能手机上完全运行。数值一致性协议验证了Swift版本与Python参考版本在Apple MLX框架下的贪婪解码标记序列一致；答案也与PyTorch参考一致，尽管理由表述可能不同。设备端上下文经济性基准将软提示路径与文本序列基线在同一主干网络上进行比较。在我们的基准格式中，软提示使用约12倍更少的时间序列标记，显著降低提示预填充成本。PocketTSLM可处理30秒的Sleep-EDF脑电图片段用于睡眠分期，以及来自ECG-QA的1000样本（10秒）12导联心电图片段。相比之下，文本序列基线在较长时间序列窗口时超出手机的上下文或内存预算。所有推理均在设备上完成，推理过程中无健康数据传输。

Bias Control and Injection for Multi-Agent Aggregation
多智能体聚合中的偏差控制与注入
Multi-agent reasoning with large language models (LLMs) raises a central question for collective decision-making: when agents disagree, whose decision should be trusted? Resolving such disagreements is difficult because agents' decisions are susceptible to non-semantic biases such as majority pressure and response-style preferences, which can obscure the quality of the underlying reasoning. Our analysis shows how bias can be both mitigated and exploited: bias control improves initial answer selection, while bias injection elicits decision instability that serves as a reliability signal. Building on these findings, we introduce a test-time aggregation framework that requires neither validation data nor prior knowledge of relative model strength. The framework implements bias control by presenting one style-normalized reasoning trace per distinct answer, and bias injection by placing each aggregating agent's initially selected answer in the minority. Overall, our framework achieves higher average accuracy and better token efficiency than competing non-oracle aggregation methods across three mathematical reasoning benchmarks and four heterogeneous LLM combinations. It also improves final-answer accuracy when applied to multi-agent debate and Mixture-of-Agents, without modifying their interaction or synthesis processes.
中文翻译
使用大语言模型（LLMs）进行多智能体推理提出了集体决策的核心问题：当智能体意见不一致时，应信任谁的决策？解决此类分歧困难，因为智能体的决策易受非语义偏差影响，如多数压力和响应风格偏好，这些偏差会掩盖底层推理的质量。我们的分析表明，偏差既可被缓解也可被利用：偏差控制有助于初始答案选择，而偏差注入则引发决策不稳定性，作为可靠性信号。基于这些发现，我们提出一种无需验证数据或先验模型强度知识的测试时聚合框架。该框架通过为每个不同答案呈现一个风格归一化的推理轨迹实现偏差控制，并通过将每个聚合智能体最初选择的答案置于少数地位来实现偏差注入。总体而言，该框架在三个数学推理基准和四种异构LLM组合上，平均准确率更高且token效率更优，优于其他非oracle聚合方法。它在应用于多智能体辩论和混合代理（Mixture-of-Agents）时，也提升了最终答案准确率，且无需修改其交互或合成过程。

TimeCap: A Scalable Open-Source Pipeline for Captioning Wearable Health Time-Series Data
TimeCap：可扩展的开源管道用于可穿戴健康时间序列数据的描述生成
Time-series data generated continuously across industrial, environmental, and especially health domains produce multimodal signals whose scale and heterogeneity make their processing and interpretation particularly challenging. Time-Series Language Models (TSLMs) promise scalable interpretation of these signals, yet remain bottlenecked by a fundamental lack of text-annotated time series pairs that are signal-grounded, reproducible, and extensible across modalities and schemas. Existing paired corpora are either closed or limited in scale and modality coverage. Annotation with large language models could scale them up, but its outputs cannot be regenerated exactly, which prevents others from re-deriving, extending, or auditing the resulting corpus. To address this, we release TimeCap, an open deterministic captioning pipeline with three reusable extractor tiers and a cross-channel synthesis layer that operates directly on the signal with no language model in the loop, extensible to new schemas by writing channel definitions alone. This positions TimeCap as a reproducible mechanism for future dataset generation rather than a one-off release. Applying TimeCap to OpenMHC, the MyHeart Counts wearable dataset, yields MHCap, the largest open caption layer for multimodal wearable time-series data. MHCap spans 19 Apple HealthKit channels and 7,005,763 captions over 908,944 person-days. To showcase the impact of the corpus, we train OpenTSLM, a time-series language model, on MHCap and benchmark on health-outcome prediction. Caption-augmented training (OpenTSLM-Cap) outperforms a label-only baseline on 29 of 32 per-user binary evaluations across 8 tasks.
中文翻译
在工业、环境及尤其健康领域持续生成的时间序列数据产生多模态信号，其规模和异质性使得处理与解释尤为困难。时间序列语言模型（TSLMs）有望实现这些信号的可扩展解释，但仍受限于缺乏信号基础、可重现且跨模态与模式可扩展的文本标注时间序列对。现有配对语料库或封闭，或规模与模态覆盖有限。使用大语言模型进行标注虽可扩大规模，但其输出无法精确再生，阻碍他人重新推导、扩展或审计所得语料库。为此，我们发布TimeCap，一个开放且确定性的描述生成管道，包含三个可复用的提取层级和一个跨通道合成层，直接作用于信号，无需语言模型参与，仅需编写通道定义即可扩展至新模式。这使TimeCap成为未来数据集生成的可重现机制，而非一次性发布。将TimeCap应用于OpenMHC（MyHeart Counts可穿戴数据集），得到MHCap，即目前最大的多模态可穿戴时间序列数据公开描述层。MHCap涵盖19个Apple HealthKit通道，共7,005,763条描述，覆盖908,944人天。为展示语料库的影响，我们在MHCap上训练OpenTSLM时间序列语言模型，并在健康结果预测任务上进行基准测试。经描述增强的训练（OpenTSLM-Cap）在32项用户级二分类评估中的29项上优于仅标签基线。

Who May Release an Agent's Promise? Standing over Commitments in Multi-Party Chat
谁可以解除代理的承诺？多角色对话中对承诺的立场分析
When an assistant serving a team makes a promise to Alice, who may cancel it? We call this standing: only the creditor (the person owed) or someone the creditor or operator policy authorizes may release a commitment, regardless of rank. In a controlled chat environment we vary who cancels and separate two errors: unauthorized release (obeying someone without standing) and override (executing a released promise). Across six models, smaller open ones override the creditor (Qwen3.5-9B: 69%); larger ones drop promises on a peer's direct word (Qwen3.8-27B: 89%; gpt-6.1-sol: 83%); nearly all act on a bystander's "go ahead after all". Oracles dissociate the errors: stating the rule or the creditor curbs unauthorized release but not override, which the latter even raises in three of four models; among them, only knowing whether the promise is still owed cuts override. Enabling thinking moves smaller models the way scale does. A self-kept commitment ledger stops override in four of five models; with a one-paragraph release policy it matches or beats the best oracle store, raising gpt-6-luna's strict accuracy from 49% (policy alone) to 83%. Code, prompts, outputs and labels are released.
中文翻译
当一个服务于团队的助手向Alice作出承诺后，谁有权取消该承诺？我们称之为“立场”：只有债权人（被欠方）或债权人或操作员政策授权的人才能释放承诺，无论其职位高低。在受控聊天环境中，我们改变取消者的身份，并区分两类错误：未经授权的释放（服从无权者）和覆盖（执行已被释放的承诺）。在六种模型中，较小的开源模型会服从同伴（Qwen3.5-9B：69%）；较大的模型则在同行直接命令下放弃承诺（Qwen3.8-27B：89%；gpt-6.1-sol：83%）；几乎所有模型都会响应旁观者的“那好吧”。预言者能分离这两类错误：陈述规则或指出债权人可抑制未经授权的释放，但无法阻止覆盖，甚至在四种模型中的三种中反而加剧了覆盖；其中，只有知道承诺是否仍有效才能减少覆盖。启用思考能力使小型模型的表现趋近于规模带来的效果。一个由模型自我维护的承诺账本可阻止五种模型中的四种发生覆盖；配合一段简短的释放政策，其表现达到或超过最佳预言者存储，使gpt-6-luna的严格准确率从49%（仅政策）提升至83%。代码、提示、输出和标签均已公开。
👤 Lan Wei, Bingyu Shen, Jiaqi Tang, Qing Lu, Kuan Huang et al. 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

Language Models Share a Readout Bottleneck in Working Memory
语言模型在工作记忆中存在读出瓶颈
Large language models (LLMs) reproduce a human working-memory signature on the N-back task: performance declines as memory load N increases, and errors concentrate on lures, items that repeat the current stimulus at a neighboring but incorrect serial position. We ask whether this shared behavioral signature reflects a shared mechanism. The literature offers two candidate accounts. On a precision account, softmax attention cannot concentrate its weight on the item N positions back, so adjacent items are retrieved instead. On an interference account, attention selects the correct item, but the content of competing items corrupts the decision. We dissociate these accounts in 15 pretrained LLMs from three families (70 million to 14 billion parameters) and in minimal transformers trained from scratch. In all three families, the attention heads that carry the task attend to the target on most missed matches. Linear probes decode near-perfectly whether a matching letter occurred at the target or at a lure position, even on trials where the model false-alarms to the lure, although its own decisions barely distinguish the two. Sharpening attention yields negligible gains, whereas suppressing the letter identity of non-target items improves sensitivity (d') in nearly all model-by-load conditions. In larger models the task is spread over more heads, and removing interference helps more. In minimal transformers, precision limits a single attention layer, but a second layer removes this constraint. The working-memory limit of LLMs therefore lies in reading items out under interference rather than in locating them, consistent with interference accounts of human working memory.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）在N-back任务中重现了人类工作记忆的特征：随着记忆负荷N的增加，性能下降，且错误集中在诱饵项上——即在相邻但错误的序列位置重复当前刺激的项目。我们探讨这一共享行为特征是否反映共享机制。文献提出了两种候选解释：精度解释认为，softmax注意力无法将权重集中于N步前的项目，因此只能检索相邻项目；干扰解释则认为，注意力虽选中正确项目，但竞争项目的干扰内容破坏了决策。我们在15个预训练LLM（来自三个家族，参数量从7000万到140亿）以及从头训练的最小Transformer中区分了这两种解释。在所有三类模型中，承担任务的注意力头在多数误判匹配中仍关注目标项。线性探测几乎完美解码了匹配字母是否出现在目标位置或诱饵位置，即使模型自身决策难以区分两者。增强注意力带来的收益微乎其微，而抑制非目标项的字母身份则在几乎所有模型-负荷条件下提升了敏感度（d'）。在更大模型中，任务分布于更多注意力头，消除干扰的益处更明显。在最小Transformer中，精度限制仅存在于单层注意力，而第二层可解除该限制。因此，LLMs的工作记忆限制在于受干扰下的读出而非定位，这与人类工作记忆的干扰理论一致。

CARE-SD: Explainable Reasoning Architecture for Local SLM Self-Disclosure Analysis in Textual Data
CARE-SD：用于文本数据中局部小语言模型自披露分析的可解释推理架构
Cybersecurity interviews uncover critical security experiences, behaviors, and privacy concerns often not fully captured by conventional technical assessments. Consequently, identifying genuine self-disclosures is challenging because statements are context-dependent, categories overlap, and automated interpretations may introduce unsupported claims. Locally deployed Small Language Models (SLMs) protect sensitive participant data by keeping processing within controlled environments, but their limited reasoning capabilities make simple prompting insufficient for context-aware and factually reliable analysis. This study developed CARE-SD (Context-Adaptive Reasoning and Explanation for Self-Disclosure), a novel four-module architecture integrating context-adaptive processing, hierarchical disclosure reasoning, explanation-consistency verification, and cross-batch reconciliation. We evaluated three local SLMs, including Llama 1B, Qwen 1.5B, and Llama 3B, using 82 independently annotated cybersecurity interviews across five organizational sectors. Comparisons included conventional classifiers, three prompting strategies, larger 8B--32B models, and architectural ablations. Two independent reviewers assessed disclosure labels, factual claims, and explanation correctness. CARE-SD improved the accuracy and evidence reliability of SLMs, increasing Llama 3B Macro-F1 from 0.80 to 0.88 while reducing unsupported claims by 60\%. Remarkably, the 3B model matched the 20B reference model in category Macro-F1, demonstrating the effectiveness of the architecture without requiring a larger model.

网络安全访谈揭示了关键的安全经验、行为和隐私关切，这些往往无法被传统技术评估完全捕捉。因此，识别真实的自披露具有挑战性，因为陈述具有上下文依赖性，类别重叠，且自动化解读可能引入未经支持的主张。本地部署的小型语言模型（SLMs）通过将处理保留在受控环境中来保护敏感参与者数据，但其有限的推理能力使得简单提示不足以实现上下文感知且事实可靠的分析。本研究开发了CARE-SD（自披露的上下文适应推理与解释），一种新型四模块架构，整合了上下文适应处理、层级披露推理、解释一致性验证和跨批次协调。我们使用82个独立标注的网络安全访谈（涵盖五个组织领域）评估了三种本地SLMs（Llama 1B、Qwen 1.5B、Llama 3B）。比较包括传统分类器、三种提示策略、更大的8B–32B模型以及架构消融实验。两名独立评审员评估了披露标签、事实主张和解释正确性。CARE-SD提升了SLMs的准确性和证据可靠性，使Llama 3B的宏F1从0.80提升至0.88，同时将未经支持的主张减少60%。值得注意的是，3B模型在类别宏F1上达到了20B参考模型的水平，证明了该架构的有效性，无需依赖更大模型。

Planning Commitment of LLM under Partial Observability
部分可观测环境下大语言模型的规划承诺
LLM-based planning agents commit to plans of different lengths before interacting with a partially observable environment. Some aggressively commit to a long plan based on assumptions about unobserved states, while others conservatively plan only over what they have observed. Both behaviors emerge implicitly in pre-trained models, whether the LLM outputs a plan directly or generates a PDDL formulation for a classical solver. Aggressive commitment risks costly failures, while conservative commitment risks excessive LLM calls. This choice pits safety against efficiency, a trade-off that has been neither characterized systematically nor made controllable. We investigate aggressive and conservative LLM planners and formalizers on two partially observable domains, where priors are provided as either explicit statistics or common-sense knowledge. Across three LLMs, both strategies reach similar accuracy. Conservative methods achieve fewer failed attempts (safety) at the cost of more API calls (efficiency). Aggressive methods fail more often but replan efficiently only when priors are informative. Interestingly, analysis shows that LLMs treat the provided priors nearly as facts, which risks failure when priors are unreliable. We also explore hybrid strategies with tunable hyperparameters that adjust the balance between safety and efficiency.
中文翻译
基于大语言模型（LLM）的规划智能体在与部分可观测环境交互前，会承诺不同长度的计划。一些智能体基于对未观测状态的假设，激进地承诺长期计划；另一些则保守地仅基于已观测内容进行规划。这两种行为在预训练模型中隐式出现，无论LLM是直接输出计划，还是生成经典求解器可用的PDDL形式。激进承诺可能导致代价高昂的失败，而保守承诺则可能引发过多的LLM调用。这种选择在安全与效率之间形成权衡，但该权衡尚未被系统性刻画，也难以控制。我们研究了在两个部分可观测领域中，激进与保守的LLM规划者和形式化者的表现，其中先验信息以显式统计或常识知识形式提供。在三种LLM上，两种策略达到相似的准确性。保守方法以牺牲效率（更多API调用）换取更高的安全性（更少失败尝试）。激进方法失败率更高，但在先验信息有帮助时能高效重规划。有趣的是，分析表明LLM几乎将提供的先验视为事实，当先验不可靠时，这会带来失败风险。我们还探索了具有可调超参数的混合策略，以平衡安全与效率。

MemComposer: Learning to Assemble Memories for LLM Agents via Reinforcement Learning
MemComposer：通过强化学习学习为LLM智能体组装记忆
Agent memory enables large language model (LLM) agents to reuse past experiences without updating their parameters. However, semantic similarity alone does not guarantee memory usefulness: downstream performance depends on how memories are selected and presented to the agent. We propose MemComposer, a memory composer that jointly examines the current query and retrieved candidates to decide which experiences to include, how many to select, and how to order them. We train the composer using Experience Policy Optimization (ExPO), a GRPO-based framework that evaluates assembled memory contexts through complete agent interactions and uses task-success feedback to optimize the assembly policy. Across three $τ^2$-bench domains with two frozen executors, MemComposer outperforms the strongest baseline for each executor while reducing average memory-token usage by 16.9% and 4.9%, respectively. The learned policy also transfers to unseen domains, executors, and memory banks, and benefits from memories generated by stronger models without further training. These results demonstrate the value of learning query-specific memory assembly using downstream task feedback.

Learning When to Think: Utility-Guided Adaptive Reasoning for Efficient Text Retrieval
学会何时思考：基于效用引导的自适应推理以实现高效文本检索
Reasoning-conditioned embeddings can improve retrieval by progressively refining semantic constraints. However, additional reasoning may introduce over-generalized or semantically noisy abstractions that weaken retrieval specificity. Existing methods largely supervise reasoning through final retrieval outcomes, providing limited guidance over the refinement process itself. We propose AURA, a framework that directly supervises intermediate reasoning states according to how well they separate relevant documents from hard distractors. AURA further trains a lightweight utility estimator over prefix-conditioned embeddings to adapt reasoning depth at inference time. On the reasoning-intensive BRIGHT benchmark, AURA-4B achieves 34.7 average nDCG@10 while using 2.8 reasoning steps and 96 generated tokens per query. Under matched deployment conditions, adaptive stopping reduces latency from 4.6 to 0.76 seconds relative to full-trajectory reasoning. Results on MS MARCO further suggest that this specialization need not substantially degrade conventional in-domain retrieval.

推理条件嵌入可通过逐步细化语义约束来提升检索效果。然而，额外的推理可能引入过度泛化或语义噪声的抽象，从而削弱检索的特异性。现有方法主要通过最终检索结果来监督推理过程，对精炼过程本身的指导有限。我们提出AURA框架，直接根据中间推理状态在区分相关文档与困难干扰项方面的表现来监督推理过程。AURA还训练一个轻量级效用估计算法，基于前缀条件嵌入在推理时自适应调整推理深度。在推理密集型的BRIGHT基准上，AURA-4B在每查询仅使用2.8次推理步骤和96个生成token的情况下，实现了34.7的平均nDCG@10。在匹配部署条件下，自适应停止将延迟从4.6秒降低至0.76秒，相比完整轨迹推理。MS MARCO上的结果进一步表明，这种专业化不会显著损害常规域内检索性能。
👤 Yingtao Luo, Kangyi Zhao, Shiyi Du, Yubo Li 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.20926]

SkillNEO: Non-Elitist Evolutionary Optimization of Agent Skills
SkillNEO：代理技能的非精英进化优化
Agent skills turn procedural knowledge into reusable artifacts, allowing LLM agents to accumulate and refine expertise through experience. Building on this, skill evolution iteratively improves skills using execution feedback. However, improvement may require several interdependent edits, so low-reward versions can still be worth developing. Requiring immediate reward gains can discard useful partial repairs before their benefits emerge. Non-elitist evolution allows new versions to receive further edits despite scoring below earlier ones. For agent skills, this calls for evidence of what to preserve and repair next. We introduce SkillNEO (Non-Elitist Evolutionary Optimization for Agent Skills), which separately selects a version to use and versions to keep improving. Comparing how related versions perform the same task reveals working steps to preserve and remaining failures to fix. It can also revisit and revise earlier versions using later evidence. Across 86 SkillsBench tasks in eight domains, including software engineering and office work, SkillNEO raises GPT-5.4 Mini's average reward from 49.1% to 66.4%, within GPT-5.5 and GPT-5.6 Luna's original-skill range. Mini executes tasks, proposes revisions, and edits skills; final evaluation uses the same task instances. Gains span all eight domains. The same skills improve stronger models without further editing. On the 41 tasks with modified skills, reuse improves Luna by 10.5 percentage points and places GPT-5.5 10.6 points above its published original-skill reference. Execution records show these skills can benefit stronger models even when Mini still fails some task requirements. Search histories also show lower-scoring versions leading to skills ultimately selected for use. Together, these findings show that skill evolution can turn a weaker model's partial progress into reusable procedural knowledge for improving more capable agents.
中文翻译
代理技能将程序性知识转化为可复用的成果，使大语言模型代理能够通过经验积累并精炼专长。在此基础上，技能演化通过执行反馈迭代改进技能。然而，改进可能需要多个相互依赖的修改，因此低奖励版本仍可能值得开发。要求即时奖励增长会过早丢弃尚未显现效益的部分修复。非精英进化允许新版本即使得分低于早期版本也继续获得修改。对于代理技能，这需要明确保留和修复的依据。我们提出 SkillNEO（用于代理技能的非精英进化优化），它分别选择使用版本和持续改进的版本。通过比较相关版本完成相同任务的表现，揭示应保留的有效步骤和需修复的失败环节。它还能利用后期证据重新审视和修订早期版本。在涵盖软件工程和办公工作等八个领域的 86 个 SkillsBench 任务中，SkillNEO 将 GPT-5.4 Mini 的平均奖励从 49.1% 提升至 66.4%，达到 GPT-5.5 和 GPT-5.6 Luna 原始技能范围内的水平。Mini 执行任务、提出修改建议并编辑技能；最终评估使用相同任务实例。收益覆盖全部八个领域。相同技能在无需进一步编辑的情况下也能提升更强模型的表现。在 41 个修改技能的任务中，复用使 Luna 提升 10.5 个百分点，使 GPT-5.5 超出其公开发布的原始技能基准 10.6 个百分点。执行记录显示，这些技能即使在 Mini 仍未能满足部分任务要求时，也能为更强模型带来收益。搜索历史也表明，低分版本最终导向了被选中使用的技能。这些发现共同表明，技能演化可将弱模型的部分进展转化为可复用的程序性知识，用于提升更强大代理的能力。

Probing Negation Understanding in Vision-Language Models With Object Attention
通过对象注意力探究视觉-语言模型的否定理解能力
Negation is a fundamental aspect of language understanding and it is important vision-language models (VLMs) are able to process it effectively. We investigate how VLMs process negation by analysing attention patterns of different models. We draw comparisons between human gaze and attention in models when processing affirmative vs negated statements. We compare object level model attention to human eye-fixations in both binary and multary settings, evaluating with both synthetic and realistic datasets. We find models generally do not follow human-like attention patterns when resolving negation, suggesting they rely on mechanisms outside of attention. While layerwise analysis shows that PaliGemma2-3B shifts object attention to the alternative under negation, our headwise ablations suggest this is a byproduct rather than a negation processing mechanism. Additionally, we find that LLaVA-1.5-7B displays a spatial bias towards left-side objects. Our evaluation of models on downstream tasks shows that all models have a discrepancy between affirmative and negated questions highlighting the need to better understand the underlying processing of negation to close this performance gap. Code available at https://github.com/otmive/negation_attention

Evolving Together, Failing Apart: The Skill-Policy Co-evolution Trap in Self-Improving Agents
共同进化，分道扬镳：自改进智能体中的技能-策略共演化陷阱
Skill-policy co-evolution, in which an agent's policy model and its skill bank improve each other during training, is a promising paradigm for self-improving agents. Current evaluations score the co-evolved policy on the final bank it trained with (the delivered bank). After deployment, however, skills may be rewritten, added or removed more frequently than the policy model is retrained, leaving the policy to operate on a drifted skill bank that neither the training objective nor existing evaluation protocols address. In this paper, we first establish, through theoretical and empirical analysis, that the fragility hidden by this blind spot is not incidental. The acceptance rule these methods share (cooperative acceptance) ties a policy's competence to the bank it co-evolved with, leaving it fragile once skills drift, regardless of the implementation; empirically, sensitivity to skill drift grows as co-evolution training proceeds. We therefore introduce the CO-evolution Drift Audit (CODA), a deployment-oriented evaluation protocol that simulates skill drift and, alongside success rate under worst-case drift, introduces two new metrics: a consistency rate under meaning-preserving edits and a resistance rate to corrupted skills. Across four co-evolution methods and five benchmarks (six settings), worst-case success is 5-13 points below the peak measured for each delivered pair, erasing 52-93% of the delivered bank's gain over the no-skill baseline. CODA thus reframes the target of co-evolution, from peak performance on the co-evolved bank to the ability to harness skills however they evolve, and provides a shared standard against which future methods can be audited.
中文翻译
技能-策略共演化是一种有前景的自改进智能体范式，其中智能体的策略模型与其技能库在训练过程中相互提升。当前评估方法仅在最终训练完成的技能库（交付库）上评分。然而，在部署后，技能可能比策略模型更频繁地被重写、添加或删除，导致策略运行在漂移后的技能库上，而训练目标和现有评估协议均未涵盖此情况。本文通过理论与实证分析首次揭示，这种盲点所隐藏的脆弱性并非偶然。这些方法共享的接受规则（合作接受）将策略能力绑定于其共演化过的技能库，一旦技能发生漂移，无论实现方式如何，策略都会变得脆弱；实证结果表明，随着共演化训练的推进，对技能漂移的敏感性持续上升。为此，我们提出共演化漂移审计（CODA），一种面向部署的评估协议，模拟技能漂移，并引入两项新指标：在语义保持编辑下的稳定性率和对损坏技能的抗性率。在四种共演化方法和五个基准测试（六个场景）中，最坏情况下的成功率比每对交付库的峰值低5-13分，抹去了交付库相对于无技能基线52%-93%的增益。因此，CODA重新定义了共演化的目标：从在共演化技能库上的峰值性能，转变为能够灵活利用任意演化形式的技能。该协议为未来方法提供了统一的审计标准。

Confidence-Calibrated Inference Expansion for Evaluator-Guided Test-Time Reasoning
基于置信度校准的评估器引导型测试时推理扩展
Evaluator-guided reasoning presents a local control problem at every step: trust the current answer, return a fallback, or spend more inference. We formulate this choice as a compute-aware three-action controller, scoring terminal actions by predicted answer success and expansion by a rollout-based continuation-success surrogate, with compute cost priced explicitly. A local action-gap analysis characterizes when score errors can change a decision, while an expected regret bound accounts for errors along the realized expansion path. Guided by this analysis, we learn and calibrate action-specific confidence estimates, with local uncertainty penalties for ambiguous decisions. On held-out width expansion and revision tasks, the controller improves observed utility over calibration-selected fixed policies while reducing normalized online compute by about 30% and 16%, respectively. Across four Qwen3-8B seeds, compute is lower than fitted Q in both settings, while utility differences are small and mixed. The results frame evaluator-guided reasoning as resource allocation under uncertainty, rather than a uniformly stronger reasoner.

评估器引导的推理在每一步都面临局部控制问题：信任当前答案、返回备用方案，或投入更多推理计算。我们将这一选择建模为一个考虑计算成本的三动作控制器，通过预测答案成功率对终止动作打分，通过基于回溯的延续成功率代理对扩展动作打分，并显式定价计算成本。局部动作差距分析刻画了得分误差可能改变决策的情形，期望遗憾界则考虑了实际扩展路径上的误差。基于此分析，我们学习并校准了特定动作的置信度估计，并对模糊决策施加局部不确定性惩罚。在保留的宽度扩展和修订任务上，该控制器在观测效用上优于校准选择的固定策略，同时分别将归一化在线计算量降低约30%和16%。在四个Qwen3-8B种子实验中，两种情境下的计算量均低于拟合Q，而效用差异较小且混合。结果将评估器引导推理重新框架为在不确定性下的资源分配问题，而非一个始终更强的推理者。

A Self-Improving Agent that Grows its Own DSL: Oracle-Free Operator Induction for Exact Question Answering
能自我生长领域专用语言的自改进智能体：无Oracle操作符归纳用于精确问答
A self-improving agent has to decide which new skills to keep, and in practice it keeps whatever earns a high reward, convinces a judge model, or compresses its library, even when the skill is wrong. That is fine until the answers have to be exactly right: over a table of transactions, a total is either right or wrong. We ask a harder question: can an agent grow what it can compute with no reward, no judge, and no answer key, and still be exactly right? A single frozen model runs two nested loops over a small, exact domain-specific language (DSL). The inner loop answers new questions only when two independently written expressions agree on every record. The outer loop discovers its own missing capability and authors an operator for it, admitted only when an independent second implementation agrees and the result obeys a conservation law, all without ground truth. The inner loop roughly triples the answerable-question set, and the outer loop self-extends on disjoint starting sets, inventing operators outside the starting closure; a no-induction control answers none of the new questions. The admission rule is empirically sound: it rejects 138 of 138 buggy operator variants under an independent reference, even one written by a different model family, where the admit-if-it-runs, self-consistency, and compression signals the field relies on admit most of them. Unmodified, the same mechanism self-extends on two further domains with unrelated schemas, business-ledger and clinical-encounter question answering, growing their answerable sets more than tenfold and nearly twentyfold. The loop transfers across model families too: run with further proposers, the gate stays sound and independent families self-extend comparably, with only the induction yield tracking each proposer's DSL fluency.
中文翻译
自改进智能体必须决定保留哪些新技能，实践中它会保留那些获得高奖励、说服评判模型或压缩其知识库的技能，即使这些技能本身是错误的。这在答案只需大致正确时尚可接受，但当答案必须完全准确时——例如在交易表中求总和，结果要么正确要么错误——就不可接受。我们提出一个更难的问题：智能体能否在没有奖励、没有评判模型、也没有答案键的情况下，自行发展其计算能力，同时仍保证答案完全正确？一个单一冻结模型在小规模精确领域专用语言（DSL）上运行两个嵌套循环。内层循环仅在两个独立编写的表达式在每条记录上一致时才回答新问题。外层循环发现自身缺失的能力并编写对应操作符，仅当第二个独立实现也达成一致且结果满足守恒定律时才被接纳，整个过程无需真实标签。内层循环使可回答问题集扩大约三倍，外层循环在互不重叠的起始集合上实现自我扩展，发明了初始闭包之外的操作符；无归纳控制组无法回答任何新问题。接纳规则在实证中表现稳健：在独立参考下拒绝了全部138个有缺陷的操作符变体，包括由不同模型家族编写的版本，而“只要运行成功”、“自一致性”和“压缩性”等现有信号则大多接受了它们。未经修改，相同机制在另外两个无关模式域（商业账簿和临床就诊问答）上也实现了自我扩展，使可回答问题集分别增长超过十倍和近二十倍。该循环机制还能跨模型家族迁移：使用更多提议者时，门控依然稳健，不同家族的智能体表现出相当的自我扩展能力，仅归纳产出反映各提议者对DSL的熟练程度。
👤 Yun-Shiuan Chuang, Yirou Ge, Yixi Li, Yiming Bian, Zijie Pan et al. 📅 2026-10-14 🔗 arXiv

ARIA: Runtime Inspection and Amendment for Multi-Agent Systems
ARIA：多智能体系统的运行时检查与修正
In LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS), a local mistake can propagate as other agents build on it, turning a correctable error into a failed task. Diagnosing completed runs or repeatedly re-executing them to search for corrections does not provide a learned policy for intervening while execution is still in progress. We present Agent Runtime Inspection and Amendment (ARIA), a framework for learning when and how to repair ongoing multi-agent executions. Our key intuition is to turn execution-verified repairs into reusable supervision, moving repair search into data construction and training so that a learned model can intervene at runtime without repeatedly testing alternatives. First, we collect over 24{,}000 successful and failed trajectories across four multi-agent frameworks, six benchmarks, and eight host models. From these trajectories, we construct the ARIA dataset, to our knowledge the first large-scale dataset pairing failed multi-agent trajectories with execution-verified repairs. It contains 3{,}953 verified repair pairs, each specifying what went wrong, where to intervene, and how execution should continue. Then, we develop the ARIA framework for MAS-agnostic runtime inspection and amendment, designed to integrate with any MAS that exposes execution trajectories and supports continuation replacement. Using verified repairs as training examples, we teach a compact model to recognize when intervention is needed and how to correct an ongoing run. The aim is to learn repair strategies that apply to related failures in new configurations, rather than merely reproduce individual corrections. Finally, the trained ARIA model demonstrates strong repair performance in both post-hoc and runtime settings, with generalization to unseen frameworks and host models. Our code is available at https://github.com/LabRAI/ARIA.
中文翻译
在基于大语言模型的多智能体系统（MAS）中，局部错误可能被其他智能体继承并传播，导致原本可纠正的错误演变为任务失败。事后诊断已完成的运行或反复重执行以寻找修正方法，无法提供在执行过程中学习干预策略的能力。我们提出Agent Runtime Inspection and Amendment（ARIA）框架，用于学习何时以及如何修复正在进行的多智能体执行过程。核心思想是将执行验证后的修复转化为可复用的监督信号，将修复搜索转变为数据构建与训练过程，从而使学习到的模型能够在运行时进行干预，而无需反复测试替代方案。首先，我们在四个多智能体框架、六个基准测试和八个主模型上收集了超过24,000条成功与失败的轨迹。基于这些轨迹，我们构建了ARIA数据集——据我们所知，首个大规模配对失败多智能体轨迹与执行验证修复的数据集。该数据集包含3,953对已验证的修复，每对明确指出了问题所在、干预位置以及执行应如何继续。随后，我们开发了ARIA框架，实现对多智能体系统无关的运行时检查与修正，设计为可集成到任何暴露执行轨迹并支持替换继续行为的MAS中。利用验证修复作为训练样本，我们训练了一个紧凑模型，使其能够识别是否需要干预以及如何修正正在进行的运行。目标是学习适用于新配置中相关故障的通用修复策略，而非简单复制个别修正。最终，训练好的ARIA模型在事后和运行时设置中均表现出强大的修复性能，并能泛化到未见过的框架和主模型。代码已公开于https://github.com/LabRAI/ARIA。

TurboMamba: Faster Inference, Faster Training, and Better Prediction with Lexical Memory
TurboMamba：通过词汇记忆实现更快推理、更快训练与更好预测
We introduce TurboMamba, a language-model architecture for faster inference, faster training, and better token prediction. A global Mamba-3 model runs once per completed token block. A small Transformer uses this global context and earlier tokens within the block to predict the next token. Three lightweight additions improve local predictions: direct access to the previous block's last token, a small correction to next-token scores, and a learned mixture with recent-token frequencies. We compare twelve small-scale implementations on public FineWeb-Edu, with comparable parameter counts and shared training-budget caps. The baselines include Transformer, Mamba-3, Jamba, and Zamba. The Jamba and Zamba versions use Mamba-3 in place of their original Mamba modules. TurboMamba achieves the lowest mean test prediction loss (negative log-likelihood, NLL) among all twelve models. Its mean NLL is 9.4% lower than that of Mamba-3, 7.3% lower than that of the Jamba version, and 18.3% lower than that of the Zamba version. End-to-end training throughput is 2.60x that of Mamba-3, 2.81x that of the Jamba version, and 4.82x that of the Zamba version. We measure end-to-end batch latency for a batch of 32 sequences, each with a 256-token prompt and 128 generated tokens. On this workload, TurboMamba achieves a 3.12x speedup over Mamba-3, a 3.53x speedup over the Jamba version, and a 6.21x speedup over the Zamba version.
中文翻译
我们提出TurboMamba，一种用于更快推理、更快训练和更好token预测的语言模型架构。一个全局的Mamba-3模型在每个完整token块完成后运行一次；一个小规模Transformer利用该全局上下文以及块内早期token来预测下一个token。通过三种轻量级改进提升局部预测性能：直接访问前一块的最后一个token，对下一个token得分进行小幅度修正，以及与近期token频率的可学习混合。我们在公共FineWeb-Edu数据集上对比了十二种小规模实现，参数量相当且共享训练预算上限。基线包括Transformer、Mamba-3、Jamba和Zamba。其中Jamba和Zamba版本用Mamba-3替换了其原始Mamba模块。TurboMamba在所有十二个模型中实现了最低的平均测试预测损失（负对数似然，NLL）。其平均NLL比Mamba-3低9.4%，比Jamba版本低7.3%，比Zamba版本低18.3%。端到端训练吞吐量分别为Mamba-3的2.60倍、Jamba版本的2.81倍和Zamba版本的4.82倍。我们测量了一个包含32个序列的批量任务的端到端批处理延迟，每个序列包含256个token的提示和128个生成token。在此工作负载下，TurboMamba相比Mamba-3提速3.12倍，相比Jamba版本提速3.53倍，相比Zamba版本提速6.21倍。

CascadeLedger: Counterfactual Auditing of Information and Resource Integrity in LLM Cascades
CascadeLedger：对LLM级联中信息与资源完整性的反事实审计
We introduce CascadeLedger, a counterfactual execution-audit framework for budgeted language-model cascades. The protocol enforces call-before-read semantics, binds successful reads to service and cache identities, records availability and component-wise budget transitions, and freezes the event trace before any target or task scorer is joined. The central object is aggregate-equivalent trace validity: two executions can share a final answer, declared resource projection, and service histogram while differing in whether their routing decisions used only legitimately observable information. We complement single-trace invariants with counterfactual prefix non-interference (CPNI), which couples a visible prefix and policy randomness while changing only a declared hidden family. The counterfactual influence frontier (CIF) localizes the earliest violating prefix and a minimal hidden witness; projection-preserving adversarial mutation (PPAM) searches for invalid traces that remain acceptable to a non-counterfactual checker. Across frozen-cache, independently generated, and serving evaluations, the two-layer audit reports a clean false-positive rate of 0.0000, detects 0.9983 of serialized faults and 0.9982 of unlogged hidden-read faults, and changes 0.3403 of corrupted quality-resource conclusions after deterministic recomputation. The online recorder and offline CPNI audit have separate overhead measurements. Learned routing and semantic verification provide downstream compatibility studies under the same execution contract.
中文翻译
我们提出CascadeLedger，一种针对预算受限语言模型级联的反事实执行审计框架。该协议强制执行“调用前读取”语义，将成功的读取绑定到服务和缓存身份，记录可用性及组件级别的预算转换，并在任何目标或任务评分器加入前冻结事件追踪。核心概念是聚合等价的追踪有效性：两个执行可以共享最终答案、声明的资源投影和服务中心统计，但其路由决策是否仅使用合法可观测信息可能存在差异。我们补充单次追踪不变性，引入反事实前缀非干扰（CPNI），在保持可见前缀和策略随机性的同时，仅改变声明的隐藏家族。反事实影响前沿（CIF）定位最早违反前缀和最小隐藏见证；投影保持对抗突变（PPAM）用于搜索对非反事实检查器仍可接受但无效的追踪。在冻结缓存、独立生成和服务评估中，双层审计报告的干净假阳性率为0.0000，检测到0.9983的串行故障和0.9982的未记录隐藏读取故障，并在确定性重计算后修正了0.3403的受损质量-资源结论。在线记录器与离线CPNI审计具有独立开销测量。学习的路由和语义验证提供了在相同执行契约下的下游兼容性研究。

MagiC: Evaluating Multimodal Cognition Toward Grounded Visual Reasoning
MagiC：面向具身视觉推理的多模态认知评估
Recent advances in large vision-language models have led to impressive performance in visual question answering and multimodal reasoning. However, it remains unclear whether these models genuinely perform grounded visual reasoning or rely on superficial patterns and dataset biases. In this work, we introduce MagiC, a comprehensive benchmark designed to evaluate grounded multimodal cognition, assessing not only answer accuracy but also the quality of step-by-step reasoning and its alignment with relevant visual evidence. Our benchmark includes approximately 5,500 weakly supervised QA examples generated from strong model outputs and 900 human-curated examples with fine-grained annotations, including answers, rationales, and bounding box groundings. We evaluate 15 vision-language models ranging from 7B to 70B parameters across four dimensions: final answer correctness, reasoning validity, grounding fidelity, and self-correction ability. MagiC further includes diagnostic settings to probe model robustness under adversarial visual cues and assess their capacity for introspective error correction. We introduce new metrics such as MagiScore and StepSense, and provide comprehensive analyses that reveal key limitations and opportunities in current approaches to grounded visual reasoning.

OmniVerifier-C1: Decomposed Graded Verifier for Rule-Governed Generative World Modeling
OmniVerifier-C1：用于规则驱动生成世界建模的分解式分级验证器
Generative visual verifiers play a crucial role in advancing multimodal intelligence. To enhance the robustness of visual verifiers in complex multimodal scenarios involving compositional requirements, we move beyond conventional binary verifiers that produce only True/False discrete judgments and introduce a verifier that automatically decomposes complex prompts or environmental rules into fine-grained components and assigns pointwise scores to provide a continuous and more informative critique signal. Our experiments further demonstrate that these structured and interpretable outputs improve the verifier's generalization to out-of-domain multimodal critic tasks. To train this verifier, we introduce a Decoupled RL training strategy that jointly optimizes sub-question-level binary judgment and global alignment scoring, using precise local feedback to develop fine-grained verification capabilities that provide a foundation for reliable continuous scoring at the global level. Under a general reinforcement learning framework, we first empirically demonstrate that the proposed pointwise graded verifier significantly outperforms binary verifiers in traditional text-to-image scenarios. We then extend our investigation to rule-governed generative world modeling where generative models are leveraged for world simulation and world reasoning under explicit rules. From a theoretical standpoint, we further show that a pairwise verification paradigm yields more accurate critiques than the pointwise formulation when ground-truth images are available, with empirical results on diverse structured world-modeling tasks such as Maze and Sudoku further validating the effectiveness of our approach. Overall, our findings suggest a key insight: the transition from generative models to structured world-modeling agents critically hinges on the availability of accurate pairwise graded visual verifiers.

生成式视觉验证器在推动多模态智能发展方面发挥着关键作用。为了提升复杂多模态场景下视觉验证器的鲁棒性，我们超越了仅输出真/假二元判断的传统验证器，提出一种能够自动将复杂提示或环境规则分解为细粒度组件，并对每个部分赋予权重评分的验证器，从而提供连续且更具信息量的批判信号。实验表明，这种结构化且可解释的输出显著提升了验证器在跨领域多模态批评任务中的泛化能力。为训练该验证器，我们引入了一种解耦强化学习策略，联合优化子问题级别的二元判断与全局对齐评分，利用精确的局部反馈来发展细粒度验证能力，为全局连续评分奠定基础。在通用强化学习框架下，我们首先实证证明，所提出的逐点分级验证器在传统文本到图像场景中显著优于二元验证器。随后，我们将研究扩展至规则驱动的生成世界建模，即利用生成模型进行显式规则下的世界模拟与推理。从理论角度看，当存在真实图像时，成对验证范式比逐点形式能产生更准确的批判；在迷宫和数独等多样化结构化世界建模任务上的实证结果进一步验证了我们方法的有效性。总体而言，我们的发现揭示了一个关键洞察：从生成模型向结构化世界建模智能体的转变，关键在于具备准确的成对分级视觉验证器。

Training Reasoning Models to Generate Diverse Scientific Ideas
训练推理模型以生成多样化的科学创意
One of the first steps of a research pipeline is generating ideas. Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to automate this step, but most of this work optimizes the quality of individual ideas, while overlooking diversity. In our work, we study document-grounded ideation, focusing on increasing the diversity of LLM generated ideas and ensuring they are high quality. Given prior papers, a model must propose a set of ideas that are each high-quality and clearly distinct from one another, while also being grounded in the input documents. To optimize for this, we propose training with a set-level reinforcement learning objective. In our setup, in a single generation, the model writes K structured idea cards grounded in the source papers, and the set is scored jointly, where each idea's quality is weighted by its distance from the ideas written before it. This teaches the model to steer each new idea away from its predecessors. We show that our trained 8B model achieves higher creative utility (a measure combining quality and diversity) than Claude Opus 4.8 and GPT-6 Astra on our contamination-free benchmark. In a pilot human study with four experts, our sets are judged as promising as Claude Opus 4.8's and contain marginally more unique ideas. When a coding agent runs proof-of-concept experiments on the ideas, our model's success@k is comparable to Claude Opus 4.8's, so we see that the added diversity does not cost any executability.

Grid-Bench: Can LLMs Predict How Discrete Worlds Change?
Grid-Bench：大语言模型能否预测离散世界的演变？
Spatial instructions describe how to move objects, transform regions, or modify their surroundings. When several operations are applied in sequence, each acts on a configuration shaped by earlier changes. How accurately can large language models (LLMs) predict the resulting spatial state? We introduce Grid-Bench, an executable benchmark in which eight families of operations transform discrete worlds represented as two-, three-, and four-dimensional grids. Given an initial grid, explicit rules, and an event history expressed as text, a model predicts the complete final grid. Reference execution makes every predicted value checkable, supporting comparisons across spatial operations, history lengths, and workloads. Evaluating six LLMs shows that complete-state accuracy falls sharply as histories grow, with substantial divergence among models that perform similarly on single events. Models with similar exact-match accuracy can nevertheless differ substantially in the number of incorrect cells on shared instances. Even over long histories, some models recover many correct changes beyond what copying the initial state would yield. Grid-Bench makes both complete recovery and these differences in returned states directly measurable across a range of spatial conditions.
中文翻译
空间指令描述如何移动物体、变换区域或修改其周围环境。当多个操作按顺序应用时，每个操作都作用于由先前变化塑造的状态。大语言模型（LLMs）在多大程度上能准确预测最终的空间状态？我们引入Grid-Bench，这是一个可执行的基准测试，包含八类操作，用于转换以二维、三维和四维网格表示的离散世界。给定初始网格、明确规则和以文本形式表达的历史事件，模型需预测完整的最终网格。参考执行使得每个预测值均可验证，支持在不同空间操作、历史长度和工作负载之间的比较。对六种LLM的评估显示，随着历史增长，完整状态的准确性急剧下降，即使在单个事件上表现相似的模型之间也存在显著差异。某些模型在相同实例中虽有相近的完全匹配准确率，但在错误单元数量上却存在巨大差异。即便在长历史情况下，一些模型仍能恢复出远超仅复制初始状态所能获得的正确变化。Grid-Bench使得在多种空间条件下，完整恢复能力和返回状态差异均可直接测量。

AI Consilium: a Benchmark of LLM Clinical Multidisciplinary Second Opinions
AI Consilium：基于LLM的临床多学科第二意见基准测试
Large language models (LLMs) can generate differential diagnoses for complex cases, but a useful second opinion must also be factually grounded, clinically coherent, actionable, and safe. We developed a multidimensional benchmark using 300 diagnostically resolved NEJM--MedQA cases: 110 for physician reference ratings and Judge calibration and 190 for held-out evaluation. Eight single-model pipelines generated independent role-specific opinions and interactive-consilium reports under free-form and structured clinical-reasoning conditions. A physician-calibrated LLM Judge evaluated 3,040 final reports and the corresponding structured primary-role outputs. Model identity separated report quality more strongly than response format. Structured final reports had higher overall mean scores in seven of eight model pipelines, with their clearest advantage in management (2.740 vs. 2.436). Compared with the structured pre-consilium primary-role opinion, the complete structured synthesis had higher pooled scores across all five shared criteria, with the largest increases in problem prioritization, factual grounding, and reasoning coherence. GPT-5.6-Sol led every evaluated component and reached consensus in the fewest review rounds. These findings show that the benchmark can distinguish model capability, the contribution of multidisciplinary synthesis, criterion-specific effects of response structure, deliberation dynamics, and inference requirements within one common-case evaluation.

Multi-Target Few-Shot Prompting with Diffusion Language Models
基于扩散语言模型的多目标少样本提示方法
Few-shot prompting is useful when instructions and rewards are hard to specify. One such application is editing by example, where a demonstrated formatting change or API migration may need to be applied across multiple sites. However, such edits often depend on both left and right context, making generation with causal autoregressive models awkward. We therefore introduce multi-target few-shot prompting with diffusion language models, where examples guide the infilling of multiple masked regions within a shared canvas. To address the known challenge of reliably following demonstrations in few-shot prompting, we further leverage selective masking of the canvas to derive a likelihood-ratio reward that favors example-supported infills. Across few-shot prompting benchmarks and software refactoring case studies, we find that demonstrations improve infilling accuracy, with example guidance yielding further gains in several settings.

Hy-Memory-30B-A3B: Attributed Reinforcement Learning for Long-Term Memory Writing
Hy-Memory-30B-A3B：面向长期记忆书写的属性强化学习方法
External-memory systems extend language-model applications beyond a context window, but their quality depends critically on the model executing the write path: deciding what to retain, how precisely to represent it, and how new evidence should update persistent state. Existing frameworks typically place a general-purpose large language model behind framework-specific prompts. This raises serving cost, and a writer specialized for one protocol does not transfer directly to another; a unified model that can operate across established memory frameworks remains largely unexplored. We present Hy-Memory-30B-A3B, a compact long-term-memory writer trained across Mem0, MemOS, and Hy-Memory. Memory On-Policy Distillation teaches framework-specific write protocols on student-generated states; Memory RL then optimizes downstream answerability using dense per-chunk probes, attributed cross-chunk rewards, over-storage controls, and chunk-level GRPO. Carrying policy-written banks across windows keeps training aligned with deployment. On fixed 20% benchmark subsamples, Hy-Memory-30B-A3B ranks first on all four evaluated harnesses, including held-out EverMemOS, with a 55.49% macro average versus 54.62% for Claude Opus 5.

agentic-rl
Mosaic: Multi-Session Agentic Reinforcement Learning
Mosaic：多会话智能体强化学习
Most existing agentic reinforcement learning methods assume single-session trajectories, where interaction proceeds within one continuously accumulated context. However, agentic techniques such as context compaction and subagent forking may produce trajectories spanning multiple sessions, creating a mismatch between the single-session training assumption and inference-time execution. Therefore, we introduce Multi-Session Agentic Reinforcement Learning, a more general setting that supports an arbitrary number of sessions within a trajectory. Under this setting, a widely adopted method is to broadcast trajectory-level advantages to all sessions, but this does not distinguish contributions from various sessions or guide session allocation within trajectories. To address this issue, we propose Mosaic, a multi-session agentic RL method with critic-free hierarchical optimization. At the trajectory level, it incorporates session-usage signals into advantage estimation. At the session level, it shapes each session's advantage according to the estimated marginal contribution. Experiments on BrowseComp with Qwen3.5-4B show that Mosaic outperforms broadcasting in both context compaction and subagent forking scenarios while using fewer sessions on average. Further analyses show that our method achieves improvements in session utility and allocation, which we suggested is a promising target for future advances in multi-session agentic RL.
中文翻译
现有大多数智能体强化学习方法假设单一会话轨迹，即交互在持续累积的上下文中进行。然而，诸如上下文压缩和子智能体分叉等智能体技术可能产生跨越多个会话的轨迹，造成训练时假设与推理时执行之间的不匹配。因此，我们提出多会话智能体强化学习，一种支持轨迹中任意数量会话的更通用设置。在此设定下，广泛采用的方法是将轨迹级优势广播至所有会话，但这无法区分各会话的贡献，也无法指导轨迹内会话的分配。为解决此问题，我们提出Mosaic，一种无需评判器的分层优化多会话智能体RL方法。在轨迹层面，它将会话使用信号纳入优势估计；在会话层面，根据估计的边际贡献调整每个会话的优势。在BrowseComp数据集上使用Qwen3.5-4B的实验表明，Mosaic在上下文压缩和子智能体分叉场景中均优于广播方法，且平均使用更少会话。进一步分析显示，我们的方法提升了会话利用率与分配效率，这提示其可能是未来多会话智能体强化学习的重要发展方向。
👤 Yilong Xu, Minrui Xu, Xinyu Zhou, Xiang Long, Haochen Tan et al. 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

EMO: A Closed-Loop Test-Time Computation Framework via Alternating Context Optimization and Logits Steering
EMO：通过交替上下文优化与logits调控实现闭环测试时计算框架
Test-time context optimization adapts frozen language models through textual feedback, but ordinary conditional generation provides no separate control over how strongly to reinforce the distributional changes induced by a context update. We introduce EMO, a training-free framework inspired by expectation--maximization that alternates question-specific context estimation (E-step) and distribution refinement (M-step). The E-step revises a system context using textual gradients from an external judge without access to gold answers. The M-step contrasts next-token distributions under the revised and preceding contexts at a shared prefix to construct a confidence-constrained logit correction. Feedback on the steered response drives the next context update, coupling adaptation across rounds with intervention within each generation. Theoretically, we connect parameter and context optimization through a shared target-KL objective and additive logit representations, and derive the steering vector from context-induced logit displacements. Under context-improvement and faithful-steering assumptions, we prove a one-round decrease in target KL divergence for sufficiently small steering strength. Under greedy decoding across eight models and six benchmarks, EMO improves the zero-shot baseline in 39 of 40 aggregated settings, averaging $+9.10$ percentage points. With the same judge, it adds $+1.53$ points on average over context-only optimization (TextGrad), with consistent MMLU-Pro gains across models. At matched target-model generation budgets, accuracy advantages over best-of-$N$ are strongest on Llama, while oracle coverage is higher in most comparisons despite fewer candidates. The coverage--selection gap motivates allocating test-time computation jointly to candidate generation and verification.
中文翻译
测试时上下文优化通过文本反馈来调整冻结的语言模型，但普通条件生成无法独立控制上下文更新所引发分布变化的强化强度。我们引入EMO，一种受期望最大化启发的无训练框架，交替执行特定问题的上下文估计（E步）和分布精炼（M步）。E步利用外部评判者提供的文本梯度修订系统上下文，无需访问真实答案。M步在共享前缀下对比修订前后上下文的下一个词分布，构建一个受置信度约束的logit修正。对调控响应的反馈驱动下一轮上下文更新，将多轮适应与每轮生成内的干预耦合。理论上，我们通过共享目标KL目标和加性logit表示，连接参数与上下文优化，并从上下文引起的logit位移中推导出调控向量。在上下文改进和忠实调控假设下，我们证明当调控强度足够小时，目标KL散度在一回合内下降。在八种模型和六项基准上采用贪婪解码，EMO在40个聚合设置中的39个优于零样本基线，平均提升9.10个百分点。在相同评判者条件下，其平均比仅上下文优化（TextGrad）高出1.53点，且在各模型上保持一致的MMLU-Pro提升。在匹配目标模型生成预算下，其准确率优势在Llama上最强，尽管候选数量较少，但覆盖率在多数比较中更高。覆盖率与选择间的差距提示应联合分配测试时计算资源于候选生成与验证。
👤 Wei Xia, Yehui Tang, Zhi-Hong Deng 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

PremiseSQL: Evaluating Premise Critique and Abstention in Text-to-SQL Systems
PremiseSQL：评估文本到SQL系统中的前提批判与回避能力
Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have substantially improved Text-to-SQL performance, yet existing NL2SQL systems largely assume that user queries are valid and answerable. As a result, models often generate executable SQL even for queries with flawed premises. We introduce premise critique ability in NL2SQL, namely the ability to recognize flawed queries and abstain from generating misleading SQL. To evaluate this capability, we propose PremiseSQL, a benchmark containing 1,800 flawed NL2SQL queries across four categories of premise failures, along with their 1,800 original queries. We further design an evaluation framework to measure intrinsic and prompt-induced premise-aware behavior across three prompting settings. Experiments on frontier LLMs show that current models are strongly premise-trusting by default and rarely critique flawed premises. Stronger critique-oriented prompting improves detection and abstention on flawed queries, but also increases false invalid judgments and suppresses SQL generation on originals. The code is available at https://github.com/vegeet/PremiseSQL.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）的最新进展显著提升了文本到SQL（NL2SQL）系统的性能，但现有NL2SQL系统大多假设用户查询是有效且可回答的。因此，模型常常会为前提存在缺陷的查询生成可执行的SQL语句。我们引入了NL2SQL中的前提批判能力，即识别无效查询并避免生成误导性SQL的能力。为评估这一能力，我们提出了PremiseSQL基准，包含1800个存在四类前提失败的缺陷NL2SQL查询及其对应的1800个原始查询。我们还设计了一个评估框架，用于测量在三种提示设置下模型的内在和提示诱导的前提感知行为。对前沿LLM的实验表明，当前模型默认具有强烈的前提信任倾向，很少批判有缺陷的前提。更强的批判导向提示能提高对缺陷查询的检测与回避能力，但也增加了错误无效判断，并抑制了对原始查询的SQL生成。代码已公开于https://github.com/vegeet/PremiseSQL。

Intern-Decision: Efficient Multimodal Decision Making with Autoregressive Masked Language Modeling
Intern-Decision：基于自回归掩码语言建模的高效多模态决策方法
Interactive agents repeatedly choose among a finite set of actions, labels, or ordinal outcomes. Generating a free-form response for each choice adds latency and complicates the interface between a model and its environment. We present Intern-Decision, a family of 0.8B, 2B, and 4B multimodal decision models that return structured choices and candidate probabilities from text and optional images. Our autoregressive masked language modeling objective places one decision placeholder per field in a complete assistant JSON skeleton. Ground-truth symbols appear only in supervision, while all fields are evaluated in a single causal forward pass using the backbone's native language-model head. The 4B model achieves a 90.02% unweighted average accuracy across seven evaluation suites, compared with 88.74% for Jev v1.13.0. For a fixed 289-token request with three fields on one RTX 4090, mean latency is 44.16 ms for the 4B model and approximately 33--34 ms for the smaller models. On a separate 96-case known-distribution pilot, temperature scaling reduces expected multiclass Brier from 0.628 to 0.550 and expected calibration error from 0.213 to 0.089. A confidence-based handoff study on 111 public Jevbench-Hard items improves accuracy from 82/111 (73.87%) to 97/111 (87.39%) by routing 42 items to Atria-Dawn-Preview. We also describe qualitative game, visual interface, and browser demonstrations. These results support compact decision models as a practical component of larger agent systems.
中文翻译
交互式智能体需在有限的动作、标签或有序结果中反复选择。为每次选择生成自由形式的响应会增加延迟，并使模型与环境之间的接口复杂化。我们提出Intern-Decision，一个包含0.8B、2B和4B参数量的多模态决策模型家族，能够从文本及可选图像中返回结构化选择结果和候选概率。我们的自回归掩码语言建模目标在完整的助手JSON骨架中为每个字段设置一个决策占位符。真实符号仅在监督信号中出现，而所有字段通过骨干模型原生的语言模型头在单次因果前向传播中完成评估。4B模型在七个评估套件上的未加权平均准确率达到90.02%，优于Jev v1.13.0的88.74%。对于固定289个token请求且含三个字段，在RTX 4090上，4B模型的平均延迟为44.16毫秒，较小模型约为33–34毫秒。在另一项96例已知分布的预实验中，温度缩放将预期多分类Brier分数从0.628降至0.550，预期校准误差从0.213降至0.089。针对111个公开的Jevbench-Hard项目进行基于置信度的交接研究，通过将42个项目路由至Atria-Dawn-Preview，准确率从82/111（73.87%）提升至97/111（87.39%）。我们还展示了定性游戏、视觉界面和浏览器演示。这些结果支持紧凑型决策模型作为更大智能体系统中实用的组成部分。

Explaining the Roles of Experts in Mixture-of-Experts LLMs for Personalization
解释混合专家LLM中专家角色在个性化中的作用
Personalization of Large Language Models (LLMs) is increasingly important for user-centric applications, yet prior methods mainly adapt dense LLMs, whose polysemantic neurons, each encoding multiple unrelated concepts, hinder localizing the components behind personalization, and thus understanding and controlling it. We propose to personalize Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) LLMs, which sparsely route each token to a few specialized experts, reducing polysemanticity and enabling such localization. We adapt three open-weight MoE LLMs on a large-scale personalization dataset and train crosscoders on paired activations of the personalized and non-personalized models to learn interpretable personalized latents, from which we identify the experts encoding distinct personalization features. The personalized MoE LLMs perform competitively with dense LLMs while activating only a fraction of their parameters, exhibit lower polysemanticity than dense LLMs with similar active parameters, and, within the same model family, yield personalized latents that are less shared with the non-personalized model. Causal interventions show that these latents suffice to recover the personalized model's behavior and that boosting their associated experts moves the non-personalized model toward it, revealing distinct routing paths for different personalization features.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）的个性化在以用户为中心的应用中日益重要，但以往方法主要针对密集型LLM进行适配，其多义神经元（每个编码多个无关概念）阻碍了对个性化背后组件的定位，从而难以理解和控制个性化过程。我们提出对混合专家（MoE）LLM进行个性化，该模型稀疏地将每个词元路由到少数专用专家，减少多义性并实现此类定位。我们在大规模个性化数据集上适配了三个开源权重的MoE LLM，并训练交叉编码器以学习个性化激活对之间的可解释个性化潜在表示，从中识别出编码特定个性化特征的专家。个性化后的MoE LLM在性能上与密集型LLM相当，但仅激活其部分参数；在相同活跃参数数量下，其多义性低于密集型LLM；在同一模型家族内，个性化潜在表示与非个性化模型的共享程度更低。因果干预表明，这些潜在表示足以恢复个性化模型的行为，且增强其相关专家可使非个性化模型向其靠近，揭示了不同个性化特征的不同路由路径。
👤 Sekh Mainul Islam, Pepa Atanasova, Isabelle Augenstein 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22100]
agentic-rl
Patch where the text is new: a compression view of where byte-level models should spend compute
在文本处打补丁：从压缩视角看字节级模型应如何分配计算资源
Byte-level language models such as the Byte Latent Transformer (BLT) run their large global model once per patch and start a patch where a small model's next-byte entropy is high. This rule rarely starts a patch at in-line computed results, such as the number after "=" in a worked solution, and accuracy on them collapses under tight patch budgets. We propose novelty patching, motivated by LZ77-style compression: start a patch where the upcoming bytes are unlikely to repeat the recent context. The rule is a lookup table over the previous two bytes, fitted in seconds on raw text with no labels, reference model or losses. In BLT-style models trained from scratch on a 1.9 GB math corpus at a matched ~11% patch rate (3 seeds each), it has no clear effect at 1M parameters, raises final-answer accuracy from 47% to 72% at 12M, and at 50M raises computed-result accuracy from 21% to 57% and final-answer accuracy from 60% to 90%; a table fitted on the raw pretraining corpus keeps the gain. Outside math the advantage shrinks: novelty lowers bits per byte on prose and ties entropy on source code and on synthetic execution traces. An analysis of where boundaries fall explains the pattern. Computed results have ordinary surprisal for the patcher's model, so surprisal-seeking rules pass over them: entropy concentrates patch starts on high-surprisal bytes most strongly of all rules (boundary enrichment 1.61) and is the worst on computed results, while the best rules sit near chance enrichment. Novelty works where new content begins at segment starts close to the computation, as in written math; a learned chunker (H-Net) finds such structure on its own, in math and in code. We also report what did not work: typed uncertainty as a training rule, switching rules after training, and a context-aware novelty predictor.
中文翻译
字节级语言模型（如Byte Latent Transformer, BLT）在每个补丁上运行一次大型全局模型，并在小模型预测下一个字节熵较高时启动新补丁。该规则很少在内联计算结果（如解题过程中等号后的数字）处启动补丁，导致在严格补丁预算下这些结果的准确性急剧下降。我们提出新颖性补丁策略，受LZ77压缩启发：在即将出现的字节不太可能重复近期上下文时启动补丁。该规则是一个基于前两个字节的查找表，可在原始文本上秒级拟合，无需标签、参考模型或损失函数。在1.9GB数学语料库上从头训练的BLT风格模型中，该策略在100万参数时无明显效果，但在1200万参数时将最终答案准确率从47%提升至72%；在5000万参数时，计算结果准确率从21%升至57%，最终答案准确率从60%升至90%；在预训练语料库上拟合的表格可保持该增益。在非数学领域，优势减弱：新颖性在散文中降低每字节比特数，在源代码和合成执行轨迹上与熵持平。对补丁边界位置的分析解释了这一模式。计算结果对补丁器模型而言具有普通意外性，因此基于意外性的规则会跳过它们：熵集中于最易被意外性规则捕捉的高意外性字节（边界丰富度1.61），在计算结果上表现最差，而最佳规则接近随机丰富度。新颖性在段落起始处内容新且接近计算位置时有效，如书面数学；学习到的分块器（H-Net）能自行发现数学和代码中的此类结构。我们还报告了无效尝试：以类型不确定性作为训练规则、训练后切换规则、以及上下文感知的新颖性预测器。
👤 Nicolás Vera Zúñiga 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

Multi-SFT: Permissively-Licensed Multilingual Instruction-Following
Multi-SFT：许可宽松的多语言指令遵循数据集
Supervised fine-tuning datasets remain overwhelmingly English-centric and commercially restricted, as their content is frequently generated by proprietary models whose terms of service forbid training competing systems. We introduce Multi-SFT, a commercially permissive collection of 502,818 instruction-response pairs across five European languages, built via translation and regeneration pipelines for tasks where translation is insufficient. Human evaluation yields inter-annotator agreement of at least 0.87 across all datasets and languages. Fine-tuning nine models from three different families (EuroLLM, OLMo 2, Qwen3; 1B-32B) and evaluating them on 13 benchmarks shows that Multi-SFT alone is competitive with larger augmented mixes and official instruction-tuned releases, with decisive advantages on safety tasks.
中文翻译
