Priming Base Models for Agentic Post-Training: An Open Midtraining Recipe
面向智能体后训练的基座模型预训练：一份开源中间训练配方
Frontier models, general-purpose and domain-specific alike, now include a midtraining stage between pretraining and post-training, but little has been published about how this stage is built. We present an open midtraining recipe that primes base models for agentic post-training, and we show its effectiveness on two Nemotron models of different sizes. Pretraining typically runs at short context and sees almost no agent interactions, while SFT data is mostly synthetic, and very long SFT stages can hurt the model's later RL trainability. Our midtraining stage sits between the two. It trains at 256K context for hundreds of billions of tokens on a diverse mixture of natural software-engineering data like packed repositories, pull requests, commit histories, and build logs together with a small share of agentic traces. On Nemotron 3.5 Lightning, 500B midtraining tokens improve SWE-bench Verified from 60.7 to 64.5, SWE-bench Multilingual from 55.8 to 62.8, and SWE-bench Pro from 32.0 to 35.5 after identical full SFT. On the larger Nemotron 3.5 Super, 280B midtraining tokens improve DeepSWE, a harder benchmark of long-horizon tasks, from 11.7 to 15.7 and SWE-bench Verified from 72.9 to 74.3 after identical full SFT. Midtraining also improves robustness across agent harnesses on both models. We report the ablations behind the recipe and an evaluation protocol built on short fixed SFT runs, since base-model proxy metrics did not correlate reliably with post-training results.
中文翻译
前沿模型，无论通用还是特定领域，现在都包含预训练与后训练之间的中间训练阶段，但关于该阶段如何构建的研究极少。我们提出了一种开源的中间训练配方，用于为智能体后训练预训练基座模型，并展示了其在两种不同规模的Nemotron模型上的有效性。预训练通常在短上下文下运行，几乎不涉及智能体交互；SFT数据大多为合成数据，且非常长的SFT阶段可能损害模型后续强化学习的可训练性。我们的中间训练阶段位于两者之间：在256K上下文长度下，使用数十亿token的多样化自然软件工程数据（如打包仓库、拉取请求、提交历史、构建日志）与少量智能体轨迹混合训练。在Nemotron 3.5 Lightning上，5000亿中间训练token使SWE-bench Verified得分从60.7提升至64.5，SWE-bench Multilingual从55.8提升至62.8，SWE-bench Pro从32.0提升至35.5（在相同完整SFT后）。在更大的Nemotron 3.5 Super上，2800亿中间训练token使更难的长周期任务基准DeepSWE得分从11.7提升至15.7，SWE-bench Verified从72.9提升至74.3（同样在相同完整SFT后）。中间训练还提升了两个模型在各类智能体测试框架下的鲁棒性。我们报告了该配方背后的消融实验，并提出基于短固定SFT运行的评估协议，因为基座模型代理指标与后训练结果未表现出可靠相关性。
👤 Soumye Singhal, Joseph Jennings, Ka Shun Shum, Alexander Bukharin, Khushi Bhardwaj et al. 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.21900]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
TEM: Temporal State Tracking Memory for Large Language Model Agents
用于大语言模型智能体的时间状态追踪记忆
An agent's memory must remain useful as information changes across conversations. A new fact may replace an earlier value without making that value irrelevant to a later question. We present Temporal State Tracking Memory (TEM), a training-free framework for tracking these changes. TEM stores facts as versioned records linked to their sources. It retrieves relevant versions, places them in source order and labels historical records before passing the evidence to the answer model. Event memory and original dialogue turns provide details missing from structured facts. We evaluated TEM on 420 questions drawn from 600 sessions involving 60 personas. Across four open-weight models, key-entity hit rates reached 75.71-97.62% overall and 96.67-100.00% on the historical-residence subset. Adding historical labels to otherwise identical, source-ordered facts increased overall hit rates by 17.14-55.95 percentage points. The evidence was compact: 94-118 tokens per answer, or 48.75-56.73% less than session-aware retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). A further evaluation on 1,540 LoCoMo questions tested hybrid memory under a fixed evidence budget. TEM exceeded session-aware RAG with both backbones; conservative score differences were 7.92 and 10.58 percentage points. The results support preserving fact versions and exposing their temporal status to the generator when answering questions about repeatedly updated information.
中文翻译
智能体的记忆必须在对话过程中信息变化时仍保持有用。新事实可能取代旧值，但旧值对后续问题仍可能相关。我们提出了时间状态追踪记忆（TEM），一种无需训练的框架来追踪这些变化。TEM将事实存储为带版本的记录，并关联其来源。它检索相关版本，按来源顺序排列，并在传递证据给回答模型前标记历史记录。事件记忆和原始对话轮次提供了结构化事实中缺失的细节。我们在600个会话中抽取的420个问题上评估了TEM，涉及60个角色。在四个开源模型上，关键实体命中率总体达到75.71%-97.62%，历史居住子集达到96.67%-100.00%。在原本相同的、按来源排序的事实中添加历史标签，使整体命中率提升了17.14-55.95个百分点。证据非常紧凑：每答案仅94-118个词元，比会话感知的检索增强生成（RAG）减少48.75%-56.73%。在1,540个LoCoMo问题上的进一步评估测试了固定证据预算下的混合记忆。TEM在两种骨干网络下均优于会话感知RAG；保守得分差异分别为7.92和10.58个百分点。结果支持在回答重复更新信息的问题时，保留事实版本并向生成器暴露其时间状态。
👤 Pengfeng Song, Yunkai Zhuang, Liye Shi 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

Reliable Knowledge Acquisition for Continual Learning Agents
持续学习智能体的可靠知识获取
As agents powered by foundation models move into open-ended environments, continual learning from experience becomes essential. For agents built on models with fixed parameters, this learning requires reusing prior task experience in context to improve performance and efficiency on future tasks. Existing memory and experience-learning systems typically focus on either retained facts or procedures, and lack a mechanism for establishing the reliability of claims extracted from the ongoing task stream. Indeed, we find that approximately 12--33\% of entries produced by representative systems harm future task performance when reused. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{RIKA} (Reliable Knowledge Acquisition), which combines hierarchical extraction of factual, conceptual, procedural, and metacognitive knowledge with Evidence-based Knowledge Admission, which uses source evidence and subsequent experience to govern which claims enter the task agent's context, without additional validation tasks. Across six task streams from Continual Learning Bench and four model backbones, RIKA achieves the highest aggregate score on every backbone.
中文翻译

Enhancing Multimodal RLVR with Pairwise Token Contrast and Adaptive Trajectory Confidence
通过成对标记对比与自适应轨迹置信度增强多模态强化学习验证奖励
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves multimodal reasoning, but group-relative policy optimization broadcasts a single sequence-level advantage to every token of a response, although errors usually localize to a few decisive steps. Recent self-distilled variants refine credit with a teacher--student likelihood gap. However, this signal cannot tell whether it relies on the image or on a language shortcut, and is applied uniformly even when the teacher is uninformative. To address this, we propose Pairwise token contrast and Adaptive trajectory ConfidencE (PACE). Pairwise token contrast scores each response under four teacher views, crossing a correct or incorrect answer hint with the image present or removed, and compares the two hints for every token, which cancels shifts shared by both hints and reveals whether the token's support relies on the image. Adaptive trajectory confidence measures how well the teacher predicts each response given the correct hint and the image, normalizes this score within the batch, and uses it to set how far the token contrast redistributes credit within that response. PACE achieves the best average accuracy on three backbones and ranks first on all evaluated multimodal reasoning benchmarks with Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct. Experiments show that both components matter: replacing pairwise token contrast with a single-view signal underperforms our method, while adaptive trajectory confidence adds further improvement.
中文翻译
带有可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）提升了多模态推理能力，但组相对策略优化会将单一序列级优势广播至响应中的每个标记，而错误通常仅集中在少数关键步骤。近期自蒸馏变体通过教师-学生似然差距来细化信用分配。然而，这一信号无法判断其依赖于图像还是语言捷径，且即使教师信息量不足也均匀应用。为解决此问题，我们提出成对标记对比与自适应轨迹置信度（PACE）。成对标记对比在四种教师视角下评估每个响应：交叉正确或错误的答案提示与图像存在或移除情况，并对每个标记比较两种提示，从而消除两者共有的偏移，揭示标记支持是否依赖于图像。自适应轨迹置信度衡量教师在给定正确提示和图像时预测每个响应的能力，对批次内得分进行归一化，并据此设定标记对比在响应内部重新分配信用的范围。PACE在三种骨干模型上取得最佳平均准确率，在所有评估的多模态推理基准上以Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct排名第一。实验表明两个组件均至关重要：用单视图信号替代成对标记对比会导致性能下降，而自适应轨迹置信度进一步提升效果。

Rethinking the Role of Tool Graph Memory in Multi-Turn Tool Use
重新思考工具图记忆在多轮工具使用中的作用
Large language model (LLM) agents solve complex tasks by calling external tools over multiple turns. Recent work has proposed tool graph memory, which stores past experience as tool transitions to guide subsequent selection. However, it remains unclear whether this component consistently improves agents' tool use or undermines it. We evaluate tool graph memory on nine models and two benchmarks, finding that it seldom improves significantly over baselines, and that its gains, where present, cost over twice the LLM calls. The memory's guidance is often incorrect, and random or no guidance retains most of the gain, implying that the benefit stems from extra computation rather than the memory itself. Moreover, even with the correct next tool suggested, many failures remain, such as giving up without a tool call. Storing the same experience instead as textual insights outperforms tool graph memory with almost no extra LLM calls. This suggests that memory serves better as a soft prior than as a prescription of the next tool, leading to a more effective and generalizable solution.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）代理通过多轮调用外部工具来解决复杂任务。近期研究提出了工具图记忆，将过往经验以工具转换的形式存储，以指导后续选择。然而，这一组件是否能持续提升代理的工具使用能力仍不明确。我们在九个模型和两个基准上评估了工具图记忆，发现其很少显著优于基线，且当有提升时，代价超过两倍的LLM调用次数。该记忆的引导经常错误，随机或无引导反而保留了大部分收益，表明优势源于额外计算而非记忆本身。此外，即使正确建议了下一个工具，仍存在大量失败，如在未调用工具的情况下放弃。将相同经验以文本洞察形式存储，几乎无需额外LLM调用即可优于工具图记忆。这表明记忆更适合作为软先验而非对下一步工具的强制规定，从而提供更有效且可泛化的解决方案。

Writability and the Pathway: A Controlled Study of Knowledge Injection into Addressed Memory
可写性与路径：面向定向记忆的知识注入控制研究
Addressed conditional memory provides a local surface for post-hoc knowledge injection, but a successful write need not transfer to a new question or preserve untargeted behavior. This study combines a controlled GPT-2 comparison with production-memory experiments on Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-FP8 and DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash. Newly initialized modules attached to frozen GPT-2 are tested in a 2 x 2 design: row-only versus row-plus-projection updates, each with or without fixed module-residual anchoring. All arms reproduce 50 trained completions in each of three sampling seeds. Natural-question target-string hits average 5.3% for both row-only arms, 28.7% for unanchored projection updates, and 22.0% with anchoring, while strict answer scores remain zero. Anchoring lowers measured drift; lexical hits do not establish entity-specific retrieval. In the archived Qwen experiments, gradient writes make the designated first answer token top-ranked for 132 independently reset targets, comprising 93 new promotions and 39 already top-ranked targets. A separate matched 24-target comparison gives 13 new promotions with Adam versus two with SPSA at equal iteration counts but unequal computational procedures. An 80-row case includes saved continuations and a replay record, while reformulations bound its scope. Six DeepSeek zero-order cases yield one new target-token promotion and one regression. These results support bounded writing feasibility and separate trained expression, question-form behavior, and preservation; first-token rankings do not establish complete-answer success or universal conditions for writable memory.

OPRD: On-Policy Reminder Distillation for Long-Horizon Coding Agents
OPRD：面向长周期编码代理的策略提醒蒸馏
Reliable repository-level coding requires agents not only to generate correct code but also to preserve constraints and respond to tool feedback across long interactions. However, our diagnostic analysis shows that agents often fail to act on relevant information already available in context, while final Pass/Fail outcomes provide only sparse process supervision. We introduce On-Policy Reminder Distillation (OPRD), which combines Multi-Scope Reminder supervision with outcome-based routing on reminder-free student rollouts. Successful rollouts provide autoregressive self-imitation targets, whereas failed rollouts receive Reminder-conditioned reverse-KL supervision from a teacher that re-evaluates the same student-generated prefixes. A Global Reminder converts final-test diagnostics into trajectory-level guidance, while Turn-local Reminders apply only to the next decision following the relevant environment observation. The teacher and student share a two-stage initialization from Coding SFT and Instruction-Following (IF) SFT. At inference, the student operates without Reminders. On SWE-bench Verified, SWE-bench Pro, and SWE-bench Multilingual, OPRD improves the benchmark-averaged task resolution rate by 11.3 points over the open-source Qwen3.5-4B base model. Under the same diagnostic protocol, it reduces the aggregate error-event count from 446 to 222, a 50.2% reduction.

可靠的仓库级编码不仅要求代理生成正确代码，还需在长期交互中保持约束并响应工具反馈。然而，我们的诊断分析显示，代理常常未能利用上下文中已有的相关信息，而最终的通过/失败结果仅提供稀疏的过程监督。我们提出一种策略提醒蒸馏（OPRD），结合多尺度提醒监督与无提醒学生回溯中的基于结果的路由机制。成功回溯提供自回归自我模仿目标，失败回溯则接收来自教师的提醒条件反KL监督，该教师会重新评估学生生成的前缀。全局提醒将最终测试诊断转化为轨迹级指导，而回合局部提醒仅作用于相关环境观测后的下一步决策。教师与学生共享来自编码SFT和指令遵循（IF）SFT的两阶段初始化。推理时，学生无需提醒即可运行。在SWE-bench Verified、SWE-bench Pro和SWE-bench Multilingual上，OPRD相较于开源Qwen3.5-4B基础模型，将基准平均任务解决率提升了11.3分。在相同诊断协议下，聚合错误事件数从446降至222，减少50.2%。

Trajectory-Level Speculation with Semantic Verification for Tool-Using Agents
基于语义验证的轨迹级推测方法用于工具使用智能体
Large language model agents solve tasks through long sequences of tool calls, and the prevailing design has a single strong model generate every step, although many steps are routine enough for a much smaller model. Existing ways of sharing this work keep the strong model as the generator: routers and collaborative agents decide which model handles each query or step, and speculative methods check a small model's drafts one step at a time, usually against the strong model's own action. We introduce SpecAgent, a collaboration protocol that turns the strong model from the author of each step into a reviewer of steps drafted by a weak model. The weak model executes read-only tool calls as it drafts, so that every step is grounded in real observations, and holds the first action that would modify the environment. The strong model then verifies the drafted segment in a single prefill pass, commits the longest prefix it judges semantically valid, and repairs the first rejected step. Because verification replaces autoregressive decoding with parallel prefill, the strong model decodes only short verdicts and occasional repairs rather than every step. On $τ^2$-Bench, SpecAgent comes within one point of the strong model's success rate while roughly halving memory traffic and wall time, and on AutomationBench and VitaBench it recovers 44-75% of the quality gap while lowering both. In an edge-cloud deployment on MCP-Atlas with three cloud models, it closes 53-75% of the weak-strong quality gap while the strong model generates at most half as many tokens. These results suggest that a strong model need not author every step of an agent: reviewing grounded drafts retains most of its quality while cutting both latency and memory traffic.
中文翻译
大型语言模型智能体通过一系列工具调用完成任务，当前主流设计是由一个强大的模型生成每一步，尽管许多步骤足够常规，可由更小的模型处理。现有工作保持强模型作为生成器：路由器和协作智能体决定每个查询或步骤由哪个模型处理，而推测方法则逐步检查小模型的草稿，通常与强模型自身的动作进行对比。我们提出SpecAgent，一种协作协议，将强模型从每一步的作者转变为弱模型草稿的审阅者。弱模型在起草时执行只读工具调用，使每一步都基于真实观察，并保留第一个会修改环境的动作。随后，强模型在单次预填充过程中验证草稿段，提交其判断为语义有效的最长前缀，并修复首个被拒绝的步骤。由于验证用并行预填充替代自回归解码，强模型仅需解码短的结论和偶尔的修复，而非每一步。在$τ^2$-Bench上，SpecAgent的成功率接近强模型，同时将内存流量和运行时间大致减半；在AutomationBench和VitaBench上，它恢复了44-75%的质量差距，且两者均降低。在MCP-Atlas的边缘-云部署中，使用三个云模型，它在强模型生成的token数最多减少一半的情况下，缩小了53-75%的弱-强质量差距。这些结果表明，强模型无需负责智能体的每一步：审阅基于现实的草稿可在保持大部分质量的同时，显著降低延迟和内存流量。

HyCo: Hybrid Drafting for Cost-Efficient Mobile-Cloud Speculative Decoding
HyCo：面向移动-云高效推测解码的混合草稿方法
Device-cloud speculative decoding reduces server computation, but growing a deep draft tree with a small language model (SLM) requires repeated passes on a mobile neural processing unit (NPU). We present HyCo, which assigns depth to the server model's native multi-token prediction (MTP) head, executed on the device, and alternative branches to one SLM pass. The server returns the hidden state, input embedding, and key-value entries needed by the head instead of executing its sequential steps. Quantization, a restricted vocabulary, and a 1K-token window fit both drafters to the NPU. An ε-threshold rule additionally relaxes exact preservation of the verifier's sampling distribution. In single-client runs with a Galaxy S26 Ultra over LTE/5G, HyCo reduces server GPU time per output token relative to server-side MTP self-speculation by 38.6% on Spec-Bench and 29.2% on 32K-token summarization. With the same acceptance rule, three Spec-Bench runs per system show 9.0 percentage points more saving and 1.9x delivery speed than an SLM-only drafter. HyCo differs from Cloud+MTP by +0.2 judge-score points (paired 95% CI: -0.9 to +1.3); exploratory translation favors SpecEdge-ε. Without relaxation, HyCo exact saves 27.7% and 11.9% on the two datasets.

Emotion Is Not External Knowledge: Internal Attention Signals for Affective State Reasoning
情感并非外部知识：用于情感状态推理的内部注意力信号
Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly operate in multi-turn interactions that require maintaining latent conversational states, not only explicit facts. We study affective state as an implicit state inferred from sparse contextual cues that remain relevant after topic shifts, and ask whether LLMs expose internal attention signals for routing such evidence at inference time. Prior attention interventions rely on labeled in-domain head profiles and externally specified target spans, limiting transfer across affective-evidence distributions. We propose Self-targeted Internal Attention Boosting (SIAB), which localizes sparse affective heads via causal localization (in detail activation patching) on synthetic minimal pairs and boosts, at the answer position, the content tokens these heads already attend to; both edited heads and target tokens are derived from the model's own behavior, enabling the same head set to be reused across tasks.We evaluate on ECC and GoEmotions across three LLMs and multiple prompting settings. Across settings, SIAB improves prompt-matched baselines by up to +10.3 macro-F1 on ECC and +5.5 on GoEmotions. Combined with PASTA-style external supervision, it achieves up to +13.84 accuracy points and +12.51 macro-F1 points, while mechanistic analyses trace the gains to sparse, emotion-enriched heads and show that layer proximity affects additivity.

OpCredit: Operation-Aware Policy Learning for Agentic Knowledge Graph Question Answering
OpCredit：面向智能体知识图谱问答的操作感知策略学习
Agentic knowledge graph question answering(KGQA) relies on sequences of executable operations, but trajectory-level rewards obscure their individual contributions. Successful trajectories may contain ineffective operations, while failed trajectories may contain useful ones. To assess individual operations, we propose OpCredit, an operationaware policy-learning framework that combines downstream outcome credit with immediate transition credit: Branch Outcome Credit (BOC) reuses ordinary rollouts to compare terminal task rewards observed after different operations under matched navigation contexts; Frontier Transition Credit (FTC) measures operation-induced changes in answer-conditioned frontier potential. The two complementary signals jointly supervise the corresponding decision spans during training, while inference uses only the learned policy and standard KG interface. Experiments on ComplexWebQuestions, WebQSP, and GrailQA across three backbones demonstrate consistent improvements in KGQA performance.
中文翻译
智能体式知识图谱问答（KGQA）依赖于一系列可执行操作，但轨迹级奖励掩盖了各操作的独立贡献。成功的轨迹可能包含无效操作，而失败的轨迹也可能包含有用操作。为评估单个操作，我们提出OpCredit，一种操作感知的策略学习框架，结合下游结果信用与即时转移信用：分支结果信用（BOC）利用普通回放，在匹配导航情境下比较不同操作后的最终任务奖励；前沿转移信用（FTC）衡量操作引起的答案条件前沿潜力变化。这两种互补信号共同监督训练期间相应的决策段，而推理阶段仅使用学习到的策略和标准KG接口。在ComplexWebQuestions、WebQSP和GrailQA三个基准上，使用三种骨干模型的实验表明KGQA性能持续提升。

Hybrid Generative-Tagging Decoding for Efficient Product Attribute-Value Extraction
混合生成-标记解码：高效的产品属性-值提取
Attribute-value extraction is important for building high-quality product catalogs in e-commerce. Existing systems either rely on generative or tagging strategies for extracting the attribute names and their corresponding values. Tagging models directly label spans in the source, making them fast and grounded, but they cannot produce values that require reformatting or generate rare attribute names outside their label space. Generative models avoid these drawbacks by generating both attribute names and values token by token, but this auto-regressive nature makes them slow and limits attribute coverage. We introduce hybrid generative-tagging decoding, a novel model architecture that combines tagging source spans and open-ended generation. For frequent attributes whose values are copyable, a tagging head extracts values directly from the source. This lets the generative head focus on the remainder - rare attributes outside the tagger's label space and non-copyable values, including implicit or normalized values. Integrated into vLLM inference, the hybrid decoder raises attribute coverage by 10% and extracts 63% more correct attribute-value pairs at no precision cost, while reducing latency by 44%.
中文翻译

VALCON: Benchmarking and Enhancing Value Consistency in Large Language Models via Dual-Role Variation
VALCON：通过双角色变异基准测试与增强大语言模型的价值一致性
Current value alignment evaluations for large language models (LLMs) heavily rely on output compliance, overlooking whether LLMs maintain value consistency when alternating between generator and evaluator roles. Such consistency is increasingly important because modern LLM agents and alignment pipelines often rely on the same LLM to both generate actions and evaluate their acceptability. To mitigate this limitation, we propose VALCON, a role-driven data construction framework that operationalizes value consistency into three benchmark suites (VAB-Consistency, VAB-Boundary, VAB-Challenge) via dual-role variation. By systematically pairing an LLM's generative behavior with its evaluative judgments under identical principles, VALCON synthesizes target evaluation data that exposes hidden value discrepancies. Experiments on 28 LLMs reveal pervasive cross-role inconsistency: models with highly compliant outputs frequently fail to align their judgments with their own generations. To understand and address this gap, we perform mechanistic probing that uncovers a role-dependent representational decoupling associated with this behavioral asymmetry. Guided by these insights, we propose a representation-directed intervention that effectively mitigates consistency failures. Our work establishes value consistency as a distinct alignment dimension and provides a complete evaluation-analysis-mitigation pipeline. Code and data are available at https://github.com/tjunlp-lab/VALCON.
中文翻译
当前大语言模型（LLMs）的价值对齐评估严重依赖输出合规性，忽视了当LLM在生成者与评估者角色间切换时是否保持价值一致性。这种一致性日益重要，因为现代LLM智能体和对齐流程通常依赖同一LLM同时生成动作并评估其可接受性。为缓解这一局限，我们提出VALCON，一种角色驱动的数据构建框架，通过双角色变异将价值一致性操作化为三个基准套件（VAB-一致性、VAB-边界、VAB-挑战）。通过系统性地将LLM的生成行为与其在相同原则下的评估判断配对，VALCON合成目标评估数据，暴露出隐藏的价值差异。在28个LLM上的实验揭示普遍存在的跨角色不一致现象：输出高度合规的模型常常无法使其判断与自身生成保持一致。为理解并解决这一差距，我们进行了机制探查，发现与这种行为不对称相关的角色依赖表示解耦。基于这些洞察，我们提出一种表示导向的干预方法，有效缓解了一致性失败。我们的工作确立了价值一致性作为一个独立的对齐维度，并提供了完整的评估-分析-缓解管道。代码与数据可在https://github.com/tjunlp-lab/VALCON 获取。

TRACER: Trajectory-Aware Tool Retrieval for LLM Agents
TRACER：面向大语言模型智能体的轨迹感知工具检索
Modern agents select tools from registries, but exposing entire registries incurs high token costs and can impair selection as they grow. We present TRACER, a retrieval front-end that indexes observed tool-call sequences in a trie and ranks continuations using a learned blend of empirical and contextual priors. On a mixed retail-and-telecom $τ^2$ environment, TRACER improves GPT-5.4's tool-selection accuracy by 4.7 points while reducing input tokens per step by 80%, exposing just 5 of 128 tools. In autonomous full-turn execution, it matches GPT-5.4's full-registry task success with 61% fewer input tokens per step. Across six environments and four models, TRACER improves tool-selection accuracy over full-registry selection in 21 of 24 model-environment pairs, with average gains of 6 to 10 points; the three exceptions trail by at most 2.2. We analyze failures in both regimes to identify directions for future work.

LLM-Based Weak Annotation for Cybersecurity Tweet Classification: A Teacher-Student Framework
基于大语言模型的网络安全推文分类弱标注：教师-学生框架
Cyber threat intelligence (CTI) relies on the timely identification of emerging threats, enabling organizations and security analysts to support situational awareness and decision-making. Social media platforms such as X (formerly Twitter) have become valuable CTI sources because vulnerabilities and attack activities are often discussed there before appearing in formal reports. However, the rapid growth of cybersecurity discussions on social media has created more content than domain experts can realistically review and annotate, making labeled data a major bottleneck for developing supervised learning approaches. While large language models (LLMs) have shown promise as weak annotators in other domains, their effectiveness for cybersecurity tweet classification and downstream training of domain-specific pre-trained language models (PLMs) remains underexplored. To address this challenge, we propose a Teacher-Student weak supervision framework in which a selected LLM serves as a Teacher to generate weak labels for unlabeled cybersecurity tweets, enabling the training of cybersecurity-specific PLMs without large-scale manual annotation. Experimental results show that PLMs trained solely on LLM-generated labels consistently achieve F1 scores above 0.92, remaining within 3 percentage points of fully human-supervised models across all evaluated architectures. These findings suggest that LLM-based weak annotation offers a practical alternative for constructing cybersecurity-specific training data when expert annotation resources are limited.

FGSS-BO: Feedback-Guided Hypothesis Management for LLM-Based Bayesian Optimization
FGSS-BO：基于反馈引导的LLM贝叶斯优化假设管理
Large language model (LLM)-guided Bayesian optimization (BO) uses past observations to guide search, but proposed directions and their revision can remain implicit. We propose Feedback-Guided Search State (FGSS), a structured hypothesis-management interface connecting language-based guidance to observed evidence, executable parameter interventions, and comparative support updates. An LLM reflector proposes and revises hypotheses; a deterministic curator validates them and maintains identities and test records. The evolving state guides candidate construction and priorities. Instantiated on LLAMBO, FGSS-BO retains its surrogate and default expected improvement selection, with a bounded testing policy. On two retrospective protocol/media tasks with nearest-neighbor response oracles, it attains higher sample mean final best and search AUC than the matched LLAMBO baseline and evaluated non-LLM baselines. Prose-reflection and EI-only controls show mixed component effects. Six direct-training hyperparameter-optimization tasks show task-dependent transfer, strongest on diabetes SVM; exact-grid and reaction controls examine other settings. FGSS exposes hypotheses, their execution, and cross-round revisions for inspection while supporting search gains in selected settings.

System1-Arena: Which Decisions Can Be Left to System 1?
System1-Arena：哪些决策可以交由系统1处理？
Large language models (LLMs) often make discrete decisions, such as flagging phishing or escalating to a human. System-1 models, such as Jev and the OpenAI Decisions API, return probabilities over options in a single forward pass, while System-2 approaches generate reasoning steps. Which decisions can System-1 models handle, and which require generative reasoning? We introduce System1-Arena, a benchmark that gives all models the same decision requests and scores their probabilities. It contains 92k questions across 105 tasks from 268 public sources, in four groups: text classification and knowledge, applied decisions, evaluation and control, and agent and action. It also includes 138 closed-loop environments, from web shopping and SWE-bench coding control to robotics, games, and driving; in real-time mode, the world evolves while a model decides. We evaluate 30 models: 7 closed LLMs, 13 open LLMs read at the answer token, and 10 System-1 models. The largest System-1 models match or exceed fast closed LLMs in non-agentic accuracy, with better-calibrated probabilities at one-quarter of the latency. A reasoning LLM is the most accurate, at about 70x the cost of a fast LLM. In closed loops, large open LLMs succeed most often (up to 32.9%); small System-1 models rarely succeed.

大语言模型（LLMs）常需做出离散决策，例如标记钓鱼邮件或升级至人工处理。系统1模型（如Jev和OpenAI决策API）在一次前向传播中返回各选项的概率，而系统2方法则生成推理步骤。哪些决策可由系统1模型完成，哪些必须依赖生成式推理？我们引入System1-Arena，一个基准测试，向所有模型提供相同的决策请求，并评分其输出概率。该基准包含来自268个公开来源的105个任务共92,000个问题，分为四类：文本分类与知识、应用决策、评估与控制、智能体与行动。此外还包含138个闭环环境，涵盖网页购物、SWE-bench编码控制、机器人、游戏和驾驶等；在实时模式下，世界随模型决策动态演化。我们评估了30个模型：7个闭源LLM、13个开源LLM在答案token处读取，以及10个系统1模型。最大规模的系统1模型在非智能体准确性上达到甚至超过快速闭源LLM，且延迟仅为后者的四分之一，概率校准更优。生成式LLM最准确，但成本约为快速LLM的70倍。在闭环环境中，大型开源LLM成功率最高（达32.9%）；小型系统1模型极少成功。

What Should We Remove? Evaluating the Reliability and Costs of RLVR Data Decontamination
我们应该移除什么？评估RLVR数据去污染的可靠性与成本
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) math corpora are routinely screened for benchmark overlap, but the reliability and data-loss costs of cleaning policies remain underexamined. We audit sixteen open RLVR math corpora comprising 1,673,514 corpus-specific training items against six benchmarks containing 1,949 questions. We evaluate eight policies based on exact, lexical, and semantic matching using 594 independently and blindly dual-reviewed training-benchmark pairs. Calibration and held-out sets have disjoint benchmark questions and training-example families. A separate review of all 83 candidate matches for 80 sampled training items measures item-level false removal. At cosine threshold 0.80, 52 of 59 sampled removals (88%) were confirmed false removals; this is not a demonstrated population rate. No semantic threshold in our preregistered grid (0.80-0.98) meets the 90% conservative-precision target on calibration. The existing judge-adjudicated policy achieves 98.1% resolved precision and 98.2% candidate-frame recall on held-out corpora. It has zero confirmed false removals among six sampled removals, with one unresolved case; this does not establish zero risk. Within the evaluated candidate frame, this policy offers a more favorable precision-recall balance than the semantic-threshold alternatives tested. Recall does not measure contamination outside the candidate frame. We release reviewed labels, per-policy decisions, and coverage accounting to support evaluation of cleaning reliability and cost rather than overlap counts alone.

基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）数学语料库通常会筛查基准重叠问题，但清洗策略的可靠性和数据损失成本仍缺乏充分研究。我们对包含1,673,514个特定语料训练项的十六个开源RLVR数学语料库进行了审计，这些语料库与包含1,949个问题的六个基准进行对比。基于精确匹配、词法匹配和语义匹配，评估了八种清洗策略，使用594个独立且盲审的训练-基准配对样本。校准集和保留集中的基准问题与训练样本家族互不重叠。对80个采样训练项中所有83个候选匹配项的单独审查，测量了项目级别的误删率。在余弦相似度阈值0.80下，59个采样删除中有52个（88%）被确认为误删；这并非可证明的总体误删率。我们预注册的语义阈值网格（0.80–0.98）中无一达到校准集上90%保守精确度的目标。现有由人工裁判裁定的清洗策略在保留语料库上实现了98.1%的解决精确度和98.2%的候选框架召回率。在六次采样删除中未发现已确认的误删，仅有一例未决；这并不意味着零风险。在所评估的候选框架内，该策略相比测试的语义阈值方案具有更优的精确度-召回权衡。召回率无法衡量候选框架外的污染情况。我们公开了审核标签、各策略决策及覆盖统计，以支持对清洗可靠性与成本的评估，而非仅依赖重叠数量。

Beyond Retrieval Failure: Understanding Why LLMs Miss Evidence in Multi-Hop RAG
超越检索失败：理解大语言模型在多跳RAG中遗漏证据的原因
Multi-hop retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) requires an agent to retrieve evidence for a sequence of dependent sub-questions. When a wrong answer lacks a required passage, the passage may be inaccessible to the retriever, or the agent may have failed to issue a query that would retrieve it. Standard retrieval evaluations conflate these two causes. We study how much apparent retrieval failure is instead caused by search drift: the search no longer tracks an unanswered sub-question. Holding the retriever, corpus, and generation model fixed, we introduce ExecutionRAG, a training-free requirement register that records open sub-questions and their supporting evidence. The register changes search guidance but not evidence access, so each gold passage it recovers certifies that the passage was reachable by the original retriever. On 1,000 MuSiQue questions, the number of wrong answers with unretrieved required evidence falls from 382 to 234 with all-MiniLM-L6-v2 and from 383 to 274 with Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B. These counts correspond to 69% to 44% and 66% to 48% of each system's wrong answers, respectively. Crucially, evidence recovery does not produce a proportional reduction in total error: premature resolutions and abstentions increase as missing-evidence failures decrease. The central finding is therefore a shift in the remaining bottleneck, from guiding search toward required evidence to verifying that the retrieved evidence is sufficient.
中文翻译
多跳检索增强生成（RAG）要求智能体为一系列相互依赖的子问题检索证据。当错误答案缺少必要段落时，可能是检索器无法访问该段落，也可能是智能体未能发出能检索到它的查询。标准检索评估将这两种原因混淆。我们研究发现，看似检索失败实际上更多是由搜索漂移导致：搜索不再追踪未回答的子问题。在固定检索器、语料库和生成模型的前提下，我们引入ExecutionRAG——一种无需训练的需求寄存器，用于记录待解决的子问题及其支持证据。该寄存器改变搜索引导方向，但不影响证据访问，因此其恢复的每一条黄金段落都证明该段落在原始检索器下是可达的。在1,000个MuSiQue问题上，使用all-MiniLM-L6-v2时，所需证据未被检索的错误答案数从382降至234；使用Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B时，从383降至274。这些数字分别占系统错误答案的69%至44%和66%至48%。关键发现是：证据恢复并未带来总错误的成比例下降——随着缺失证据失败减少，过早求解和回避现象增加。因此，核心瓶颈已从引导搜索向所需证据转移，转变为验证所检索证据是否充分。
👤 Jiaqi Wei, Wenxuan Huang, Chengdong Xu, Xiang Zhang, Wanli Ouyang 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

agentic-rlagent-benchmark
Stop Training Against Soft Users: Mentalized Adversarial Self-Play for Proactive Dialogue
停止针对温和用户的训练：面向主动对话的理性化对抗自对弈
Proactive dialogue agents must guide multi-turn interactions toward task goals while adapting to users whose intentions and attitudes evolve. Existing planners improve system side action selection through search, learned policies, or user aware planning, but are often trained and evaluated against fixed or overly compliant simulators; as a result, high simulated success can reflect soft user bias rather than robust dialogue ability. We introduce MASP, a Mentalized Adversarial Self Play framework that treats proactive dialogue as a partially observable game between a system and a rationally resisting user. MASP represents the user's evolving mental state with a BDI based latent state, separates reward side teacher mentalization from policy side student mentalization, and alternates self play updates for the system and user policies. This turns robustness to rational resistance into a training objective rather than only an evaluation condition. Across diverse proactive dialogue benchmarks, compared with the widely used SFT and Planner-only approaches, MASP substantially improves success rate (SR) and rationality constrained success (RCSR), reduces average turns (AT), and performs better against hard users who make the task goal more difficult to achieve.
中文翻译
主动对话智能体必须在多轮交互中引导对话向任务目标前进，同时适应用户意图和态度不断变化的情况。现有规划方法通过搜索、学习策略或用户感知规划来改进系统端的动作选择，但通常是在固定或过于顺从的模拟器上进行训练和评估；因此，高模拟成功率可能反映的是温和用户偏差，而非真正的对话鲁棒性。我们提出了MASP（理性化对抗自对弈）框架，将主动对话视为系统与理性抵抗用户之间的部分可观测博弈。MASP使用基于BDI的潜在状态表示用户不断演变的心理状态，分离奖励端教师的心理建模与策略端学生心理建模，并交替更新系统与用户策略。这使对理性抵抗的鲁棒性成为训练目标，而不仅仅是评估条件。在多个主动对话基准测试中，与广泛使用的SFT和仅规划方法相比，MASP显著提升了成功率（SR）和理性约束成功率（RCSR），减少了平均对话轮次（AT），并在面对使任务目标更难达成的强硬用户时表现更优。

From Observation to Intervention: Distilling Self-Generated In-Context Demonstrations via Executable Causal Intervention
从观察到干预：通过可执行因果干预提炼自生成上下文示范
Self-generated In-Context Learning (ICL) enables LLMs to synthesize their own few-shot demonstrations, often outperforming manual prompts. However, existing methods filter demonstrations by observation, assuming a rationale is high-quality if it leads to the correct output. We show such filtering suffers from \textit{observational equivalence}, whereby both faithful causal rationales and spurious post-hoc rationales can yield same outputs and are statistically indistinguishable from observation alone. Inspired by independent causal mechanisms, we argue that faithful rationales imply \textit{transfer invariance}, in which causal rationales should remain effective across logically similar samples. We instantiate this criterion as \textbf{E}xecutable \textbf{C}ausal \textbf{I}ntervention for \textbf{D}emonstrations \textbf{D}istillation (ECI2D), which abstracts language rationales into executable programs and applies the causal interventions ($do$-operator) on logically similar samples, retaining only those demonstrations that imply transfer invariance. Experiments on math, logical and commonsense reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that ECI2D consistently outperforms observation-based ICL while retaining far fewer demonstrations, with gains exceeding 7 points as the test distribution shifts.
中文翻译
自生成上下文学习（ICL）使大语言模型能够自主合成少量示范，通常优于人工提示。然而，现有方法通过观察筛选示范，假设只要输出正确即为高质量推理。我们指出这种筛选存在 extit{观测等价性}问题，即忠实的因果推理与虚假的事后推理可能产生相同输出，在仅依赖观察的情况下无法区分。受独立因果机制启发，我们认为忠实推理应具备 extit{迁移不变性}，即因果推理应在逻辑相似样本上保持有效性。我们提出ECI2D（可执行因果干预示范提炼）方法，将语言推理抽象为可执行程序，并对逻辑相似样本应用因果干预（$do$-操作），仅保留体现迁移不变性的示范。在数学、逻辑和常识推理基准上的实验表明，ECI2D始终优于基于观察的ICL，且所需示范数量大幅减少，当测试分布发生变化时性能提升超过7个百分点。
👤 Minming Ye, Min Liu, Haoang Chi, Ciheng Wu, Naiyang Guan 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

CoSTA: Compressed Speech Token Adaptation for Efficient Spoken Dialogue Models
CoSTA：用于高效语音对话模型的压缩语音标记适配
Spoken Dialogue Models (SDMs) typically share a speech-token space for generation and synthesis, leading to long autoregressive sequences and costly inference. Lower-rate speech tokenizers shorten sequences but may introduce compression-quality trade-offs and costly replacement without breaking this coupling. To address this limitation, we propose CoSTA, a compatibility-preserving post-hoc framework that decouples generation and synthesis token spaces while retaining the original speech tokenizer and decoder. CoSTA extends the generation token space with compressed representations via mean-initialized vocabulary extension, pronunciation adaptation, and dialogue adaptation, followed by deterministic expansion back to the original speech-token space before synthesis. On bilingual speech-dialogue benchmarks, CoSTA achieves approximately $1.7\times$ expanded speech-token generation speed, lower full-response latency, and higher throughput while preserving response quality. Analyses show more sustained interaction under limited budgets and a practical trade-off among compression, decoding efficiency, and quality. Further experiments demonstrate cross-architecture transfer and complementarity to low-rate tokenization.
中文翻译
语音对话模型（SDMs）通常共享生成与合成的语音标记空间，导致长自回归序列和高昂的推理成本。低速率语音标记器虽可缩短序列，但可能引入压缩质量权衡，且替换过程代价高昂，仍无法打破这一耦合。为解决此问题，我们提出CoSTA——一种兼容性保持的后处理框架，解耦生成与合成的标记空间，同时保留原始语音标记器和解码器。CoSTA通过均值初始化词汇扩展、发音适应和对话适应，扩展生成标记空间以包含压缩表示，随后在合成前确定性地展开回原始语音标记空间。在双语语音对话基准上，CoSTA实现了约1.7倍的语音标记生成速度提升，降低完整响应延迟，提高吞吐量，同时保持响应质量。分析显示，在有限预算下能实现更持续的交互，并在压缩、解码效率与质量之间提供实用权衡。进一步实验表明其具备跨架构迁移能力，并可与低速率标记化互补。

Compression Does Not End at Selection: Post-Selection Positional Geometry for Sparse KV Caches
压缩并非止于选择：稀疏KV缓存的后选择位置几何结构
Sparse KV compression reduces long-context inference costs by retaining selected historical states, yet these states typically keep their coordinates from the full history. Under Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE), gaps left by evicted tokens continue to affect query-key matching, potentially limiting the use of retained evidence. We study post-selection positional geometry on a fixed selector-produced cache, examining how positional organization affects the use of retained information and which coordinate and continuation rules improve answer quality. To reorganize this geometry, we propose RoPERelay, which assigns compact, order-preserving RoPE coordinates, relocates cached key phases through inverse rotation and re-rotation, and synchronizes the current query and subsequent generation. It reuses existing K/V without renewed selection, historical re-prefill, or additional cache budget. Across 36 comparisons spanning two models, six sparse selectors, and three long-term memory tasks, RoPERelay improves answer quality in 32 cases. For Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct with BM25 on LongMemEval-S, accuracy increases from 23.6% to 35.4%. Measured complete-request time overhead is approximately 1%-3% in the evaluated configurations. These findings establish positional organization as a distinct optimization dimension for sparse KV caches after content selection.
中文翻译
稀疏KV压缩通过保留选定的历史状态来降低长上下文推理成本，但这些状态通常保留其来自完整历史的坐标。在旋转位置嵌入（RoPE）下，被剔除标记留下的空隙仍会影响查询-键匹配，可能限制保留证据的有效使用。我们研究了由固定选择器生成的缓存中的后选择位置几何结构，考察位置组织如何影响保留信息的利用，以及哪些坐标与延续规则能提升答案质量。为重新组织该几何结构，我们提出RoPERelay，它分配紧凑且顺序保持的RoPE坐标，通过逆旋转与再旋转重新定位缓存的键相位，并同步当前查询与后续生成。该方法无需重新选择、历史重填充或额外缓存预算即可复用现有K/V。在涵盖两个模型、六个稀疏选择器和三个长期记忆任务的36组对比中，RoPERelay在32种情况下提升了答案质量。以Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct在LongMemEval-S上使用BM25为例，准确率从23.6%提升至35.4%。在所评估配置中，完整请求时间开销约为1%-3%。这些发现确立了内容选择后的位置组织是稀疏KV缓存的一个独立优化维度。

ToolBottleneckBench: Measuring Tool-Agent Performance under Controlled Pressures and Fixed Harness Budgets
ToolBottleneckBench：在受控压力与固定工具集预算下测量工具智能体性能
The success score of a tool agent reflects both its model and the budgets of the harness that runs it. We present ToolBottleneckBench, a benchmark that measures tool-agent performance under controlled pressures and fixed harness budgets. Synthetic enterprise tasks stay fixed while one pressure varies: the registered tool catalog, the visible tool menu, the certified workflow, or the encoding of returned records. An exact grader records the first unmet obligation of each failed episode and the harness limit, if any, that ended it. Across 21 model and harness configurations, the visible menu is the only one of the three pressures that act on the model with a large penalty, and this penalty tracks truncation at the completion cap. Raising the cap sixteenfold removes most of the penalty in the truncation-heavy configurations. Most failures end at a harness limit, and the failure record identifies the assigned pressure no more reliably than a count of tool calls. The repairs that help change which tools reach the model or who issues the calls, and only two repairs, neither improving model behavior, beat every configuration switch. We recommend reporting pressure effects with the harness budgets under which they were measured. We release the benchmark at https://github.com/zichuanxu/tool-bottleneck-bench.
中文翻译
工具智能体的成功评分不仅反映其模型能力，还取决于运行它的工具集预算。我们提出了ToolBottleneckBench，一个在受控压力和固定工具集预算下衡量工具智能体性能的基准测试。合成的企业任务保持不变，而单一压力因素变化：注册的工具目录、可见的工具菜单、认证的工作流程或返回记录的编码方式。精确的评分器记录每个失败实验中首次未满足的义务以及导致终止的工具集限制（如有）。在21种模型与工具集配置下，只有可见菜单这一压力因素对模型产生显著惩罚，且该惩罚与完成上限处的截断行为相关。将上限提高十六倍可消除大多数截断密集型配置中的惩罚。多数失败发生在工具集限制处，失败记录中识别出的压力因素并不比工具调用次数更可靠。能够改善表现的修复措施仅改变哪些工具到达模型或由谁发起调用，且仅有两种修复措施有效，但均未提升模型行为本身。我们建议报告压力效应时应注明其测量所用的工具集预算。我们已在https://github.com/zichuanxu/tool-bottleneck-bench发布该基准。
👤 Zichuan Xu, Yuyang Dong, Makoto Onizuka, Chuan Xiao 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

PremiseCheck: Efficient Fact-Checking of Human and AI Peer Reviews
PremiseCheck：高效的事实核查人类与AI同行评审
Peer review is under strain from the growth of submissions, and review comments written by humans or AI can misstate what a paper says. We introduce ReviewFact, a benchmark that labels such factual errors in review comments under one definition across NLP, machine learning, and the natural sciences, and that measures cost and latency alongside accuracy. A frontier LLM checks comments well but must read the whole paper for each check, while cheap decision models, which score whether a statement holds in a text, fall far short on whole comments that mix several implicit assertions. PremiseCheck is a framework that combines the two. An LLM rewrites each comment as explicit premises without reading the paper, and a decision model checks each premise against the paper. When each comment is checked on its own, PremiseCheck reaches accuracy comparable to GPT-5.5 across all three fields at about half its cost, and on NLP reviews at a quarter of its cost and less than half its latency.
中文翻译
随着投稿数量的增长，同行评审正面临压力，而人类或AI撰写的评审意见可能歪曲论文内容。我们引入ReviewFact，一个在自然语言处理、机器学习和自然科学领域统一定义下标注评审意见中事实错误的基准测试，并同时衡量成本与延迟。前沿大模型虽能良好检查评审意见，但需阅读整篇论文才能完成每次检查；而廉价的决策模型虽能判断文本中某陈述是否成立，但在混合多个隐含断言的完整评审意见上表现远不及。PremiseCheck是一个结合两者的框架：大模型将每条评审意见重写为明确的前提，无需阅读论文；随后由决策模型逐一核对每个前提与原文。当单独检查每条意见时，PremiseCheck在三个领域内达到与GPT-5.5相当的准确率，成本约为其一半，在NLP评审上成本仅为四分之一，延迟不足一半。

ReviewPilot: A Multi-Agent System for Screening and Data Extraction in Systematic Reviews
ReviewPilot：一种用于系统性综述中筛选与数据提取的多智能体系统
Systematic literature reviews follow the PRISMA process of screening scholarly works and extracting evidence from included studies. Deep research tools based on large language models (LLMs) can search and summarize the literature but do not record which studies are excluded or where extracted values come from. We develop a conversational multi-agent system called ReviewPilot that automates the PRISMA workflow from paper screening to information extraction. Researchers can tailor criteria and schemas through dialogue, and a project memory keeps their configurations and revisions. Each screening decision comes with its reason and each extracted value with its source. On epidemiological expert reviews, ReviewPilot outperforms a single-prompt LLM and AgentSLR across three LLMs, with screening macro F1 up to 0.776 and extraction F1 up to 0.788. Case studies in biomedicine and human-computer interaction show close agreement with human annotators, suggesting that ReviewPilot can transfer beyond epidemiology. Code is available at https://github.com/ZhaoqianXue/ReviewPilot.

Learning to Read Out: Unembedding Dynamics in Language Model Pretraining
学会读出：语言模型预训练中解嵌入动态的研究
When does a language model acquire a capability? Developmental studies usually answer this by tracking when models start producing the correct token. But that prediction passes through the unembedding matrix, or readout, which is itself still being learned during pretraining. We show that a model's hidden states can encode task information before its own readout expresses it, so timelines read from outputs conflate what the model encodes with what its readout expresses. To track the readout directly, we introduce parameter-trajectory crosscoding, which fits one sparse dictionary to every token's readout row at every checkpoint. Unlike crosscoders on activations, it needs only checkpoint weights. We use it to locate a narrow early window, 0.4-1.1% of the way through Pythia's pretraining, in which the readout reorganizes and WordNet word categories become decodable from its rows. By then, linear probes on the hidden states already decode task information that the model's own outputs do not yet show. To test whether such gaps depend on how fast the readout learns, we pretrain small models from scratch that differ only in the readout's learning rate. We find that raising it makes the readout develop earlier and the gap close sooner, with little effect on final loss. Together, these results imply that early in training, where many developmental transitions are dated, a behavioral date can reflect when the readout learns to express task information and not when the model learns it.
中文翻译
语言模型何时获得某种能力？发展性研究通常通过观察模型开始生成正确标记的时间来回答。但这一预测经过了未嵌入矩阵（即读出层），而该读出层本身在预训练过程中仍在学习。我们发现，模型的隐藏状态可以在其自身读出表达之前就编码任务信息，因此仅从输出推断的时间线混淆了模型编码的信息与读出层表达的信息。为直接追踪读出过程，我们引入参数轨迹交叉编码方法，为每个检查点上的每个标记的读出行拟合一个稀疏字典。与基于激活的交叉编码器不同，该方法仅需检查点权重。利用该方法，我们定位到Pythia预训练早期的一个狭窄窗口（0.4%-1.1%处），此时读出层重新组织，WordNet词类可从其行中解码。此时，对隐藏状态的线性探测已能解码任务信息，而模型自身的输出尚未体现。为测试这种差距是否依赖于读出层的学习速度，我们从头预训练小型模型，仅改变读出层的学习率。结果表明，提高学习率可使读出层更早发展，缩短差距，且对最终损失影响甚微。这些结果表明，在训练早期（许多发展性转变常被归于此阶段），行为时间点反映的是读出层学会表达任务信息的时间，而非模型真正学会的时间。
👤 Matteo He, William F. Shen, Alex Iacob, Andrej Jovanovic, Xinchi Qiu et al. 📅 2026-10-14 🔗 arXiv

Same Context, Different Agents: A Behavioral Study of Semantic Layers, Orchestration and Compaction in Data Agents
相同上下文，不同智能体：数据智能体中语义层、编排与压缩的行为研究
Answering questions over heterogeneous data means combining evidence from files, databases, documents and the web. Data agents automate this, but their success depends on context. Three context-engineering techniques (CETs) help by shaping it: semantic layers describe the data, orchestration splits work among agents, and compaction condenses interaction history. Existing studies validate each CET alone, leaving open how agent behavior shapes its effect. We present a comprehensive analysis of the three CETs, alone and combined, with seven large language models (LLMs) on 1,514 long-horizon tasks from five benchmarks, covering behavior, accuracy and cost. We find that data agents without CETs form three behavior groups: investigators, premature answerers that answer without inspecting data, and early stoppers that give up. Each CET delivers its known benefit, from more investigation to smaller prompts, but only the behavior group it matches turns that benefit into accuracy, such as premature answerers under orchestration. For investigators, the same CETs can hurt: the semantic layer stops them from looking, orchestration makes them overconfident, costing 5 percentage points, and compaction makes them repeat steps. Combining CETs yields smaller gains and losses than their sum. We derive guidance for choosing CETs by agent behavior, task demands and accuracy-cost trade-offs.
中文翻译
针对异构数据回答问题需要整合来自文件、数据库、文档和网络的证据。数据智能体自动化这一过程，但其成功取决于上下文质量。三种上下文工程技巧（CETs）有助于优化上下文：语义层描述数据结构，编排将任务分配给多个智能体，压缩则精简交互历史。现有研究分别验证了每种CET的效果，但未揭示智能体行为如何影响其效果。我们对这三种CET进行了全面分析，单独及组合使用，基于七种大型语言模型在五个基准测试的1514个长周期任务上展开，涵盖行为、准确率和成本。我们发现，未使用CET的数据智能体形成三类行为模式：调查者、过早作答者（未检查数据即作答）和提前放弃者（中途放弃）。每种CET均带来其已知优势（如更多调查、更短提示），但只有与之匹配的行为组才能将优势转化为准确率提升，例如过早作答者在编排下表现更好。而对于调查者，相同的CET反而有害：语义层使其停止探索，编排使其过度自信，导致准确率下降5个百分点；压缩则使其重复步骤。组合使用CETs带来的增益和损失小于各自独立作用之和。我们据此提出根据智能体行为、任务需求和准确率-成本权衡来选择CET的指导原则。
👤 Zhivar Sourati, Marianne Menglin Liu, Zheng Wang, Rongguang Wang, Sai Ashish Somayajula et al. 📅 2026-10-14 🔗 arXiv

Prune the Summary, Not the History: Source-Judged Compaction for Long-Horizon Agents
修剪摘要，而非历史：面向长时程智能体的源判断压缩
Long-horizon LLM agents rely on context compaction: when the interaction history outgrows the context window, a compressor replaces it with a natural-language summary. We show that these summaries contain more than a record of what happened. Compaction prompts ask for decisions and next steps, so each summary mixes facts established by the environment with reasoning written by the compressor, and the agent cannot tell the two apart afterwards. In an analysis of a widely used structured compressor, 83% of failed runs trace back to an action that carries out a specific summary entry, most of which no environment output supports. We propose Source-Judged Compaction (SJC), a training-free post-processing step that scores each summary entry for whether it has a source in the environment outputs or the previous summary, and removes the entries that do not. It changes neither the agent nor the compressor and adds one inexpensive judge request per compaction. The judge is Jev, a non-generative decision model that answers the source question with a calibrated probability instead of text. On AppWorld and OfficeBench with two agent models, SJC improves cross-run reliability over the underlying compressor by 2.1%-8.9% without increasing the agent's token usage.
中文翻译
长时程大语言模型智能体依赖于上下文压缩：当交互历史超出上下文窗口时，压缩器会用自然语言摘要替代。我们发现这些摘要不仅记录了发生的事，还包含压缩器生成的推理内容。压缩提示要求输出决策和下一步行动，导致每个摘要混合了环境确定的事实与压缩器编写的推理，智能体事后无法区分两者。对一个广泛使用的结构化压缩器的分析显示，83%的失败运行可追溯至执行特定摘要条目所采取的动作，而这些条目大多缺乏环境输出的支持。我们提出源判断压缩（SJC），一种无需训练的后处理步骤，通过评分判断每条摘要条目是否源自环境输出或前序摘要，并移除无来源的条目。该方法不改变智能体或压缩器，每次压缩仅增加一次低成本的判断请求。判断模型Jev是一个非生成性决策模型，以校准概率回答来源问题而非生成文本。在AppWorld和OfficeBench上使用两种智能体模型进行测试，SJC在不增加智能体token使用量的前提下，使底层压缩器的跨运行可靠性提升2.1%-8.9%。

CoTranslator: Reverse to Forward Chain-of-Thought Translator for Reasoning Distillation
CoTranslator：用于推理蒸馏的反向到正向思维链翻译器
Reasoning distillation requires chain-of-thought (CoT) data with correct answers, but obtaining such data efficiently remains challenging. Forward CoT generation (FCG) followed by rejection sampling (RS) produces verified, question-only responses, yet may require many teacher calls for difficult problems. Reverse CoT generation (RCG) is cheaper because it conditions on the known answer, but access to this answer can lead to post-hoc rationalization and a mismatch with the student's answer-free inference setting. We propose CoTranslator, which learns to translate reverse CoT responses toward the distribution of verified forward responses. Trained on a small set of paired reverse and rejection-sampled forward responses, it supports large-scale generation and verification of student training data. Across six student models evaluated on six mathematics benchmarks, CoTranslator improves average accuracy over semantic suppression by up to $5.82$ percentage points. For two 4B students, it retains $95.0\%$ of rejection sampling's average accuracy while using $35.9\%$ of its estimated compute. Without retraining, the same translator transfers to a new mathematics dataset, a different teacher, and STEM problems, improving average STEM accuracy by $4.88$ and $4.31$ percentage points for 4B and 8B students, respectively. These results show that CoTranslator offers an efficient and transferable approach to reasoning distillation.
中文翻译
推理蒸馏需要带有正确答案的思维链（CoT）数据，但高效获取此类数据仍具挑战性。正向思维链生成（FCG）结合拒绝采样（RS）可产生经验证的仅含问题的回答，但对难题可能需要大量教师调用。反向思维链生成（RCG）成本更低，因其基于已知答案进行条件生成，但访问答案可能导致事后合理化，并与学生模型无答案推理设置不匹配。我们提出CoTranslator，学习将反向思维链响应翻译为符合经验证正向响应分布的结果。在少量配对的反向与拒绝采样正向响应上训练后，它支持大规模生成和验证学生训练数据。在六个数学基准上评估六名学生模型，CoTranslator相比语义抑制方法，平均准确率最高提升5.82个百分点。对于两个4B规模的学生模型，其平均准确率保留了拒绝采样95.0%的水平，同时仅使用其约35.9%的估算计算资源。无需重新训练，同一翻译器可迁移至新数学数据集、不同教师及STEM问题，分别使4B和8B学生模型的平均STEM准确率提升4.88和4.31个百分点。结果表明，CoTranslator提供了一种高效且可迁移的推理蒸馏方法。

PRISM: Transfer and Limits of Activation Interventions in Tool-Calling Language Models
PRISM：工具调用语言模型中激活干预的迁移性与局限性
Recent literature shows that unsafe tool calls can often accompany textual refusals in language models. To investigate this hidden vulnerability, we introduce PRISM, a framework for evaluating the transfer of activation interventions across precision, languages, and scales. Our findings demonstrate that leveraging lower-resource settings (specifically quantized NF4 labels and cross-lingual transfer) offers a robust pathway to expose and improve model alignment. Notably, using behavioral labels from an NF4 copy enables stable intervention estimation even with very few scenarios. Furthermore, these alignment improvements transfer effectively across languages: five-language NF4-supervised directions successfully transfer to held-out Arabic, reducing unsafe violations in Llama-3.1-8B by 21.95 percentage points without target-language tuning. By mapping paired benign activations, we also successfully transfer these safety interventions to much larger models (up to 70B parameters). Ultimately, PRISM highlights how auxiliary supervision from compressed models and multilingual data can uncover and mitigate hazardous tool-calling behaviors across different model families. We release all experimental code and supporting artifacts at https://github.com/GiKerche/prism, alongside an interactive explorer of recorded model responses and experimental results at https://gikerche.github.io/prism/.
中文翻译
近期文献表明，语言模型中的文本拒绝行为常伴随不安全的工具调用。为探究这一隐藏漏洞，我们提出了PRISM框架，用于评估激活干预在精度、语言和规模之间的迁移能力。研究发现，利用低资源设置（特别是量化后的NF4标签和跨语言迁移）可有效暴露并改进模型对齐。值得注意的是，使用NF4副本的行为标签即使在极少场景下也能实现稳定的干预估计。此外，这些对齐改进在跨语言间具有良好的迁移性：五种语言的NF4监督方向成功迁移到未见的阿拉伯语，使Llama-3.1-8B模型的不安全违规减少21.95个百分点，且无需目标语言微调。通过映射成对的良性激活，我们还将这些安全干预成功迁移至更大规模模型（高达700亿参数）。最终，PRISM揭示了来自压缩模型和多语言数据的辅助监督如何在不同模型家族中发现并缓解危险的工具调用行为。所有实验代码及相关资源已发布于https://github.com/GiKerche/prism，同时提供交互式模型响应与实验结果探索页面https://gikerche.github.io/prism。

Reference-Guided Turn-Taking Style Transfer for Full-Duplex Spoken Language Models
参考引导的全双工语音语言模型对话轮次风格迁移
Full-duplex spoken language models decide at every frame whether to take the floor, defining a conversational personality -- yet each set of weights encodes exactly one, which human speakers bracket rather than share. Existing controls mostly act from outside; none identifies the quantity that sets how much the model speaks. We identify it: a single late-layer residual-stream direction, the initiative vector, extracted from the model's own conversations without training or annotation. The same direction reads the decision, anticipating onsets up to 800 ms ahead, and shifts it: scaled during generation, it moves speech ratio from 6-12% to 42-61% (no steering: 21-30%), consistently across conversations, weights, and corpora. A short reference recording suffices to transfer a speaker's turn-taking style to the model, and the transfer is reference-specific: swapping in another speaker's reference worsens the match in 93-98% of cases, whereas the best uniform dose is indistinguishable from no steering. Turn-taking style is not frozen into the weights but a direction that can be read, shifted, and calibrated per person. Audio examples are available at https://elpsykongloo.github.io/initiative-vector/.
中文翻译
全双工语音语言模型在每一帧决定是否抢话，从而定义对话个性——但每组权重仅编码一种风格，而人类说话者通常交替而非共享。现有控制方法大多依赖外部干预；尚无方法识别决定模型发言比例的核心变量。我们识别出该变量：一个单一的晚期层残差流方向，即“主动性向量”，从模型自身对话中提取，无需训练或标注。该方向可读取决策，提前约800毫秒预测发言起始，并在生成过程中通过缩放实现调整：使发言比例从6–12%提升至42–61%（无引导时为21–30%），在各类对话、权重和语料库中保持一致。仅需一段简短参考录音，即可将某说话者的轮次风格迁移至模型，且迁移具有参考特异性：更换另一说话者的参考录音会使匹配度下降93–98%的情况，而最佳统一剂量与无引导效果无异。轮次风格并非固化于权重中，而是可读取、可调节、可按人校准的方向。

Does Personalization Break Trust? Open-Set Rejection Calibration Under Few-Shot On-Device Intent Adaptation
个性化会破坏信任吗？少样本本地意图适应下的开集拒绝校准
Always-on virtual assistants increasingly personalize on-device to new user-specific commands via few-shot fine-tuning (e.g., LoRA), but this adaptation is evaluated almost exclusively on whether it learns the new commands, not on whether the assistant can still recognize an input that matches none of its intents - the out-of-scope (OOS) rejection problem. We show that naive few-shot LoRA personalization degrades OOS-rejection AUROC (area under the ROC curve) on every one of five public intent-classification benchmarks (mean drop $0.055$--$0.258$, five seeds each), while in-scope accuracy is preserved. A scorer-invariant check shows part of this drop is an artifact of the standard confidence scorer's own miscalibration under adaptation, though a smaller, genuine effect remains under any scorer; we trace it to a measurable shrinkage of the embedding margin around known-intent regions (pooled Spearman rho=0.61$). A deployment-cheap fix is scoring rejection by Mahalanobis distance instead of softmax confidence without extra training/retraining recovers most of the lost quality on 3 of 5 datasets at no accuracy cost, beats two further OOD scoring baselines, and generalizes across shot count, LoRA rank, and a second base encoder. We report honestly where the fix falls short and release all code for reproducibility.
中文翻译
始终在线的虚拟助手越来越多地通过少样本微调（如LoRA）在设备端进行个性化以适应新用户指令，但此类适应几乎仅评估是否学会新命令，而未关注助手能否仍识别与所有意图都不匹配的输入——即“范围外”（OOS）拒绝问题。我们发现，简单的少样本LoRA个性化在五个公开意图分类基准上均导致OOS拒绝AUROC下降（平均降幅0.055–0.258，五次种子测试），而内部准确率得以保持。一项评分不变性检查表明，部分下降源于标准置信度评分器在适应下的自身校准偏差，尽管即便在任何评分器下仍存在较小但真实的负面影响；我们将其归因于已知意图区域嵌入边距的可测量缩小（合并Spearman相关系数=0.61）。一种部署成本低廉的解决方案是改用马氏距离而非Softmax置信度进行拒绝评分，无需额外训练即可在三个数据集上恢复大部分丢失的质量，优于两种其他OOD评分基线，并在样本数量、LoRA秩及第二基础编码器之间具有良好泛化性。我们如实报告该方案的局限性，并发布全部代码以供复现。

CoTS: Competitive Token Selection for Latent Reasoning in Large Language Models
CoTS：大语言模型中潜在推理的竞争力标记选择
Recent latent reasoning methods move beyond discrete Chain-of-Thought (CoT) by replacing sampled tokens with continuous soft embeddings or hidden states, enabling richer intermediate representations and preserving alternative reasoning paths. However, the need for latent reasoning varies across decoding steps: confident predictions favor discrete commitment, whereas ambiguous steps benefit from preserving multiple plausible alternatives. Existing approaches typically handle this variation through either global soft activation or hard switching based on uncertainty thresholds, treating latent intervention as an all-or-nothing decision rather than continuously adapting its strength. To address this limitation, we propose Competitive Token Selection (CoTS), a training-free framework that reframes latent intervention from binary mode selection into continuous allocation at the token level. CoTS uses the probability competition between the two most likely candidates to dynamically balance discrete commitment and soft exploration at each reasoning step. The second candidate represents the strongest alternative to the model's preferred continuation, so the separation between the two directly reflects the stability of the current discrete choice. When one candidate clearly dominates, CoTS keeps the representation close to the sampled discrete token to preserve a stable reasoning trajectory; as the competition strengthens, it increases the contribution of the soft representation to retain plausible alternatives. This competition-based allocation requires neither manually tuned switching thresholds nor mixing coefficients, and introduces no additional forward passes. Experiments across six reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that CoTS consistently outperforms standard CoT and state-of-the-art training-free latent reasoning methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/OkinoYu/CoTSReasoning.
中文翻译
最近的潜在推理方法超越了离散的思维链（CoT），通过用连续的软嵌入或隐藏状态替换采样的标记，实现了更丰富的中间表示并保留了多种可能的推理路径。然而，不同解码步骤对潜在推理的需求不同：信心充足的预测倾向于离散承诺，而模糊的步骤则受益于保留多个合理备选方案。现有方法通常通过全局软激活或基于不确定性阈值的硬切换来处理这种变化，将潜在干预视为非此即彼的决策，而非持续调整其强度。为解决这一局限性，我们提出了一种无需训练的框架——竞争力标记选择（CoTS），将潜在干预从二元模式选择重构为在标记层面的连续分配。CoTS利用两个最可能候选项之间的概率竞争，在每个推理步骤中动态平衡离散承诺与软探索。第二个候选项代表模型首选延续的最强替代方案，因此两者之间的分离直接反映了当前离散选择的稳定性。当一个候选项明显占优时，CoTS保持表示接近采样的离散标记，以维持稳定的推理轨迹；随着竞争加剧，它增加软表示的贡献以保留合理的备选方案。这种基于竞争的分配无需手动调参的切换阈值或混合系数，且不引入额外前向传播。在六个推理基准上的实验表明，CoTS始终优于标准CoT和最先进的无训练潜在推理方法。代码已公开于 https://github.com/OkinoYu/CoTSReasoning。
👤 Jie Yu, Yuxi Sun, Zhaoxu Li, Chunxiao Liu, Xudong Jiang 📅 2026-10-08 🔗 arXiv

AbridgeKV: Compressing Long Reasoning into the KV Cache of Learned Pause Tokens
AbridgeKV：将长推理压缩至学习暂停标记的KV缓存中
Long chain-of-thought decoding is memory-bound: the KV cache of the generated tokens grows linearly with the test-time compute being spent. Evicting cache entries does not fix this, because a model never trained on a cache with entries removed generates longer, often up to the length limit. We instead train the model to compress its own output. AbridgeKV compresses each completed block of generated text into the KV entries of a few learned pause tokens. These are extra input tokens with trained embeddings that produce no output. Once the pause tokens have encoded the block, AbridgeKV frees the block's original cache; subsequent tokens attend to the pause tokens' KV entries in its place. Three novel choices make this work: the pause tokens' KV entries take positions inside the block they replace, the cache budget is allocated across layers according to the measured value of each layer's entries, and compression operates on relatively large blocks. On 446 competition problems from AIME, HMMT, AMC, BRUMO, CMIMC and SMT with 32K-token generations, keeping a quarter of the generated cache matches an identically trained full-KV model in accuracy and output length; the strongest published evictor at a matched budget writes far longer, and the closest trained compressor, retrained on the same data, loses 10 points. Served in vLLM, the cache is 3.5x smaller per sequence and a saturated H100 finishes a problem 3.2x faster than with full KV.
中文翻译
长思维链解码是内存受限的：生成标记的KV缓存随测试时计算量线性增长。清除缓存条目无法解决此问题，因为从未在移除条目的缓存上训练过的模型会生成更长的输出，通常达到长度限制。我们转而训练模型压缩自身输出。AbridgeKV将每个已完成的生成文本块压缩到少数学习得到的暂停标记的KV条目中。这些是带有训练嵌入的额外输入标记，不产生任何输出。一旦暂停标记编码了该块，AbridgeKV便释放原始缓存；后续标记则改由暂停标记的KV条目进行注意力计算。三个新设计使该方法有效：暂停标记的KV条目占据其替换块内的位置，缓存预算根据各层条目的实际价值跨层分配，且压缩操作作用于相对较大的块。在来自AIME、HMMT、AMC、BRUMO、CMIMC和SMT的446道竞赛题上，使用32K标记生成，仅保留四分之一的生成缓存即可达到与全KV缓存模型相同的准确率和输出长度；在相同预算下，最强的公开淘汰策略写得远更长，而最近训练的压缩器在相同数据上重新训练后损失10分。在vLLM中部署时，每序列缓存缩小3.5倍，饱和的H100完成一个问题的速度快3.2倍。
👤 Jack Cai, Lijie Yang, Vasilis Kontonis, Dimitris Papailiopoulos, Shivam Garg 📅 2026-10-07 🔗 arXiv

Exploring the Design Space of Permutation Diffusion LLMs
探索排列扩散语言模型的设计空间
Permutation generation with language models requires both text-conditioned ranking and a global one-to-one assignment between candidates and output positions. We study this problem through three diffusion-LLM parameterizations: hard-assignment prediction over shuffled candidate-index sequences, soft-assignment prediction over doubly stochastic matrices, and direct ordering-score prediction followed by sorting. The three families place permutation constraints at different stages of prediction and decoding and therefore support different training objectives and sampling procedures. We evaluate these designs on recommendation reranking across three Amazon domains with candidate sets of size 10 and 20. At one function evaluation, the hard- and soft-assignment variants achieve Recall@1 comparable to a constrained masked-diffusion baseline, whereas direct ordering-score prediction guarantees valid permutations without Hungarian assignment or Sinkhorn normalization. Additional function evaluations do not consistently improve ranking quality, and their effect depends strongly on the state representation and training objective. These results show that corruption, prediction parameterization, objective, and decoder should be designed jointly for permutation diffusion with language-model denoisers.
中文翻译
使用语言模型进行排列生成需要同时满足文本条件排序和候选项与输出位置之间的全局一一对应关系。我们通过三种扩散-语言模型参数化方式研究该问题：在打乱的候选索引序列上进行硬分配预测、在双随机矩阵上进行软分配预测，以及直接预测排序分数后进行排序。这三类方法在预测和解码的不同阶段施加排列约束，因此支持不同的训练目标和采样过程。我们在三个亚马逊领域上评估这些设计，候选集大小分别为10和20。在一次函数评估中，硬分配和软分配变体的Recall@1可与受约束的掩码扩散基线相当，而直接排序分数预测无需匈牙利算法或Sinkhorn归一化即可保证有效排列。额外的函数评估并未一致提升排名质量，其效果强烈依赖于状态表示和训练目标。这些结果表明，在使用语言模型去噪器进行排列扩散时，应联合设计扰动、预测参数化、目标和解码器。
👤 Zhe Xu, Mingfu Liang, Kavosh Asadi, Jean-Pascal Billaud, Tianyi Chen et al. 📅 2026-10-01 🔗 arXiv

Jev-as-a-Judge for RAG Claim Verification: Confidence-Gated Cascading Between a Non-Generative Verifier and a Frontier LLM
Jev-as-a-Judge用于RAG声明验证：非生成验证器与前沿大模型间的置信度门控级联
Retrieval-augmented generation asks one question at volume: does this evidence support this claim? The answer is one bit, yet most pipelines obtain it from a text generator and pay generation prices for the prose wrapped around it. We compare a non-generative decision model, which returns a typed value from a declared enumeration together with a native probability and no text, against a frontier LLM configured as an engineer would deploy it for binary classification. Both answer the same 495 human-labelled claims from LLM-AggreFact, so every comparison is within-item. We report three things. First, the two systems are statistically indistinguishable on the metric this task is conventionally scored by, a paired difference of -0.56 points, Jev minus Astra, at a 187-fold cost difference. Second, that tie carries almost no information about deployment: the systems disagree on 15.2% of claims, and the cheaper one admits 23.2% of the claims annotators marked unsupported against the frontier model's 14.1%. A component whose job is to catch unsupported claims should be judged on that rate, and the conventional headline hides it. Third, because the errors differ they compose: routing the claims the cheap model is confident about and escalating the rest reaches 74.10 at USD 2.92 per thousand claims against 73.83 at USD 8.93, with the threshold chosen out of fold. But a matched random-escalation control at in-sample thresholds, spending the same budget by coin flip, does as well, so what buys the saving is the escalation share and not the probability that picks what to escalate. Measured against the cheap model it is built from, the cascade costs 61-fold more for 0.83 points, 95% CI [-3.36, 4.86], and it is the more permissive guard of the two. Equal accuracy conceals unequal errors. All predictions, prompts, pricing and code are released.
中文翻译
检索增强生成一次性提出大量问题：这些证据是否支持该声明？答案仅为一个比特，但大多数流水线仍通过文本生成器获取，并为此付出冗长叙述的代价。我们比较了一个非生成决策模型（返回预定义枚举中的类型值及原生概率，无文本）与一个配置为工程师部署方式的前沿大模型（用于二分类）。两者均回答来自LLM-AggreFact的495个经人工标注的声明，因此每次比较均为同项目内。我们报告三点发现：第一，两种系统在该任务常规评分指标上统计上无差异，Jev减去Astra的配对差值为-0.56分，但成本相差187倍；第二，这种平局几乎不反映部署情况：两系统在15.2%的声明上存在分歧，且廉价模型接受23.2%的标注者标记为不支持的声明，而前沿模型仅为14.1%。一个负责捕捉不支持声明的组件应以此拒绝率来评判，而传统标题掩盖了这一点；第三，由于错误模式不同，二者可组合：将廉价模型有把握的声明路由，其余升级处理，可实现74.10分，每千条仅花费2.92美元，而对比方案为73.83分，花费8.93美元（阈值在外部选取）。但使用相同预算的随机升级控制组（按抛硬币决定）表现相当，因此节省成本的关键在于升级比例，而非概率判断哪些需升级。相对于其构建基础的廉价模型，该级联系统成本高出61倍，仅提升0.83分（95%置信区间[-3.36, 4.86]），且是两个系统中更为宽松的守门人。同等准确率掩盖了不等的错误。所有预测、提示、定价和代码均已公开。

MeddeX: A Retrieval-Augmented LLaMA-3 System with Gemini Vision for Evidence-Backed Medical Report Analysis

Modern healthcare generates increasingly complex medical reports that challenge clinical professionals in terms of time and interpretive burden. Manual review processes are slow, error-prone, and difficult to scale. This paper presents MeddeX, a Medical Report Analyzer that combines a QLoRA fine-tuned LLaMA-3 (8B) model with Retrieval-Augmented Gen eration (RAG) and a multimodal Gemini Vision layer to deliver accurate, concise, and evidence-backed summaries of medical documents. Beyond single-report Q&A, MeddeX introduces report scoped retrieval for precise contextual grounding, an automated longitudinal report comparison engine, and a self-healing backfill pipeline that lazily processes legacy documents on demand. A FastAPI backend, Supabase vector store, and Next.js fron tend complete the end-to-end architecture. PRISM: PRIvileged Sibling-guided Multi-Agent Topology Design
PRISM：基于特权兄弟引导的多智能体拓扑结构设计
Multi-agent systems (MAS) based on large language models (LLMs) have become a powerful way to address complex problems, and their effectiveness depends critically on the collaboration topology that decides which agents participate and how they communicate. Recent work learns to generate this topology from the task query, fitting the generator to corpora of execution-verified graphs. The post-training method for such generators remains a limitation: a corpus lists the graphs that solved a query but never ranks them, leaving continued supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with no per-query preference to exploit. Without such a preference, the generator drifts toward the topologies the corpus contains most often rather than those that suit each query, so a gain on one task can come at a cost on another. We propose PRISM, which recovers per-query signal from the same corpus by learning with privileged information: a teacher reads, through a zero-initialized low-rank branch, a sibling graph that also solved the query and is unavailable at deployment, and the student matches its per-decision targets on trajectories the student samples itself. Across six diverse benchmarks PRISM improves over every topology-design baseline evaluated, and matched-teacher controls support the contribution of privileged query information. Code is available in this link.
中文翻译
基于大语言模型（LLMs）的多智能体系统（MAS）已成为解决复杂问题的强大方式，其有效性关键取决于决定哪些智能体参与以及如何通信的合作拓扑结构。近期工作尝试从任务查询中学习生成该拓扑结构，但训练生成器时依赖于经过执行验证的图谱语料库。然而，这种后训练方法存在局限性：语料库仅列出解决某一查询的图谱，但从不对其进行排序，导致持续的监督微调（SFT）缺乏每查询偏好信号。由于缺乏此类偏好，生成器会趋向于语料库中最常见的拓扑结构，而非最适合每个查询的结构，从而导致某些任务性能提升可能以其他任务性能下降为代价。为此，我们提出PRISM，通过学习特权信息恢复每查询信号：教师通过一个零初始化的低秩分支读取另一个也解决了该查询的兄弟图谱（部署时不可用），学生则在自身采样的轨迹上匹配教师的每决策目标。在六个不同基准测试中，PRISM均优于所有评估的拓扑设计基线，且与教师匹配的对照实验支持了特权查询信息的贡献。代码已公开。

DeepUnfolding_Agent: A Classical_Quantum Adaptive Control API
DeepUnfolding_Agent：经典-量子自适应控制API
Adaptive optimization workflows in machine learning, federated learning, quantum machine learning, and hybrid classical quantum systems often embed controller logic directly inside task specific training scripts. This coupling limits reuse, reproducibility, safety validation, and fair comparison across execution environments. This paper presents DeepUnfolding Agent, a schema-driven FastAPI platform that exposes deep unfolded adaptive control as an external software service. External executors register a strategy schema, submit round-level diagnostic variables, receive schema-bounded control actions, execute one optimization round, and return feedback for controller update. The framework separates controller intelligence from domain specific execution through a strategy registry, diagnostic to state adapter, controller factory, safety guard, executor abstraction, feedback update path, SQLite persistence layer, Docker deployment, and reproducible benchmark scripts. The implementation supports classical, quantum-aware, hybrid, Qiskit Aer, and optional IBM quantum processing execution pathways. We validate the software through unit, schema, API, safety, orchestration, Quantum execution, checkpointing, and Docker tests, and demonstrate external user fixed versus adaptive workflows across finance, healthcare, genomics, transport, noisy quantum simulation, and hardware backed IBM Quantum execution. The results show that DeepUnfolding-Agent provides a reusable and traceable adaptive control layer for heterogeneous optimization workflows. Our quantum experiments are interpreted as API level quantum control and hardware portability evidence.

FDE-Gym: The Last Mile of AI Delivery
FDE-Gym：AI交付的最后一步
Existing agent benchmarks typically evaluate agents on well-specified tasks defined by issue reports, specifications, or task tickets. Real-world customer delivery is different: forward-deployed engineers (FDEs) must clarify ambiguous requirements, work in live systems, and uncover unstated constraints. We introduce FDE-Gym, a benchmark of 95 end-to-end delivery tasks from 20 customer scenarios across 18 sectors, each adapted by industry experts from real-world delivery projects. In simulated customer environments with scripted stakeholders and modifiable customer systems, agents interact with stakeholders and operate on production-like systems to deliver working solutions. FDE-Gym scores a delivery by how much it improves the customer's own success metric, normalized between 0 (unchanged system) and 1 (reference solution), with penalties for stakeholder interactions (messages, trial runs, and waiting time). An online platform lets humans practice FDE skills under the same grading criteria. We evaluate 13 model configurations from seven providers. In the realistic setting, where unstated constraints must be discovered, the best configuration achieves a mean FDE-Gym score of 0.56 and resolves only 14 of the 95 tasks (a score of at least 0.8 with no incident). Discovering unstated constraints is the primary bottleneck: performance drops sharply when models must uncover them without explicit guidance. Yet information discovery alone does not account for the gap: even on the 23 tasks where the best configuration obtains every key fact from stakeholders, it reaches a normalized business uplift of 0.8 or above on only 10. These results reveal a substantial gap between solving well-specified tasks and delivering effective AI systems for real customers.
中文翻译
现有的智能体基准测试通常在由问题报告、规格说明或任务工单定义的明确任务上评估智能体。但现实中的客户交付则不同：前向部署工程师（FDEs）必须澄清模糊的需求，在实时系统中工作，并发现未明说的约束条件。我们提出了FDE-Gym，这是一个涵盖18个行业20个客户场景的95个端到端交付任务的基准，每个任务均由行业专家根据真实交付项目改编而成。在模拟客户环境中，包含脚本化利益相关者和可修改的客户系统，智能体与利益相关者互动并在类似生产环境的系统上运行以交付可用解决方案。FDE-Gym通过衡量交付对客户自身成功指标的提升程度来评分，分数范围为0（系统无变化）到1（参考解决方案），并因利益相关者交互（消息、试运行和等待时间）施加惩罚。一个在线平台允许人类在相同评分标准下练习FDE技能。我们评估了来自七家供应商的13种模型配置。在需要发现未明说约束的真实情境中，最佳配置的平均FDE-Gym得分为0.56，仅解决95个任务中的14个（得分至少0.8且无事故）。发现未明说约束是主要瓶颈：当模型在无明确指导的情况下必须自行发现这些约束时，性能急剧下降。然而，仅靠信息发现无法解释这一差距：即使在23个任务中，最佳配置已从利益相关者处获取所有关键事实，其标准化业务提升仍仅在10个任务中达到0.8或以上。这些结果揭示了在解决明确任务与为真实客户交付有效AI系统之间存在显著差距。

MLExpert: Towards a Generalist Agentic Model for Autonomous Machine Learning Engineering
MLExpert：迈向通用自主机器学习工程智能体模型
Large language models (LLMs) are expanding from assisting scientific research and engineering to autonomously developing and improving AI systems, advancing AI for AI (AI4AI) as a pathway toward recursive self-improvement (RSI). Workflow-driven machine learning engineering (MLE) systems provide a practical path toward this pursuit through complex orchestration that manages long-horizon experimentation, while recent MLE systems adopt model-directed interaction, allowing models to autonomously organize experiments and adapt optimization strategies based on execution feedback. However, feedback from each experiment reflects several preceding decisions, and a long MLE run contains many such costly experiments. Training must therefore select useful supervision from long interaction trajectories and learn from intermediate outcomes. We propose a post-training method for autonomous long-horizon MLE optimization and apply it to train MLExpert, a 35B Mixture-of-Experts agentic model. We design the Kaggle-MLE Factory to construct executable tasks and an interaction harness for model-directed experimentation. Hierarchical agentic SFT transfers general tool-use and coding capabilities to MLE through outcome-guided supervision. Windowed Interaction RL (WIRL) treats the multi-turn decisions between successive experiment results as an execution window and assigns it a shared outcome-based reward. Asynchronous collection and policy correction support policy updates before the trajectory ends. On MLE-Bench Lite, MLExpert improves the average medal rate of the base model from 37.9% to 72.7%, achieving performance comparable to larger frontier models such as Kimi-K3 and GLM-5.3. Further experiments show that MLExpert improves performance on NatureBench Lite, agent scaffold optimization, and algorithm optimization, suggesting that the learned optimization capability generalizes to other tasks.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）正从辅助科研和工程扩展到自主开发和改进AI系统，推动AI for AI（AI4AI）的发展，作为实现递归自我改进（RSI）的一条路径。基于工作流的机器学习工程（MLE）系统通过复杂编排管理长周期实验，为这一目标提供了可行路径；而近期的MLE系统采用模型导向的交互方式，使模型能够自主组织实验并根据执行反馈调整优化策略。然而，每次实验的反馈反映了此前多个决策的结果，一次长周期MLE运行包含大量此类高成本实验。因此，训练必须从长期交互轨迹中选择有用监督，并从中间结果中学习。我们提出一种用于自主长周期MLE优化的后训练方法，并将其应用于训练MLExpert——一个350亿参数的专家混合（Mixture-of-Experts）智能体模型。我们设计了Kaggle-MLE Factory以构建可执行任务和模型导向实验的交互框架。分层智能体SFT通过结果引导的监督，将通用工具使用和编码能力迁移至MLE领域。窗口化交互强化学习（WIRL）将连续实验结果之间的多轮决策视为一个执行窗口，并赋予其共享的结果导向奖励。异步收集与策略修正支持在轨迹结束前进行策略更新。在MLE-Bench Lite上，MLExpert将基础模型的平均奖牌率从37.9%提升至72.7%，性能接近更大的前沿模型如Kimi-K3和GLM-5.3。进一步实验表明，MLExpert在NatureBench Lite、智能体支架优化和算法优化任务上也表现更优，表明所学优化能力具有良好的泛化性。
