Learning Self-Orchestration in Decentralized Agent Swarms
在去中心化智能体群体中学习自我协调
The capabilities of an agent swarm depend on both its individual members and how they coordinate. Yet existing multi-agent systems often train agents within fixed orchestration structures, separating the learning of individual capabilities from the organization of collective behavior. We introduce market-based post-training (MPT), a framework for jointly learning action policies and self-orchestration in decentralized agent swarms. Agents express their willingness to participate through bids, which determine who acts and how collective outcomes provide local training credit. Learning both action and bidding behavior allows individual capabilities and patterns of participation to adapt together without a central coordinating agent. We evaluate MPT on embodied crafting, robot navigation, and deep research. Results show gains over monolithic post-training and confirm that agent participation indeed evolves with learning. With the same post-trained action policies, learned self-orchestration outperforms alternative coordination strategies. Ablations show that both action and bidding benefit from learning, and that learned bids provide more effective action-training signals. Beyond the original training objectives, trained swarms adapt faster to related tasks and achieve better performance with larger populations at inference time. Larger populations also suffer smaller performance losses under agent dropout, demonstrating that learned self-orchestration supports reuse, scaling, and robustness in decentralized agent swarms. Code and trained models are available at https://github.com/Embodied-Minds-Lab/MPT.
中文翻译
智能体群体的能力既取决于其个体成员，也取决于它们如何协调。然而，现有多智能体系统通常在固定编排结构中训练智能体，将个体能力学习与集体行为组织分离。我们引入基于市场的后训练（MPT），一种用于在去中心化智能体群体中联合学习动作策略与自我协调的框架。智能体通过出价表达参与意愿，出价决定谁执行动作，而集体结果则提供局部训练信用。同时学习动作与出价行为，使个体能力与参与模式能够协同适应，无需中央协调智能体。我们在具身制造、机器人导航和深度研究任务上评估MPT。结果表明，相比单体后训练，MPT取得显著提升，并证实智能体参与模式确实随学习而演化。在相同的后训练动作策略下，学习到的自我协调优于其他协调策略。消融实验显示，动作与出价行为均从学习中受益，且学习到的出价提供了更有效的动作训练信号。除了原始训练目标外，训练后的群体在面对相关任务时适应更快，推理时更大规模群体表现更好。大规模群体在智能体脱落情况下也表现出更小的性能损失，证明学习到的自我协调支持复用、扩展性和鲁棒性。代码与训练模型可在https://github.com/Embodied-Minds-Lab/MPT获取。

CAT-alyzing Honesty: Continuous Adversarial Training for Transferable LLM Honesty
CAT-alyzing 坦诚：用于可迁移大语言模型坦诚性的连续对抗训练
Large language models (LLMs) are becoming prevalent in scenarios where honesty is critical. However, despite alignment training, these models still exhibit dishonest behaviour, such as outputting claims that contradict some knowledge or are not supported by what is accessible in context. We introduce Continuous Adversarial Training for Consistent Honesty (CATCH), inspired by a method originally developed to robustly refuse harmful queries. CATCH trains the model towards an honest answer and away from a dishonest one while maintaining the model's capabilities. We evaluate two architectures (Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct and Qwen3-3.6-27B). We show that trained with only hundreds of targets built from self-reporting scenarios, CATCH learns an honest behaviour that robustly generalizes and transfers across distinct benchmarks. It consistently reduces dishonesty under pressure on MASK, increases confessions in the multi-agent game of Among Us, and improves the discovery rates of the model's hidden behaviours in AuditBench. We show that CATCH outperforms prompting and supervised fine-tuning in improving honesty, and it is on par with state-of-the-art honesty activation steering. However, CATCH surpasses honesty steering in maintaining utility, thanks to a novel choice of utility data.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）在需要坦诚性的场景中日益普及。然而，尽管经过对齐训练，这些模型仍表现出不诚实行为，例如输出与某些知识相矛盾或缺乏上下文支持的主张。我们提出了连续对抗训练以实现一致坦诚性（CATCH），该方法最初用于稳健拒绝有害查询。CATCH 在保持模型能力的同时，训练模型趋向诚实回答并远离不诚实回答。我们在两种架构（Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct 和 Qwen3-3.6-27B）上进行了评估。结果显示，仅使用数百个源自自我报告场景的目标，CATCH 学习到一种鲁棒泛化且跨不同基准可迁移的坦诚行为。它在 MASK 上持续降低压力下的不诚实率，在《Among Us》多智能体游戏中增加坦白行为，并提升 AuditBench 中模型隐藏行为的发现率。我们证明 CATCH 在提升坦诚性方面优于提示工程和监督微调，且与最先进的坦诚性引导方法相当。然而，由于采用了新颖的效用数据选择，CATCH 在维持实用性方面表现更优。

Librarian Agents: Training LLMs to Curate Long-Context Workspaces that Anticipate Future Queries
图书管理员智能体：训练大语言模型以策划长上下文工作区以预见未来查询
Reasoning over long document contexts remains a challenge for large language models (LLMs). Standard retrieval methods are inherently reactive, bottlenecking agents with complex, cross-document reasoning at inference time, while precomputed structures like knowledge graphs often fall short because they rely on rigid heuristics. Instead, can we allow agents to perform anticipatory directed curation: learning to proactively reorganize unstructured corpora prior to inference to efficiently serve diverse future queries. We build on a key asymmetry in modern agentic harnesses (e.g., Claude Code): while LLM agents are adept at navigating existing filesystems to solve an immediate task, they are rarely trained to construct reusable workspaces for future use. To instantiate this capability, we introduce Librarian Agents, a framework in which a builder agent transforms raw documents into a hierarchical filesystem of synthetic files and reference links, which forms a workspace tailored for a downstream answering agent. We formulate workspace construction as a Markov decision process and optimize the builder policy via reinforcement learning to directly maximize downstream answering accuracy. Empirically, Librarian Agents outperforms the strongest retrieval and precomputation baselines by 33 percentage points on BrowseComp-Plus, amortizes upfront construction costs across repeated queries, and generalizes zero-shot to unseen domains on BRIGHT with a 31-point average accuracy improvement.

Training-Free On-Policy Distillation
无需训练的在线策略蒸馏
On-policy distillation consolidates specialist models into one student at the price of rollouts, teacher passes and gradient steps for every teacher. We show that the consolidation does not need training: with forward passes only, the capabilities of separately tuned specialists can be aggregated into one model that is, on average, more accurate than either specialist alone. When specialists share a base model, distillation has an exact layer-wise solution: each linear layer of the fused model must reproduce every specialist's output on the inputs that specialist receives while reading its own answers, a least-squares problem whose solution needs only the second moments of those inputs. One forward pass over a few hundred rollouts provides them. We call this training-free on-policy distillation (\method). What the rollouts contain matters more than the solver: answers truncated in repetition loops are few among a mathematics specialist's rollouts but carry a large share of their tokens, and removing them lifts the fused model on AIME25 from task arithmetic's level to the specialist's at matched precision. On Olmo~3 7B, a few GPU-hours of forward computation per specialist yield a generalist that keeps most of each specialist's gain on each of seven mathematics and code benchmarks.
中文翻译
在线策略蒸馏通过每次教师的回放、教师传递和梯度步数，将多个专精模型整合进一个学生模型。我们证明这种整合无需训练：仅通过前向传播，即可将各自独立调优的专精模型能力聚合为一个模型，其平均性能优于任一专精模型。当专精模型共享基础模型时，蒸馏存在精确的逐层解法：融合模型的每一层线性层必须在特定输入下重现每个专精模型的输出，同时读取其自身答案，这是一个最小二乘问题，其解仅需这些输入的二阶矩。仅需对几百次回放进行一次前向传播即可获得。我们称此方法为无需训练的在线策略蒸馏（ exttt{method}）。回放内容的重要性超过求解器：数学专精模型的回放中，重复循环截断的答案虽少，却占大量 token，去除后使 AIME25 上融合模型的表现从任务算术水平跃升至专精模型水平，且精度匹配。在 Olmo~3 7B 上，每名专精模型仅需数小时 GPU 计算，即可生成一个通用模型，保留各专精模型在七项数学与代码基准上的大部分优势。
👤 Bangji Yang, Jiajun Fan, Zhaoyu Zhu, Hongbo Ma, Ge Liu 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

HTN-Orchestrate: A Hierarchical Task Network Framework for Multi-Agent Orchestration
HTN-Orchestrate：一种用于多智能体编排的层次任务网络框架
Orchestrating multiple agents on a complex task requires reconciling local subtask execution with global task planning. Dynamic dispatch approaches provide execution flexibility but do not explicitly model subtask hierarchies or dependencies, while predefined workflows impose this structure but fix the decomposition before execution begins. Therefore, we introduce HTN-Orchestrate, a two-layer framework that separates these responsibilities through a hierarchical task network (HTN). The Planner maintains subtask hierarchies and dependencies, deferring decomposition until prerequisite results become available, while local Orchestrators schedule workers to execute each leaf subtask. Results propagate upward under \textbf{AND} semantics, and accumulated findings inform a rebuilt task network when the current decomposition cannot resolve the task. On five benchmarks spanning web research, scientific reasoning, and research workflows, HTN-Orchestrate outperforms ReAct, Agent Team, and DeepSeek Harness using the same base model, achieving 89.73% accuracy on BrowseComp and 73.44% on SUPERChem, exceeding Agent Team by 2.45 and 4.69 percentage points, respectively.
中文翻译
在复杂任务上协调多个智能体，需要调和局部子任务执行与全局任务规划之间的关系。动态调度方法提供执行灵活性，但未显式建模子任务层级或依赖关系；而预定义工作流虽强加结构，却在执行前固定分解方式。因此，我们提出 HTN-Orchestrate，一种两层框架，通过层次任务网络（HTN）分离这些职责。规划器维护子任务层级与依赖关系，推迟分解直至前置结果可用；本地编排器则调度工作者执行每个叶节点子任务。结果按 extbf{AND} 语义向上传播，当当前分解无法解决任务时，累积发现将用于重建任务网络。在涵盖网页研究、科学推理和研究工作流的五个基准上，HTN-Orchestrate 使用相同基础模型超越 ReAct、Agent Team 和 DeepSeek Harness，分别在 BrowseComp 上达到 89.73% 准确率，在 SUPERChem 上达到 73.44%，分别优于 Agent Team 2.45 和 4.69 个百分点。

AutoSFT: An Agentic System for Automated Data Research in Supervised Fine-Tuning
AutoSFT：一种用于监督微调中自动化数据研究的智能体系统
Language model agents increasingly automate post-training, but when given the full training stack they converge on supervised fine-tuning and spend most effort revising training data. Existing systems give the agent one kind of control, selecting rows from a fixed pool or calling a generator, whereas a human data engineer adjusts proportions, prunes, repairs, replaces and synthesizes the data. We design a data action space for these changes and ask whether an automated system can make the full range rather than one of them, and what structure that requires. We first run this loop with a single agent, and it reaches only one action, adjusting category proportions, while its context fills with execution output and is discarded mid-search. We therefore introduce \textbf{AutoSFT}, a multi-agent system where a meta agent separates execution from analysis, gathers proposals from independent angles, and calls a synthesis subagent for capabilities the pool cannot cover, with the model, the trainer, and the budget fixed within each comparison. Adding each component in turn helps: the executor halves context loss, the analysis subagents lift the best IFEval by $+6.5$, and synthesis adds $+3.4$ IFEval and $+2.9$ IFBench. The same loop transfers to code, where analysis adds $+3.7$ MBPP+ over a single agent that does not improve. Every result comes from runs that retrain from the base model each round, so each component's effect is individually attributable. Our findings suggest that automating SFT data research depends more on covering diverse data actions than on a stronger single action.
中文翻译
语言模型智能体越来越多地自动化后训练过程，但当赋予完整的训练栈时，它们会聚焦于监督微调，并将大部分精力用于修改训练数据。现有系统仅赋予智能体单一控制权，例如从固定数据池中选择行或调用生成器，而人类数据工程师则会调整比例、修剪、修复、替换并合成数据。我们为此类变化设计了一个数据操作空间，并探讨自动化系统能否实现全部操作而非仅其中一种，以及这需要怎样的结构。我们首先使用单个智能体运行该循环，结果仅能执行一种操作——调整类别比例，且其上下文因执行输出堆积而被中途丢弃。因此，我们提出 extbf{AutoSFT}，一个由元智能体主导的多智能体系统，将执行与分析分离，从多个独立角度收集建议，并调用合成子智能体以弥补数据池能力不足，同时在每次比较中固定模型、训练器和预算。逐步引入每个组件均带来提升：执行器使上下文损失减半，分析子智能体使最佳IFEval提升+6.5，合成模块进一步带来+3.4 IFEval和+2.9 IFBench。该循环同样可迁移至代码任务，在单个智能体无法改进的情况下，分析模块带来+3.7 MBPP+的提升。所有结果均来自每轮从基础模型重新训练的实验，因此每个组件的影响均可独立归因。我们的发现表明，自动化SFT数据研究的关键在于覆盖多样化的数据操作，而非依赖更强的单一操作。

EVA-Cap: Optimizing Audiovisual Video Captioning via Event-Centric Alignment
EVA-Cap：通过事件中心对齐优化视听视频字幕生成
Recent advances in Omni Language Models (OLMs) provide a unified solution for audiovisual video captioning. Nevertheless, existing approaches predominantly treat captioning as an unstructured text generation process, neglecting the inherent semantic structure of videos and leading to suboptimal fine-grained audiovisual alignment. To bridge this gap, we propose EVA-Cap, a novel framework that decomposes captions into atomic audiovisual events and transforms unstructured text supervision into event-centric alignment. We train EVA-Cap via Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on a self-curated dataset, EVA-Data, which is built with event-centric quality control. During the GRPO phase, the model is explicitly optimized against three event-centric alignment targets: global event completeness, intra-event attribution, and inter-event synchronization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EVA-Cap significantly outperforms existing methods in event-centric evaluation (e.g., +9.0% average gain on ChronusAV), while achieving competitive performance on general audiovisual captioning benchmarks (e.g., +3.3% on WorldSense).

Effectiveness of Hidden-State Probes for Localizing Reasoning Failures
隐藏状态探针在定位推理失败中的有效性
Verifying the final answer of a long reasoning trace may show that the trace is wrong but it does not distinguish three cases. The problem may lie beyond the model's capability, another attempt would succeed, or one step in an otherwise recoverable trace made the trace fail. Standard hidden-state probes are trained on final-answer correctness, a label that conflates these cases. Monte Carlo rollouts distinguish the cases and locate the failing step at additional inference cost. We test the hidden states of 4B and 8B reasoning models for the failure type and the failing step and compare each probe with the strongest baseline that uses no hidden states. On incorrect 8B traces, the confidence probe, a calibrated correctness probe at a midpoint cutoff, misses 81% of cases in which a single step makes the trace unrecoverable and 47% of cases in which another attempt would succeed. A probe with a different training design over the same states reverses the ordering of these two kinds of failure, so either ordering can be read from those states. At both scales, no single tested probe locates the failing step more accurately than a rule based only on trace length, and at 4B a probe trained to predict that step fails to outperform this rule in any of 10 runs on identical traces. The confidence probe therefore indicates that an answer is wrong, but its failure-type ordering depends on its training design.
中文翻译
验证长推理链的最终答案可能发现其错误，但无法区分三种情形：问题超出模型能力、另一次尝试可能成功，或在本可恢复的链路中某一步骤导致失败。标准隐藏状态探针基于最终答案正确性进行训练，该标签混淆了上述三种情况。蒙特卡洛回溯虽可区分这些情况并定位失败步骤，但需额外推理成本。我们测试了4B和8B规模推理模型的隐藏状态，以识别失败类型与失败步骤，并将每种探针与不使用隐藏状态的最强基线进行比较。在8B模型的错误推理链上，置信度探针（在中间截断点校准的正确性探针）在81%的单步致败案例中漏检，且在47%的其他尝试可能成功的案例中漏检。另一种训练设计相同的隐藏状态探针改变了这两种失败类型的排序，说明该排序可从隐藏状态中读取。在两种规模下，没有任何单一探针在定位失败步骤方面优于仅基于推理链长度的规则；在4B规模下，一个旨在预测该步骤的探针在相同推理链上的10次运行中均未能超越该规则。因此，置信度探针仅表明答案错误，其失败类型排序取决于训练设计。

WEAVE: Weaving Real-World Code Evolution into Dependency-Structured Tasks for Benchmarking and Training Code Agents
WEAVE：将真实世界代码演进融入依赖结构化任务以用于代码智能体的基准测试与训练
Real-world software engineering rarely consists of isolated changes. Features, migrations, and systematic fixes often unfold across multiple pull requests (PRs), requiring coordinated modifications across a repository. Yet most executable repository-level benchmarks retain a single issue or PR as the task unit, while existing multi-PR settings rely on coarse aggregation and structure-agnostic evaluation. This mismatch obscures dependencies among changes and limits progress measurement and learning signals for long-horizon code agents. We introduce WEAVE, a framework that transforms verified PR histories into executable, dependency-structured tasks. WEAVE leverages repository-calibrated typed change loci to organize related PRs into bounded DAGs and instantiate each as a unified executable task. We further introduce Graph Credit, a deterministic, test-grounded metric that measures dependency-consistent partial progress and supports dense, verifiable rewards for reinforcement learning. Experiments on task construction, agent evaluation, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning show that WEAVE yields coherent executable tasks, enables more faithful progress measurement than flat scoring, and provides effective supervision and verifiable rewards for training code agents.

现实世界中的软件工程很少是孤立的变更。功能开发、迁移和系统性修复通常跨越多个拉取请求（PR），需要在仓库内协调修改。然而，大多数可执行的仓库级基准仍以单一问题或PR作为任务单元，而现有的多PR设置依赖粗粒度聚合和无结构评估。这种不匹配掩盖了变更之间的依赖关系，限制了长时程代码智能体的进展测量和学习信号。我们提出WEAVE框架，将经验证的PR历史转化为可执行的、依赖结构化的任务。WEAVE利用仓库校准的类型化变更位置，将相关PR组织成有界有向无环图（DAG），并将每个任务实例化为统一的可执行任务。我们进一步引入Graph Credit，一种确定性的、基于测试的度量，用于衡量依赖一致的部分进展，并支持强化学习中的密集且可验证的奖励。在任务构建、智能体评估、监督微调和强化学习的实验表明，WEAVE生成连贯的可执行任务，相比扁平评分能更忠实反映进展，并为训练代码智能体提供有效监督和可验证奖励。
👤 Weihao Li, Zihang Xu, Zhongqiu Wang, Songwei Yu, Wenqiang Zhu et al. 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22029]
agent-benchmarkagent-memoryagentic-rl
Selection Is Not Learning: Choosing Model Futures
选择并非学习：模型未来的抉择
Self-evolving language models train on tasks they generate, so every round must decide which updated model the next round inherits. Current loops such as R-Zero, Agent0, and Skill Self-Play make this decision through the task, admitting tasks that look learnable and inheriting every resulting update. We identify a selection-to-update gap in these loops, where a task that looks learnable need not produce a useful update. Most updates on verified tasks fail to make correct answers more likely, yet these loops inherit them all. HeirCommit freezes the updated model and uses fresh evaluation questions to decide whether to inherit it or retain the current model. EvoHeir separates parameter inheritance from experience inheritance, using current and archived experience to repair failures and preserve successes before final confirmation. Our analysis characterizes selection-induced optimism and decomposes regret into selection, absorption, and confirmation errors. HeirCommit cuts the harmful share of accepted updates by 68-83% relative to random inheritance at the same acceptance rate. Across four backbones, EvoHeir achieves the best mathematics and general-reasoning averages, raising the math average by 5.3% relative to the strongest baseline, Agent0. The framework lets useful experience persist even when the model that produced it is not inherited.
中文翻译
自演化语言模型在自身生成的任务上进行训练，因此每一轮都必须决定下一阶段继承哪个更新后的模型。当前的循环机制如R-Zero、Agent0和技能自对弈通过任务来做出这一决策，接受那些看起来可学习的任务，并继承所有由此产生的更新。我们识别出这些循环中的“选择-更新差距”：一个看似可学习的任务未必会产生有用的更新。在已验证任务上，大多数更新未能提高正确答案的概率，但这些循环仍继承了全部更新。HeirCommit冻结更新后的模型，并使用新评估问题判断是否继承或保留当前模型。EvoHeir将参数继承与经验继承分离，利用当前和归档经验修复失败并保留成功，再进行最终确认。我们的分析刻画了选择引发的乐观偏差，并将遗憾分解为选择、吸收和确认误差。HeirCommit在相同接受率下，相较于随机继承，将被接受更新中有害部分减少了68%-83%。在四种骨干模型上，EvoHeir在数学和通用推理平均表现上达到最优，使数学平均分相对于最强基线Agent0提升了5.3%。该框架使得即使产生经验的模型未被继承，有用的经验也能持续保留。
👤 Haoyi Yang, Kshitij Mishra, Nils Lukas, Salem Lahlou 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

RoleQ: Role-Aligned Scales for Converting Language Models to Ternary
RoleQ：用于将语言模型转换为三值权重的角色对齐尺度
Weight quantization below four bits shrinks memory and bandwidth at a perplexity that looks acceptable, but often at the cost of factual knowledge and task accuracy. Ternary weights have mostly been obtained by training from scratch or long fine-tuning. We introduce RoleQ, a quantization framework that places scales by the functional roles of Transformer units rather than by the layout of the weight matrix, as conventional formats do. Vectors that read from the residual stream take one scale per output unit; those that write to it, one per input unit. The rule is structural, needs no search, calibration or stored state, and holds on integer and ternary grids. In a rotated basis it matches a per-layer axis search at half the solver work, and at ternary it cuts perplexity by up to half against layout-based placement in two families at every size from 0.6B to 14B, where per-row scales leave MMLU at chance. Our lightweight post-training pipeline converts an existing checkpoint to 1.59 bits per weight in a few GPU-hours, a fraction of the cost of learned ternarization, and leads every post-training ternary method at matched or lower memory-resident bits on perplexity, zero-shot accuracy and MMLU, most widely on knowledge. A sparse second ternary plane placed by a per-unit Fisher saliency keeps the matmul multiplication-free and recovers what ternary loses first: at 8B, a fraction of a bit returns about half the MMLU that conversion costs, well ahead of random or Hessian-based placement at equal bits.
中文翻译
低于四位的权重量化在保持可接受困惑度的同时缩小了内存和带宽，但通常以牺牲事实知识和任务准确率为代价。三值权重大多通过从头训练或长时间微调获得。我们提出RoleQ，一种量化框架，根据Transformer单元的功能角色而非传统格式中的权重矩阵布局来放置尺度。从残差流读取的向量每个输出单元使用一个尺度；写入残差流的向量每个输入单元使用一个尺度。该规则具有结构性，无需搜索、校准或存储状态，并适用于整数和三值网格。在旋转基下，其性能相当于每层轴搜索的一半求解工作量，在三值情况下，相较于基于布局的放置方法，在0.6B至14B的所有规模上，困惑度降低高达一半，而按行设置尺度则使MMLU降至随机水平。我们的轻量级后训练流程可在数小时GPU时间内将现有检查点转换为每权重1.59比特，成本仅为学习型三值化的极小部分，并在匹配或更低内存驻留比特下，于困惑度、零样本准确率和MMLU方面优于所有后训练三值方法，尤其在知识方面表现广泛领先。通过每个单元的Fisher显著性放置的稀疏第二三值平面保持乘法-free，并恢复了三值化最先损失的内容：在8B规模下，仅需不足一比特即可恢复转换所损失的约一半MMLU，远超相同比特下的随机或Hessian基放置方法。

DeciCAD: Harness-Grounded Learning of Action Policies for CAD Agents
DeciCAD：面向CAD智能体的动作策略的Harness基础学习
Large Language Model agents operate through closed-loop action policies whose decision space is defined by an agent harness. We investigate how this operating space shapes agent behavior and policy learning in computer-aided design (CAD), a professional domain characterized by persistent state, geometric constraints, and long-horizon procedures. We instantiate this perspective in DeciCAD, a domain-native harness that exposes semantic CAD actions, decision-oriented observations, environment feedback, and persistent state transitions. Building on this harness, we develop a learning pipeline that extracts causal decision supervision from interaction trajectories and improves the policy through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and geometry-grounded online reinforcement learning (RL). Across four CAD benchmarks, DeciCAD achieves the best F1 and intersection over union (IoU) while reducing model-output tokens by 45--88% relative to a general-purpose coding harness. Under matched supervision, its SFT policy improves F1 from 0.6883 to 0.7437 and outperforms the coding policy; subsequent online RL further raises geometry availability from 66.5% to 73.5% and F1 to 0.7880. These results demonstrate that the decision space induced by a harness materially affects both agent execution and policy learning in CAD.

FIRM: Fisher-Informed Retention Merging for Federated LLM Unlearning
FIRM：用于联邦大模型遗忘的Fisher感知保留合并
Federated LLM unlearning addresses targeted knowledge removal when forget and retain data remain distributed across private clients and are inaccessible to the server. The resulting locally trained adapters encode conflicting objectives, making their aggregation fundamentally different from conventional federated optimization. A key failure mode is magnitude explosion, whereby unnormalized cross-client aggregation increases the merged update magnitude and degrades retained utility under stronger unlearning. We propose FIRM (Fisher-Informed Retention Merging), a single-round framework for stable and retention-aware server-side aggregation. FIRM uses client-local Fisher statistics as a unified sensitivity signal to construct a Fisher-normalized base merge and impose explicit control over retain-sensitive parameters. Its second-order retention projection admits a unique minimum-distance solution, while the corresponding dual scales with the number of retain clients rather than the model dimension. Experiments across WMDP, TOFU, and MUSE-HP using Zephyr-7B and Llama-3-8B show utility gains at comparable forgetting on WMDP and MUSE-HP, with metric-dependent trade-offs on TOFU. Controlled comparisons support benefits of the combined base merge and additional retention correction at selected operating points.
中文翻译
联邦大模型遗忘处理在遗忘数据和保留数据分散于私有客户端且服务器无法访问的情况下，实现目标知识移除的问题。由此产生的本地训练适配器包含冲突目标，使其聚合与传统的联邦优化根本不同。一个关键失败模式是幅度爆炸：未归一化的跨客户端聚合会增加合并更新的幅度，在更强遗忘条件下导致保留性能下降。我们提出FIRM（Fisher感知保留合并），一种单轮稳定且保留感知的服务器端聚合框架。FIRM利用客户端本地的Fisher统计信息作为统一敏感性信号，构建Fisher归一化基础合并，并对保留敏感参数施加显式控制。其二阶保留投影具有唯一最小距离解，而对应的对偶随保留客户端数量增长，而非模型维度。在WMDP、TOFU和MUSE-HP三个数据集上，使用Zephyr-7B和Llama-3-8B的实验显示，在WMDP和MUSE-HP上遗忘程度相当时，性能有所提升，而在TOFU上存在指标依赖性权衡。受控对比支持在特定操作点上结合基础合并与额外保留修正的优势。

Human Interventions as Labels: Localizing, Steering, and (Sometimes) Editing the Computations Behind LLM Agent Failures
人类干预作为标签：定位、引导并（有时）编辑LLM智能体失败背后的计算过程
When an LLM agent misbehaves, a human steps in, and that intervention is usually discarded or replayed as training data. We treat it instead as a measurement: a label pointing at the internal computation that failed. We study it in a controlled agent environment, on one failure (asking a clarifying question when the request was already unambiguous), with labels from a rule-based overseer validated against blind human judgment. Claims cover eligible models, which both over-ask and sometimes act on matched unambiguous requests: five of thirteen qualified (Qwen2.5-3B, gemma-2-2b, OLMo-3-7B, Mistral-Nemo-12B, Phi-4). Four findings. First, the ask-vs-act decision is causally localizable in every eligible family: a direction learned from intervention labels flips 72-100% of held-out over-asks at a definite residual-stream depth, with site and dose chosen on training data, and random directions under the same procedure flip at most one episode. Second, over-asking has two mechanistic signatures: in four families the model demonstrably knows the request is unambiguous (the belief is decodable, causally inert and orthogonal to the decision) and asks anyway; in the fifth, the rare over-asks track different world-representations. Third, steering gated on a probe readout removes most of the simulated oversight burden while legitimate questions survive (bootstrap 95% intervals exclude zero); a prompt instruction makes it worse. Fourth, consolidating the fix into the weights yields a map, not a rule: on one model a LoRA confined to the causal band passes every criterion fixed in advance while behavior cloning on identical data breaks retention; on three families the reverse holds; on the fifth the outcome crosses the boundary across seeds. A geometric rule written down in advance to predict the regime failed on two out-of-family tests; we report the failure and stop rather than fit a replacement.
中文翻译
当LLM智能体出现异常行为时，人类会介入，而这种干预通常被丢弃或作为训练数据重放。我们将其视为一种测量：指向内部失败计算的标签。我们在受控智能体环境中研究这一现象，聚焦于一种失败情况（请求已明确时仍要求澄清问题），并使用基于规则的监督者标签与盲人人类判断进行验证。涉及的合格模型包括五种：Qwen2.5-3B、gemma-2-2b、OLMo-3-7B、Mistral-Nemo-12B、Phi-4。四项发现：第一，询问与执行决策在所有合格模型家族中均可因果定位：从干预标签中学习的方向能在确定的残差流深度上翻转72%-100%的保留过问行为，且位置与剂量在训练数据上选择，相同程序下随机方向最多翻转一次事件。第二，过问行为具有两种机制特征：在四个家族中，模型明显知道请求已明确（信念可解码、因果无关且正交于决策）但仍提问；在第五个家族中，罕见的过问行为追踪不同的世界表征。第三，基于探测读出的引导能消除大部分模拟监督负担，而合法问题仍能保留（自举95%置信区间不包含零）；提示指令反而使情况恶化。第四，将修复固化到权重中生成的是映射而非规则：在一个模型中，局限于因果带的LoRA通过了所有预先设定的标准，而行为克隆在相同数据上却破坏了保留；在三个家族中情况相反；在第五个家族中，结果随种子跨越边界。事先写下的几何规则用于预测范式，在两个跨家族测试中失败，我们报告失败并停止，而非拟合替代方案。

Diffusion Language Models as Open Quantum Systems
扩散语言模型作为开放量子系统
Diffusion language models generate text by reversing a classical diffusion process over token sequences. We show that their continuous-time forward and reverse dynamics can be embedded in the framework of open quantum systems, which describes quantum systems interacting with their environment. This connection provides a basis for extending diffusion sampling to superpositions of sentence states and for modifying the underlying generation dynamics. We construct a $τ$-leaping unraveling scheme that simulates stochastic trajectories of these states while updating multiple token positions at each step. This enables generation conditioned on superposed prompts or thoughts, which we call Superposition of Prompts (SoP) and Superposition of Thoughts (SoT). The same formulation yields Quantum-Inspired Remasking (QIR): perturbing the masked-diffusion dynamics produces a confidence-based rule for revising previously generated tokens. Experiments with pretrained diffusion language models show that superposition-based generation can retain information from conditioning components not selected at the final measurement, while QIR improves LLM-assessed text quality when applied at an appropriate stage of generation.

👤 Akinori Tanaka, Yukinao Akamatsu, Takuki Tomita, Yuji Hirono 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

The Self-Improvement Problem
自我改进问题
With the rise of large language models, agentic systems are becoming increasingly autonomous, as they write code, call tools, revise prompts, and propose modifications to their own components. This autonomy raises a fundamental question for safe self-improvement: can an automated gate like a reward model or an automated reviewer reliably decide whether a proposed update should be accepted? The difficulty is structural. The gate must be a terminating, computable procedure, while the property we ultimately care about, namely whether an update is a genuine semantic improvement, depends on potentially unbounded program behavior. We formalize this mismatch by modeling agents as programs and defining improvement in terms of a semantic score that need not be computable. We study two evaluation abstractions. The first is a certifier that approves or rejects an update. The second is a search procedure that, given access to a proposal generator, attempts to output some improving candidate if one exists. We show that, in the fully general setting, no terminating procedure can simultaneously guarantee reliability (never accepting a non-improvement) and non-conservatism (never missing an available improvement): self-referential constructions can force any such gate or searcher to fail. Randomized gates with bounded error face the same limit. We then identify a standard decidable regime used in practice (bounded evaluation and a finite proposal budget) under which exhaustive simulation yields a terminating search procedure with the desired guarantees. We also show the limit of this recovery: a chain of accepted updates can raise the bounded score in every round while the true score falls. Our results draw a boundary between unbounded, universal settings where correctness guarantees collapse and bounded settings where they can be recovered by design.
中文翻译
随着大语言模型的发展，智能体系统正变得越来越自主，能够编写代码、调用工具、修改提示词并对其自身组件提出改进建议。这种自主性引发了关于安全自我改进的根本问题：像奖励模型或自动化评审员这样的自动门能否可靠地判断一个提议的更新是否应被接受？困难在于结构层面。该门必须是一个终止且可计算的过程，而我们真正关心的属性——即更新是否为真正的语义改进——取决于可能无界的程序行为。我们通过将智能体建模为程序，并以无需可计算的语义得分来定义改进，形式化了这一不匹配。我们研究了两种评估抽象：第一种是批准或拒绝更新的验证器；第二种是给定提案生成器后，尝试输出某个改进候选者的搜索过程。我们证明，在完全一般的情形下，任何终止过程都无法同时保证可靠性（从不接受非改进）和非保守性（从不遗漏可用改进）：自指构造可迫使任何此类门或搜索器失败。具有有界误差的随机门也面临相同限制。随后，我们识别出实践中常用的标准可判定情形（有限评估和有限提案预算），在此情形下，穷尽模拟可产生具有所需保证的终止搜索过程。我们还展示了这种恢复的局限性：一系列被接受的更新可在每轮中提升有界得分，而真实得分却在下降。我们的结果划清了无界、通用设置中正确性保证崩溃与有界设置中可通过设计恢复保证之间的界限。

Waypoint-OPD: Transferring RL-Induced Policy Shifts via Gated On-Policy Distillation
Waypoint-OPD：通过门控在线策略蒸馏转移RL引起的策略偏移
Policy-shift distillation reuses the difference between a teacher's log probabilities before and after reinforcement learning (RL) as supervision on student-generated trajectories. However, this endpoint policy shift cannot reveal whether local action preferences evolved consistently during teacher training. The same endpoint shift can result from early and late shifts that reinforce or partially cancel each other. We investigate whether agreement between early and late teacher policy shifts can guide selective transfer. We introduce Waypoint-guided On-Policy Distillation (Waypoint-OPD), which uses an intermediate teacher checkpoint from the same RL run to compare early and late shifts in relative action preferences at student-visited prefixes. We gate the endpoint shift according to the directional agreement between the early and late shifts, suppressing it when they point in opposing directions. Compared with endpoint-only transfer, our method requires only one additional frozen-teacher scoring pass. Using RL-trained Qwen3-4B teachers, we evaluate our method with Qwen3-8B, Qwen3-14B, and Qwen3-30B-A3B as student models in mathematical and code reasoning. In all six domain--student settings, Waypoint-OPD achieves the highest domain-average score among the compared distillation methods. Ablations with the Qwen3-8B student show that removing or shuffling gates reduces mathematical reasoning performance. These results support the use of directional agreement between early and late teacher policy shifts to weight the endpoint shift during student training.

策略偏移蒸馏利用教师模型在强化学习（RL）前后对数概率的差异作为学生生成轨迹的监督信号。然而，这种端点策略偏移无法揭示教师训练过程中局部动作偏好是否一致演化。相同的端点偏移可能源于早期和晚期偏移，这些偏移可能相互增强或部分抵消。我们探讨早期和晚期教师策略偏移的一致性是否可以指导选择性转移。我们引入了路径点引导的在线策略蒸馏（Waypoint-OPD），使用同一RL训练运行中的中间教师检查点，比较学生访问前缀处的早期和晚期偏移在相对动作偏好上的差异。根据早期和晚期偏移的方向一致性对端点偏移进行门控，在方向相反时抑制其影响。与仅使用端点转移相比，我们的方法只需额外一次冻结教师评分遍历。使用RL训练的Qwen3-4B教师模型，我们在数学和代码推理任务中以Qwen3-8B、Qwen3-14B和Qwen3-30B-A3B作为学生模型评估该方法。在所有六个领域-学生组合中，Waypoint-OPD在对比的蒸馏方法中取得了最高的领域平均分。对Qwen3-8B学生的消融实验显示，移除或打乱门控会降低数学推理性能。这些结果支持利用早期和晚期教师策略偏移的方向一致性来加权学生训练期间的端点偏移。

Do Agents Need a New Kind of Paper? SPRA: Structured Paper Representation for Agents
智能体是否需要一种新类型的论文？面向智能体的结构化论文表示SPRA
Scientific agents must connect research problems, methods, and findings dispersed through narrative articles. We introduce SPRA (Structured Paper Representation for Agents), a paper-level representation of persistent research objects and typed relations. Its empirical-AI profile organizes Problems, Contributions, Components, ExperimentSetups, Measures, and Findings for querying, traversal, and reuse. Selected paper-level citation relations record dependencies, component use, and comparisons with prior work; source locations support verification. We instantiate SPRA across more than one million AI papers, with 19.95 million research units and 24.07 million internal relations. Evaluations access SPRA through Schema-RAG with task evidence fixed before inference. Across seven subfields, SPRA improves source-conditioned scientific QA by 0.381/5 over passage retrieval while remaining close to Full Compact (0.078/5 lower on average). In directed cross-paper reasoning, qualification improves by 0.184/5 over passage retrieval; Top-5 synthesis shows mixed quality effects. Across all paired RQ2 tasks, including Top-5, input+output tokens decrease by 28.5% relative to passage retrieval and 95.3% relative to Full Compact. In idea generation, a 0.067-point lower Overall score than Full Compact accompanies 93.7% fewer input+output tokens. Together, these results support SPRA as a usable representation of scientific content and a foundation for research workflows that connect, inspect, and reuse paper-level research objects. The SPRA resource is accessible through the SciVerse scholarly-data platform.

Ranking and Partitioning of Attention Heads for Sparse and Continual Reinforcement Learning Fine-tuning
用于稀疏与持续强化学习微调的注意力头排序与划分
We study sparse and continual reinforcement learning fine-tuning of language models through attention-head ranking and partitioning. For sparse learning, we rank attention heads by gradient norm to identify a small subset of heads that can be trained effectively. For continual learning, we partition heads across tasks to reduce cross-task interference and preserve previously learned capabilities. Theoretically, we relate gradient norm to training effectiveness, and further characterize why sparse, separated head updates can remain stable. Empirically, across math, instruction-following, and coding tasks, tuning higher-ranked heads consistently outperforms tuning lower-ranked ones while often matching denser baselines, and head partitioning improves retention while preserving adaptation to new tasks.
中文翻译
我们通过注意力头排序与划分，研究语言模型的稀疏与持续强化学习微调。对于稀疏学习，我们根据梯度范数对注意力头进行排序，以识别出一小部分可有效训练的头。对于持续学习，我们将头在不同任务间划分，以减少跨任务干扰并保留先前学习的能力。理论上，我们将梯度范数与训练有效性关联，并进一步阐明为何稀疏且分离的头更新能保持稳定。实证上，在数学、指令遵循和编码任务中，对高排名头进行微调始终优于低排名头，且常能达到更密集基线的表现；头划分则提升了保留能力，同时保持对新任务的适应性。
👤 Ruizhe Shi, Zaiwen Yang, Qiubo Wang, Banghua Zhu, Maryam Fazel 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

DocPilot: Interactive Agent Orchestration and Training for Long Visual Document Understanding
DocPilot：面向长视觉文档理解的交互式智能体编排与训练
Multimodal long-document question answering requires models to seek sparse evidence across pages and modalities, understand heterogeneous document content, and reason over the collected evidence. Even advanced vision-language models (VLMs) can overlook sparse evidence and struggle with cross-modal reasoning in a single pass. Existing document agents further introduce multi-step interaction, but some still rely on predefined workflows or coarse-grained actions, limiting the flexibility and granularity of document reasoning. We introduce DocPilot, an agentic document framework that supports expressive orchestration and makes complex document solving learnable by small language models (SLMs). DocPilot orchestrates evidence seeking through page-level navigation and fine-grained evidence localization, and integrates local visual inspection, code-based numerical reasoning, and cross-modal reasoning for multimodal understanding. To transfer these capabilities to small models, we propose curriculum-based agentic training that progresses from single-ability learning to adaptive orchestration through supervision from data-grounded reasoning traces and executable interaction trajectories. Across MMLongBench-Doc, DocBench, FinRAGBench-V, and MADQA, DocPilot achieves strong gains with frontier VLM backbones, while DocPilot-4B delivers competitive performance against substantially larger models, demonstrating strong effectiveness and generalization across diverse long-document reasoning settings. The code is available at https://github.com/HKUSTDial/DocPilot.
中文翻译
多模态长文档问答要求模型在跨页和跨模态中寻找稀疏证据，理解异构文档内容，并对收集的证据进行推理。即使先进的视觉-语言模型（VLMs）也可能忽略稀疏证据，并在单次遍历中难以处理跨模态推理。现有文档智能体虽引入多步交互，但仍依赖预定义工作流或粗粒度动作，限制了文档推理的灵活性与粒度。我们提出DocPilot，一种支持表达性编排的智能体文档框架，使小型语言模型（SLMs）能够学习复杂文档求解。DocPilot通过页面级导航和细粒度证据定位实现证据搜寻，并整合局部视觉检查、基于代码的数值推理和跨模态推理以实现多模态理解。为将这些能力迁移至小型模型，我们提出基于课程的智能体训练方法，通过数据驱动的推理轨迹和可执行交互路径的监督，从单一能力学习逐步过渡到自适应编排。在MMLongBench-Doc、DocBench、FinRAGBench-V和MADQA等多个基准上，DocPilot在前沿VLM骨干模型上取得显著提升，而DocPilot-4B在性能上与远超其规模的模型相当，展现出在多样化长文档推理场景中的强大有效性与泛化能力。代码已公开于https://github.com/HKUSTDial/DocPilot。

ZoomCap: Evidence-Routed Visual Zooming for Recoverable Visual Information Transfer
ZoomCap：基于证据路由的视觉缩放以实现可恢复的视觉信息传递
Detailed captions transfer visual information into vision-language model supervision, but quality is easily conflated with length: long single-pass outputs still miss small objects, text, and spatial relations or promote uncertain observations into confident claims. We formulate fine-grained captioning as recoverable visual information transfer, in which a teacher acquires missing evidence under a finite observation budget and converts it into reusable supervision. ZoomCap uses a draft caption to route conditional knowledge retrieval, OCR-sensitive reading, coverage-gap recovery, and adaptive visual zooming, allocating deeper crops only to the most complex eligible region, deriving spatial relations from grounded geometry, and fusing non-duplicate evidence. We also introduce FactCycle, which scores caption-derived atomic claims on two blind anchors--source image (truthfulness) and reconstruction (realizability)--separating fabrication from generator loss claim by claim; human and multi-judge comparisons complement this fact-level view. Against Qwen3.5-397B-A17B captions, ZoomCap achieves an 81.9% human tie-adjusted preference score with a teacher over 10x smaller, and FactCycle counts 52.4 transferred claims per image vs. 31.5 for ScaleCap. The evidence also transfers: in continual pre-training, ZoomCap-450K improves the 11-benchmark average by 1.31-2.38 points over the strongest competing corpus and Ling-Tiny-VL-v3 gains 1.4 points on a production document/OCR monitor suite (1.5 on perception) at 60B tokens; in medical pre-training, 2.64M ZoomCap-captioned images improve five VQA benchmarks by 3.04 points (vs. 1.76 for same-image Gemini-3.1-Pro captions).
中文翻译
详细描述将视觉信息传递给视觉-语言模型监督，但质量容易与长度混淆：长篇一次性输出仍可能遗漏小物体、文本和空间关系，或把不确定的观察误认为确定性断言。我们将细粒度描述建模为可恢复的视觉信息传递，其中教师在有限观测预算下获取缺失证据，并将其转化为可重复使用的监督。ZoomCap利用草稿描述来引导条件知识检索、OCR敏感读取、覆盖空白恢复和自适应视觉缩放，仅对最复杂的合格区域分配更深层次的裁剪，从接地几何中推导空间关系，并融合非重复证据。我们还引入FactCycle，通过两个盲锚——源图像（真实性）和重构结果（可行性）——对生成的原子断言进行评分，逐条分离虚构与生成器损失；人工及多评委比较补充了这一事实层面的视角。与Qwen3.5-397B-A17B的描述相比，ZoomCap在教师规模小于10倍的情况下实现了81.9%的人类调整后偏好得分，FactCycle每张图像传递52.4个断言，而ScaleCap仅为31.5个。证据也实现了有效传递：在持续预训练中，ZoomCap-450K使11个基准平均提升1.31-2.38分，优于最强竞争语料库；Ling-Tiny-VL-v3在生产级文档/OCR监控套件上获得1.4分提升（感知任务达1.5分），使用600亿参数；在医疗预训练中，264万张ZoomCap标注图像使五个VQA基准提升3.04分（相同图像Gemini-3.1-Pro标注仅提升1.76分）。

PocketTSLM: Deploying an On-Device Soft-Prompt Time-Series Language Model for Privacy-Preserving Smartphone Reasoning
PocketTSLM：用于隐私保护智能手机推理的设备端软提示时间序列语言模型部署
Consumer phones and wearables increasingly collect physiological time-series data, yet most mobile systems treat these streams as summaries or text rather than as temporal evidence. This representation is poorly matched to personal health systems that need to retrieve, route, or summarize sensor evidence while preserving temporal structure. Cloud-hosted time-series language models avoid smartphone limits, but they repeatedly move sensitive physiology off the device and add latency and per-query compute cost. Mobile model alternatives that aggregate time-series data into statistical features or serialize raw samples as text either compress temporal structure or exhaust the context and memory budgets of small on-device models. We present PocketTSLM, a mobile-native deployment of OpenTSLM SoftPrompt, the soft-prompt variant of the OpenTSLM time-series language model. PocketTSLM runs fully on a consumer smartphone by injecting encoded time-series windows as embeddings instead of tokenizing time series as text. A numerical parity protocol verifies identical greedy-decoded token sequences between the Swift port and a Python reference using Apple's MLX machine-learning framework. Answers also match the PyTorch reference, although rationale wording can differ. An on-device context-economics benchmark compares the soft-prompt path with a text-serialized baseline on the same backbone. In our benchmark format, soft-prompting uses approximately 12 times fewer time-series tokens and reduces prompt-prefill cost. PocketTSLM processes 30-second Sleep-EDF electroencephalography clips for sleep staging and a 1,000-sample (10 s) 12-lead electrocardiogram clip from ECG-QA. In comparison, text-serialized baselines exceed the phone's context or memory budget at longer time-series windows. All inference runs entirely on the device, with no health data transmitted during inference.
中文翻译
消费级手机和可穿戴设备日益收集生理时间序列数据，但大多数移动系统将这些流视为摘要或文本，而非时间证据。这种表示方式与需要检索、路由或总结传感器证据并保持时间结构的个人健康系统不匹配。云端托管的时间序列语言模型虽能规避手机限制，但反复将敏感生理数据移出设备，增加延迟和每查询计算成本。移动端替代方案将时间序列数据聚合为统计特征，或将原始样本序列化为文本，要么压缩时间结构，要么耗尽小型设备模型的上下文和内存预算。我们提出PocketTSLM，是OpenTSLM SoftPrompt（OpenTSLM时间序列语言模型的软提示变体）的原生移动端部署。PocketTSLM通过将编码的时间序列窗口作为嵌入注入，而非将时间序列分词为文本，在消费级智能手机上完全运行。数值一致性协议验证了Swift版本与Python参考版本在Apple MLX框架下的贪婪解码标记序列一致；答案也与PyTorch参考一致，尽管理由表述可能不同。设备端上下文经济性基准将软提示路径与文本序列基线在同一主干网络上进行比较。在我们的基准格式中，软提示使用约12倍更少的时间序列标记，显著降低提示预填充成本。PocketTSLM可处理30秒的Sleep-EDF脑电图片段用于睡眠分期，以及来自ECG-QA的1000样本（10秒）12导联心电图片段。相比之下，文本序列基线在较长时间序列窗口时超出手机的上下文或内存预算。所有推理均在设备上完成，推理过程中无健康数据传输。

Bias Control and Injection for Multi-Agent Aggregation
多智能体聚合中的偏差控制与注入
Multi-agent reasoning with large language models (LLMs) raises a central question for collective decision-making: when agents disagree, whose decision should be trusted? Resolving such disagreements is difficult because agents' decisions are susceptible to non-semantic biases such as majority pressure and response-style preferences, which can obscure the quality of the underlying reasoning. Our analysis shows how bias can be both mitigated and exploited: bias control improves initial answer selection, while bias injection elicits decision instability that serves as a reliability signal. Building on these findings, we introduce a test-time aggregation framework that requires neither validation data nor prior knowledge of relative model strength. The framework implements bias control by presenting one style-normalized reasoning trace per distinct answer, and bias injection by placing each aggregating agent's initially selected answer in the minority. Overall, our framework achieves higher average accuracy and better token efficiency than competing non-oracle aggregation methods across three mathematical reasoning benchmarks and four heterogeneous LLM combinations. It also improves final-answer accuracy when applied to multi-agent debate and Mixture-of-Agents, without modifying their interaction or synthesis processes.
中文翻译
使用大语言模型（LLMs）进行多智能体推理提出了集体决策的核心问题：当智能体意见不一致时，应信任谁的决策？解决此类分歧困难，因为智能体的决策易受非语义偏差影响，如多数压力和响应风格偏好，这些偏差会掩盖底层推理的质量。我们的分析表明，偏差既可被缓解也可被利用：偏差控制有助于初始答案选择，而偏差注入则引发决策不稳定性，作为可靠性信号。基于这些发现，我们提出一种无需验证数据或先验模型强度知识的测试时聚合框架。该框架通过为每个不同答案呈现一个风格归一化的推理轨迹实现偏差控制，并通过将每个聚合智能体最初选择的答案置于少数地位来实现偏差注入。总体而言，该框架在三个数学推理基准和四种异构LLM组合上，平均准确率更高且token效率更优，优于其他非oracle聚合方法。它在应用于多智能体辩论和混合代理（Mixture-of-Agents）时，也提升了最终答案准确率，且无需修改其交互或合成过程。
👤 Sungjae Lee, Soheil Abbasloo, Jabin Koo, Jungseul Ok 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

Auditing Escalation Reliability in Healthcare LLM Agents: A Perturbation-Controlled Benchmark of Workflow-State Failures
医疗LLM智能体中升级可靠性的审计：工作流状态失败的扰动控制基准
Trustworthy biomedical AI requires reliable action selection when evidence is missing, stale, conflicting, or late, not only accurate answers on static inputs. We instantiate an episode-level evaluation protocol as 400 synthetic healthcare workflow episodes: 40 hand-authored semantic seeds across four task classes, two surface parameterizations, and five controlled variants, scored on ANSWER/ASK CLARIFICATION/ABSTAIN/ESCALATE under a pre-declared 5:1 missed-versus-unnecessary escalation cost. Reference actions are single-author study-policy labels, not clinician-adjudicated ground truth. Across five open-weight configurations (1.7B--9.7B), clean decision accuracy reaches 0.97, yet uncertainty-prompted models miss 32--97% of required escalations. The stronger 9.7B configuration narrows but does not remove the gap, falling from 0.97 clean accuracy to 0.78 under conflict. Verbalized confidence is inconsistent (ECE 0.10--0.54; failure AUROC 0.48--0.77), and a development-tuned confidence-only policy yields no utility gain. A transparent workflow-aware controller improves 5:1 utility for every model and reduces missed escalations to 0.12--0.24, at specificity 0.83--0.93. However, a 30-concept lexical stress test reduces each rule detector's recall from 1.00 on trigger phrases to 0.00 on unseen paraphrases, limiting the controller claim to this benchmark. Seed-clustered statistics, ablations, cross-task transfer, raw outputs, and manifests are packaged for audit. The results motivate explicit workflow-state evaluation while showing that apparently effective routing controls need their own generalization tests.
中文翻译
可信生物医学AI要求在证据缺失、过期、冲突或延迟时具备可靠的行动选择能力，而不仅仅是对静态输入给出准确答案。我们构建了一个基于episode级别的评估协议，包含400个合成医疗工作流episode：40个手工编写的语义种子，分布在四个任务类别中，两种表面参数化形式，以及五种受控变体，依据ANSWER/ASK CLARIFICATION/ABSTAIN/ESCALATE进行评分，预设5:1的漏报与误报升级成本比。参考动作为单作者研究策略标签，而非临床医生裁定的真实答案。在五个开源权重配置（1.7B–9.7B）中，干净决策准确率达0.97，但不确定性提示模型漏掉32%–97%的必要升级。更强的9.7B配置缩小但未消除差距，冲突条件下准确率从0.97降至0.78。口头置信度不一致（ECE 0.10–0.54；失败AUROC 0.48–0.77），且开发调优的仅置信度策略未带来实用增益。透明的工作流感知控制器改善了所有模型的5:1效用，将漏升级降至0.12–0.24，特异性达0.83–0.93。然而，30个概念的词汇压力测试使每个规则检测器的召回率从触发短语上的1.00降至未见改写上的0.00，限制了该控制器在本基准之外的适用性。种子聚类统计、消融实验、跨任务迁移、原始输出和清单均已打包用于审计。结果强调了显式工作流状态评估的重要性，同时表明看似有效的路由控制也需要自身的泛化测试。

Entertain, then Cancel: How Thinking Makes Reasoning Models Read Some and Or Literally
先娱乐，后取消：思考如何使推理模型理解‘某些’与‘或’的字面意义
When a speaker says ``some students passed'', listeners infer that not all of them did. Reasoning models now let users decide how long a model thinks before it answers, but we do not know what extra thinking does to such everyday inferences. We study this directly: we keep a model's weights fixed, change only how much it thinks, and record which reading it chooses. A second tool cuts the model's own reasoning at successive points and asks for an answer, showing where the reading changes. Thinking makes models read logical words as logic: it cancels the implicatures of \emph{some} (\emph{not all}) and \emph{or} (\emph{not both}) in most tested open reasoning models and in GPT-5, while context-driven inferences such as indirect answers improve. The trace shows why: models first entertain the enriched reading and cancel it later. Answering from that early reading, an exploratory inference-time remedy, recovers these implicatures without losing literal accuracy. Evaluations of reasoning models should measure how they interpret, not only how accurate they are.
中文翻译
当说话者说“一些学生通过了”时，听者会推断并非所有人都通过。如今的推理模型允许用户决定模型思考多久后再作答，但我们尚不清楚额外思考对这类日常推论有何影响。我们直接研究这一问题：固定模型权重，仅改变其思考时间，并记录模型选择的解读。第二个工具在模型自身推理过程中逐点切断并请求回答，揭示解读变化的位置。研究表明，思考使模型将逻辑词理解为逻辑含义：在多数测试的开放推理模型及GPT-5中，它取消了“some”（非全部）和“or”（非两者都）的隐含意义，同时上下文驱动的间接推理有所改善。追踪分析显示原因：模型首先考虑丰富解读，随后予以取消。从早期解读作答，作为一种探索性的推理期修复策略，可在不损失字面准确性的情况下恢复这些隐含意义。对推理模型的评估应衡量其如何理解，而不仅仅是准确性。
👤 Zhizhuo Kou, Jian Yang, Pengfei Hao, Junyu Luo, Jingshu Peng et al. 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

Who May Release an Agent's Promise? Standing over Commitments in Multi-Party Chat
谁可以解除代理的承诺？多角色对话中对承诺的立场分析
When an assistant serving a team makes a promise to Alice, who may cancel it? We call this standing: only the creditor (the person owed) or someone the creditor or operator policy authorizes may release a commitment, regardless of rank. In a controlled chat environment we vary who cancels and separate two errors: unauthorized release (obeying someone without standing) and override (executing a released promise). Across six models, smaller open ones override the creditor (Qwen3.5-9B: 69%); larger ones drop promises on a peer's direct word (Qwen3.8-27B: 89%; gpt-6.1-sol: 83%); nearly all act on a bystander's "go ahead after all". Oracles dissociate the errors: stating the rule or the creditor curbs unauthorized release but not override, which the latter even raises in three of four models; among them, only knowing whether the promise is still owed cuts override. Enabling thinking moves smaller models the way scale does. A self-kept commitment ledger stops override in four of five models; with a one-paragraph release policy it matches or beats the best oracle store, raising gpt-6-luna's strict accuracy from 49% (policy alone) to 83%. Code, prompts, outputs and labels are released.
中文翻译
当一个服务于团队的助手向Alice作出承诺后，谁有权取消该承诺？我们称之为“立场”：只有债权人（被欠方）或债权人或操作员政策授权的人才能释放承诺，无论其职位高低。在受控聊天环境中，我们改变取消者的身份，并区分两类错误：未经授权的释放（服从无权者）和覆盖（执行已被释放的承诺）。在六种模型中，较小的开源模型会服从同伴（Qwen3.5-9B：69%）；较大的模型则在同行直接命令下放弃承诺（Qwen3.8-27B：89%；gpt-6.1-sol：83%）；几乎所有模型都会响应旁观者的“那好吧”。预言者能分离这两类错误：陈述规则或指出债权人可抑制未经授权的释放，但无法阻止覆盖，甚至在四种模型中的三种中反而加剧了覆盖；其中，只有知道承诺是否仍有效才能减少覆盖。启用思考能力使小型模型的表现趋近于规模带来的效果。一个由模型自我维护的承诺账本可阻止五种模型中的四种发生覆盖；配合一段简短的释放政策，其表现达到或超过最佳预言者存储，使gpt-6-luna的严格准确率从49%（仅政策）提升至83%。代码、提示、输出和标签均已公开。
👤 Lan Wei, Bingyu Shen, Jiaqi Tang, Qing Lu, Kuan Huang et al. 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

Judging the Judges: Confidence and Disagreement in Rubrics as Rewards
评判评判者：评分标准中的置信度与分歧作为奖励
Rubrics as Rewards (RaR) extends reinforcement learning to non-verifiable domains by using an LLM judge to score responses against a structured rubric. Prior work has focused on improving the rubric or strengthening the single judge, with comparatively little attention to how different single or multiple judges might be used during RaR training. We first show that judge identity alone produces a substantial spread in scores when training Qwen3-4B-Base on RaR-Medicine and RaR-Science across five frontier and open-weight judges. Building on this, we propose RaRE (Rubrics as Rewards with Ensembles), a novel judge ensemble strategy for RaR with two mechanisms: a soft-Likert reward that uses judge logprobs to weight more confident judges more heavily, and a drop-on-disagreement mask that excludes high-disagreement rollouts from the GRPO update. We find that this design is critical: while a naive average of judge scores fails to outperform the best single judge, RaRE outperforms every single-judge RaR variant on both HealthBench and GPQA Diamond, with per-theme analysis showing that it inherits the complementary strengths of its constituent judges. Our findings establish judge ensembling as a compelling axis for improving RaR.

CARE-SD: Explainable Reasoning Architecture for Local SLM Self-Disclosure Analysis in Textual Data
CARE-SD：用于文本数据中局部小语言模型自披露分析的可解释推理架构
Cybersecurity interviews uncover critical security experiences, behaviors, and privacy concerns often not fully captured by conventional technical assessments. Consequently, identifying genuine self-disclosures is challenging because statements are context-dependent, categories overlap, and automated interpretations may introduce unsupported claims. Locally deployed Small Language Models (SLMs) protect sensitive participant data by keeping processing within controlled environments, but their limited reasoning capabilities make simple prompting insufficient for context-aware and factually reliable analysis. This study developed CARE-SD (Context-Adaptive Reasoning and Explanation for Self-Disclosure), a novel four-module architecture integrating context-adaptive processing, hierarchical disclosure reasoning, explanation-consistency verification, and cross-batch reconciliation. We evaluated three local SLMs, including Llama 1B, Qwen 1.5B, and Llama 3B, using 82 independently annotated cybersecurity interviews across five organizational sectors. Comparisons included conventional classifiers, three prompting strategies, larger 8B--32B models, and architectural ablations. Two independent reviewers assessed disclosure labels, factual claims, and explanation correctness. CARE-SD improved the accuracy and evidence reliability of SLMs, increasing Llama 3B Macro-F1 from 0.80 to 0.88 while reducing unsupported claims by 60\%. Remarkably, the 3B model matched the 20B reference model in category Macro-F1, demonstrating the effectiveness of the architecture without requiring a larger model.

网络安全访谈揭示了关键的安全经验、行为和隐私关切，这些往往无法被传统技术评估完全捕捉。因此，识别真实的自披露具有挑战性，因为陈述具有上下文依赖性，类别重叠，且自动化解读可能引入未经支持的主张。本地部署的小型语言模型（SLMs）通过将处理保留在受控环境中来保护敏感参与者数据，但其有限的推理能力使得简单提示不足以实现上下文感知且事实可靠的分析。本研究开发了CARE-SD（自披露的上下文适应推理与解释），一种新型四模块架构，整合了上下文适应处理、层级披露推理、解释一致性验证和跨批次协调。我们使用82个独立标注的网络安全访谈（涵盖五个组织领域）评估了三种本地SLMs（Llama 1B、Qwen 1.5B、Llama 3B）。比较包括传统分类器、三种提示策略、更大的8B–32B模型以及架构消融实验。两名独立评审员评估了披露标签、事实主张和解释正确性。CARE-SD提升了SLMs的准确性和证据可靠性，使Llama 3B的宏F1从0.80提升至0.88，同时将未经支持的主张减少60%。值得注意的是，3B模型在类别宏F1上达到了20B参考模型的水平，证明了该架构的有效性，无需依赖更大模型。

Kolab: Benchmarking Human-Agent Collaboration in Long-Horizon Workflows
Kolab：长周期工作流中人机协作的基准测试
When humans collaborate with AI agents on long-horizon tasks, what happens to the agents' contributions? For solving long-horizon tasks, humans place greater importance on ongoing interaction with AI agents (e.g., revising plans) than on an agent's ability to complete the task in a single attempt. However, most agentic benchmarks have focused more on automation. Strong performance on such benchmarks does not imply effective real-world collaboration. Professional workflows offer a useful setting, as they often involve interdependent steps. To address this gap, we introduce Kolab, a new dataset of 115+ hours of screen recordings of 50 professionals from 40 occupations collaborating with agents on realistic, long-horizon problems. Kolab also contains participants' collaboration and output quality judgments. Our analysis reveals diverse collaboration patterns, highlighting the importance of when and how professionals coordinate with agents. A collaborative agent must do two things that automation benchmarks never test: anticipate whether experts will adopt, modify, or reject its contribution once it leaves the chat window to guide its next steps, and assess collaboration quality from the unfolding workflow without constant expert feedback. Our benchmark operationalizes both: (1) forecasting how professionals use agent contributions at diverse workflows, and (2) verifying their judgments of collaboration satisfaction and output quality. Findings show that frontier models, both agentic and non-agentic, overestimate collaboration with experts and struggle to distinguish between high- and low-quality collaboration. Longer workflow context does not consistently improve these predictions. As frontier agents cannot yet understand long-horizon collaboration with experts, we position Kolab as a valuable dataset for studying human-agent collaboration grounded in real professional workflows.
中文翻译
当人类与AI智能体协作完成长周期任务时，智能体的贡献会发生什么变化？为解决长周期任务，人类更重视与AI智能体的持续互动（如修订计划），而非智能体一次性完成任务的能力。然而，大多数智能体基准测试更侧重自动化。在这些基准上表现优异并不意味着在真实世界中具备有效协作能力。专业工作流程提供了一个有用场景，因其通常包含相互依赖的步骤。为填补这一空白，我们引入Kolab，一个包含50名来自40种职业的专业人士在真实长周期问题中与智能体协作的115小时以上屏幕录制数据集。Kolab还包含参与者对协作过程和输出质量的判断。我们的分析揭示了多样的协作模式，强调了专业人士何时何地与智能体协调的重要性。一个协作智能体必须做到两件事，而自动化基准从未测试过：一是预测专家在智能体离开聊天窗口后是否会采纳、修改或拒绝其贡献，从而指导下一步行动；二是无需持续专家反馈，就能从正在进行的工作流中评估协作质量。我们的基准实现了这两点：(1) 预测专家在不同工作流中如何使用智能体贡献；(2) 验证其对协作满意度和输出质量的判断。研究发现，前沿模型（无论是否具备代理特性）普遍高估与专家的协作，并难以区分高质量与低质量协作。更长的工作流上下文并未持续改善这些预测。由于前沿智能体尚无法理解与专家的长周期协作，我们将Kolab定位为基于真实专业工作流研究人机协作的重要数据集。

Planning Commitment of LLM under Partial Observability
部分可观测环境下大语言模型的规划承诺
LLM-based planning agents commit to plans of different lengths before interacting with a partially observable environment. Some aggressively commit to a long plan based on assumptions about unobserved states, while others conservatively plan only over what they have observed. Both behaviors emerge implicitly in pre-trained models, whether the LLM outputs a plan directly or generates a PDDL formulation for a classical solver. Aggressive commitment risks costly failures, while conservative commitment risks excessive LLM calls. This choice pits safety against efficiency, a trade-off that has been neither characterized systematically nor made controllable. We investigate aggressive and conservative LLM planners and formalizers on two partially observable domains, where priors are provided as either explicit statistics or common-sense knowledge. Across three LLMs, both strategies reach similar accuracy. Conservative methods achieve fewer failed attempts (safety) at the cost of more API calls (efficiency). Aggressive methods fail more often but replan efficiently only when priors are informative. Interestingly, analysis shows that LLMs treat the provided priors nearly as facts, which risks failure when priors are unreliable. We also explore hybrid strategies with tunable hyperparameters that adjust the balance between safety and efficiency.
中文翻译
基于大语言模型（LLM）的规划智能体在与部分可观测环境交互前，会承诺不同长度的计划。一些智能体基于对未观测状态的假设，激进地承诺长期计划；另一些则保守地仅基于已观测内容进行规划。这两种行为在预训练模型中隐式出现，无论LLM是直接输出计划，还是生成经典求解器可用的PDDL形式。激进承诺可能导致代价高昂的失败，而保守承诺则可能引发过多的LLM调用。这种选择在安全与效率之间形成权衡，但该权衡尚未被系统性刻画，也难以控制。我们研究了在两个部分可观测领域中，激进与保守的LLM规划者和形式化者的表现，其中先验信息以显式统计或常识知识形式提供。在三种LLM上，两种策略达到相似的准确性。保守方法以牺牲效率（更多API调用）换取更高的安全性（更少失败尝试）。激进方法失败率更高，但在先验信息有帮助时能高效重规划。有趣的是，分析表明LLM几乎将提供的先验视为事实，当先验不可靠时，这会带来失败风险。我们还探索了具有可调超参数的混合策略，以平衡安全与效率。

MemComposer: Learning to Assemble Memories for LLM Agents via Reinforcement Learning
MemComposer：通过强化学习学习为LLM智能体组装记忆
Agent memory enables large language model (LLM) agents to reuse past experiences without updating their parameters. However, semantic similarity alone does not guarantee memory usefulness: downstream performance depends on how memories are selected and presented to the agent. We propose MemComposer, a memory composer that jointly examines the current query and retrieved candidates to decide which experiences to include, how many to select, and how to order them. We train the composer using Experience Policy Optimization (ExPO), a GRPO-based framework that evaluates assembled memory contexts through complete agent interactions and uses task-success feedback to optimize the assembly policy. Across three $τ^2$-bench domains with two frozen executors, MemComposer outperforms the strongest baseline for each executor while reducing average memory-token usage by 16.9% and 4.9%, respectively. The learned policy also transfers to unseen domains, executors, and memory banks, and benefits from memories generated by stronger models without further training. These results demonstrate the value of learning query-specific memory assembly using downstream task feedback.

智能体记忆使大型语言模型（LLM）智能体能够在不更新参数的情况下复用过往经验。然而，仅靠语义相似性并不能保证记忆的有效性：下游性能取决于记忆如何被选择和呈现给智能体。我们提出了MemComposer，一种联合考察当前查询和检索候选的记忆组合器，以决定包含哪些经验、选择多少以及如何排序。我们使用基于GRPO的Experience Policy Optimization（ExPO）框架训练该组合器，通过完整的智能体交互评估组装的记忆上下文，并利用任务成功反馈优化组装策略。在三个τ²-bench领域中，使用两个冻结执行器，MemComposer在每个执行器上均优于最强基线，同时分别将平均记忆token使用量减少了16.9%和4.9%。所学策略还能迁移至未见过的领域、执行器和记忆库，并能从更强模型生成的记忆中获益而无需进一步训练。这些结果证明了利用下游任务反馈学习查询相关的记忆组装的价值。

Athanor: An Agent-Native System for Autonomous Post-Training Research
Athanor：用于自主后训练研究的原生智能体系统
Recursive self-improvement, in which AI systems build stronger AI systems, is one promising route to superintelligence. We study a weaker form that can be measured today: frontier agents post-training a small raw base model with public data, as a test of their research potential. We build Athanor, an open agent-native system for autonomous post-training research (AutoPostTrain) on real GPUs. We give the agents full research autonomy, low-friction engineering tools, and efficient starter code. The agents carry out the research themselves: they read datasets and evaluation results, build their training data, write and modify training algorithms, tune hyperparameters, and analyze the results to decide what to try next. They work in a sandbox and cannot use external teacher models. Athanor's services supply frozen evaluation, managed jobs, decontaminated data, a persistent research record, and a held-out suite the agents are unaware of. In a 104-hour campaign, a team of four Claude Opus 5.5 agents, each on an eight-GPU node and given no research advice, improves Qwen3-4B-Base from 15.9 to 67.3 on a six-task development suite and from 9.2 to 48.2 on a held-out suite, against 66.5 and 47.9 for the official Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration that a team of frontier agents can autonomously post-train a raw base model into a cross-domain generalist model that matches or surpasses the official instruct version on average. The campaign's full record shows that the agents reach this level by recombining known methods and each other's models: SFT on public reasoning data, RL with a rollout change one agent introduced, weight merges across agents, and rounds of self-distillation. With execution handled, today's agents already have the engineering and research ability to conquer this task. We hope Athanor provides a foundation for scaling autonomous AI research.

递归自我改进，即AI系统构建更强大的AI系统，是通向超智能的一条有前景路径。我们研究了一种较弱但可度量的形式：前沿智能体在训练后使用公开数据对小型原始基础模型进行微调，以此测试其科研潜力。我们构建了Athanor，一个开放的原生智能体系统，可在真实GPU上实现自主后训练研究（AutoPostTrain）。我们赋予智能体完全的研究自主权、低门槛工程工具和高效启动代码。智能体自行开展研究：阅读数据集和评估结果，构建训练数据，编写和修改训练算法，调整超参数，并分析结果以决定下一步尝试。它们在沙盒环境中工作，无法使用外部教师模型。Athanor的服务提供冻结评估、托管任务、去污染数据、持久的研究记录以及智能体未知的保留套件。在为期104小时的实验中，四名Claude Opus 5.5智能体（每台配备8个GPU节点，且未获得任何研究建议）将Qwen3-4B-Base在六任务开发套件上的表现从15.9提升至67.3，在保留套件上从9.2提升至48.2，分别超过官方Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507的66.5和47.9。据我们所知，这是首次展示一组前沿智能体能够自主地将原始基础模型微调为跨领域通用模型，并在平均表现上达到或超越官方指令版本。整个实验记录显示，智能体通过重新组合已知方法和彼此模型实现了这一成果：在公共推理数据上进行监督微调、引入一个智能体提出的滚动变化的强化学习、跨智能体权重合并以及多轮自蒸馏。在执行层面已由今日智能体完成的前提下，它们已具备征服此任务的工程与科研能力。我们希望Athanor能为自主AI研究的规模化提供基础。

Learning When to Think: Utility-Guided Adaptive Reasoning for Efficient Text Retrieval
学会何时思考：基于效用引导的自适应推理以实现高效文本检索
Reasoning-conditioned embeddings can improve retrieval by progressively refining semantic constraints. However, additional reasoning may introduce over-generalized or semantically noisy abstractions that weaken retrieval specificity. Existing methods largely supervise reasoning through final retrieval outcomes, providing limited guidance over the refinement process itself. We propose AURA, a framework that directly supervises intermediate reasoning states according to how well they separate relevant documents from hard distractors. AURA further trains a lightweight utility estimator over prefix-conditioned embeddings to adapt reasoning depth at inference time. On the reasoning-intensive BRIGHT benchmark, AURA-4B achieves 34.7 average nDCG@10 while using 2.8 reasoning steps and 96 generated tokens per query. Under matched deployment conditions, adaptive stopping reduces latency from 4.6 to 0.76 seconds relative to full-trajectory reasoning. Results on MS MARCO further suggest that this specialization need not substantially degrade conventional in-domain retrieval.
中文翻译
推理条件嵌入可通过逐步细化语义约束来提升检索效果。然而，额外的推理可能引入过度泛化或语义噪声的抽象，从而削弱检索的特异性。现有方法主要通过最终检索结果来监督推理过程，对精炼过程本身的指导有限。我们提出AURA框架，直接根据中间推理状态在区分相关文档与困难干扰项方面的表现来监督推理过程。AURA还训练一个轻量级效用估计算法，基于前缀条件嵌入在推理时自适应调整推理深度。在推理密集型的BRIGHT基准上，AURA-4B在每查询仅使用2.8次推理步骤和96个生成token的情况下，实现了34.7的平均nDCG@10。在匹配部署条件下，自适应停止将延迟从4.6秒降低至0.76秒，相比完整轨迹推理。MS MARCO上的结果进一步表明，这种专业化不会显著损害常规域内检索性能。

[2610.20951]
agentic-rl
LEV: Thinking Fast and Deep with Looped Decision Models
LEV：通过循环决策模型实现快速而深入的思考
Decision models answer typed questions about an input with one probability per option, computed in a single forward pass without generating text. Like the fast System 1 of dual-process theory, they answer at once, but their depth is fixed at training time, so they spend the same computation on easy and hard questions. Models that reason in generated text can think longer, but they pay for every extra step in decoded tokens. We present LEV, a decision model that thinks deeper by rerunning its layers in a loop instead of generating tokens. LEV builds on a language model pretrained as a loop, reads the options after every loop step and trains all steps jointly, so one checkpoint can answer at any depth. On three looped backbones, accuracy rises with depth on the same weights, mostly on hard questions, and a 2.6B LEV goes from 63.2% at depth one to 76.2% at depth four on JevBench. On-demand depth, set per question by a small stopper with a threshold fitted on unlabelled requests, saves 15% to 24% of the computation at a cost of under one accuracy point, level with or slightly above a fixed mix of depths. On a locked test set, the 1.4B and 3B models are 5.4 to 8.8 points more accurate than dense (non-looped) models and cascades of the same latency below 50 ms.
中文翻译
决策模型以单次前向传播计算每个选项的概率，无需生成文本即可回答关于输入的类型化问题。类似于双过程理论中的快速系统1，它们即时作答，但其深度在训练时固定，因此对简单和复杂问题投入相同的计算量。能够通过生成文本进行推理的模型可以思考更久，但每增加一步都会消耗额外的解码 token。我们提出 LEV，一种通过循环重运行其层而非生成 token 来实现更深层次思考的决策模型。LEV 基于预训练为循环结构的语言模型，在每次循环步骤后读取选项，并联合训练所有步骤，使得一个检查点可支持任意深度的推理。在三个循环骨干网络上，相同权重下准确率随深度提升，主要体现在难题上；一个 26 亿参数的 LEV 在 JevBench 上从深度 1 的 63.2% 提升至深度 4 的 76.2%。按需设置深度（由小型停止器根据未标注请求拟合的阈值决定）可在牺牲不到一个准确率点的情况下节省 15% 到 24% 的计算量，性能与固定深度混合方案相当或略优。在锁定测试集上，14 亿和 30 亿参数模型比同延迟下（低于 50 毫秒）的密集型（非循环）模型和级联模型分别高出 5.4 至 8.8 个百分点。
👤 Dingjie Song, Zhiling Yan, Yuxuan Zhang, Lichao Sun 📅 2026-10-14 🔗 arXiv
[2610.20926]
agent-benchmarkagent-memoryagentic-rl
SkillNEO: Non-Elitist Evolutionary Optimization of Agent Skills
SkillNEO：代理技能的非精英进化优化
Agent skills turn procedural knowledge into reusable artifacts, allowing LLM agents to accumulate and refine expertise through experience. Building on this, skill evolution iteratively improves skills using execution feedback. However, improvement may require several interdependent edits, so low-reward versions can still be worth developing. Requiring immediate reward gains can discard useful partial repairs before their benefits emerge. Non-elitist evolution allows new versions to receive further edits despite scoring below earlier ones. For agent skills, this calls for evidence of what to preserve and repair next. We introduce SkillNEO (Non-Elitist Evolutionary Optimization for Agent Skills), which separately selects a version to use and versions to keep improving. Comparing how related versions perform the same task reveals working steps to preserve and remaining failures to fix. It can also revisit and revise earlier versions using later evidence. Across 86 SkillsBench tasks in eight domains, including software engineering and office work, SkillNEO raises GPT-5.4 Mini's average reward from 49.1% to 66.4%, within GPT-5.5 and GPT-5.6 Luna's original-skill range. Mini executes tasks, proposes revisions, and edits skills; final evaluation uses the same task instances. Gains span all eight domains. The same skills improve stronger models without further editing. On the 41 tasks with modified skills, reuse improves Luna by 10.5 percentage points and places GPT-5.5 10.6 points above its published original-skill reference. Execution records show these skills can benefit stronger models even when Mini still fails some task requirements. Search histories also show lower-scoring versions leading to skills ultimately selected for use. Together, these findings show that skill evolution can turn a weaker model's partial progress into reusable procedural knowledge for improving more capable agents.

代理技能将程序性知识转化为可复用的成果，使大语言模型代理能够通过经验积累并精炼专长。在此基础上，技能演化通过执行反馈迭代改进技能。然而，改进可能需要多个相互依赖的修改，因此低奖励版本仍可能值得开发。要求即时奖励增长会过早丢弃尚未显现效益的部分修复。非精英进化允许新版本即使得分低于早期版本也继续获得修改。对于代理技能，这需要明确保留和修复的依据。我们提出 SkillNEO（用于代理技能的非精英进化优化），它分别选择使用版本和持续改进的版本。通过比较相关版本完成相同任务的表现，揭示应保留的有效步骤和需修复的失败环节。它还能利用后期证据重新审视和修订早期版本。在涵盖软件工程和办公工作等八个领域的 86 个 SkillsBench 任务中，SkillNEO 将 GPT-5.4 Mini 的平均奖励从 49.1% 提升至 66.4%，达到 GPT-5.5 和 GPT-5.6 Luna 原始技能范围内的水平。Mini 执行任务、提出修改建议并编辑技能；最终评估使用相同任务实例。收益覆盖全部八个领域。相同技能在无需进一步编辑的情况下也能提升更强模型的表现。在 41 个修改技能的任务中，复用使 Luna 提升 10.5 个百分点，使 GPT-5.5 超出其公开发布的原始技能基准 10.6 个百分点。执行记录显示，这些技能即使在 Mini 仍未能满足部分任务要求时，也能为更强模型带来收益。搜索历史也表明，低分版本最终导向了被选中使用的技能。这些发现共同表明，技能演化可将弱模型的部分进展转化为可复用的程序性知识，用于提升更强大代理的能力。

Wrong Votes, Right Gradients: Why Majority-Vote Rewards Track Verifiable RL
错误投票，正确梯度：为何多数投票奖励能追踪可验证的强化学习
Reinforcement learning with self-generated labels, such as majority vote over a model's own samples, often recovers most of the gain of training with verifiable rewards. This is puzzling from the usual prompt-level view, in which majority vote only returns a model's modal answer and cannot add information about correctness. We argue that the relevant unit is the batch-level update. Because parameters are shared, a prompt's own gradient accounts for less than 1\% of the change in that prompt's output at practical batch sizes, so a wrong label matters only to the extent that label errors share a direction across prompts. We decompose the majority-vote update into shrinkage and misdirection relative to the gold update, and show that misdirection vanishes with batch size whenever errors are incoherent across prompts, however often the labels are wrong. Measuring per-response gradients on five models, we find that majority-vote errors are as incoherent as a random-reward null, that the majority-vote update agrees with the gold update as closely as two independent gold updates agree with each other, and that its noise-corrected cosine with the gold update is 1 within error. Randomly flipping up to 30\% of gold labels leaves the update direction intact, while the same fraction flipped by a shared rule destroys it, and rewards that favor a kind of response on every prompt, such as a learned reward model, fall measurably short. The equivalence holds at every checkpoint we examine but is local: gold-reward training still ends ahead on held-out majority accuracy. Error coherence, not error rate, determines when self-generated labels can stand in for verification.
中文翻译
使用自生成标签的强化学习（如模型自身样本的多数投票）通常能恢复大部分使用可验证奖励训练所获得的收益。从传统的提示级别视角来看，这一现象令人困惑——多数投票仅返回模型的最可能答案，无法提供关于正确性的额外信息。我们认为关键单位是批次级别的更新。由于参数共享，在实际批次大小下，某个提示自身的梯度对输出变化的影响不足 1%，因此错误标签的影响仅取决于标签错误在提示间是否具有方向一致性。我们将多数投票更新分解为相对于理想更新的收缩与误导向，并证明当错误在提示间不一致时，无论标签错误频率如何，误导向会随着批次规模增大而消失。在五个模型上测量每响应梯度，我们发现多数投票错误与随机奖励零假设一样不一致，多数投票更新与理想更新的一致性相当于两个独立的理想更新之间的相关性，且其噪声校正后的余弦相似度在误差范围内等于 1。随机翻转最多 30% 的理想标签不会破坏更新方向，而由统一规则翻转相同比例则会彻底破坏方向；那些在每个提示上偏好某种回答（如学习得到的奖励模型）的奖励函数则明显表现不足。该等价关系在我们考察的所有检查点上均成立，但具有局部性：使用理想奖励训练的模型在保留的多数投票准确率上仍领先。错误的一致性，而非错误率，决定了自生成标签能否替代验证。

Probing Negation Understanding in Vision-Language Models With Object Attention
通过对象注意力探究视觉-语言模型的否定理解能力
Negation is a fundamental aspect of language understanding and it is important vision-language models (VLMs) are able to process it effectively. We investigate how VLMs process negation by analysing attention patterns of different models. We draw comparisons between human gaze and attention in models when processing affirmative vs negated statements. We compare object level model attention to human eye-fixations in both binary and multary settings, evaluating with both synthetic and realistic datasets. We find models generally do not follow human-like attention patterns when resolving negation, suggesting they rely on mechanisms outside of attention. While layerwise analysis shows that PaliGemma2-3B shifts object attention to the alternative under negation, our headwise ablations suggest this is a byproduct rather than a negation processing mechanism. Additionally, we find that LLaVA-1.5-7B displays a spatial bias towards left-side objects. Our evaluation of models on downstream tasks shows that all models have a discrepancy between affirmative and negated questions highlighting the need to better understand the underlying processing of negation to close this performance gap. Code available at https://github.com/otmive/negation_attention

Evolving Together, Failing Apart: The Skill-Policy Co-evolution Trap in Self-Improving Agents
共同进化，分道扬镳：自改进智能体中的技能-策略共演化陷阱
Skill-policy co-evolution, in which an agent's policy model and its skill bank improve each other during training, is a promising paradigm for self-improving agents. Current evaluations score the co-evolved policy on the final bank it trained with (the delivered bank). After deployment, however, skills may be rewritten, added or removed more frequently than the policy model is retrained, leaving the policy to operate on a drifted skill bank that neither the training objective nor existing evaluation protocols address. In this paper, we first establish, through theoretical and empirical analysis, that the fragility hidden by this blind spot is not incidental. The acceptance rule these methods share (cooperative acceptance) ties a policy's competence to the bank it co-evolved with, leaving it fragile once skills drift, regardless of the implementation; empirically, sensitivity to skill drift grows as co-evolution training proceeds. We therefore introduce the CO-evolution Drift Audit (CODA), a deployment-oriented evaluation protocol that simulates skill drift and, alongside success rate under worst-case drift, introduces two new metrics: a consistency rate under meaning-preserving edits and a resistance rate to corrupted skills. Across four co-evolution methods and five benchmarks (six settings), worst-case success is 5-13 points below the peak measured for each delivered pair, erasing 52-93% of the delivered bank's gain over the no-skill baseline. CODA thus reframes the target of co-evolution, from peak performance on the co-evolved bank to the ability to harness skills however they evolve, and provides a shared standard against which future methods can be audited.
中文翻译
技能-策略共演化是一种有前景的自改进智能体范式，其中智能体的策略模型与其技能库在训练过程中相互提升。当前评估方法仅在最终训练完成的技能库（交付库）上评分。然而，在部署后，技能可能比策略模型更频繁地被重写、添加或删除，导致策略运行在漂移后的技能库上，而训练目标和现有评估协议均未涵盖此情况。本文通过理论与实证分析首次揭示，这种盲点所隐藏的脆弱性并非偶然。这些方法共享的接受规则（合作接受）将策略能力绑定于其共演化过的技能库，一旦技能发生漂移，无论实现方式如何，策略都会变得脆弱；实证结果表明，随着共演化训练的推进，对技能漂移的敏感性持续上升。为此，我们提出共演化漂移审计（CODA），一种面向部署的评估协议，模拟技能漂移，并引入两项新指标：在语义保持编辑下的稳定性率和对损坏技能的抗性率。在四种共演化方法和五个基准测试（六个场景）中，最坏情况下的成功率比每对交付库的峰值低5-13分，抹去了交付库相对于无技能基线52%-93%的增益。因此，CODA重新定义了共演化的目标：从在共演化技能库上的峰值性能，转变为能够灵活利用任意演化形式的技能。该协议为未来方法提供了统一的审计标准。

Confidence-Calibrated Inference Expansion for Evaluator-Guided Test-Time Reasoning
基于置信度校准的评估器引导型测试时推理扩展
Evaluator-guided reasoning presents a local control problem at every step: trust the current answer, return a fallback, or spend more inference. We formulate this choice as a compute-aware three-action controller, scoring terminal actions by predicted answer success and expansion by a rollout-based continuation-success surrogate, with compute cost priced explicitly. A local action-gap analysis characterizes when score errors can change a decision, while an expected regret bound accounts for errors along the realized expansion path. Guided by this analysis, we learn and calibrate action-specific confidence estimates, with local uncertainty penalties for ambiguous decisions. On held-out width expansion and revision tasks, the controller improves observed utility over calibration-selected fixed policies while reducing normalized online compute by about 30% and 16%, respectively. Across four Qwen3-8B seeds, compute is lower than fitted Q in both settings, while utility differences are small and mixed. The results frame evaluator-guided reasoning as resource allocation under uncertainty, rather than a uniformly stronger reasoner.

评估器引导的推理在每一步都面临局部控制问题：信任当前答案、返回备用方案，或投入更多推理计算。我们将这一选择建模为一个考虑计算成本的三动作控制器，通过预测答案成功率对终止动作打分，通过基于回溯的延续成功率代理对扩展动作打分，并显式定价计算成本。局部动作差距分析刻画了得分误差可能改变决策的情形，期望遗憾界则考虑了实际扩展路径上的误差。基于此分析，我们学习并校准了特定动作的置信度估计，并对模糊决策施加局部不确定性惩罚。在保留的宽度扩展和修订任务上，该控制器在观测效用上优于校准选择的固定策略，同时分别将归一化在线计算量降低约30%和16%。在四个Qwen3-8B种子实验中，两种情境下的计算量均低于拟合Q，而效用差异较小且混合。结果将评估器引导推理重新框架为在不确定性下的资源分配问题，而非一个始终更强的推理者。

Gibbs-Gap Policy Optimization for Off-Policy RL with Verifiable Rewards
可验证奖励下的非策略强化学习的吉布斯-间隙策略优化
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards often trains language models on responses generated by a different, possibly stale behavior policy. Correcting this mismatch with learner-to-behavior importance weights can introduce high variance for long responses. We introduce Gibbs-Gap Policy Optimization (GGPO), which minimizes a log-mean-exp contrast of KL-adjusted rewards among responses to the same prompt, without learner-to-behavior importance weights. We derive GGPO from KL-regularized policy optimization: on policy, its population loss is exactly the KL-regularized optimality gap, and under suitable coverage the learner's single-response expected reward is at least the behavior policy's best-of-$K$ reward, minus terms for the raw finite-group loss, regularization, and finite training data. We characterize reference refresh through exact Gibbs-target composition and a finite-data final-policy reward bound under local reference control and tokenwise behavior mismatch. This refresh is empirically important: in an ablation, fixed-reference GGPO scores $32.96$ on the five-benchmark problem-weighted average, versus $46.58$ with the default refresh schedule. Experiments on mathematical reasoning further show that GGPO outperforms GRPO and AGRO under training-inference mismatch and on fixed offline data, while maintaining its performance as controlled policy staleness grows.

具有可验证奖励的强化学习通常在由不同且可能过时的行为策略生成的响应上训练语言模型。使用学习者到行为策略的重要性权重纠正这种不匹配，可能导致长响应的高方差。我们提出吉布斯-间隙策略优化（GGPO），通过最小化同一提示下多个响应之间的KL调整奖励的对数-均值-指数对比，无需学习者到行为策略的重要性权重。我们从KL正则化策略优化推导出GGPO：在策略上，其总体损失恰好是KL正则化的最优性差距；在适当覆盖条件下，学习者的单次响应期望奖励至少等于行为策略的最佳K个响应奖励，减去原始有限组损失、正则化项和有限训练数据带来的项。我们通过精确吉布斯目标组合和局部参考控制与逐标记行为不匹配下的有限数据最终策略奖励边界，刻画了参考刷新机制。该刷新在实证中至关重要：在消融实验中，固定参考的GGPO在五基准问题加权平均上得分为32.96，而默认刷新调度下得分为46.58。在数学推理实验中进一步表明，GGPO在训练-推理不匹配和固定离线数据下优于GRPO和AGRO，同时在受控策略陈旧性增长时仍保持性能稳定。
