A Self-Improving Agent that Grows its Own DSL: Oracle-Free Operator Induction for Exact Question Answering
能自我生长领域专用语言的自改进智能体：无Oracle操作符归纳用于精确问答
A self-improving agent has to decide which new skills to keep, and in practice it keeps whatever earns a high reward, convinces a judge model, or compresses its library, even when the skill is wrong. That is fine until the answers have to be exactly right: over a table of transactions, a total is either right or wrong. We ask a harder question: can an agent grow what it can compute with no reward, no judge, and no answer key, and still be exactly right? A single frozen model runs two nested loops over a small, exact domain-specific language (DSL). The inner loop answers new questions only when two independently written expressions agree on every record. The outer loop discovers its own missing capability and authors an operator for it, admitted only when an independent second implementation agrees and the result obeys a conservation law, all without ground truth. The inner loop roughly triples the answerable-question set, and the outer loop self-extends on disjoint starting sets, inventing operators outside the starting closure; a no-induction control answers none of the new questions. The admission rule is empirically sound: it rejects 138 of 138 buggy operator variants under an independent reference, even one written by a different model family, where the admit-if-it-runs, self-consistency, and compression signals the field relies on admit most of them. Unmodified, the same mechanism self-extends on two further domains with unrelated schemas, business-ledger and clinical-encounter question answering, growing their answerable sets more than tenfold and nearly twentyfold. The loop transfers across model families too: run with further proposers, the gate stays sound and independent families self-extend comparably, with only the induction yield tracking each proposer's DSL fluency.
中文翻译
自改进智能体必须决定保留哪些新技能，实践中它会保留那些获得高奖励、说服评判模型或压缩其知识库的技能，即使这些技能本身是错误的。这在答案只需大致正确时尚可接受，但当答案必须完全准确时——例如在交易表中求总和，结果要么正确要么错误——就不可接受。我们提出一个更难的问题：智能体能否在没有奖励、没有评判模型、也没有答案键的情况下，自行发展其计算能力，同时仍保证答案完全正确？一个单一冻结模型在小规模精确领域专用语言（DSL）上运行两个嵌套循环。内层循环仅在两个独立编写的表达式在每条记录上一致时才回答新问题。外层循环发现自身缺失的能力并编写对应操作符，仅当第二个独立实现也达成一致且结果满足守恒定律时才被接纳，整个过程无需真实标签。内层循环使可回答问题集扩大约三倍，外层循环在互不重叠的起始集合上实现自我扩展，发明了初始闭包之外的操作符；无归纳控制组无法回答任何新问题。接纳规则在实证中表现稳健：在独立参考下拒绝了全部138个有缺陷的操作符变体，包括由不同模型家族编写的版本，而“只要运行成功”、“自一致性”和“压缩性”等现有信号则大多接受了它们。未经修改，相同机制在另外两个无关模式域（商业账簿和临床就诊问答）上也实现了自我扩展，使可回答问题集分别增长超过十倍和近二十倍。该循环机制还能跨模型家族迁移：使用更多提议者时，门控依然稳健，不同家族的智能体表现出相当的自我扩展能力，仅归纳产出反映各提议者对DSL的熟练程度。
👤 Yun-Shiuan Chuang, Yirou Ge, Yixi Li, Yiming Bian, Zijie Pan et al. 📅 2026-10-14 🔗 arXiv
[2610.20805]
agentic-rl
When Reasoning Traces Conflict with Evidence: How Answer Configuration Shapes Evidence Following
当推理轨迹与证据冲突时：答案配置如何影响对证据的遵循
A reasoning trace retained at answer time plays two roles: it can raise accuracy, and it can compete with the current evidence when the two disagree. We disentangle these roles by crossing verified evidence versions with the traces generated from each and answering from the same traces and evidence under two configurations: continuation of the model's own thinking and a separate answer call. Across three model families (gpt-oss, Gemma4, Qwen3) on HotpotQA and MuSiQue, the separate call raises evidence-following over continuation by 29.9 to 60.6 points. On the same pairs, removing the trace lowers accuracy by similar amounts under both configurations. Yet continuation repeats the stale conclusion in most conflicts, and the separate call follows the current evidence far more often. Benefiting from a trace and deferring to it are therefore distinct properties. Further analyses show that the final line of a trace carries most of its value, gold evidence helps most before the trace is written, distractors weaken correction under a separate call, and renewed reasoning, unlike a verification instruction, follows the current evidence in most conflicts. Because trace deletion alone cannot reveal which source an answer follows, evaluations should report both properties together with the answer configuration.
中文翻译
在生成答案时保留的推理轨迹具有双重作用：它可能提升准确性，但在与当前证据不一致时也可能与其竞争。我们通过将验证过的证据版本与每种轨迹生成结果交叉，并在两种配置下从相同轨迹和证据中作答，来分离这两种作用：延续模型自身的思考过程，以及调用独立的答案生成。在HotpotQA和MuSiQue数据集上，针对三个模型家族（gpt-oss、Gemma4、Qwen3），独立调用相比延续方式使对证据的遵循度提升了29.9至60.6个百分点。在同一组实验中，无论哪种配置下移除轨迹都会导致准确率下降相似幅度。然而，延续模式在多数冲突情况下重复旧结论，而独立调用则更频繁地遵循当前证据。因此，受益于轨迹与依赖轨迹是两个不同的属性。进一步分析表明，轨迹的最后一行承载了大部分价值；黄金证据在轨迹书写前最有助于提升性能；干扰项会削弱独立调用下的纠正能力；而重新推理（不同于验证指令）在多数冲突中能更好地遵循当前证据。由于仅删除轨迹无法揭示答案所依据的来源，评估应同时报告这两个属性及答案配置。
👤 Shaoang Li, Yanhang Shi, Jian Li 📅 2026-10-14 🔗 arXiv
[2610.20780]

ARIA: Runtime Inspection and Amendment for Multi-Agent Systems
ARIA：多智能体系统的运行时检查与修正
In LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS), a local mistake can propagate as other agents build on it, turning a correctable error into a failed task. Diagnosing completed runs or repeatedly re-executing them to search for corrections does not provide a learned policy for intervening while execution is still in progress. We present Agent Runtime Inspection and Amendment (ARIA), a framework for learning when and how to repair ongoing multi-agent executions. Our key intuition is to turn execution-verified repairs into reusable supervision, moving repair search into data construction and training so that a learned model can intervene at runtime without repeatedly testing alternatives. First, we collect over 24{,}000 successful and failed trajectories across four multi-agent frameworks, six benchmarks, and eight host models. From these trajectories, we construct the ARIA dataset, to our knowledge the first large-scale dataset pairing failed multi-agent trajectories with execution-verified repairs. It contains 3{,}953 verified repair pairs, each specifying what went wrong, where to intervene, and how execution should continue. Then, we develop the ARIA framework for MAS-agnostic runtime inspection and amendment, designed to integrate with any MAS that exposes execution trajectories and supports continuation replacement. Using verified repairs as training examples, we teach a compact model to recognize when intervention is needed and how to correct an ongoing run. The aim is to learn repair strategies that apply to related failures in new configurations, rather than merely reproduce individual corrections. Finally, the trained ARIA model demonstrates strong repair performance in both post-hoc and runtime settings, with generalization to unseen frameworks and host models. Our code is available at https://github.com/LabRAI/ARIA.
中文翻译
在基于大语言模型的多智能体系统（MAS）中，局部错误可能被其他智能体继承并传播，导致原本可纠正的错误演变为任务失败。事后诊断已完成的运行或反复重执行以寻找修正方法，无法提供在执行过程中学习干预策略的能力。我们提出Agent Runtime Inspection and Amendment（ARIA）框架，用于学习何时以及如何修复正在进行的多智能体执行过程。核心思想是将执行验证后的修复转化为可复用的监督信号，将修复搜索转变为数据构建与训练过程，从而使学习到的模型能够在运行时进行干预，而无需反复测试替代方案。首先，我们在四个多智能体框架、六个基准测试和八个主模型上收集了超过24,000条成功与失败的轨迹。基于这些轨迹，我们构建了ARIA数据集——据我们所知，首个大规模配对失败多智能体轨迹与执行验证修复的数据集。该数据集包含3,953对已验证的修复，每对明确指出了问题所在、干预位置以及执行应如何继续。随后，我们开发了ARIA框架，实现对多智能体系统无关的运行时检查与修正，设计为可集成到任何暴露执行轨迹并支持替换继续行为的MAS中。利用验证修复作为训练样本，我们训练了一个紧凑模型，使其能够识别是否需要干预以及如何修正正在进行的运行。目标是学习适用于新配置中相关故障的通用修复策略，而非简单复制个别修正。最终，训练好的ARIA模型在事后和运行时设置中均表现出强大的修复性能，并能泛化到未见过的框架和主模型。代码已公开于https://github.com/LabRAI/ARIA。
👤 Bolin Shen, Yushun Dong 📅 2026-10-14 🔗 arXiv

TurboMamba: Faster Inference, Faster Training, and Better Prediction with Lexical Memory
TurboMamba：通过词汇记忆实现更快推理、更快训练与更好预测
We introduce TurboMamba, a language-model architecture for faster inference, faster training, and better token prediction. A global Mamba-3 model runs once per completed token block. A small Transformer uses this global context and earlier tokens within the block to predict the next token. Three lightweight additions improve local predictions: direct access to the previous block's last token, a small correction to next-token scores, and a learned mixture with recent-token frequencies. We compare twelve small-scale implementations on public FineWeb-Edu, with comparable parameter counts and shared training-budget caps. The baselines include Transformer, Mamba-3, Jamba, and Zamba. The Jamba and Zamba versions use Mamba-3 in place of their original Mamba modules. TurboMamba achieves the lowest mean test prediction loss (negative log-likelihood, NLL) among all twelve models. Its mean NLL is 9.4% lower than that of Mamba-3, 7.3% lower than that of the Jamba version, and 18.3% lower than that of the Zamba version. End-to-end training throughput is 2.60x that of Mamba-3, 2.81x that of the Jamba version, and 4.82x that of the Zamba version. We measure end-to-end batch latency for a batch of 32 sequences, each with a 256-token prompt and 128 generated tokens. On this workload, TurboMamba achieves a 3.12x speedup over Mamba-3, a 3.53x speedup over the Jamba version, and a 6.21x speedup over the Zamba version.
中文翻译
我们提出TurboMamba，一种用于更快推理、更快训练和更好token预测的语言模型架构。一个全局的Mamba-3模型在每个完整token块完成后运行一次；一个小规模Transformer利用该全局上下文以及块内早期token来预测下一个token。通过三种轻量级改进提升局部预测性能：直接访问前一块的最后一个token，对下一个token得分进行小幅度修正，以及与近期token频率的可学习混合。我们在公共FineWeb-Edu数据集上对比了十二种小规模实现，参数量相当且共享训练预算上限。基线包括Transformer、Mamba-3、Jamba和Zamba。其中Jamba和Zamba版本用Mamba-3替换了其原始Mamba模块。TurboMamba在所有十二个模型中实现了最低的平均测试预测损失（负对数似然，NLL）。其平均NLL比Mamba-3低9.4%，比Jamba版本低7.3%，比Zamba版本低18.3%。端到端训练吞吐量分别为Mamba-3的2.60倍、Jamba版本的2.81倍和Zamba版本的4.82倍。我们测量了一个包含32个序列的批量任务的端到端批处理延迟，每个序列包含256个token的提示和128个生成token。在此工作负载下，TurboMamba相比Mamba-3提速3.12倍，相比Jamba版本提速3.53倍，相比Zamba版本提速6.21倍。

Same Answer, New Box: How Much of RLVR's Out-of-Domain Gain Is Answer Format?
同一答案，新盒子：RLVR的域外增益有多少来自答案格式？
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is credited with gains beyond the domain its rewards check, such as higher MMLU accuracy after training on mathematics. Such accuracies are usually read out of the model's text by an answer extractor. A model that learns to write its answer where the extractor looks can therefore score higher without answering better. We measure how much of the gain reported by extractors copied from public code arises this way. We generate outputs from a base model and its RL model and score them twice: with those extractors, and with one extractor of our own that reads a stated answer whether it is in the answer box \boxed{} or in the text. On four released 7B SimpleRL base-RL pairs, 82% of the out-of-domain multiple-choice gain that these extractors report disappears under ours: it is a gain in answer format, not in answers. The base models already state most of their answers in text, and RL moves them into the box, where these extractors look first. Independent LLM readers, in place of our extractor, put the figure at 77%. With a prompt that itself asks for the box, our extractor puts it at 79%. On a 32B pair trained with another recipe, almost none of the reported gain is format gain: its base model boxes its answers when asked, and its RL model rarely does when not. A reported gain is partly a property of the extractor that read it, and re-scoring stored outputs with a second extractor shows how much, without re-running RL.
中文翻译
强化学习结合可验证奖励（RLVR）被认为带来了超出其奖励检查领域之外的收益，例如在数学训练后提高了MMLU的准确率。这类准确率通常由答案提取器从模型文本中读取。因此，一个学会将答案写在提取器查找位置的模型，即使回答质量未提升，也能获得更高分数。我们测量了从公开代码中复制的提取器所报告的增益中有多少是源于这种格式变化。我们从基础模型及其RL模型生成输出，并进行两次评分：使用这些提取器，以及使用我们自研的提取器——该提取器无论答案位于\boxed{}框内还是文本中都会读取。在四个发布的7B SimpleRL基础-RL配对中，这些提取器报告的域外多选题增益有82%在我们的提取器下消失：这实际上是格式上的增益，而非答案本身的提升。基础模型已在文本中陈述了大部分答案，而RL模型将其移入框内，正是这些提取器优先查找的位置。使用独立的LLM阅读器替代我们的提取器，该比例为77%。若使用要求框内答案的提示，我们的提取器显示该比例为79%。在另一个用不同方法训练的32B配对中，几乎不存在所谓的格式增益：其基础模型在被要求时会框出答案，而其RL模型在未被要求时不常这样做。因此，报告的增益部分取决于读取它的提取器本身，通过使用第二个提取器重新评分存储的输出，即可在不重新运行RL的情况下揭示其占比。
👤 Zongye Lyu 📅 2026-10-02 🔗 arXiv

Love for Believable AI: Artificial Partiality and Relationship Persistence as an Engineerable Stance
对可信人工智能的偏爱：可工程化的偏袒与关系持续性作为一种立场
We study a behavioral mechanism for artificial partiality in conversational agents. The paper reports no human-subjects data and makes no claim about attachment, trust, perceived mind, or machine interiority. Partiality has two components: caring, defined as allocation of a finite interaction surplus above a guaranteed per-user service floor, and particularity, defined as a per-user state estimate accumulated from relationship history on fixed inference machinery. We implement both as a persistence layer over one open-weight base model and evaluate them on constructed multi-session relationships with known hidden-state schedules. A four-channel divergence compares known-user and stranger conditions on identical probes with paired generation seeds; the stranger prompt is not length-matched, and one channel (initiative) is an allocator output rather than generated behavior. The full mechanism reproduces the ordinal shape, but not the magnitude, of an analytically specified curve and is the only arm satisfying the protocol-defined joint signature on the calibrated battery. The initial mechanism fails probe-quality equivalence because relationship content reduces topical relevance. A guarded revision satisfies equivalence on the calibrated battery but not on a second battery not used in calibration, where it scores higher than the unmodified baseline. On that second battery, the specificity margin is 0.0198, below the 0.02 protocol threshold. The paired mean right-user--wrong-user estimation-accuracy contrast is 0.375 but is not positive in every run. The mechanism is therefore a candidate for a later perception study, not evidence that users perceive love. The versioned protocol record is not independently time-stamped and is not described as a preregistration. Ethical constraints include a stranger-treatment floor, a bounded surplus, disclosure, and de-intensification.
中文翻译
我们研究了对话智能体中人工偏袒的行为机制。本文未涉及人类受试者数据，也未声称存在依恋、信任、感知心智或机器内在性。偏袒包含两个组成部分：关怀，定义为在保证每位用户服务底线之上分配有限的交互盈余；特殊性，定义为基于固定推理机制从关系历史中累积的用户状态估计。我们将两者均实现为一个开放权重基础模型上的持久化层，并在具有已知隐藏状态调度的构建型多会话关系上进行评估。四通道差异比较了已知用户与陌生人条件下的相同探测任务，使用配对生成种子；陌生人提示未进行长度匹配，且其中一个通道（主动性）为分配器输出而非生成行为。完整机制再现了分析指定曲线的序数形状，但未再现其量级，且是唯一满足校准电池协议定义联合特征的方案。初始机制因关系内容降低主题相关性而未能满足探测质量等价性。经过谨慎修订的版本在校准电池上满足等价性，但在未用于校准的第二组电池上不满足，尽管其得分高于未经修改的基线。在该第二组电池上，特异性差距为0.0198，低于0.02的协议阈值。配对平均的正确用户-错误用户估计准确性对比为0.375，但在每次运行中并非均为正值。因此，该机制是后续感知研究的候选方案，而非用户感知到爱的证据。版本化协议记录未独立时间戳，也未被描述为预注册。伦理约束包括对陌生人处理的最低标准、盈余上限、披露以及去强化机制。
👤 Sebastian Cochinescu 📅 2026-10-07 🔗 arXiv

[2610.22300]
Knowledge That Is Linear Before Fine-Tuning Generalizes After It
微调前线性可读的知识在微调后具有更强的泛化能力
Not all facts a language model can recall support further learning equally well. We distinguish active facts, which support generalization beyond their own recall, from inert facts, which can be recalled but do not support such generalization in a given task. We study this distinction by constructing new relations from existing ones. Take the relation that maps a place to its continent (Paris -> Europe) and assign each of its answers an arbitrary new label (Europe -> crab, Asia -> zebra). The new relation then maps Paris -> crab. We fine-tune the model on the new relation for a subset of subjects, without telling it the original relation or the relabeling rule, and test whether it assigns the corresponding labels to held-out subjects that share an answer in the original relation. Having learned Paris -> crab, does it answer Munich -> crab? Across eleven language models and 70 relations, how linearly readable the original relation is in pretrained representations strongly predicts this generalization. Within each model, the two correlate across relations at Pearson r = 0.87 to 0.92. This association persists when controlling, separately and jointly, for the number of fine-tuning subjects and answers, the model's accuracy on the original relation, and how often the subjects occur in pretraining text. It is also robust to re-randomizing the label assignment and fine-tuning seed, and to replacing the animal labels with labels from thirteen other semantic categories. When we map a fine-tuned model's wrong label back to the original answer it was assigned to, Munich -> zebra becomes Munich -> Asia. Answers recovered this way are more often among the linear probe's top answers for the subject than among the pretrained model's own. Whether a new relation generalizes depends less on whether its facts can be recalled than on how readily their shared structure can be read out linearly.
中文翻译
并非所有语言模型能够回忆的事实都能同等支持后续学习。我们区分了活跃事实（支持超出自身回忆范围的泛化）与惰性事实（可被回忆但无法在特定任务中支持泛化）。我们通过从现有关系构建新关系来研究这一区别。例如，将地点映射到其所属大陆的关系（巴黎 → 欧洲），并将每个答案分配一个任意新标签（欧洲 → 螃蟹，亚洲 → 斑马），则新关系变为巴黎 → 螃蟹。我们在部分主体上对模型进行新关系的微调，但不告知其原始关系或重新标记规则，并测试其是否能为共享原始关系答案的未见主体分配相应标签。在学习了巴黎 → 螃蟹后，它是否会回答慕尼黑 → 螃蟹？在十一款语言模型和70个关系上，原始关系在预训练表示中的线性可读性强烈预测了这种泛化能力。在每款模型内部，这两个变量在不同关系间的相关系数为皮尔逊r = 0.87至0.92。即使分别或联合控制微调主体数量、答案数量、模型在原关系上的准确率以及主体在预训练文本中出现频率，该关联依然存在。该现象对重新随机分配标签、微调种子以及用其他十三个语义类别替换动物标签也保持稳健。当我们把微调后模型的错误标签回溯到其原本分配的原始答案时，慕尼黑 → 花生 变为 慕尼黑 → 亚洲。通过这种方式恢复的答案，比预训练模型自身的答案更常出现在线性探测器对主体的前几名中。新关系能否泛化，不取决于其事实是否可被回忆，而更取决于其共享结构是否能被线性地读取出来。
👤 Faridoun Mehri, Hinrich Schütze 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22249]
Not X, but When: Diagnosing a Mandarin LLM Writing Tic through Contextual Appropriateness
不是X，而是何时：通过语境恰当性诊断中文LLM写作习惯
Recurring expressions in model-written text are often treated as signatures of machine authorship, yet most of them serve legitimate functions. Whether such an expression is a tic depends on whether the model knows when it is called for. This judgment of appropriateness, central to communicative competence, remains largely unexamined for language models. We study it through the Mandarin corrective construction bushi X, ershi Y ("not X, but Y"). Drawing on Gricean pragmatics and corrective negation, we introduce a decision tree that classifies uses as substantive, optional, or defective by testing whether the context makes X a live alternative and whether rejecting X is informative. Five commercial models use the construction 2.5 to 9.2 times as densely as a human reference. Of 307 annotated uses, only 1.30% are defective while 52.8% are optional, that is, correct but unneeded, so models fall short in appropriateness far more often than in correctness. Several reward models favor the construction even over quality-matched variants, an LLM judge favors it even where the context does not license it, and both supervised fine-tuning and preference optimization tend to raise its frequency in open-weight models. We hope this work encourages the development of models attentive to the appropriateness of their expressions.
中文翻译
模型生成文本中的重复表达常被视为机器作者的标志，但大多数此类表达具有合法功能。判断这种表达是否为习惯性用法，关键在于模型是否知道其适用时机。这一恰当性判断是交际能力的核心，但在语言模型中仍鲜有研究。我们通过分析汉语纠正结构“不是X，而是Y”（bushi X, ershi Y）来探讨该问题。结合Grice语用学和纠正性否定理论，我们提出一个决策树，通过检验上下文是否使X成为可行选项以及否弃X是否具有信息量，将使用分类为实质性、可选或缺陷性。五款商用模型使用该结构的密度是人类参考样本的2.5至9.2倍。在307个标注用例中，仅有1.30%为缺陷性，而52.8%为可选性，即正确但非必要，说明模型在恰当性上远不如在正确性上表现差。多个奖励模型即使在质量匹配的变体上也偏好该结构，LLM裁判甚至在上下文不支持的情况下仍倾向使用该结构，且监督微调和偏好优化均倾向于在开放权重模型中增加其频率。我们希望本工作能推动更关注表达恰当性的模型发展。
👤 Yihang Peng, Yi Jing, Dewen Liu, Weihan Meng, Ying Liu 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22187]
Change the Number, Change the Answer: Assessing Rating Scale Sensitivity in LLM-Based Text Scoring
改变数字，改变答案：评估基于大语言模型文本评分的评分量表敏感性
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in computational social science to assign scalar scores to social and psychological constructs. However, studies use different rating scales, and whether their scores remain comparable across scale designs is understudied. To address this gap, we define and systematically investigate scale sensitivity, the variation in LLM-generated scores caused by rating scale design. We distinguish numerical sensitivity, changes in normalized scores, from semantic sensitivity, changes in score interpretation. To assess both, our experimental design crosses 2 formats (continuous vs. ordinal), 4 ranges (3, 5, 11, and 21 points), and 2 signs (signed vs. unsigned) across 3 tasks (political leaning, sentiment, and toxicity), 2 prompting strategies (zero-shot and rubric-based), and 5 LLMs. We find that changing one design element shifts normalized scores by about 0.10 on average and changes the category of roughly 20% of texts. Sensitivity is greatest for toxicity, the only unipolar construct examined. Also, rubric-based prompting does not reduce sensitivity compared with zero-shot prompting. We provide practical guidance for designing more reliable LLM-based text scoring and highlight the need for robustness checks across alternative scales.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）在计算社会科学中被越来越多地用于为社会和心理构念分配标量分数。然而，现有研究使用不同的评分量表，而这些分数在不同量表设计下是否仍具可比性尚未得到充分研究。为填补这一空白，我们定义并系统研究了量表敏感性，即评分量表设计导致的LLM生成分数的变化。我们区分了数值敏感性（归一化分数的变化）与语义敏感性（分数解释的变化）。为评估两者，我们的实验设计涵盖了2种格式（连续型与序数型）、4种范围（3、5、11和21点）、2种符号类型（带符号与无符号），共涉及3项任务（政治倾向、情感分析、毒性检测）、2种提示策略（零样本与基于评分标准）以及5个LLM。结果发现，改变一个设计要素平均会使归一化分数变动约0.10，并使约20%的文本分类发生变化。敏感性在毒性这一唯一单极构念中最强。此外，基于评分标准的提示策略并未降低敏感性，相比零样本提示。我们提供了设计更可靠LLM文本评分的实践建议，并强调需在不同量表间进行鲁棒性检验。

[2610.22167]
CLUE: Uncovering Social Context for Pragmatic Understanding
CLUE：揭示用于语用理解的社会语境
Understanding a conversation requires connecting what is said to the speakers' relationships, expectations, and earlier exchanges. Prior work shows that supplying relevant context can improve models' social judgments, but much of this information may already be present in the conversation. This motivates training models to discover the cues that matter and use them to support an interpretation. We propose CLUE, a two-stage training pipeline that combines supervised evidence reasoning with GRPO guided by Grounded Information Utilization ($\mathrm{IU}_{\mathrm{G}}$). This reward credits new, evidence-supported social inferences while accounting for reasoning effort. We train on a small set of conversational examples to assess effectiveness and generalization across tasks, languages, and genres. At inference time, the trained model reasons over the supplied interaction in a self-contained manner. Compared with base Qwen, CLUE achieves relative gains of 17.3% in-domain and 9.3% out-of-domain in average weighted F1, with higher F1 on four of five out-of-domain evaluations. These gains are accompanied by over 80% fewer reasoning tokens on average in both domains. Human evaluation further finds that CLUE more often grounds its explanations in specific conversational content, with closer alignment between its quoted evidence and human-selected context.
中文翻译
理解对话需要将所说内容与说话者的关系、期望及先前交流联系起来。已有研究表明，提供相关语境可提升模型的社会判断能力，但许多此类信息可能已存在于对话本身。这促使我们训练模型发现关键线索并利用它们支持解释。我们提出CLUE，一种结合监督证据推理与GRPO（基于地面信息利用$$\mathrm{IU}_{}$$）引导的两阶段训练流程。该奖励机制在承认推理努力的同时，奖励新产生的、有证据支持的社会推断。我们在少量对话示例上进行训练，以评估其在任务、语言和体裁上的有效性与泛化能力。推理时，训练后的模型可自主地对给定互动进行推理。相较于基础Qwen模型，CLUE在域内平均加权F1提升17.3%，域外提升9.3%，且在五个域外评估中的四个取得更高F1。这些提升伴随着两个领域平均推理token减少80%以上。人工评估还发现，CLUE更常将其解释基于具体对话内容，其引用证据与人类选择的上下文具有更强的一致性。

[2610.22110]
Unfragmenting Nawatl NMT: Single-Variant Specialization for Masewalkopa
重构纳瓦特NMT：面向Masewalkopa的单变体专业化方法
In minoritized (low-resource) language technologies, a common response to data sparsity is to aggregate text across multiple varieties and treat the combined data as a single corpus. However, mixed corpora of closely related indigenous variants can produce an artificial register that is accountable to no single speaker community, a structural failure we call variant fragmentation. This issue is acute for minoritized languages native to Mexico, such as Nawatl. Building on a 70,000-pair multi-variant foundation, we specialize an mT5-large checkpoint to Masewalkopa (nhn), the Nawatl variety spoken in Santa Ana Tlacotenco. We curate 2,665 parallel pairs, reserve all sentence-level data for evaluation, and incorporate 192 elder-transcribed target sentences via asymmetric backtranslation. A custom finite-state analyzer reduces unanalyzed surface forms from 82.0% to 19.9% and supplies functional syntax tags as plain-text prefixes, allowing the model to reuse existing subword embeddings rather than relying on frozen special tokens. The resulting LoRA-v9c-b model achieves 58.46 dev chrF++ and 58.20/57.90 directional test chrF++. Finally, CTranslate2 int8 quantization reduces CPU latency from 137.5 to 1.8-2.6 seconds per request, enabling cost-bounded Cloud Run deployment.

[2610.22071]
The Right Byte First: How UTF-16 Helps Multilingual Boundary Prediction
先取正确字节：UTF-16如何提升多语言边界预测
Many hierarchical byte-level language models group bytes into patches using a learned boundary predictor whose view of the next codepoint depends on encoding and lookahead. In UTF-8, codepoints of many scripts take three bytes: 8 of 24 bits mark length and continuation, and the first byte carries only 4 high codepoint bits. After the same left context, a mark extending a grapheme cluster and a letter starting the next can thus share their first byte. Universal Byte-Level Encoding (UBE) keeps one- and two-byte UTF-8 encodings but writes these codepoints as two UTF-16 bytes, low byte first, so its first symbol carries 8 low codepoint bits that can distinguish such continuations. We train small causal predictors on matched FineWeb-2 text and evaluate on FLORES-200 under UTF-8, UBE, and UBE's same-length big-endian variant. At one-symbol lookahead, balanced grapheme-boundary error at codepoint boundaries across 17 languages in 13 Brahmic scripts is 8.76% with UTF-8, 8.62% with the big-endian variant, and 0.06% with UBE. When the target instead retains a parent tokenizer's subword cuts at grapheme boundaries, UBE lowers the mean error relative to UTF-8 at the same lookahead by 72.91% in Korean, 35.29% in Chinese, and 67.32-99.55% in eight more scripts. In the seven of them whose main Unicode block fits on one 256-codepoint page, the big-endian variant stays within 3.0% of UTF-8, so the gain follows byte order, not length. In Korean and Chinese, UBE's mean error falls below UTF-8's final mean error after only 25% and 37.5% of training. Which byte comes first thus affects how much lookahead a byte-level boundary predictor needs.
中文翻译
许多层次化的字节级语言模型使用学习到的边界预测器将字节分组为块，其对下一个码点的判断依赖于编码方式和前瞻长度。在UTF-8中，许多脚本的码点占用三个字节：24位中的8位用于标记长度和延续，首字节仅携带4位高位码点信息。因此，在相同左文上下文中，一个延伸音节簇的标记与下一个字母的起始可能共享首个字节。通用字节级编码（UBE）保留了UTF-8的一字节和二字节编码，但将这些码点写为两个UTF-16字节，低字节优先，使得其首个符号携带8位低位码点信息，能够区分此类延续。我们在匹配的FineWeb-2文本上训练小型因果预测器，并在FLORES-200上评估UTF-8、UBE及其同长度大端序变体的表现。在单符号前瞻下，17种语言中13种婆罗米系脚本的码点边界处平衡音节边界错误率分别为：UTF-8为8.76%，大端序变体为8.62%，UBE仅为0.06%。当目标保留父分词器在音节边界处的子词切分时，UBE在韩语中相对于UTF-8将平均错误率降低72.91%，中文降低35.29%，其余八种脚本降低67.32%至99.55%。在七种主要Unicode区块可容纳于单个256码点页的脚本中，大端序变体与UTF-8差距不超过3.0%，说明收益源于字节顺序而非长度。在韩语和中文中，UBE的平均错误率在训练仅完成25%和37.5%后即低于UTF-8的最终平均错误率。由此可见，字节的先后顺序影响字节级边界预测器所需的前瞻量。
👤 Hyunsik Kim 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22053]
Name-Bias in Large Language Models
大型语言模型中的姓名偏见
Names carry demographic indicators such as age, gender, and race, which makes them a subtle but strong target of bias in large language models (LLMs). In this work, we present an evaluation of name-based bias across 6 local large language models spanning multiple providers and parameter scales. Using a fill-in-the-blank task, we prompt these models to choose between pairs of first names drawn from U.S. Social Security Administration datasets and a race classification dataset by Rosenman et al.. Our experimental de- sign isolates age and gender effects through balanced name pairings and sentence templates that share a fixed syntactic structure. Across 300,000 local LLM responses, we find consistent and measurable name bias. Models prefer names that are less stereotypically male, older, popular, and white. Furthermore, we find that name-bias is highly sentence-dependent, but not more than associated stereotypes, and gender is the most significant bias, followed by age, name popularity, and race. Our results demonstrate that name-based bias remains pervasive even in local LLMs, highlighting the necessity for standardized evaluation methods and mitigation strategies.
中文翻译

Learning Speech Attribute Representations via Multi-Teacher Distillation
通过多教师蒸馏学习语音属性表示
Recent post-training approaches have shown that self-supervised speech encoders can be adapted to learn a single utterance-level speech attribute through knowledge distillation. In this work, we investigate whether a single speech encoder can simultaneously preserve multiple complementary utterance-level speech representations. We propose a multi-attribute multi-teacher distillation framework that learns semantic, speaker, language, and emotion representations from four frozen teacher models using a shared SSL backbone and attribute-specific branches. Experiments on multilingual semantic retrieval, speaker verification, language identification, and speech emotion recognition show that the proposed model achieves performance close to dedicated single-task students while requiring only a single shared encoder. [2610.21862]
Dynamic ordering in sequence generation in Transformers: experiments on low-resource Tagging scenarios
Transformer中序列生成的动态排序：低资源标注场景的实验
This paper introduces TagInsert, a Transformer-based model that challenges the common left-to-right (L2R) generation paradigm in Transformers, for sequence labelling tasks where dependencies may not naturally align with a L2R order. TagInsert lifts the L2R constraint by removing the decoder-side attention mask and enabling the model to decide a preferred order of tag generation at inference time. We experiment on the common Part-of-Speech tagging and the more complex Combinatory Categorial Grammar (CCG) supertagging tasks on multiple languages and low-resource data settings. We show that, although encoder-only models like BERT remain strong performers for tagging tasks, TagInsert frequently outperforms a L2R encoder-decoder model, achieves results comparable to encoder-only models under data-scarce conditions, and shows better results on the long-tail of rare categories. Our analyses show that TagInsert alleviates error propagation typical of autoregressive models by learning to defer difficult predictions to later stages in the decoding process.

[2610.21807]
Content associations with answer selection and engagement in supply chain community question answering
供应链社区问答中内容关联与答案选择及参与度的关系
Community question-answering platforms are important ecosystems for knowledge sharing, yet research on what makes an answer best or a question engaging has often focused on either individual lexical features or broad topics. This exploratory study examines both levels in Yahoo! Chiebukuro, asking which nouns and semantic noun clusters are associated with best-answer selection and question engagement. We analyze a large corpus of questions and answer-tier posts collected under the keyword logistics, using an offline machine-translation pipeline with manually validated noun-level fidelity. Best-answer selection is modeled with conditional logistic regression within question threads, controlling for answer length, order, and question-answer noun overlap. Engagement is modeled in two stages: whether a question receives any response and, conditional on receiving one, how many responses it attracts. Operational logistics nouns such as distribution, warehouse, and center are associated with best-answer selection, whereas broader civic and infrastructure nouns such as car, tax, and shinkansen are associated with higher engagement. At the cluster level, most semantic noun clusters are associated with best-answer selection, while the clusters associated with response likelihood and response volume are largely distinct. These findings reveal differentiated content-outcome profiles across answer and question levels and provide a domain-wide descriptive map of engagement and answer selection in logistics-related cQA discourse.
中文翻译

[2610.21799]
Behavioral Forgetting Is Not Privacy in Machine Unlearning
行为遗忘并非机器去学习中的隐私
Machine unlearning aims to remove targeted information from large language models, but existing evaluations often measure forgetting only on a designated benchmark distribution. We argue that such behavioral forgetting is an insufficient standard for privacy-oriented unlearning, and that evaluation should instead test whether forgetting persists across the access paths through which the same information may be recovered. We demonstrate this with a controlled case study of cross-lingual querying: across multiple multilingual model families, forgetting varies substantially when semantically equivalent information is queried in different languages and scripts. A single access path is therefore enough to expose a real gap between behavioral forgetting and information inaccessibility, motivating broader evaluation of access-path generalization and adversarial recoverability alongside in-distribution forgetting.
中文翻译
机器去学习旨在从大型语言模型中移除特定信息，但现有评估通常仅在指定基准分布上衡量遗忘程度。我们认为，这种行为遗忘作为面向隐私的去学习标准是不充分的，评估应改为重点测试遗忘是否在相同信息可能被恢复的所有访问路径中持续存在。我们通过一个受控的跨语言查询案例研究证明了这一点：在多个多语言模型族中，当语义等价的信息以不同语言和文字形式查询时，遗忘程度存在显著差异。因此，单一访问路径就足以暴露行为遗忘与信息不可访问性之间的真实差距，这促使我们应在分布内遗忘之外，更广泛地评估访问路径泛化性和对抗性可恢复性。

通过对抗训练与蒸馏方法缓解音频增强型大语言模型中的偏见
Audio-enabled large language models (AuLLMs) allow users to interact with them simply by talking to them, without the need for an intermediate transcription. This expands the model's accessibility and capabilities by encoding non-verbal cues. However, using non-verbal cues may lead to biased behaviour based on the attributes inferred (or assumed) from them. [2610.21737]
SceneWeave: Audience-Guided Scene Exploration for Dramatic Adaptation
SceneWeave：观众引导的场景探索用于戏剧改编
Adapting prose into a script requires transforming narration and inner experience into scenes that convey relationships and psychological contradictions. Planning specifies intended effects but leaves their realization in dialogue and action unresolved. Drawing on screenwriters' accounts of revising scenes and their connections to the surrounding work, we present SceneWeave, which uses alternative scene drafts to revise the adaptation as a whole. It writes contrasting treatments of a focal scene, compares the interactions they create, and revises characterization, preceding preparation, subsequent development, and scene order before generating the complete script. The central contribution is to use actual scene writing to inform adaptation decisions and propagate their consequences across the work. We also develop a task-specific writing model and a human-preference model for comparing script drafts. The writing model learns from adaptation examples and human preferences; the preference model combines human and synthetic supervision. Blind reader comparisons favor SceneWeave over a baseline workflow and description-based controls, while content assessment indicates reduced narrative redundancy and strong psychological treatment.

[2610.21690]
Abstracts or full text? Evaluating input scope and computational efficiency in biomedical lay summarization
摘要还是全文？评估生物医学通俗摘要中的输入范围与计算效率
Biomedical research articles are mainly written for experts and often contain terms that are difficult for the general public to understand. Automated lay summarization has become a solution to make biomedical knowledge easier for patients and the public to understand. However, generating lay summaries from full-text articles requires more computing power because the articles are long and costly to process. Fine-tuning large language models for this task also requires substantial resources. This study asks two questions: (1) whether abstracts contain enough information to produce lay summaries as good as those based on full-text articles, and (2) whether few-shot learning performs as well as task-specific fine-tuning. The study used two public biomedical lay summarization datasets and two recent models, evaluated for readability, factual consistency, semantic similarity, and runtime efficiency. Results show that abstract-based summarization performs better overall. Across both datasets and models, abstract fine-tuning is 4.70 times faster, while abstract zero- and few-shot prompting is 5.31 times faster than full-text methods. These results show that abstract-based prompting reduces runtime cost while maintaining lay summary quality.
中文翻译
生物医学研究论文主要面向专家撰写，常包含普通公众难以理解的术语。自动化通俗摘要已成为使生物医学知识更易被患者和公众理解的解决方案。然而，从全文生成通俗摘要需要更多计算资源，因为文章较长且处理成本高。为此任务微调大型语言模型也需大量资源。本研究提出两个问题：(1) 摘要是否包含足够信息以生成与基于全文摘要质量相当的通俗摘要；(2) 少样本学习是否能达到特定任务微调的效果。研究使用两个公开的生物医学通俗摘要数据集和两种近期模型，评估可读性、事实一致性、语义相似性和运行效率。结果表明，基于摘要的摘要生成整体表现更优。在两个数据集和模型上，摘要微调速度快4.70倍，而摘要零样本和少样本提示则比全文方法快5.31倍。这些结果表明，基于摘要的提示可降低运行时间成本，同时保持通俗摘要质量。

[2610.21681]
Data Strategies for Large Language Models: A Unified Perspective on the Training Lifecycle
大语言模型的数据策略：训练生命周期的统一视角
Data strategy plays a central role in training large language models (LLMs). It governs the selection, weighting, and scheduling of training data, thereby determining the capabilities a model can acquire from a fixed data budget. We treat high-quality data as having high training-dependent utility, which depends on the target objective, the model, and its training state rather than on the text alone. Research in this area now spans pretraining and post-training, but related ideas use inconsistent terminology and evaluation protocols. This work reviews data strategies across the training lifecycle and formulates them as policies that allocate a training budget over candidate examples. We organise methods by their decision granularity, model dependence, and temporal adaptivity, then compare their computational requirements and empirical support. Fine-grained or state-dependent policies offer more control but incur additional scoring, search, or rollout costs. Evidence for transfer across model scales and comparable end-to-end cost remains limited. A continuously updated list of papers, along with their public implementations, is available at https://github.com/xuyj233/data_strategy_survey.
中文翻译
数据策略在训练大语言模型（LLMs）中起核心作用。它决定了训练数据的选择、加权和调度方式，从而影响模型在固定数据预算下所能获得的能力。本文将高质量数据视为具有高度依赖训练的实用性，这种实用性取决于目标任务、模型及其训练状态，而非仅由文本本身决定。当前该领域的研究涵盖预训练和后训练阶段，但相关概念使用不一致的术语和评估协议。本文综述了整个训练生命周期中的数据策略，并将其形式化为在候选样本间分配训练预算的策略。我们根据决策粒度、模型依赖性和时间自适应性对方法进行分类，并比较其计算需求和实证支持。细粒度或状态依赖型策略虽提供更高控制力，但带来额外的评分、搜索或回放成本。跨模型规模的迁移证据以及端到端成本可比性的支持仍有限。一份持续更新的论文列表及公开实现可在 https://github.com/xuyj233/data_strategy_survey 获取。

[2610.21679]
No Single Tokenizer Feature Reliably Predicts Downstream Language Model Performance
单一分词器特征无法可靠预测下游语言模型性能
Tokenization is a critical component of the language modeling pipeline, but it is poorly understood. Tokenizers can impact downstream performance, but there is currently mixed evidence about which tokenizer features predict a better language model without requiring a full training run. In this paper, we analyze approximately 200 language models trained on nine different languages. We evaluate both models and their tokenizers and test whether there is a relationship between any proposed intrinsic tokenizer evaluations and model performance, as measured by a wide variety of benchmarks. Despite the quantity and variety of proposed intrinsic tokenizer metrics, we find that none consistently predicts downstream performance across model families, tasks, and languages. This motivates the need for novel tokenizer evaluation metrics and approaches. We propose novel linguistically grounded metric introduced in this work shows that new metrics can capture aspects of tokenizer quality that existing metrics miss, though their predictive power is likely to vary across model families, languages, and tasks.

[2610.21633]
ContextLift: Breaking the Spurious Correlation Behind Conditional Emergent Misalignment
ContextLift：打破条件涌现错位背后的虚假相关性
Finetuning a language model on narrowly misaligned data can make it broadly misaligned. Interleaving generic safety data is the most effective known in-training defense, but the misalignment returns as soon as a prompt resembles the finetuning context. We argue that this conditional misalignment is a spurious correlation: in the training mix, the context of the finetuning domain occurs only with misaligned answers, so the model keys its behavior on the context rather than on the request. We propose ContextLift, a counterfactual augmentation that breaks this correlation without any model, labels or knowledge of the domain: half of the interleaved safety rows are duplicated with a context sentence lifted verbatim from a random training prompt, their aligned answer unchanged. On Qwen2.5-7B finetuned on bad security, legal and medical advice, generic interleaving leaves 18.9%, 41.9% and 58.2% misaligned answers under a domain cue; ContextLift reduces this to 2.6%, 6.1% and 12.8% at the same data and compute budget, leaves the narrow task and benign finetuning tasks intact, and works equally when the misaligned rows are a 10% minority of the finetuning set. Code: https://github.com/davidkaczer/contextlift
中文翻译
在狭义错位数据上微调语言模型可能导致其广泛错位。目前最有效的训练中防御方法是穿插通用安全数据，但一旦提示与微调上下文相似，错位现象就会重现。我们认为这种条件错位是一种虚假相关性：在训练混合中，微调领域上下文仅与错位回答一同出现，因此模型将行为依据于上下文而非请求本身。我们提出ContextLift，一种反事实增强方法，无需模型、标签或领域知识即可打破这种相关性：将穿插的安全行中一半复制，并将随机训练提示中的上下文句原样提取替换，其对齐答案保持不变。在Qwen2.5-7B上微调不良安全、法律和医疗建议数据后，通用穿插方法在领域提示下仍分别有18.9%、41.9%和58.2%的错位回答；而ContextLift在相同数据和计算预算下将其降至2.6%、6.1%和12.8%，同时保持窄任务和良性微调任务不受影响，且当错位行占微调集10%时仍有效。代码：https://github.com/davidkaczer/contextlift
👤 Florian Mai, David Kaczér 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.21456]
Translation Circuit Discovery and Targeted Optimization for Low-Resource Multilingual Translation
低资源多语言翻译中的翻译电路发现与针对性优化
Recent multilingual large language models achieve strong translation performance, yet the internal mechanisms responsible for low-resource multilingual translation remain insufficiently understood. In this paper, we study translation circuits in multilingual decoder-only LLMs for Hindi-to-Urdu and Hindi-to-Sindhi (Devanagari). We propose TITAN, a mechanistic framework that combines causal intervention, sparse feature decomposition, and targeted preference optimization to identify and optimize crucial translation components in the transformer. Unlike conventional full-parameter fine-tuning, TITAN restricts optimization to sparse, causally important circuits, enabling scalable and parameter-efficient adaptation while updating fewer than 5% of model parameters. Experimental results show that TITAN achieves BLEU improvements of 33.75% on Hindi-to-Urdu and 45.86% on Hindi-to-Sindhi over the strongest multilingual baselines. Human expert evaluation further confirms substantial gains in adequacy and fluency across both language pairs. Additional analyses reveal that multilingual translation is implemented through sparse, structured circuits operating over a shared semantic subspace rather than through an English-centric pivot representation. Overall, our findings demonstrate that mechanistic circuit discovery can directly improve low-resource multilingual translation while providing interpretable insights into multilingual transformer behavior.
中文翻译
近期的多语言大语言模型取得了优异的翻译性能，但其在低资源多语言翻译中的内部机制仍不够清晰。本文研究了多语言仅解码器LLM中针对印地语到乌尔都语和印地语到信德语（天城文）的翻译电路。我们提出了TITAN，一种结合因果干预、稀疏特征分解和针对性偏好优化的机制框架，用于识别并优化Transformer中的关键翻译组件。与传统的全参数微调不同，TITAN将优化限制在稀疏且因果重要的电路中，实现了可扩展且参数高效的适应，更新的模型参数少于5%。实验结果显示，TITAN在印地语到乌尔都语上的BLEU提升达33.75%，在印地语到信德语上提升达45.86%，优于最强的多语言基线。人工专家评估进一步证实了两个语对在准确性和流畅性上的显著提升。额外分析表明，多语言翻译是通过作用于共享语义子空间的稀疏、结构化电路实现的，而非依赖以英语为中心的中间表示。总体而言，我们的研究证明，机制电路发现可直接提升低资源多语言翻译性能，同时为多语言Transformer行为提供可解释的洞见。

[2610.21403]
Beyond Invariance: Factor-wise Selective Treatment of Shifts for Multimodal Learning
超越不变性：多模态学习中对偏移的因子级选择性处理
Distribution shifts pose a fundamental challenge to robust multimodal learning. Existing methods typically promote invariance by suppressing environment-dependent variations, implicitly applying a uniform treatment to shifted information. However, shifted information can have different predictive consequences: some contributes little to prediction, some remains reliably informative, and some exhibits environment-dependent predictive effects. This raises a fundamental question: which shifted information should be suppressed, preserved, or attenuated? To address this question, we propose FaShift, a factor-wise shift framework that diagnoses latent representation factors according to their distributional changes, intervention effects, and cross-environment stability. By factorizing multimodal representations and evaluating these complementary properties, FaShift distinguishes non-predictive factors, stably predictive factors, and predictively useful but unstable factors. It then selectively suppresses, preserves, or attenuates their contributions, rather than enforcing uniform invariance. Experiments on MOSI, MOSEI, and CH-SIMS show that FaShift achieves the best ID accuracy and F1 across all three datasets. Compared with the strongest competitors reporting OOD results, it improves OOD accuracy/F1 by 1.5/3.6 points on MOSI and 1.7/2.7 points on MOSEI, while achieving the best reported OOD accuracy and F1 on CH-SIMS.
中文翻译
分布偏移对鲁棒的多模态学习构成了根本性挑战。现有方法通常通过抑制依赖环境的变化来促进不变性，隐式地对偏移信息采取统一处理。然而，偏移信息可能具有不同的预测意义：有些对预测贡献甚微，有些则始终具有可靠性，有些则表现出依赖环境的预测效应。这引发了一个基本问题：哪些偏移信息应被抑制、保留或减弱？为解决此问题，我们提出了FaShift，一种基于因子的偏移框架，能够根据其分布变化、干预效果和跨环境稳定性诊断潜在表示因子。通过分解多模态表示并评估这些互补属性，FaShift 区分了非预测性因子、稳定预测性因子以及虽有预测价值但不稳定的因子。随后，它选择性地抑制、保留或减弱这些因子的贡献，而非强制统一不变性。在 MOSI、MOSEI 和 CH-SIMS 上的实验表明，FaShift 在所有三个数据集上均实现了最佳的内部识别（ID）准确率和 F1 分数。与报告外部泛化（OOD）结果的最强竞争者相比，它在 MOSI 上提升了 OOD 准确率/F1 1.5/3.6 个百分点，在 MOSEI 上提升了 1.7/2.7 个百分点，同时在 CH-SIMS 上达到了最佳报告的 OOD 准确率和 F1。

[2610.21383]
Calibration Language Shapes Block-Influence Pruning in Multilingual Language Models
校准语言影响多语言语言模型中的块影响剪枝
Block Influence (BI) ranks Transformer blocks for depth pruning from their input-output cosine change, but the ranking depends on the calibration distribution. We study whether English calibration transfers to Indian languages in Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct, Llama-3.2-1B, and Gemma-2-2B. English, Hindi, Marathi, and Tamil use disjoint Wikipedia articles for calibration and evaluation; every selector receives the same 150-chunk budget, and Tamil is excluded from multilingual aggregation to test cross-script transfer. Across all three models, late-block BI is 20.7-55.5% higher for the three Indian languages than for English, and every predeclared late-minus-middle contrast has a 95% bootstrap interval above zero. This mismatch changes the low-BI blocks selected for removal despite high global rank correlations. Averaging BI over equal English, Hindi, and Marathi budgets mitigates the resulting perplexity damage for Qwen at six removed blocks and for Gemma at all tested depths, including held-out Tamil. The mitigation is not universal: on Llama, balancing improves Hindi and Marathi at two removed blocks but severely harms Tamil, while larger pruning budgets select the same blocks and collapse perplexity regardless of calibration. Rank-mean and minimax-rank aggregators do not dominate the raw mean. These results support a diagnostic claim rather than a universal pruning rule: English-only BI systematically misses late-layer activity for Indian-language text, but multilingual calibration must still be validated per architecture and pruning budget.
中文翻译
块影响（Block Influence, BI）通过输入输出余弦变化对 Transformer 块进行深度剪枝排序，但该排序依赖于校准分布。我们研究了在 Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct、Llama-3.2-1B 和 Gemma-2-2B 模型中，英语校准是否可迁移至印度语言。使用不重叠的维基百科文章作为英语、印地语、马拉地语和泰米尔语的校准与评估数据；每个选择器获得相同的 150 块预算，且泰米尔语被排除在多语言聚合之外以测试跨文字迁移能力。在所有三个模型中，对于三种印度语言，后期块的 BI 比英语高出 20.7% 至 55.5%，且每个预定义的后期减中期对比的 95% Bootstrap 置信区间均高于零。这种不匹配改变了低 BI 块的选择，尽管全局排名相关性较高。在等量英语、印地语和马拉地语预算下平均 BI 可缓解 Qwen 在移除六个块时的困惑度损失，以及 Gema 在所有测试深度（包括未见的泰米尔语）下的损失。但这种缓解并非普遍适用：在 Llama 上，平衡校准在移除两个块时改善了印地语和马拉地语表现，却严重损害泰米尔语性能；更大的剪枝预算则无论校准方式如何都会选择相同块并导致困惑度崩溃。秩均值和极小极大秩聚合器并未优于原始均值。这些结果支持一种诊断性主张而非普适剪枝规则：仅用英语的 BI 会系统性遗漏印度语言文本的后期层活动，但多语言校准仍需针对具体架构和剪枝预算进行验证。
👤 Vedant Pandhare, Piyush Dhoka 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.21378]
DPA-MoE: A Runtime Precision Control Framework for MoE Inference
DPA-MoE：面向 MoE 推理的运行时精度控制框架
Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models reduce computation by routing each token to a small set of experts, but each selected expert is still used at a fixed precision. We present DPA-MoE, a runtime precision control framework that makes routed experts any-precision. DPA-MoE encodes expert feed-forward weights as prefix-decodable chunks using SharedScale AnyBCQ, profiles per-expert reconstruction residuals offline, and allocates chunks at inference time using live router gate scores. This post-routing allocation lets a single encoded MoE checkpoint operate at multiple effective weight budgets without retraining or re-encoding. Experiments on Qwen3-30B-A3B and DeepSeekMoE-16B show that DPA-MoE improves KL divergence and downstream accuracy over uniform chunk allocation at matched budgets, with modest steady-state throughput overhead in our prototype.
中文翻译
混合专家（Mixture-of-Experts, MoE）模型通过将每个 token 路由到少数专家来减少计算量，但每个选定的专家仍以固定精度使用。我们提出 DPA-MoE，一种运行时精度控制框架，使路由后的专家可实现任意精度。DPA-MoE 使用 SharedScale AnyBCQ 将专家前馈权重编码为前缀可解码块，离线分析每个专家的重建残差，并在推理时根据实时路由器门控分数分配块。这种后路由分配机制使得单个编码的 MoE 检查点可在无需重新训练或重新编码的情况下，支持多种有效权重预算。在 Qwen3-30B-A3B 和 DeepSeekMoE-16B 上的实验表明，DPA-MoE 在匹配预算下相较于均匀块分配，显著提升了 KL 散度和下游任务准确率，原型系统仅带来适度的稳态吞吐量开销。

Making every stage of medical device document assessment with large language models deterministic
使用大语言模型实现医疗设备文档评估每个阶段的确定性
To show that a medical device complies with regulation, experts judge whether documents and records meet each requirement and record the judgment for others to verify. For each assessment item that experts define, large language models could prepare a preliminary assessment: a label such as Satisfied or Not satisfied, a reason and quoted evidence. We sent fourteen commercial deployments of such models (seven models from three providers, through six routes) the same request repeatedly, with the deterministic sampling settings each accepted; every deployment returned differing assessments for at least one request, and in twelve the label changed. Reducing reasoning where the interface allows it did not reduce the variation, and prompts of retrieved passages removed it only for the smallest models. The same open-weight model was reproducible or not depending only on how it was served. A pipeline we built to be deterministic at every stage -- text extraction and optical character recognition (OCR), chunking, embedding, indexing, retrieval and inference -- returned byte-identical output at each stage across reruns, interruptions, edits, machines of one graphics processing unit (GPU) model and days, for blood glucose meter and infusion pump documents. OCR, embedding and approximate retrieval varied when their own serving was not made invariant, and outputs changed with the GPU model and with the inference engine's scheduling settings, which therefore belong in the recorded configuration. End-to-end determinism is achievable and gives a testable basis for audit and change control.
中文翻译
为了证明医疗设备符合法规要求，专家需要判断文件和记录是否满足各项规定，并将判断结果记录下来供他人验证。对于专家定义的每一项评估内容，大语言模型可以生成初步评估：如“满足”或“不满足”的标签、理由以及引用证据。我们向十四个商业部署的大语言模型（来自三家供应商的七个模型，通过六种路径）重复发送相同请求，并采用确定性采样设置；每个部署在至少一项请求中返回了不同的评估结果，其中十二个部署的标签发生了变化。减少接口允许范围内的推理并未降低差异性，而使用检索段落的提示仅对最小的模型消除了这种差异。同一开源模型在不同服务方式下可能可复现或不可复现。我们构建了一个在每个阶段都保持确定性的流程——文本提取与光学字符识别（OCR）、分块、嵌入、索引、检索和推理——在多次运行、中断、编辑、单个GPU模型及多日测试中，均返回字节完全相同的输出，适用于血糖仪和输液泵文档。当OCR、嵌入或近似检索自身的服务方式未保持不变时，其输出会发生变化，且输出随GPU模型和推理引擎调度设置而改变，因此这些因素应纳入记录配置。端到端的确定性是可实现的，并为审计和变更控制提供了可测试的基础。

[2610.21164]
Automatic Sentence Completion Using Bengali Audio Speech with a Transformer-Based Early Speech Recognition System
基于Transformer早期语音识别系统的孟加拉语语音自动句子补全
Dementia severely impairs communication abilities and turns everyday conversations into challenging experiences. Individuals affected by dementia frequently struggle to express their thoughts or complete sentences, making it difficult for family members and caregivers to understand their needs. This study proposes a novel approach to assist dementia patients by developing an integrated system that detects signs of dementia through Bangla speech and text analysis and provides sentence auto-completion support during conversations. To achieve this, recorded voices from individuals with and without dementia were analysed, and key acoustic features were extracted to capture vocal patterns associated with cognitive decline. A transformer-based model was employed to transcribe voice signals into text for linguistic analysis. In the classification task, several Machine Learning (ML) and Deep Learning (DL) models were evaluated using both acoustic and textual representations, and the LSTM model architectures demonstrated strong capabilities for modelling contextual patterns in speech and language data. For the sentence auto-completion task, a sequential learning model based on a Bidirectional LSTM (Bi-LSTM) network with an attention mechanism was developed to sequentially complete partially expressed Bangla sentences during conversation. This research bridges the gap in dementia analysis in an underrepresented language and implements a sentence auto-completion framework for cognitive disorders.

[2610.21062]
Multimodal AI Detection for Math-Heavy Scientific Text
面向数学密集型科学文本的多模态AI检测
As large language models become increasingly capable of generating, and assisting with, scientific writing, there is a need for greater transparency and disclosure around the use of AI in research. Consequently, a veritable subfield of research and a number of commercial services aim to flag AI-generated content automatically. However, existing detectors which rely only on text extracted from PDFs fail to benefit from the rich visual information contained in the formatting of math-heavy scientific content. To study this, we select human-written proof and prose passages from pre-2022 arXiv LaTeX sources and re-generate them using 15 modern LLMs within the context of the same paper. This leaves us with coupled human and AI outputs in three different representations: the LaTeX source, a rendered image of the PDF, and the text extracted from the PDF. We find that the accuracy of the state-of-the-art Pangram 4 drops significantly as the mathematical content in a passage increases, especially when evaluated on text as opposed to LaTeX. However, this is not an inherent limitation of detecting AI-generated math: finetuning Qwen-3.5-27B based detectors on this domain recovers the gap, with LaTeX inputs performing significantly better than text since they preserve more formatting information. Since LaTeX sources are rarely available in practice, we also train SnapJudge: an image-based detector operating on screenshots of both prose and proofs from papers. At 0.1% false positive rate, SnapJudge achieves a detection rate of 92% on proofs and 93% on prose generated by frontier models, substantially outperforming both Pangram 4 and our text-based detector. We release our data, models and code at https://nlplab.cs.duke.edu/snapjudge.
中文翻译
随着大型语言模型在生成和辅助科学写作方面的能力不断增强，研究中使用AI的透明度与披露需求日益迫切。因此，催生了一个专门的研究子领域及多项商业服务，旨在自动标记AI生成内容。然而，现有仅依赖PDF提取文本的检测器无法利用数学密集型科学内容中丰富的视觉信息。为此，我们选取了2022年前arXiv LaTeX源文件中的人类撰写证明与叙述段落，并使用15个现代LLM在同一篇论文上下文中重新生成。由此获得三种不同表示形式的成对人类与AI输出：LaTeX源码、PDF渲染图像以及从PDF提取的文本。我们发现，当段落中数学内容增加时，最先进的Pangram 4检测器准确率显著下降，尤其是在仅使用文本而非LaTeX进行评估时。但这并非AI生成数学内容检测的固有局限；对Qwen-3.5-27B检测器在此领域进行微调后，可恢复性能差距，且LaTeX输入表现显著优于文本输入，因其保留了更多格式信息。由于LaTeX源码在实际中很少可用，我们还训练了SnapJudge：一种基于图像的检测器，可处理论文中叙述与证明的截图。在0.1%误报率下，SnapJudge对前沿模型生成的证明和叙述的检测率分别达到92%和93%，显著优于Pangram 4和我们的文本基检测器。我们已公开数据、模型与代码：https://nlplab.cs.duke.edu/snapjudge。

[2610.20859]
Does Morphological Correctness Matter for Tokenization? A Scrambled Control Study in Tagalog
形态正确性对分词重要吗？塔加洛语中的打乱对照研究
Typology-aware tokenizers are motivated by the claim that segmentation which respects morpheme boundaries preserves information that frequency-driven segmentation destroys. Studies routinely establish that it helps while assuming the boundaries must be linguistically correct. We separate them. Tagalog marks voice with infixes (s-um-ulat 'wrote', s-in-ulat 'was written'), so the root is not contiguous and no sequence of BPE merges can recover it. We build MorphAustro-Tok, a tokenizer that emits the root as a contiguous unit followed by a positional infix token, and compare it against a corpus-matched BPE baseline and, at two of three fertility levels, against a scrambled-morpheme control that preserves morph count, invertibility and per-type consistency while randomising the pseudo-infix. Across 33 runs, MorphAustro-Tok improves Tagalog machine translation over matched BPE by 0.84, 1.49 and 3.40 mean sentence-level chrF++ points. Token count does not explain the gain, since it grows as segmentation coarsens, and neither does capacity: at the coarsest level the scrambled arm carries more parameters and scores lower. Scrambling the analysis moves the gain by less than between-seed dispersion at both controlled levels, so we detect no contribution from morphological correctness at this scale.
中文翻译
基于类型学的分词器源于一个主张：尊重词素边界的分词能保留频率驱动分词所破坏的信息。现有研究普遍认为这有帮助，但通常假设边界必须在语言学上正确。我们对此进行了分离检验。塔加洛语通过中缀标记语气（如 s-um-ulat '写'，s-in-ulat '被写'），因此词根不连续，任何BPE合并序列都无法恢复。我们构建了MorphAustro-Tok分词器，将词根作为连续单元输出，并附加位置中缀标记，与匹配语料的BPE基线以及在三个生育水平中的两个水平上的打乱词素对照组（保持词素数量、可逆性和类型一致性，但随机化伪中缀）进行比较。在33次实验中，MorphAustro-Tok在塔加洛语机器翻译上分别比匹配BPE高出0.84、1.49和3.40的平均句级chrF++得分。词数增长并不能解释这一提升，因为随着分词粗化，词数反而上升；容量也不成因：在最粗粒度级别，打乱组参数更多但得分更低。打乱分析带来的变化小于受控水平间的种子差异，因此在该尺度下未检测到形态正确性的贡献。
👤 Abdul Rehman Antall, Baidya Nath Saha 📅 2026-10-14 🔗 arXiv
[2610.20830]
PEDBench: Can Speech Models Detect Phonetic Errors?
PEDBench：语音模型能否检测语音错误？
Phonetic error detection (PED) aims to identify phoneme-level substitutions, deletions, and insertions and is essential for educational and clinical speech assessment and intervention. Despite recent advances in speech models for phoneme recognition, their ability to reliably detect fine-grained phonetic errors remains unclear because of a lack of high-quality evaluation data. To address this gap, we introduce PEDBench, the first PED benchmark spanning a continuum from natural to controlled speech. It comprises real-world recordings of spoken errors made by children (N=334), scripted error productions by human actors (N=1,188), and synthetic error productions generated using phone-conditioned text-to-speech (N=1,083). All phonetic annotations were transcribed and verified by two phonetically trained annotators. Our evaluation of 13 state-of-the-art speech models shows that they fail to reliably detect phonetic errors, even under controlled conditions with acted and synthetic speech, where acoustic variability is reduced. The models also exhibit low precision, frequently misclassifying correctly pronounced phonemes as errors. Our speech-language models, fine-tuned on synthetic data, outperform commercial and open-source baselines on word-level PED, although a sim-to-real domain gap remains. We plan to publicly release our code and recipes, along with our acted and synthetic datasets, to support the development of more accurate PED systems.
中文翻译
语音错误检测（PED）旨在识别音素层面的替换、删除和插入，对于教育和临床语音评估与干预至关重要。尽管语音模型在音素识别方面取得进展，但其可靠检测细粒度语音错误的能力仍不明确，主要由于高质量评估数据的缺乏。为填补这一空白，我们引入PEDBench，首个覆盖从自然到受控语音连续谱的PED基准。它包含儿童真实语音错误录音（N=334）、人类演员的脚本化错误发音（N=1,188）以及使用音素条件文本转语音生成的合成错误发音（N=1,083）。所有语音标注均由两名接受过语音训练的标注员转录并验证。我们对13个最先进的语音模型进行评估，发现它们即使在受控条件下（如表演和合成语音，声学变异性降低）也难以可靠检测语音错误。模型还表现出低精度，常将正确发音误判为错误。我们在合成数据上微调的语音-语言模型在词级PED上优于商业和开源基线，但仍存在模拟到真实场景的域差距。我们计划公开发布代码、训练配方以及表演和合成数据集，以支持更精确PED系统的发展。

Wolof-Adapted Industry-Ready Open-Weight Language Models
沃洛夫语适配的工业就绪开源权重语言模型
Wolof is spoken by 23M people, mostly in Senegal, but remains weakly supported by open-weight language models, while API models raise cost, governance, and sovereignty constraints for local deployments. In this paper, we present 0.6B-14B open-weight models adapted to Wolof through instruction tuning, preference alignment, and model merging. After presenting the pipeline and data, extensive experiments on 63~public and in-house tasks show good performance on Wolof understanding and generation while remaining effective on other languages and cross-lingual skills, with the 8B and 14B models being particularly competitive with substantially larger commercial systems. The models are currently being evaluated in internal customer-care pilots and are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/collections/Orange/wolof-lm .

[2610.20630]
A Large-Scale Dataset for Multi-Class Financial News Classification in Brazilian Portuguese
面向巴西葡萄牙语多类别金融新闻分类的大规模数据集
The development of Financial Natural Language Processing (NLP) systems for Brazilian Portuguese is hindered by the scarcity of domain-specific datasets. To address this, we present a large-scale corpus of approximately 160,000 Brazilian Portuguese financial news articles collected from web sources. To establish initial benchmarks for a weakly supervised multi-class classification approach based on semantic similarity, we annotated subsets of this corpus both manually and using a Large Language Model (LLM). We evaluated three similarity-based methods -- cosine similarity, K-Nearest Neighbors (KNN), and FAISS-based nearest-neighbor search -- using multilingual semantic embeddings. Our experiments reveal that the task is highly sensitive to label quality and class distribution. On the manually labeled subset, KNN achieved the best performance (47.3% accuracy, 31.9% F1-score), closely followed by FAISS. Conversely, on the LLM-annotated subset, while accuracy reached approximately 50%, F1-scores dropped to near 20%, highlighting the negative impact of label imbalance and noisy weak annotations. These findings establish foundational baselines for a challenging task, underscoring the critical need for high-quality manual annotations. Ultimately, this dataset and our preliminary results provide a reference point for future research in Brazilian Portuguese Financial NLP.

[2610.22333]
Evaluation Awareness Beyond English: Do LLMs Act Differently When Tested in Indic Languages?
超越英语的评估意识：当在印地语系语言中测试时，LLMs的行为是否不同？
Large language models (LLMs) can often tell when they are being tested. This ability, known as evaluation awareness, can lead models to act more cautiously during safety evaluations than in real use. Existing work has studied evaluation awareness almost entirely in English, although models serve users in many languages. Indic languages are spoken by over a billion people, yet evaluation awareness remains almost entirely unstudied in them. We test four instruction-tuned models from three families in five Indic languages alongside English. A set of cues varies whether the model is told that it is monitored and whether the monitoring is a safety test. Models refuse harmful requests more often when they are told that they are being tested. This increase appears in all four models and all six languages. In Indic languages, refusal of harmful requests rises by 14.4 percentage points on average. The test cue also brings Indic refusal of harmful requests level with English, but raises refusal of benign Indic requests from 6.9% to 11.3%. We release all 47,880 judged responses to support further work. These results suggest that safety evaluations in Indic languages may not reflect how models treat their everyday users.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）常常能察觉自己正在被测试。这种能力被称为评估意识，可能导致模型在安全评估中表现得比实际使用时更加谨慎。现有研究几乎完全集中在英语环境下探讨评估意识，尽管模型服务于多种语言的用户。印地语系语言使用者超过十亿人，但其评估意识仍几乎未被研究。我们在五种印地语系语言及英语中测试了来自三个模型家族的四种指令微调模型。通过设置提示，判断模型是否被告知正在被监控，以及监控是否为安全测试。当模型被告知正在被测试时，它们拒绝有害请求的频率更高。这一现象在所有四个模型和六种语言中均出现。在印地语系语言中，拒绝有害请求的比例平均上升14.4个百分点。测试提示还使印地语系模型对有害请求的拒绝水平达到与英语相当的程度，但同时将对良性印地语请求的拒绝率从6.9%提高到11.3%。我们公开了全部47,880条经过评判的响应，以支持后续研究。这些结果表明，印地语系语言中的安全评估可能无法反映模型对日常用户的实际处理方式。
👤 Trizal Garg, Sibayan Mitra, Arya Karanjkar, Advait Singh, Jagat Sesh Challa et al. 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.22043]
Momentum, Commentary Tone, and Earnings Surprise in LLM Financial Forecasts
动量、评论语气与LLM财务预测中的收益意外
Which inputs drive a language model's financial forecast? We construct synthetic earnings announcements that independently reverse 20-day momentum, non-informational commentary tone, and whether fixed actual EPS beats a manipulated consensus. Six instruction/chat checkpoint conversions from four families (0.49-7.61B base parameters) receive 2,016 prompts each under a direct-answer Q8_0 protocol. The design includes 24 numerical blocks, six commentary pairs, profits and losses, rising and falling annual EPS, and two surprise sizes. Both A/B answer assignments and U/D cover all 384 primary scenarios per conversion. We estimate matched changes in conditional answer-prefix scores, not calibrated market probabilities. Across these checkpoints, the primary EPS contrast ranges from -0.19 to 86.80 percentage points. The magnitude of the primary signed tone contrast exceeds that of EPS in 5 of six models, and momentum exceeds it in 5. Natural-word numerical diagnostic accuracy ranges from 51.0 to 95.8 percent. Answer-encoding, wording, context, and conversion controls qualify these results. The experiment measures observable input reliance and numerical recognition; it establishes neither an internal reasoning mechanism nor realized return predictability. Public benchmark prompts, recorded scores, and an interactive recorded-results explorer accompany the study.

哪些输入驱动语言模型的财务预测？我们构建了合成的收益公告，独立地反转20天动量、非信息性评论语气以及实际EPS是否超过人为操纵的共识。来自四个模型家族（基础参数量0.49-7.61B）的六种指令/聊天检查点，在直接回答Q8_0协议下各接收2,016个提示。实验设计包含24个数值模块、六组评论对、盈亏情况、逐年上升或下降的EPS，以及两种意外程度。每种转换下均覆盖所有384个主要场景的A/B答案分配和U/D设置。我们估计的是条件答案前缀得分的变化，而非校准后的市场概率。在这些检查点中，主要EPS对比范围为-0.19至86.80个百分点。在六个模型中有五个中，主要语气对比的绝对值超过EPS对比；在五个模型中，动量对比超过EPS。自然词数值诊断准确率在51.0%到95.8%之间。答案编码、措辞、上下文和转换控制确保了结果的有效性。该实验衡量的是可观察输入依赖性和数值识别能力，但并未揭示内部推理机制或实现回报可预测性。研究附带公开基准提示、记录分数及交互式结果探索工具。

[2610.21518]
SCRIBE: Scalable Contextual Representations of Interpretable Brain-Grounded Semantics
SCRIBE：可扩展的可解释脑基语义上下文表示
Understanding how the human brain constructs meaning from real-world language remains a fundamental challenge, as semantics depend on context that varies in natural settings. To enable the study of real-world linguistic meaning in the brain, we introduce SCRIBE, a framework grounded in a neuroscience-informed semantic space that leverages an ensemble of open-source large language models to generate interpretable, word-level semantic representations of arbitrary text. In a behavioral study with 921 participants, we show that human semantic feature judgments vary systematically with context, and that SCRIBE captures these shifts, reaching 75-81% of the estimated human noise ceiling. Using brain activity recorded while participants listen to naturalistic stories, we further demonstrate that SCRIBE predicts neural responses in language-selective brain regions, significantly outperforming non-contextual semantic representations. Variance partitioning reveals that SCRIBE explains unique brain-predictive information beyond syntactic features, suggesting that its representations capture aspects of linguistic meaning not accounted for by the measured syntactic features. Together, these findings establish SCRIBE as a scalable and interpretable foundation for studying natural language semantics in the brain.
中文翻译
理解人类大脑如何从现实世界语言中构建意义仍是根本挑战，因为语义依赖于自然环境中变化的上下文。为了促进大脑中真实语言意义的研究，我们提出了SCRIBE，一种基于神经科学启发的语义空间的框架，利用一组开源大语言模型生成任意文本的可解释、词级语义表示。在包含921名参与者的心理行为研究中，我们发现人类语义特征判断系统性地随上下文变化，而SCRIBE能够捕捉这些变化，达到估计的人类噪声上限的75%-81%。利用参与者聆听自然叙事时记录的大脑活动，我们进一步证明SCRIBE能有效预测语言选择性脑区的神经反应，显著优于非上下文语义表示。方差分解显示，SCRIBE解释了超出句法特征的独特脑预测信息，表明其表示捕捉到了测量句法特征未涵盖的语言意义方面。综合来看，这些发现确立了SCRIBE作为研究大脑中自然语言语义的可扩展且可解释的基础。
👤 Camila Kolling, Omer Moussa, Emin Çelik, Mariya Toneva 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.21508]
Decomposing Brain-LLM Alignment with Interpretable Semantic Representations
基于可解释语义表示的脑-大语言模型对齐分解
Large language models (LLMs) predict neural responses during naturalistic language comprehension, but the linguistic information underlying this brain alignment remains difficult to interpret. We use SCRIBE, an interpretable contextual semantic representation spanning 65 neurocognitively grounded semantic properties, as a lens for decomposing the brain alignment of one LLM, LLaMA2-7B. Using variance partitioning between LLaMA2-7B and SCRIBE on naturalistic fMRI data from seven participants listening to narrative stories (Moth Radio Hour), we separate brain-predictive variance shared between the two representations from variance unique to each. We find that shared variance is the dominant component across the whole brain and language-related regions, while both representations also retain significant unique contributions. We then examine whether the concreteness of individual semantic properties relates to shared and SCRIBE-unique brain-predictive variance. Concrete properties show greater shared variance with LLaMA2-7B than abstract properties across the whole brain and most examined regions, whereas abstract properties do not show greater SCRIBE-unique variance. Finally, property ablations reveal that semantic contributions extend across multiple dimensions, with cumulative effects on both shared and SCRIBE-unique variance. Together, these results show that, for LLaMA2-7B on this dataset, a substantial portion of brain-LLM alignment is associated with interpretable semantic information, while revealing complementary brain-predictive semantic information beyond the LLaMA2-7B representation.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）能够预测自然语言理解过程中的神经响应，但这种脑-模型对齐背后的语言信息仍难以解释。我们使用SCRIBE——一种涵盖65个神经认知基础语义属性的可解释上下文语义表示——作为分析工具，对LLaMA2-7B模型的脑-模型对齐进行分解。基于七名参与者聆听叙事故事（Moth Radio Hour）的自然主义fMRI数据，通过LLaMA2-7B与SCRIBE之间的方差分解，我们将两者共享的脑预测方差与各自独有的方差分离开来。结果发现，在全脑及语言相关区域中，共享方差是主导成分，而两种表示也均保留了显著的独有贡献。随后我们考察了单个语义属性的具体性是否与共享及SCRIBE独有的脑预测方差相关。结果显示，具体属性在全脑和大多数区域中与LLaMA2-7B的共享方差更大，而抽象属性并未表现出更强的SCRIBE独有方差。最后，属性消融实验表明，语义贡献跨越多个维度，对共享和SCRIBE独有方差均有累积效应。综上，这些结果表明，在该数据集上，LLaMA2-7B的大部分脑-模型对齐与可解释的语义信息相关，同时揭示了超越LLaMA2-7B表示的互补性脑预测语义信息。

[2610.21261]
Behavioral Compositionality of Sparse Autoencoder Representations
稀疏自编码器表示的行为组合性
Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) represent model activations with sparse, linearly decoded features, but the downstream effects of those features need not compose additively. We introduce SAE Additivity Residuals (SAR), which measure the behavioral variation left unexplained above a chosen interaction order. Under independent feature subset sampling, population SAR is exactly the normalized energy of interactions above that order and bounds coalition ordering error for the optimal low order approximation. To distinguish compositionality associated with the SAE representation from that induced by downstream computation, we compare learned SAE coordinates with sum preserving random rotations of the same selected subspace and find lower first order residuals for the learned coordinates across all 18 model and behavior settings. The coordinates are nevertheless not fully additive: pairwise terms improve held out coalition ordering in every setting and outperform parameter matched models using randomly selected third order terms. In a combined steering experiment, pairwise modeling raises rank fidelity from 0.5874 to 0.7790 and reduces ordering error from 0.1444 to 0.0606. Together, these results show that SAE coordinates support comparatively compositional behavioral descriptions while faithful joint explanations and interventions still require interactions beyond individual feature effects. Code is available at https://github.com/OpenSAE/SAE-Behavioral-Compositionality.
中文翻译
稀疏自编码器（SAEs）以稀疏且线性解码的特征来表示模型激活，但这些特征的下游效应未必具有可加性。我们引入了SAE可加性残差（SAR），用于衡量在选定交互阶数之上未被解释的行为变化。在独立特征子集采样下，总体SAR恰好等于该阶数以上交互的归一化能量，并界定了最优低阶近似中的联盟排序误差。为区分SAE表示本身带来的组合性与下游计算引发的组合性，我们将学习到的SAE坐标与同一子空间的保和随机旋转进行比较，发现所有18种模型和行为设置下，学习到的坐标具有更低的一阶残差。然而，这些坐标并非完全可加：成对项在每种设置中均提升了保留联盟的排序表现，且优于使用随机选择的三阶项的参数匹配模型。在联合操控实验中，成对建模将排名保真度从0.5874提升至0.7790，排序误差从0.1444降至0.0606。综合结果表明，SAE坐标支持相对可组合的行为描述，但忠实的联合解释与干预仍需超越单个特征效应的交互作用。代码已公开于https://github.com/OpenSAE/SAE-Behavioral-Compositionality。
👤 Haotian Ma, Ziye Mei, Frederic Sala 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv
[2610.21256]
Isolating a Language Model's Judgment with a Label-Free Subspace
通过无标签子空间隔离语言模型的判断
Language models are often asked to make judgments: whether a proposed answer is correct, whether one statement follows logically from another, or whether a response is safe. Yet changing the instructions that specify how the judgment should be expressed can make the final answer less reliable, even for the same input. We ask whether this reflects a change in internal judgment or a failure to express it. Comparing hidden-state vectors for the same input under different instructions, we find that their differences concentrate in a low-dimensional expression subspace. We identify this subspace without benchmark labels and remove its contribution from a linear predictor to obtain an isolated judgment. Across correctness, entailment, and safety, the isolated judgment remains reliable under instructions not used to identify the subspace, even when the model's final answer and the original predictor degrade. When the judgment question is negated, isolation raises the predictor's AUROC from 0.504 to 0.825 for entailment and from 0.480 to 0.891 for safety. We also find that judgment is readable in earlier layers, before the expression subspace begins to disrupt the predictor. Together, these results provide a way to separate judgment from expression and recover internal judgments even when the model's final answers are unreliable.
中文翻译
语言模型常被要求做出判断：如某个答案是否正确、一个陈述是否逻辑上蕴含另一个，或响应是否安全。然而，改变指定判断表达方式的指令可能导致最终答案可靠性下降，即使输入相同。我们探讨这是否反映了内部判断的变化，还是表达失败。通过比较同一输入在不同指令下的隐藏状态向量，发现其差异集中在低维表达子空间中。我们无需基准标签即可识别该子空间，并从线性预测器中移除其贡献以获得孤立的判断。在正确性、蕴含关系和安全性任务中，孤立后的判断在未用于识别子空间的指令下依然可靠，即使模型最终答案和原始预测器性能下降。当判断问题被否定时，孤立使蕴含关系的预测器AUROC从0.504提升至0.825，安全性从0.480提升至0.891。我们还发现，判断信号可在早期层读取，在表达子空间开始干扰预测器之前即已存在。这些结果提供了一种将判断与表达分离的方法，即使模型最终输出不可靠，也能恢复其内部判断。

[2610.21240]
Representation Redistribution in Sparse Autoencoders
稀疏自编码器中的表示再分配
Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose language model activations into sparse features, but prompt transformations designed to preserve the underlying request can redistribute activity across them. A feature change observed on one transformed prompt need not recur, and even a recurring change need not affect a downstream computation. We introduce the redistribution sensitivity score (RSS), which identifies large feature responses that recur in a consistent direction, and evaluate the resulting supports on variants excluded from selection. Across 27 pretrained SAEs spanning eight language models, the tested prompt transformations produce a reproducible redistribution profile on these excluded variants. We then separate recurring representation change from signed readout contribution and decision failure. For a fixed linear readout, an exact decomposition shows how response magnitude, readout coefficients, directional alignment, and the clean margin determine downstream consequences. In two frozen safety readouts, combining RSS support erosion with the full clean margin yields recurrent failure AUROCs of 0.935 and 0.958 using median supports of 20 and 19 features, compared with 0.965 and 0.964 using full erosion. RSS therefore provides a way to identify recurring sparse representation changes and quantify their contribution to a downstream readout without equating representation change with failure. Code is available at https://github.com/OpenSAE/Redistribution-Sensitivity-Score
中文翻译
稀疏自编码器（SAEs）将语言模型激活分解为稀疏特征，但旨在保持底层请求不变的提示变换可能重新分配这些特征间的活动。某一变换提示下观察到的特征变化未必重复出现，即使重复，也不一定影响下游计算。我们提出再分配敏感度评分（RSS），用于识别在一致方向上反复出现的大特征响应，并评估排除选择的变体上的相应支持。在涵盖八种语言模型的27个预训练SAE中，测试的提示变换在这些排除变体上产生了可重现的再分配模式。随后，我们将重复的表示变化与符号读出贡献及决策失败区分开来。对于固定线性读出，精确分解显示响应幅度、读出系数、方向对齐以及干净边际如何决定下游后果。在两个冻结的安全读出中，结合RSS支持衰减与完整干净边际，使用中位20和19个特征的支持，得到的重复失败AUROC分别为0.935和0.958，而使用完整衰减则分别为0.965和0.964。因此，RSS提供了一种识别重复稀疏表示变化并量化其对下游读出贡献的方法，而不将表示变化等同于失败。代码已公开于https://github.com/OpenSAE/Redistribution-Sensitivity-Score。

[2610.20906]
Task-Centric Multimodal Learning for Cognitive Impairment Detection
面向认知障碍检测的任务中心多模态学习
Dementia affects millions of older adults worldwide, yet early detection remains difficult outside clinical settings. Traditional models primarily rely on text or voice-based responses, capturing only a limited view of human cognition despite the inherently multimodal nature of human expression. This work proposes a contactless, multimodal screening framework that leverages video, audio, and text signals collected during structured voice-based interactions with Amazon Alexa. By incorporating these modalities, our approach captures a more holistic representation of human behavior encompassing speech patterns, vocal characteristics, facial expressions, and linguistic structure, which may enable more accurate detection of cognitive decline. Different participants completed three conversational tasks designed to probe spontaneous speech, comprehension, and recall. We pursued three objectives: (1) evaluate which of the three Alexa tasks provides the most informative cognitive signals, (2) compare the effectiveness of unimodal models, and (3) assess the benefits of multimodal fusion models. Task-level analysis shows that the video model achieves its best performance on Command-Reading, with an accuracy of 0.65 (AUC 0.69), while the audio model performs best on Question-Answering, reaching an accuracy of 0.67 (AUC 0.73). In contrast, the multimodal model demonstrates consistently stable performance across tasks, achieving an accuracy of 0.76 (AUC 0.75) on Command-Reading and 0.76 (AUC 0.73) on Question-Answering. These findings demonstrate that multimodal behavioral cues captured through naturalistic interactions can reliably distinguish MCI from healthy aging, highlighting the limitations of traditional diagnostic paradigms and supporting the shift toward multimodal, real-world cognitive assessment frameworks.

[2610.20877]
CogDrift-4D: Hierarchical Multimodal Modeling of Temporal Behavioral Decay for MCI Detection
CogDrift-4D：面向 MCI 检测的时序行为衰减的分层多模态建模
Early detection of mild cognitive impairment remains a critical bottleneck in dementia care, demanding scalable tools capable of capturing subtle behavioral decay. To capture the subtle temporal changes of cognitive decline, we introduce a behavioral dataset collected across seven unscripted multimodal interactions over an 18-month horizon. This dataset formalizes continuous, multi-session interactions into a dense 4D tensor (Batch * Session * Time * Feature) that explicitly preserves concurrent local dynamics alongside long-term changes. To effectively process these complex behavioral patterns, we propose CogDrift-4D: a novel, hierarchical 4D Transformer framework. To capture the complex trajectory of cognitive decline, CogDrift-4D implements a dual-level behavioral fatigue and drift modeling architecture. At the intra-session level, the model computes localized least-squares regression slopes to quantify the acute ``fading'' of frame-level facial action units and acoustic energy. At the inter-session level, a recursive cross-attention feedback encoder acts as a cumulative fatigue accumulator, mapping the patient's continuous behavioral drift across successive sessions. To mitigate environmental noise and track failures across sessions, a localized Quality Gate dynamically filters low-integrity features before final fusion, also an early fusion that integrates semantic insights with raw physical dynamics. Evaluated on structured verbal and linguistic interactions, our architecture successfully detects MCI with a classification accuracy of 0.733 +- 0.13 and an F1-score of 0.716 +- 0.14, outperforming advanced baselines. The framework provides a mathematically rigorous, scalable, and robust foundation for home-based, real-time dementia screening.
中文翻译
轻度认知障碍的早期检测仍是痴呆症护理中的关键瓶颈，亟需能够捕捉细微行为衰减的可扩展工具。为捕捉认知衰退的微妙时间变化，我们引入了一个历时 18 个月、涵盖七次非脚本化多模态交互的行为数据集。该数据集将连续、多会话交互形式化为密集的四维张量（批量 * 会话 * 时间 * 特征），显式保留局部动态与长期变化。为有效处理这些复杂的行为模式，我们提出 CogDrift-4D：一种新颖的分层四维 Transformer 框架。为捕捉认知衰退的复杂轨迹，CogDrift-4D 实现了双层级行为疲劳与漂移建模架构。在会话内层级，模型计算局部最小二乘回归斜率，量化帧级面部动作单元和声能的急性“衰减”；在会话间层级，递归交叉注意力反馈编码器充当累积疲劳累加器，映射患者在连续会话中的持续行为漂移。为减轻环境噪声并跟踪跨会话的失败，局部质量门动态过滤低完整性特征，同时实现语义洞察与原始物理动态的早期融合。在结构化口头和语言交互上的评估表明，该架构成功检测 MCI，分类准确率为 0.733 ± 0.13，F1 得分为 0.716 ± 0.14，优于先进基线。该框架为家庭环境中基于实时痴呆筛查提供了数学严谨、可扩展且鲁棒的基础。
